# ClawVille — Architecture

> **Strict rule:** every code change that adds, removes, or repurposes a route,
> middleware, service, table, event type, or deploy mechanic MUST update this
> doc in the same diff. Reverse holds too. Mismatch is a bug.
>
> Companions: `WorldContent.md` (scene manifest), `3dStructure.md` (3D specs),
> `GameFeatures.md` (gameplay reference). This doc is the **tech** spine — if
> a question is "where does X happen in code", the answer is on this page.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-08 (9): **Durable autonomy — server-side re-enrollment so deploys are invisible to away-agents (§B.1, founder-directed).** A browser-closed persisting Autonomous agent (D6, 24h TTL) was re-enrolled into `agentAutonomyDriver` ONLY by the CLIENT keepalive after an API restart — so every production deploy silently killed away-users' agents until they returned. FIX: PERSIST the enrollment intent + RECONCILE it server-side. **New durable flag** `openclaw_bots.autonomy_enrolled` (boolean, default false; additive idempotent DDL `migrations-manual/2026-07-08_add_openclaw_autonomy_enrolled.sql`, NO db:push): SET true by `activateAutonomyForOwner` AFTER a successful enroll (by `agent_id`); cleared on EVERY teardown — `deactivateAutonomyForOwner` clears it FIRST + by `user_id` (crash-safe ordering: a crash after the clear can never re-enroll a user who left; keyed by userId so logout tears down intent even when the in-memory entry is absent post-restart), and the 24h TTL sweep + `/disconnect` (`agent-session-sweeper.ts`) clear it ATOMICALLY in their mark-swept `SET`. **New `services/agent-autonomy-reconcile.ts`** `reconcileDurableAutonomy()`: driven by the driver on tick 1 (~30s post-boot) + every `RECONCILE_EVERY_N_TICKS` (10 × 30s ≈ 5 min, also heals a crash mid-teardown), LAZY-imported by the driver to avoid a static import cycle (reconcile→activation→driver). It queries `autonomy_enrolled=true AND is_house=false AND session_expires_at > now()` (live TTL, fail-closed on null) and for each owner NOT already `isOwnerEnrolled` and NOT currently `isAgentHumanControlled`, calls the SAME idempotent `activateAutonomyForOwner` the client toggle uses (re-mints the §B.2 session, respells the deterministic `ocb-` body, enrolls) — NO client involvement. Respects `MAX_AUTONOMOUS_USER_AGENTS`: over-cap rows STAY flagged + retry next pass (never silently cleared); a module-level overlap guard prevents a slow pass stacking. **Cannot reproduce the old boot-rehydrate double-body/override-lockout:** it re-enrolls ONLY hosted-avatar (nanoclaw, self-managed, AVATAR-mode) sessions via the DETERMINISTIC `ocb-<base64url(agentId)>` id (idempotent re-register, no second copy — never override-mode / partner bodies); `activateAutonomyForOwner` runs `releaseHumanControlledOpenClaw` (no stale-suppression freeze); and a currently-human-driving owner is SKIPPED (the human's suppressed proxy is authoritative, their next flip re-enrolls). The client 5-min keepalive stays as a belt for the live-browser case. Gate: db pkg rebuilt; api `tsc --noEmit` 0; `agent-autonomy-reconcile.test.ts` + `agent-autonomy-activation.test.ts §9` (34 across 2 files) + full `src/services/__tests__` **1040/1040**; api build exit 0. Codex adversarial pass on the money-path + new DB-state diff REQUIRED before staging→master. PARITY note: unchanged — settlement still binds to the owner's avatar via `world-teacher-chat` once-per-day probes; `[ACTION:]`/`PROTOCOL_VERSION` untouched. Founder's real test: activate → kill the client entirely → restart api → agent resumes settling with NO client. Prior audit follows.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-08 (8): **`/me/agent-session` reports mode:'hosted' for a hosted avatar-agent at ALL times (§B.2 label fix — founder-directed).** After §B.2 mints a hosted avatar-agent's internal tool-surface session (an `openclaw_bots` row keyed to the owner), `GET /api/auth/me/agent-session` mis-reported **mode:'external-active'** instead of **'hosted'** — the bot-row branch matched the minted row before the hosted classification could, misrepresenting the user's OWN hosted agent as a BYO/external connection. FIX: the hot-branch classification is extracted into a PURE, DB-free, unit-tested `services/agent-session-classify.ts` (`classifyAgentSessionHot` + `isHostedAvatarAgentSessionRow` + the shared `hostedAgentSessionResponse`), and `routes/auth.ts` now calls it (the deliberate lazy `is_guest` cold-read is preserved via a `cold-fallthrough` sentinel — hot branches still pay nothing). A minted hosted session is identified WITHIN the user's own account by THREE conjuncts (Codex-hardened 2026-07-08): the row's `agentId` IS the user's avatar-agent's `platform_agents.id` (`avatars.platformAgentId` — a UUID we generate) AND the row's `identityType` is exactly what the §B.2 mint writes (`HOSTED_AVATAR_IDENTITY_TYPE` = 'nanoclaw') AND the avatar's harness ∈ HOSTED_HARNESSES. The identityType conjunct closes a Codex-flagged gap: `agentId` + `identityType` are both caller-suppliable at `POST /api/agent/connect` (identityType is a public enum that includes 'nanoclaw'), so a HARNESS-only discriminator could mislabel a same-user BYO agent (matching agentId, non-nanoclaw identity) as hosted and mask its dead state. Cross-user is impossible (the query is keyed by the AUTHED user's own id); the sole residual is a user self-spoofing all three conjuncts of their OWN label (zero security/economy impact). Such a row SHORT-CIRCUITS to the hosted response (connected:true, mode:'hosted', expiresAt/lastSeenAt null) BEFORE the expired/idle/active logic — a hosted agent's cognition runtime is always alive server-side, independent of the tool-surface session's TTL. The two 'hosted' returns (pre-mint no-bot branch, fix 30352e60, and the post-mint short-circuit) share ONE builder so they can't drift. **GENUINE BYO/external rows are BYTE-IDENTICAL** (agentId ≠ platformAgentId, or a non-hosted harness → the unchanged external-active/idle/expired logic). Gate: api `tsc --noEmit` 0; `agent-session-classify.test.ts` 15/15 (both directions + pre-mint + hosted-beats-TTL + dismissed + cold-fallthrough + the same-user BYO-collision case) + auth/session suites 127/127; api build exit 0. Codex adversarial review 2 rounds → **NO BLOCKING ISSUES** (round 1 flagged the discriminator gap; the identityType conjunct closed it). PARITY note: pure label/reporting fix — no economy/settlement path touched; `[ACTION:]` whitelist + `PROTOCOL_VERSION` untouched (no bump). Prior audit follows.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-08 (7): **User-owned agents ride the FULL autonomy driver (§B.1 — closes the model-doc §4 full-scope gap).** Flipping **Autonomous** now enrolls a signup user's HOSTED avatar-agent into the SAME `agentAutonomyDriver` the house fleet rides (perceive→decide→**act via the real `[ACTION:]` whitelist**→settle REAL CT + leaderboard to the OWNER's avatar via `world-teacher-chat`→teacher loop + earned-skill folding) — previously a user got only the thinner `avatar-simulation-bridge`. **TRIGGER:** NEW `POST /api/world/autonomy { active:boolean }` (`routes/world.ts`) — Lucia-cookie authed, idempotent, 10/min/IP; the agent identity is derived SERVER-SIDE from the owner's active avatar (body carries ONLY the boolean → no force-enroll/force-spend of another user's agent); response NEVER carries a bearer (no-bearer-refetch invariant untouched). **ENROLLMENT:** NEW `services/agent-autonomy-activation.ts` `activateAutonomyForOwner(userId)` resolves the active avatar → guards guest/no-avatar/no-`platformAgentId` → capacity pre-check → `ensureHostedAvatarAgentSession` (§B.2 session + deterministic `ocb-` body) → `agentAutonomyDriver.registerUserAgent`; `deactivateAutonomyForOwner` unenrolls + re-establishes suppression. `agent-autonomy-driver.ts` gains a PARALLEL `userAgents` registry (disjoint from `houseAgents` — teardown can NEVER touch the fleet), a per-entry `isHouse` flag threaded into the lazy runtime warm (`{ isHouse: entry.isHouse }`: user=`false` → `hosted-user` inference route + normal 30-min sweep, house stays `true`=fleet route + sweep-exempt, byte-identical), `registerUserAgent` (idempotent keepalive fast-path preserving the phase machine, one-enrollment-per-owner, typed capacity rejection), `unregisterUserAgent`, `isOwnerEnrolled`. **COST GUARDRAILS:** SEPARATE cap `MAX_AUTONOMOUS_USER_AGENTS` (env, default 12, floor 1 — never shares the house 64 cap); over-cap → LOUD HTTP **429 `autonomy_capacity`** (client toasts + reverts to Controlled), never a silent drop. Driver runs its own 30s interval (never the 200ms sim tick). **NB the `hosted-user` inference route DEFAULTS to OpenAI** (`inference-config.ts`) — local-model routing needs `INFERENCE_ROUTE_HOSTED_USER` on the box, so the cap is the load-bearing spend guard. **TWO-BODY SUPPRESSION (state machine):** on activate, `npcSimulation.releaseHumanControlledOpenClaw(userId, agentId)` (NEW public method — drops the launch binding + the immediate suppression until-entry) so the driver body isn't frozen by the UNCONDITIONAL 5 Hz `/api/world/position` `refreshHumanControlledOpenClawForUser` (which clears the body's A\* path); on deactivate, `bindHumanControlledOpenClawLaunch` + a 15s mark hide+freeze the `ocb-` body while the human drives. §B.2 session STAYS live on deactivate (D6). **MUTUAL EXCLUSION:** the heartbeat (`routes/avatars.ts`) SKIPS `bridge.register` for driver-enrolled owners (checked pre-branch AND inside the async avatar lookup — the C1 race) so the bridge never double-drives/double-credits. **RESTART (B3):** driver registry is process-memory; the client re-POSTs `{active:true}` on a 5-min keepalive so Autonomous survives an API restart. **LIFECYCLE TEARDOWN (money-path, 2026-07-08):** enrollment settles REAL CT, so it must never outlive its session. AUTHORITATIVE server-side teardown on the EXPLICIT exits: (1) `POST /api/auth/logout` calls `deactivateAutonomyForOwner(user.id)` BEFORE `lucia.invalidateSession` (cookie-independent — the client's fire-and-forget deactivate races the cookie kill and can't be the only guard); (2) the `agent-session-sweeper` 24h-TTL sweep calls `unregisterUserAgent(agentId)` on genuine expiry AND on a TTL-re-read failure (fail money-safe — a leaked enrollment settles CT, unlike a cosmetic leaked body). D6 preserved: a raw browser/tab close hits `/world/leave` (not `/logout`) and never expires the TTL, so the agent persists to its 24h TTL; idle-despawn (session stays live) never unenrolls. Client belt: `handleLogout` runs `resetStore()` (which fires the client deactivate) before `api.logout()`. **`[ACTION:]` whitelist / `skill-protocol.ts` / `PROTOCOL_VERSION` are UNTOUCHED** — enrollment reuses the existing executor, so NO partner-wire change and NO version bump (the mock-Hatcher harness stays green). Frontend: `stores/game.ts setControlMode` wires the toggle + keepalive + capacity toast. Gate: api `tsc --noEmit` 0; `agent-autonomy-activation.test.ts` 17/17 + full `src/services/__tests__` 996/996 green; api build exit 0. Codex adversarial pass on the session/money/suppression diff REQUIRED before staging→master. **PARITY note:** human path = Controlled mode drives the avatar (`/me/heartbeat`); agent path = `POST /api/world/autonomy` → the full driver acts + settles REAL CT to the SAME owner's avatar (`entry.avatarId`); settlement binds via the once-per-day `world-teacher-chat` probes, NOT the `[ACTION:]` parser. **Known limitation (3da):** visible double-body (driver `ocb-` body + the owner's local player avatar) in Autonomous is a render follow-up. Prior audit follows.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-08 (6): **Hosted avatar-agent internal bearer session (§B.2 — closes the E5 tool-surface parity gap).** A signup user's HOSTED avatar-agent (`platform_agents` type `'avatar-agent'`, bound to the owner's avatar via `avatars.platformAgentId`, provisioned by `avatar-agent-provisioning.ts`) previously had NO `openclaw_bots` row and so NO agent bearer session — it could not use the `/api/agent/:sessionId` tool surface (tools.json, events replay, Cove/Poker agent API) a BYO/Hatcher agent can. NEW `services/hosted-avatar-agent-session.ts` (+ pure `-plan.ts`) mints an INTERNAL, server-held bearer session on demand: `ensureHostedAvatarAgentSession(platformAgentId)` upserts a `nanoclaw`-identity `openclaw_bots` row bound to the OWNER (`userId`=owner, `is_house`=false, 24h sliding `session_expires_at`, atomic `session_key_hash`), registers a self-managed deterministic `ocb-<base64url(agentId)>` body, and holds the raw bearer in a PROCESS-LOCAL registry (`getHostedAvatarAgentBearer`) — NEVER emitted to the browser (the human plays the human-path via Controlled mode; this is the agent-path credential for server-side consumers: the §B.1 autonomy driver, the future §6 cognition adapter). agentId = the avatar-agent's `platform_agents.id` VERBATIM (opaque UUID, non-reserved — NO schema change). ledgerCapable=true AND boundUserId=owner (BOTH) so `resolveAgentSession` keeps it real-CT-capable (its rebind backstop still demotes on a userId change). Cognition stays on the `platform_agents` ElizaOS runtime — 'nanoclaw' is the no-outbound-gateway, restorable, self-managed identity (the house-agent precedent); the session is PURELY the tool-surface credential. Serialization = `withKeyedMutex(agentId)` + `pg_advisory_xact_lock` (distinct `hosted-avatar-agent:` key namespace), atomic hash in the SAME tx, commit-first-spawn-after (mirrors partner-hatcher). RESTART recovery = RE-MINT (registry wiped on restart → next consumer demand re-mints ledger-capable), which deliberately SIDESTEPS the restore path's non-ledger demotion of no-gateway types WITHOUT weakening that contract; because the bearer is never emitted, overwriting the row hash strands nothing. **NO partner-wire change** — 'nanoclaw' already exists; the bearer/TTL gate (`require-auth-or-agent.ts`), shared openclaw types, `[ACTION:]` whitelist, and `PROTOCOL_VERSION` are ALL untouched (no bump). TWO-BODY/trap-1: the `ocb-` body registers self-managed with the deterministic id so the existing human-control suppression + idle-despawn apply unchanged; the recommended TRIGGER (Autonomous-mode activation) is wired by §B.1, as is the `bindHumanControlledOpenClawLaunch` binding that suppresses the body while the human drives (a §B.1 dependency, flagged, NOT minted here). Gate: api tsc 0; `bun test hosted-avatar-agent-session.test.ts` 12/12 green (DB-free plan + sim mechanics; the DB upsert/advisory-lock rides the proven partner-hatcher pattern and is exercised on the staging wire). Codex adversarial round 1 (vs 28bf7abb): invariants 1-3,5-7 HOLD; FIXED — the lookup now binds ONLY the owner's ACTIVE avatar (mint == settlement target), and a post-commit `session_key_hash` recheck fences the tx-scoped-advisory-lock-released-before-registration gap belt-and-suspenders on top of the single-replica invariant (Codex confirmed NO CT-theft path; the gate rejects any stale bearer). Two-body/trap-1 is gated to the (unbuilt) §B.1 trigger. Codex re-review on the amended diff REQUIRED before staging→master; staging mock-Hatcher harness must stay GREEN (partner wire is byte-identical). PARITY note: human path = Controlled mode drives the same avatar; agent path = the internal bearer → `/api/agent/:sessionId/*` tool surface; settlement binds to the owner's avatar via `resolveAgentSession` (userId===boundUserId). Prior audit follows.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-08 (5): **Hosted-OpenClaw host-it-for-me — `openclaw-local` in-world cognition wire (`PROTOCOL_VERSION` 10→11; the exact mirror of D7 hermes-local).** The login promise "all three run hosted by ClawVille" now covers a connect-namespace OpenClaw agent: a GATEWAY-LESS `openclaw` connect (an openclaw agent that arrives WITHOUT its own `gatewayUrl`) has its reactive/ambient cognition served by a ClawVille-hosted local OpenClaw runtime when the operator flips `OPENCLAW_LOCAL_GATEWAY_ENABLED=true` (default off; `.env.example`). Design mirrors hermes-local exactly: `agent-session-config.ts` gains `OPENCLAW_LOCAL_GATEWAY_URL` (HARDCODED server-side `http://localhost:8643`, never caller-suppliable — the general SSRF guard on caller URLs is untouched) + the boot-time `OPENCLAW_LOCAL_GATEWAY_ENABLED` gate + `OPENCLAW_LOCAL_GATEWAY_KEY` (optional same-box bearer, unset ⇒ bare POST) + `OPENCLAW_LOCAL_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 10s, clamp [1s,30s]) — the last two mirroring the real-Hermes-deploy prerequisites (`HERMES_LOCAL_GATEWAY_KEY`/`HERMES_LOCAL_TIMEOUT_MS`), since the real OpenClaw gateway is assumed no friendlier. The openclaw identity adapter becomes `protocolKind:'openclaw-gated'` (ironclaw/custom stay `declared-gateway` — openclaw ONLY), and `resolveInWorldProtocol` routes a gateway-less openclaw to the new server-internal `'openclaw-local'` wire ONLY when the gate is on AND no real gateway is declared. **PRECEDENCE (pinned by tests):** a BYO openclaw WITH its own gateway is byte-identical under BOTH gate states — the hosted path never captures it (`pickProtocol` threads the gateway signal via `hasRealDeclaredGateway`, fail-safe to declared-gateway when the signal is absent). `agent-substrate-client.ts` gains `chatOpenclawLocal` (mirrors `chatHermesLocal`: `{model:'openclaw'}` OpenAI-compat POST, `OPENCLAW_LOCAL_TIMEOUT_MS` leash, optional `Bearer` when `OPENCLAW_LOCAL_GATEWAY_KEY` set, `redirect:'manual'`, bounded 4000-char reply, fail-soft `''`, no SSRF on the compile-time constant); `ping()` treats `openclaw-local` as always-reachable. `PROTOCOL_CAPABILITIES['openclaw-local'] = {emitsInWorldActions:true, proximityGateExempt:false}` — it emits `[ACTION:]` like hermes-local but STAYS proximity-gated (the exemption is Hatcher-only, anti-abuse backbone). `restorableFromRow` stays FALSE (a gateway-less hosted openclaw reconnects after a restart — fail-safe, byte-identical to today's openclaw restore). Three-surface propagation same diff: `PROTOCOL_VERSION` 10→11 + §3a manual note (future→live, operator-gated) in `skill-protocol.ts`; Nori `town-guide.ts` knowledge line; `docs/hatcher-integration-spec.md` version refs 10→11; hosted-runtime injection is version-driven. The `[ACTION:]` executor whitelist is UNCHANGED (verbs/params/bounds byte-identical — still move/emote/enter_building/talk_to_npc/enter_cove/enter_poker_room) and the Hatcher partner WIRE is byte-identical (hatcher rows pinned BYTE-INERT under both gate states by `agent-session-config-openclaw.test.ts`). Real OpenClaw recon (2026-07-08): `github.com/openclaw/openclaw`'s Gateway natively exposes OpenAI-compat `POST /v1/chat/completions` + `/v1/responses` (port via `OPENCLAW_GATEWAY_PORT`, backend = any OpenAI-compat provider), so the wire matches the real runtime — run recipe = start the gateway bound 127.0.0.1:8643. D8 harness: `scripts/agent-connect/mock-openclaw-server.ts` (port 8643, 127.0.0.1-only, marker `OPENCLAW_MOCK_REPLY_V1`) + `openclaw-local-e2e.ts`. §6 avatar-cognition-adapter design sketch: `docs/plans/per-harness-avatar-cognition-adapter.md`. Gate: api tsc 0; `bun test src/services/__tests__/` green (incl. NEW `agent-session-config-openclaw` suite, hermes + hatcher inertness pinned). Codex adversarial pass = NO BLOCKING (all 8 invariants HOLD); staging mock-Hatcher harness run REQUIRED post-deploy (the orchestrator runs the harness; this build cannot). Prior audit follows.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-08 (4): **hermes-local optional bearer (`HERMES_LOCAL_GATEWAY_KEY`) — real-runtime deploy prerequisite.** Deploying the REAL Hermes runtime (NousResearch/hermes-agent, native OpenAI-compat server `gateway/platforms/api_server.py`) surfaced a contract drift: hermes >=0.12 REFUSES to start its API server without `API_SERVER_KEY`, even loopback-bound — the D7 'bare POST' contract cannot carry a real runtime. `agent-session-config.ts` gains `HERMES_LOCAL_GATEWAY_KEY` (module-load read, optional); `chatHermesLocal` sends it as `Authorization: Bearer` when set, byte-identical bare POST when unset (mock-hermes harness unchanged). Same-box shared secret, never logged. Staging infra: hermes runs as docker sidecar `hermes-local` joined to the api container netns (systemd `hermes-attach.timer` re-attaches after each deploy; recipe /usr/local/bin/hermes-attach.sh on the staging box), model backend = local qwen3.6:27b via tailnet. Prior audit follows.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-08 (3): **isHostedHarness namespace-clarity fix (docs/tests only, zero behavior change).** An external review mis-read `auth.ts` `HOSTED_HARNESSES` as a latent bug because `isHostedHarness()`'s doc comment + test titles still claimed it 'drives the /me/agent-session hosted advertisement' — wiring it there was DELIBERATELY EXCLUDED in commit e1b78a49 (it would demote working hermes-HARNESS avatar-agents, which run real harness-agnostic ElizaOS runtimes via their platform_agents row, behind the disabled `HERMES_LOCAL_GATEWAY_ENABLED` connect-namespace flag). Fixed the lie at its source: `agent-session-config.ts` (`hosted` field + `isHostedHarness` docs now scoped to the CONNECT namespace, marked no-production-call-site), the test describe/titles, and a ⚠️ DELIBERATE comment at `auth.ts` `HOSTED_HARNESSES` so no future session 'fixes' it wrongly. Route behavior byte-identical; truth table: avatar-namespace hosting = platform_agents row (bot-row liveness branch still takes precedence for BYO agents); connect-namespace hermes hosting = the env gate. Prior audit follows.

**Last Audited:** 2026-07-09 (4): **Withdraw revisions — founder-requested, still fully DARK (worktree `feat/wallet-withdraw`; `WALLET_WITHDRAW_ENABLED` default-OFF; no flag flips).** (1) DAILY CAPS REMOVED entirely (founder decision): `WALLET_WITHDRAW_DAILY_CAP_{SOL,USDC,CLV}` no longer read; `daily_cap_exceeded` code + its 429 route mapping deleted; the cap SUM/markPendingFailed DB methods deleted — the balance/fee guards are the only per-request limits and the exactly-once machine is UNTOUCHED. (2) CLV HOLD CONSENT GATE (informed consent, NOT enforcement — the land rent sweeper keeps enforcement): a CLV withdrawal that would drop the custodial balance below the avatar's stacked AGENT-subject land-hold requirement (mirrors the sweeper's semantics: `tenure='hold' AND hold_subject='agent' AND grandfathered=false`; `hold_threshold_ct` is CLV uiAmount; compared in atomic BigInt at 6dp) refuses PRE-ROW **409 `hold_at_risk`** carrying `holdAtRisk {requiredUiAmount, postUiAmount (6dp decimal string), parcels[{parcelCode, holdThresholdCt}]}` UNLESS the body carries `acknowledgeHoldLoss: true` (bypasses ONLY the consent gate; NOT part of the idempotency identity — the refusal created no row, so the acked retry reuses the same Idempotency-Key cleanly); FAIL-OPEN on the threshold-query error ONLY (the sweeper's grace window catches an unbacked hold next pass). Threshold query is an injectable dep (`WalletWithdrawDb.getAgentHoldRequirement`). (3) RESUME WORKER wired: `startWithdrawResumeWorker()`/`stopWithdrawResumeWorker()` boot-wired in index.ts beside the market-listing-expiry sweeper but DARK-GATED — starts ONLY when the flag is `'true'` (index.ts condition + internal re-assert); env `WALLET_WITHDRAW_RESUME_POLL_MS` (default 300000, floor 60000); each tick = `runWithdrawResumeTick` (FORWARD-ONLY — a captured signature is never re-signed/re-sent) and every row resolving to 'reconcile' pages ops via `alertError` (warning, source `wallet-withdraw-resume`, ids/sigs only — never key material); graceful-shutdown stop added. Tests 53/0; tsc exit 0 (api+database). Prior audit (3): **Custodial wallet WITHDRAW shipped DARK (worktree `feat/wallet-withdraw`; default-OFF `WALLET_WITHDRAW_ENABLED`; no flag flips)** — users move their OWN deposited on-chain SOL/USDC/CLV out of their custodial avatar wallet: NEW `services/wallet-withdraw-executor.ts` (server-side custodial signing, capture-before-send exactly-once, ambiguous⇒reconcile-never-retry, resume forward-only, mainnet-only guard) + `routes/wallet-withdraw.ts` (`POST /api/wallet/withdraw` w/ REQUIRED Idempotency-Key + read-only `GET /api/wallet/balances`) + `withdrawals` table (migration 0021). E5 human+agent parity (non-ledger agent 403, guests 403); LEDGER-UNTOUCHED (never claw-token-ledger / avatars.clawTokens). Tests 39/0 + regression suites green; tsc exit 0 (api+database). See the new rows in this doc + the top 2026-07-08 entry in the changes log. Prior audit (2): **GoLive executors — Codex re-review punch list APPLIED (worktree `feat/econ-golive-executors`; everything stays DARK — all flags default-OFF, the `CLV_SWAP_EXECUTE` module-load throw INTACT, no ledger imports, migrations untouched).** (1) BLOCKING economic bug: `market-listings.ts` narrows `TRANSFERABLE_TENURES` to `['owned']` — a `'hold'` parcel now refuses the NEW typed `hold_transfer_not_supported` (409, distinct from the generic `not_transferable_tenure` kept for rented/deposit/starter) at LIST time, because the deed-flip normalizes tenure=`'owned'` + NULLs `hold_threshold_ct`/`hold_subject`, which would permanently strip the CLV-hold obligation AND drop the parcel from the weekly tenure sweep (`tenureSweepIdx` excludes 'owned'); data-driven: ZERO 'hold' parcels on prod/staging, so the narrowing is costless. Buyer-INHERITS-obligation transfer (re-stamped `hold_subject`=buyer + independent CLV-threshold check) is the designed follow-up — FEATURE_GATE `market_hold_deed_transfer` in `market-listings.ts`; the deed-transfer executor's tenure "FLAG for land review" is RESOLVED (consistent by construction — only 'owned' can enter the pipe). (2) BOOT TRAP fixed: index.ts wires the CLV swap worker CONDITIONALLY (`CLV_SWAP_EXECUTE==='true'` ⇒ dynamic-import `startClvSwapLiveWorker()`, else the dry-run worker; graceful shutdown mirrors the condition) — the live branch is DEAD while the module-load throw stands (dark-safe today), and the REAL go-live diff is now remove-ONLY-the-throw; the old `clv-swap-live.ts` header claim of a "one-line un-gate onto unchanged boot wiring" was FALSE (it would have crash-looped the whole API on the dry-run worker's `assertNoLiveClvSwapExecution()` gate — index.ts's catch deliberately `process.exit(1)`s that message) and is corrected. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-07 (2): **Inference router: PRIMARY-PREFERRED-OVERFLOW replaces per-request round-robin (`packages/agent-runtime/src/inference/inference-router.ts` + `inference-config.ts`).** Founder ruling: the 7900 XTX box (qwen3.6:27b, endpoint id `local-secondary`) is THE primary — maxed out and kept hot; the backup box (johns-pc qwen3:14b, `local-primary`) takes work ONLY while the primary is saturated or its breaker is open. Mechanism: route-list order is now the PREFERENCE order (a per-endpoint `inflight` counter replaces the old `rrCursor`); when a route has ≥2 locals the router starts at the FIRST local whose `inflight < INFERENCE_PRIMARY_MAX_INFLIGHT` (default 3) and whose breaker allows an attempt; when EVERY local is saturated it keeps preference order so load queues on the primary rather than stampeding the overflow box. Cloud endpoints keep their terminal position (OpenAI stays last-resort). Failover/breaker semantics unchanged. `stats()` gains `inflight`; a one-line `[InferenceRouter] served route=… by=… inflight=… Nms` ops log fires on every LOCAL success (live-verification receipt). **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-07: **Agent-start init-reliability fix — plugin-sql migration advisory-lock no longer wedges agent runtimes under load (`packages/agent-runtime/src/eliza-runtime.ts`, R2).** ROOT CAUSE (live-diagnosed on staging with `LOG_LEVEL=debug`): `@elizaos/core` `AgentRuntime.initialize()` runs plugin-sql's schema migration on EVERY agent start, and that migration takes a GLOBAL per-plugin advisory lock (`pg_advisory_lock(648842597483074957)` — id derived from plugin name, identical for all agents). Over the Supabase **transaction-mode pooler (:6543)** a session-scoped advisory lock orphans on a pooled Supavisor backend and never releases, so the next agent's `pg_advisory_lock` blocks until our 90s init timeout → `"Failed to start avatar agent runtime"` 500 (the 3× "Service … registration timed out (30s)" lines are downstream noise — bootstrap services wait on `initPromise`, which only resolves at the END of `initialize()`). With dozens of agents per box each re-running the identical no-op migration, this was the dominant agent-start flake under load (the reason milady/hermes started but the 3rd runtime — openclaw — hung). FIX (two layers): (1) module-level `migrationsCompleted` guard — the FIRST successful init migrates (under the existing `InitMutex`, so it's the sole migrator, no contention); every init afterward passes `initialize({ skipMigrations: true })`, never touching the advisory-lock path (also faster inits). Flag flips only on SUCCESS so a failed first migrate retries. (2) the ElizaOS adapter now uses a SESSION-mode connection (:5432) so that one remaining migration's lock is held on a stable backend and released cleanly on disconnect (can't orphan). The session URL is DERIVED IN CODE from the effective `DATABASE_URL` via `toSupabaseSessionModeUrl()` (swap the Supabase pooler port :6543→:5432, same host/creds/DB); `ELIZA_DATABASE_URL` is an OPTIONAL explicit override. Deriving (not a separate Coolify env) is deliberate: the Coolify env row is overridden at runtime by a baked `.env.local`, so a hand-set `ELIZA_DATABASE_URL` silently drifted the adapter to a STALE DB ref (`wheuidgiyyccqyoppxoa` vs the real staging `mtpixvtclsjqjguouxes`) — derivation guarantees the adapter hits the SAME DB the app runs on. NO schema/migration/wire change; agent-runtime tsc clean. Same-diff: `CLAUDE.md` env-var list (`ELIZA_DATABASE_URL`). Prior audit follows.

The `[ACTION:]` executor whitelist is UNCHANGED and the Hatcher partner register/PATCH/stats/error WIRE is byte-identical — the bump is an eager re-embed signal for NEW agent-facing docs only. Same-diff: `docs/hatcher-integration-spec.md` (`PROTOCOL_VERSION 10` refs + 9→10 changelog + "partner wire unchanged" note) + this §6 slice-6 callout. New error codes `payai_rail_disabled` (503) / `payai_unavailable` (502, pre-claim, retryable) / `payai_release_failed` (502, post-claim, terminal `failed` — mirrors the on-chain broadcast-unknown posture) / `rail_mixed_forbidden` (409) mapped in `routes/bounties.ts` + `routes/sap.ts`. GATES: env `SAP_PAYAI_SETTLEMENT_ENABLED` (default OFF) on top of `SAP_ENABLED`+`SAP_ESCROW_ENABLED`+`SAP_USDC_ESCROW_ENABLED`, under `SAP_DRY_RUN` (default true ⇒ verify-only) + the `SAP_ALLOW_MAINNET` code gate; the silently-defaulted CDP facilitator is REFUSED for this rail (PayAI preset / staging mock / explicit URL only — the #1 “all x402 through PayAI” constraint enforced in code). Rail marker lives in the EXISTING `metadata` jsonb — NO schema delta, NO migration. The weekly rent is the SERVER-stamped `land_parcels.rent_ct_weekly` (read from the row, never the body — same discipline as `price_ct`). **Schema (additive, `schema/land.ts`):** `land_parcels` += `tenure` (`land_tenure` enum rented|owned|starter, NULL=available), `rent_ct_weekly` int, `rent_paid_through` + `grace_until` timestamptz, partial index `land_parcels_rent_sweep_idx` on `(rent_paid_through) WHERE tenure='rented'`; `land_structures` += `status` (`land_structure_status` enum active|archived, default active); `land_transaction_kind` += `rent_payment` + `eviction` (promoted from reserved). Buy + claim-starter now stamp `tenure` ('owned'/'starter'). Both acquire paths reconcile an eviction-archived structure under the parcel lock (`reconcileArchivedStructureOnAcquire`: same-avatar re-acquire → restore, different avatar → purge); owned/structure reads exclude `status='archived'`. **NEW service `services/land-rent-sweeper.ts`** (hourly `setInterval`, started/stopped in `index.ts` like the body-idle sweeper): per due rented parcel, under the SAME advisory+row lock, charges the week (`source:'system'`, advances `rent_paid_through = now()+period`, clears grace) → on insufficient CT opens a `RENT_GRACE_DAYS` grace window → on elapsed grace EVICTS (parcel `status='available'` + owner/tenure/rent cleared, structure `status='archived'`, `land_transactions(eviction)`, `broadcastLandEvent(status:'available')` so the for-sale sign reappears live). Re-entrant + per-row try/catch (one bad row never aborts the pass); side effects (cache bust + SSE) run AFTER each parcel's tx commits. Constants (`@clawville/shared land-economy.ts`): buy ladder raised (C 2k-4k, B 10k-24k, A 40k-80k), new `LAND_RENT_LADDER` (C 50-100, B 250-550, A 1k-2.4k weekly), `RENT_PERIOD_DAYS=7`, `RENT_GRACE_DAYS=3`. **Idempotent migration `apps/api/scripts/migrate-land-tenure.ts`** (explicit `SEED_DATABASE_URL` only, never `.env.local`; `--dry-run` default-safe): creates the enums, adds the columns/index, adds the `eviction` value, reprices existing `price_ct` to the new ladder ("existing == new" founder decision) + stamps `rent_ct_weekly` + back-fills tenure. **PARITY (E5):** rent binds to `identity.avatarId` from `requireAuthOrAgentSession` (human cookie OR `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar) so a connected/hosted agent rents to its OWN avatar with REAL CT; guest → 401; no `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump (rent matches buy's deferred Phase-3 agent-action exposure). Gate GREEN: shared + database + api `tsc --noEmit` exit 0; migration dry-run exit 0. **ACTIVATION GAP (no scaffolding theater):** the current 2-ring world geometry has ZERO c/b/a parcels (`PARCEL_TIER_COUNTS {founder:10, a/b/c:0, starter:26}`) — founder is USDC/auction (reserved, unchanged) and starter is free-entry, so NO parcel is CT-rentable today. The rent engine is tier-agnostic and rents whatever tier carries a `rent_ct_weekly`; it goes LIVE only once a CT-priced tier ring is added to the world (founder decision 2026-06-24: grow the world with a new outer ring — geometry build is the follow-up slice). NOT pushed, NO DB touched. `GameFeatures.md` + Nori `town-guide.ts knowledge[]` update WITH the world-expansion slice (when rent is user-reachable). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-23: **Land 2.1 - cross-player LIVE land-sync over SSE.** `apps/api/src/routes/world.ts` adds a module-level `landStreamSubscribers` Set (each `/api/world/:roomId/stream` registers its SSE writer on connect, removes it on abort + failed keepalive) plus an exported `broadcastLandEvent(payload)` that fans a single named `land` SSE event to EVERY connected world-stream subscriber across ALL rooms - land is GLOBAL state, so the fan-out is cross-room, not per-room. `broadcastLandEvent` is FULLY GUARDED (outer try/catch around `JSON.stringify` + the sync part of `writeSSE`, inner per-subscriber try/catch, async `.catch` on the write) so it can NEVER throw to its caller: it runs AFTER a committed buy/claim, and a thrown notification would otherwise turn an already-settled sale into a client-facing 500. `apps/api/src/routes/land.ts` calls it fire-and-forget after the buy + claim-starter commits (right after the leaderboard event) - notification only, NO settlement/ledger/route-shape change, E5 parity intact. Client: `apps/web/src/hooks/use-world-stream.ts` adds `es.addEventListener('land', …)` that invalidates the shared `['landParcels']` query (extracted to `apps/web/src/lib/land-query-keys.ts`, a `three`-free module re-exported by `land-state-hydrator.tsx` so the SSE hook never pulls the R3F graph) so `LandStateHydrator` refetches and the in-world for-sale signs update within ~1s of another player's buy - no reload, no 60s wait. world-presence OWNS the SSE seam; land CONSUMES it (minimal additive - no snapshot/presence/tick change). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-22: **Poker CASH house-bots — adversarial-audit BLOCKING fixes (scope-leak + turn-clock settlement; no schema change).** Two HIGH money-path holes in the house auto-scaler/bots build, fixed in place. **(1) SCOPE LEAK — a user could self-serve a house-bank-funded bot table.** The create route (`cove-cash-poker.ts`) accepted `source: z.enum(['house','player-public'])` from any caller and `createTable` imposed no creator restriction, so `POST /tables {source:'house',…}` minted a user-owned table whose seats filled with house-bank-debited bots AND was self-driven by the tick — a house-bank exposure/drain vector any player could summon. FIX (both belt + braces): the route enum now allows ONLY `source:'player-public'` for the public branch (`z.literal('player-public')`), so a user POST can never reach a house table; AND `cash-table-manager.ts createTable` now rejects `source==='house'` unless the creator's avatarId IS the house-bank avatar (resolved via `houseBankAvatarProvider`) → `house_table_creation_forbidden` (403). The scaler is unaffected (it builds its creator subject from the house-bank avatar and calls the manager directly). **(2) Turn-clock auto-fold ran OUTSIDE `withTableLock` and never settled.** The sim's armed `setTimeout` fired `onTurnTimeout` directly — mutating the SimTable + advancing + resolving the hand with NO per-table lock (racing a concurrent human action at an `await` suspension point) and leaving an undrained `pendingResults` entry that no tick drains on a no-tick (player-public/private) table → escrow stranded vs Σ seat stacks indefinitely. FIX: `poker-table-sim.ts` gains a `setTurnTimeoutHook(fn)` seam; when set (the cash manager sets it in its constructor), the armed timer routes the expiry to the hook with the tableId INSTEAD of calling `onTurnTimeout` directly (WS-demo/MTT sims set no hook → unchanged inline behavior). The cash manager's new `handleTurnTimeout(tableId)` acquires the SAME `withTableLock`, then `sim.onTurnTimeout` → `settleIfComplete` → `driveSeededAgents` → `settleIfComplete` → `startAndAdvance`, so the timeout-resolved hand is auto-folded/checked AND settled in one locked critical section — never interleaving with a human action, never leaving stranded escrow on ANY table type. Belt-and-braces: `getTableState` lazily settles a surviving `pendingResults` under the lock before a public read so escrow is never reported inflated. **Conservation invariant unchanged** (timeouts move chips between seats at settle; the ONLY mint stays the single guarded boot bankroll credit; bot buy-ins still DEBIT the house bank). Tests: `cash-table-manager.test.ts` +3 cases (create-time scope-guard rejection, non-house-bank-creator rejection, fill-time faucet defense-in-depth) + a new `ControllableClock` describe asserting a fired turn timeout settles the hand with `escrow==Σstacks` and `Σdebits==Σcredits+escrow`, all WITHOUT a human action. Gate GREEN: `DATABASE_URL='' bun test apps/api/src/services/poker/` 91 pass / 0 fail; `apps/api bunx tsc --noEmit` clean for touched files (only the pre-existing `@clawville/wager-program` module-resolution error remains). PARITY (E5) unchanged: a connected agent (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session`) sits at the SAME house tables via the SAME REST path with REAL-CT settlement. NEEDS staging deploy + live smoke + sign-off (Rule E4). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-22: **F4 — blackjack "bust shows but the table is stuck" freeze (client-only fix in `BlackjackModal.tsx`).** A busted hand could strand the modal in `player-turn` on a fully-terminal hand: Hit/Stand disabled via `activeResolved`, and the "Next Hand" button is settled-phase-only → dead end. **Server is NOT at fault** — a live staging-API repro (`/session/open`→`/hand/deal`→`/action hit` to >21) confirmed a busting hit returns `200 status:settled` with the full busted hand; `isHandTerminal` settles a single-hand bust correctly. The strand is a one-off CLIENT race: a stale `GET /hand/current` read taken inside the server's narrow commit→settle window (script-commit txn done, settle txn not yet — hand momentarily `status='in_progress'` AND terminal) returns the hand as live, and `restoreHandFromServer` applies it AFTER `applySettled`, doing `setHand(live)` + `setSettled(null)` → back to player-turn on a terminal hand. **Fix (no server/ledger change):** (1) ROOT-CAUSE GUARD — `settledHandIdsRef` tracks settled handIds; `restoreHandFromServer` early-returns if a "live" hand's id is already-settled (a stale read can't overwrite a settled hand); set in `applySettled`, cleared on modal close. (2) SELF-HEAL — a new effect: when `phase==='player-turn'` && every sub-hand `isResolved` && nothing in flight (`!inFlight && !busyRef && !agentBusyRef`) && not already healed this handId, calls `resyncAfterStaleDecision()` → `/hand/current` returns `CurrentHandNone {hand:null}` (server settled) → `restoreHandFromServer(…, allowClear=true)` clears to idle → player gets the betting UI back. Once-per-handId (`healedHandIdRef`) so no loop; `busyRef` still-true during the normal settle window prevents any false-fire. PARITY: manual + autonomous both covered (autonomous settled→next-hand auto-advance untouched); guests can't reach the race (their `/hand/current` 401s → null → no restore-overwrite). Gate: web `next build` exit 0 (turbo 5/5). `GameFeatures.md` same-diff. NEEDS staging deploy + founder cold-test (Rule E4). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-20: **Poker CASH (ring) games — P1 SHIPPABLE wire + money atomicity (new `cove-cash-poker.ts` row in §2).** Mounted `coveCashPokerRouter` at `/api/cove/poker/cash` (`index.ts`) + exported the `poker-cash` schema (`packages/database/src/schema/index.ts`) — the route + 4 `poker_cash_*` tables were authored but un-wired. **Money-atomicity fix (`services/poker/cash-table-manager.ts`):** `seatSubject`, `cashOutSeat`, and `settleHand` did debit/credit + insert + update as SEPARATE round-trips with NO `db.transaction` (only an in-process mutex → ZERO DB atomicity: a crash mid-sequence could debit CT with no seat, or double-cash-out on retry). Each now runs in ONE `db.transaction` with the parent table/seat row `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` first, and the ledger `debit/creditClawTokens(input, tx)` composes INTO the same tx (mirroring `tournament-manager.ts registerEntrant`). `cashOutSeat` re-checks the seat status under the lock → a retry replays (no second credit). `settleHand` re-reads `settled_at` under the lock (race-safe idempotency). **Cross-game wiring (Rule E5 keystone):** the settle now writes one `cove_game_events` row per REAL (non-seeded) subject per hand (`gameType='poker'`, `sessionId=shoeId=tableId`, `betAmount=totalCommitted`, `payout=won`, `engineVersion='poker-cash-v1'`; seat packed into the nonce `handNumber*100+seatIndex` to dodge the `(game_type, session_id, nonce)` unique-index collision across a hand's seats). `cove-history.ts` `GAME_TYPES` += `'poker'` (read filter + verify dispatch) and a new `poker` `/verify` branch (`sha256(seed)==hash` + stored-vs-authoritative `poker_cash_hands.pot_result_json` consistency; full multi-player re-deal deferred — P1 persists only the settled outcome). > **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-06: **InferenceRouter — the single text-gen router (replaces the interim global `OPENAI_BASE_URL` hack).** NEW `packages/agent-runtime/src/inference/inference-router.ts` (the `InferenceRouter` class — named endpoints, ordered per-consumer routes, per-endpoint circuit-breaker failover, metering; framework-agnostic, no ElizaOS import) + `inference-config.ts` (env→endpoints/route-table + lazy singleton `getInferenceRouter()` + `resolveInferenceRoute(agentType, isHouse)`). **Endpoints:** `openai` (always present; `OPENAI_SMALL_MODEL`/`OPENAI_LARGE_MODEL` now define ITS models), `local-primary` (`INFERENCE_LOCAL_PRIMARY_URL`, default `qwen3:14b`), optional `local-secondary` (`qwen3.6:27b`) — each its OWN url/model/auth. **Routes (ordered, failover walks the list):** `teacher`→`[openai]` (10 residents `location-agent` + Nori `system-agent`), `fleet`→`[local-primary,(local-secondary,)openai]` (our `is_house` agents — the ONLY route that touches local), `hosted-user`/`default`→`[openai]`; OpenAI auto-appended as ultimate fallback to every route (never total-blackout). **Fleet LOAD-BALANCES:** a route with ≥2 local endpoints round-robins the starting box per request (work distributed across both boxes, the other is immediate failover, OpenAI last). Both boxes kept WARM via `keep_alive` on local requests (`INFERENCE_LOCAL_KEEP_ALIVE` default `60m`) + a boot pre-warm (`getInferenceRouter().warmup()` fire-and-forget in `apps/api/src/index.ts`, before the autonomy driver starts). **Integration:** `openai-text-provider.ts` (priority 95) stops reading any global URL and delegates to `getInferenceRouter().generateText({route,size,messages})`; the route is passed per-runtime via new `ElizaRuntimeConfig.inferenceRoute`, set in `agent-orchestrator.startAgent` from `resolveInferenceRoute(agent.type, isHouseAgent)` where `isHouseAgent = opts.isHouse || config.houseAgentId` (robust to warm-path order); house agents carry NO `openclawGateway` so they hit priority 95 → router → local. The `npc-conversation-engine.ts` NPC-banter path (a SECOND hardcoded `api.openai.com` call) now also routes through the router (`route:'default'`→OpenAI, behavior-identical, never throws). **Breaker:** K consecutive failures (`INFERENCE_FAIL_THRESHOLD`, default 3) → open for `INFERENCE_COOLDOWN_MS` (30s) → single half-open probe → close on success. **Local wire:** local endpoints default to the Ollama-native `/api/chat` wire with `think:false` (`provider:'ollama'`, `INFERENCE_LOCAL_*_PROVIDER` to override) — LOAD-BEARING because qwen3 is a reasoning model that otherwise spends the fleet's whole decide() token budget inside `<think>` (verified >50s empty on a complex prompt → past the driver's 15s timeout; `think:false` → valid `[ACTION:]` in ~2s). Local timeout default 60s — MUST outlast a cold model-load (Ollama cancels an aborted load, so a shorter cutoff kills the recovery/failover load and the box never warms — a 12s cutoff stranded johns-pc on OpenAI after a restart); a DOWN box still fails over fast via ECONNREFUSED, and the driver's own 15s decide() timeout bounds latency. The driver now runs the SAME cadence regardless of human presence; real per-agent decide rate is naturally paced by the 30s tick + walk time + the 60s talk-cooldown (~2–3 min per full decide→walk→arrive→talk→cooldown loop), so always-on cost for one agent is modest and the fleet-scale cost lever is the planned open-source-model migration (config, not code), NOT dialing agents down when unwatched. `npcSimulation.getActiveHumanCount()` remains (now unused by the driver; harmless). tsc 0; `agent-autonomy-p1.test.ts` 17/17 (no test referenced the throttle). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-02: **Agent-metaverse P1 — RELIABILITY + money/security + tests fix pass (R1–R5, M1–M5, T1–T3; backend only, NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump — still 7).** **R1 (BLOCKING — "stuck on one boot" root cause):** ElizaOS `runtime.initialize()` (`packages/agent-runtime/src/eliza-runtime.ts`) takes the plugin-sql `pg_advisory_lock`; a hung holder made both a queued `InitMutex.acquire()` waiter AND `initialize()` itself block FOREVER, wedging the box on one boot. Now bounded by an **IN-PROCESS rejecting `Promise.race`** (`INIT_TIMEOUT_MS=90_000`) — NEVER an external kill (an external kill orphans the bun proc still squatting the lock, the documented root cause); `acquire(timeoutMs)` rejects + splices its queued resolver out; `start()` uses `let acquired` so the outer `finally` releases the mutex ONLY if it was actually held. On timeout it REJECTS → `catch` (state='error'+onError) + `finally` (release) run IN-PROCESS, so `agent-orchestrator.ensureAgentRuntime`'s `finally` clears `recoveryInProgress` (a hang previously stranded it → every future call fell into the wait-2s-return-null branch permanently). Residual (accepted, no external-kill mitigation): `Promise.race` does not cancel the losing `initialize()`, so a genuinely lock-holding init stays held in-proc until its connection closes. **R2 (warming watchdog):** `agent-autonomy-driver.ts` `warming` is now a `Map<agentId,warmingSince>`; a warm older than `WARM_WATCHDOG_MS=90s` is evicted so a fresh warm relaunches (a never-settling warm can't wedge an agent forever); `.finally` compare-and-deletes so a watchdog relaunch isn't clobbered by the old warm's late settle. **R3 (nondeterministic/duplicable platformAgentId):** the seeder's arbitrary `.find()` over openclaw-bot rows (no ORDER BY, no unique constraint) let concurrent boots insert 2 `platform_agents` rows for one `openclawBotId` and bind a different one each boot. Now `.orderBy(asc(agents.createdAt))` → binds the earliest + best-effort stop+delete of extras; NEW partial unique index `platform_agents_openclaw_bot_singleton` on `(user_id, type, (config->>'openclawBotId')) WHERE type='openclaw-bot' AND (config->>'openclawBotId') IS NOT NULL` (schema `agents.ts`, mirrors the `platform_agents_system_singleton` precedent) via idempotent `migrations-manual/2026-07-02_platform_agents_openclaw_bot_singleton.sql` (dedupe DELETE keeping earliest, THEN `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`) + `packages/database/scripts/apply-platform-agents-openclaw-bot-singleton.ts` (explicit `AGENTS_DATABASE_URL`, NOT db:push). **R4 (silent empty-decide spin):** `withTimeout` collapses timeout AND error to `''`, so a persistent empty decide (OpenAI 429/quota) span silently — now a per-entry `consecutiveEmptyDecides` counter WARNs past `EMPTY_DECIDE_WARN_THRESHOLD=3` (health SIGNAL only; drive behavior + the `''` contract + the `[AutonomyDriver][debug]` instrumentation unchanged). **R5 (sweeper belt-and-suspenders):** `agent-body-idle-sweeper.ts` house-exemption set is now the UNION of `agentAutonomyDriver.getHouseAgentIds()` and the DB `is_house` flag, so a dropped/renamed column can't silently un-exempt + reap a live house body. **Reconciler fixes (2026-07-02 — post-adversarial):** **(codex BLOCKING)** `agent-orchestrator.ensureAgentRuntime` had an `await db.query` BETWEEN the `recoveryInProgress.has()` guard and the `.add()`, so two concurrent callers (e.g. multiple agents chatting the SAME system NPC) could both pass → duplicate `startAgent`/`runtime.start()` + overwritten `runningAgents`; now `.add()` runs with NO await after the guard (atomic check-then-set) and the DB lookup moved inside the try/finally. **(adversary HIGH — same class as M1)** the `ct-` connect ticket (`ct-<base64url(24)>`, bound to the pending avatar on possession alone, polled repeatedly as `GET /api/agent/connect-status/:token` path param) was NOT covered by the redaction regex → leaked unredacted to stdout/Coolify; `BEARER_TOKEN_RE` now covers `(oc|ag|hat|ct)-` (`services/log-redact.ts` + a new `ct-` test case). **M1 (bearer leak):** `app.onError` (`index.ts`) `console.error(err)` + the `alertError` `Uncaught error on <method> <path>` message + `err.stack` carried the raw `/api/agent/oc-<bearer>/…` real-CT bearer to stdout/Coolify + Telegram — now `redactBearerTokens`'d generally in `services/alert-error.ts` (message top-level + `console.warn` fallback + `JSON.stringify(context)` block) AND at the `index.ts` `console.error`; JSON 500 shape unchanged. **M2 (CT faucet):** `/visit-building` + `/building/:buildingId/chat` credited 1 CT unconditionally → a parked ledger-bound agent could loop-farm; NEW `creditBuildingRewardOncePerDay()` gates ONLY the `creditClawTokens` call (leaderboard event untouched) with an idempotent per-(avatar, building, reason, UTC-day) dedup inside one `db.transaction` that `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`s the SAME `avatars` row the ledger locks, then existence-probes `claw_token_transactions` before crediting — no double-credit under concurrency, no legit-visit regression. **M3/M4:** `/building/:id/chat` + `/move` now use the shared `resolveBuildingCenter` own-property guard (NEW dependency-free `services/building-center.ts`) + `BUILDING_INTERACTION_RADIUS` (was bare `NPC_BUILDING_CENTERS[id]` + `CHAT_RADIUS=2000`), closing the prototype-key NaN proximity-bypass on both, matching `/visit-building`. **M5:** NEW `packages/database/scripts/apply-openclaw-is-house.ts` (explicit `AGENTS_DATABASE_URL`, NOT db:push) applies the idempotent `2026-07-01_add_openclaw_is_house.sql`. **T1/T2/T3:** F1 test now exercises the REAL exported `resolveBuildingCenter`; Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-24: **Land builder-economics Slice 1 - RENT-or-BUY acquisition + eviction sweep (engine built; ACTIVATION gated on a world expansion).** New `POST /api/land/parcels/:parcelId/rent` (`land.ts`) mirrors the buy route's exact money discipline: per-avatar `pg_advisory_xact_lock` (outer) + `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` (inner) + `claw-token-ledger.debitClawTokens` IN-TX (first week charged up front, `source:'api'`, never a raw `avatars.clawTokens` write) + `land_transactions(rent_payment)` audit row, single-acquire safety via the `available`→`owned` flip (replay → 409). Sets `tenure='rented'`, `rent_paid_through = now() + RENT_PERIOD_DAYS`. Errors: 404 `parcel_not_found`, 409 `parcel_not_available`/`parcel_cap_reached`, 400 `rent_not_available` (tier carries no `rent_ct_weekly`)/`insufficient_clawtokens`, 501 `founder_not_in_v1`. The weekly rent is the SERVER-stamped `land_parcels.rent_ct_weekly` (read from the row, never the body — same discipline as `price_ct`). **Schema (additive, `schema/land.ts`):** `land_parcels` += `tenure` (`land_tenure` enum rented|owned|starter, NULL=available), `rent_ct_weekly` int, `rent_paid_through` + `grace_until` timestamptz, partial index `land_parcels_rent_sweep_idx` on `(rent_paid_through) WHERE tenure='rented'`; `land_structures` += `status` (`land_structure_status` enum active|archived, default active); `land_transaction_kind` += `rent_payment` + `eviction` (promoted from reserved). Buy + claim-starter now stamp `tenure` ('owned'/'starter'). Both acquire paths reconcile an eviction-archived structure under the parcel lock (`reconcileArchivedStructureOnAcquire`: same-avatar re-acquire → restore, different avatar → purge); owned/structure reads exclude `status='archived'`. **NEW service `services/land-rent-sweeper.ts`** (hourly `setInterval`, started/stopped in `index.ts` like the body-idle sweeper): per due rented parcel, under the SAME advisory+row lock, charges the week (`source:'system'`, advances `rent_paid_through = now()+period`, clears grace) → on insufficient CT opens a `RENT_GRACE_DAYS` grace window → on elapsed grace EVICTS (parcel `status='available'` + owner/tenure/rent cleared, structure `status='archived'`, `land_transactions(eviction)`, `broadcastLandEvent(status:'available')` so the for-sale sign reappears live). Re-entrant + per-row try/catch (one bad row never aborts the pass); side effects (cache bust + SSE) run AFTER each parcel's tx commits. Constants (`@clawville/shared land-economy.ts`): buy ladder raised (C 2k-4k, B 10k-24k, A 40k-80k), new `LAND_RENT_LADDER` (C 50-100, B 250-550, A 1k-2.4k weekly), `RENT_PERIOD_DAYS=7`, `RENT_GRACE_DAYS=3`. **Idempotent migration `apps/api/scripts/migrate-land-tenure.ts`** (explicit `SEED_DATABASE_URL` only, never `.env.local`; `--dry-run` default-safe): creates the enums, adds the columns/index, adds the `eviction` value, reprices existing `price_ct` to the new ladder ("existing == new" founder decision) + stamps `rent_ct_weekly` + back-fills tenure. **PARITY (E5):** rent binds to `identity.avatarId` from `requireAuthOrAgentSession` (human cookie OR `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar) so a connected/hosted agent rents to its OWN avatar with REAL CT; guest → 401; no `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump (rent matches buy's deferred Phase-3 agent-action exposure). Gate GREEN: shared + database + api `tsc --noEmit` exit 0; migration dry-run exit 0. **ACTIVATION GAP (no scaffolding theater):** the current 2-ring world geometry has ZERO c/b/a parcels (`PARCEL_TIER_COUNTS {founder:10, a/b/c:0, starter:26}`) — founder is USDC/auction (reserved, unchanged) and starter is free-entry, so NO parcel is CT-rentable today. The rent engine is tier-agnostic and rents whatever tier carries a `rent_ct_weekly`; it goes LIVE only once a CT-priced tier ring is added to the world (founder decision 2026-06-24: grow the world with a new outer ring — geometry build is the follow-up slice). NOT pushed, NO DB touched. `GameFeatures.md` + Nori `town-guide.ts knowledge[]` update WITH the world-expansion slice (when rent is user-reachable). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-23: **Land 2.1 - cross-player LIVE land-sync over SSE.** `apps/api/src/routes/world.ts` adds a module-level `landStreamSubscribers` Set (each `/api/world/:roomId/stream` registers its SSE writer on connect, removes it on abort + failed keepalive) plus an exported `broadcastLandEvent(payload)` that fans a single named `land` SSE event to EVERY connected world-stream subscriber across ALL rooms - land is GLOBAL state, so the fan-out is cross-room, not per-room. `broadcastLandEvent` is FULLY GUARDED (outer try/catch around `JSON.stringify` + the sync part of `writeSSE`, inner per-subscriber try/catch, async `.catch` on the write) so it can NEVER throw to its caller: it runs AFTER a committed buy/claim, and a thrown notification would otherwise turn an already-settled sale into a client-facing 500. `apps/api/src/routes/land.ts` calls it fire-and-forget after the buy + claim-starter commits (right after the leaderboard event) - notification only, NO settlement/ledger/route-shape change, E5 parity intact. Client: `apps/web/src/hooks/use-world-stream.ts` adds `es.addEventListener('land', …)` that invalidates the shared `['landParcels']` query (extracted to `apps/web/src/lib/land-query-keys.ts`, a `three`-free module re-exported by `land-state-hydrator.tsx` so the SSE hook never pulls the R3F graph) so `LandStateHydrator` refetches and the in-world for-sale signs update within ~1s of another player's buy - no reload, no 60s wait. world-presence OWNS the SSE seam; land CONSUMES it (minimal additive - no snapshot/presence/tick change). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-22: **Poker CASH house-bots — adversarial-audit BLOCKING fixes (scope-leak + turn-clock settlement; no schema change).** Two HIGH money-path holes in the house auto-scaler/bots build, fixed in place. **(1) SCOPE LEAK — a user could self-serve a house-bank-funded bot table.** The create route (`cove-cash-poker.ts`) accepted `source: z.enum(['house','player-public'])` from any caller and `createTable` imposed no creator restriction, so `POST /tables {source:'house',…}` minted a user-owned table whose seats filled with house-bank-debited bots AND was self-driven by the tick — a house-bank exposure/drain vector any player could summon. FIX (both belt + braces): the route enum now allows ONLY `source:'player-public'` for the public branch (`z.literal('player-public')`), so a user POST can never reach a house table; AND `cash-table-manager.ts createTable` now rejects `source==='house'` unless the creator's avatarId IS the house-bank avatar (resolved via `houseBankAvatarProvider`) → `house_table_creation_forbidden` (403). The scaler is unaffected (it builds its creator subject from the house-bank avatar and calls the manager directly). **(2) Turn-clock auto-fold ran OUTSIDE `withTableLock` and never settled.** The sim's armed `setTimeout` fired `onTurnTimeout` directly — mutating the SimTable + advancing + resolving the hand with NO per-table lock (racing a concurrent human action at an `await` suspension point) and leaving an undrained `pendingResults` entry that no tick drains on a no-tick (player-public/private) table → escrow stranded vs Σ seat stacks indefinitely. FIX: `poker-table-sim.ts` gains a `setTurnTimeoutHook(fn)` seam; when set (the cash manager sets it in its constructor), the armed timer routes the expiry to the hook with the tableId INSTEAD of calling `onTurnTimeout` directly (WS-demo/MTT sims set no hook → unchanged inline behavior). The cash manager's new `handleTurnTimeout(tableId)` acquires the SAME `withTableLock`, then `sim.onTurnTimeout` → `settleIfComplete` → `driveSeededAgents` → `settleIfComplete` → `startAndAdvance`, so the timeout-resolved hand is auto-folded/checked AND settled in one locked critical section — never interleaving with a human action, never leaving stranded escrow on ANY table type. Belt-and-braces: `getTableState` lazily settles a surviving `pendingResults` under the lock before a public read so escrow is never reported inflated. **Conservation invariant unchanged** (timeouts move chips between seats at settle; the ONLY mint stays the single guarded boot bankroll credit; bot buy-ins still DEBIT the house bank). Tests: `cash-table-manager.test.ts` +3 cases (create-time scope-guard rejection, non-house-bank-creator rejection, fill-time faucet defense-in-depth) + a new `ControllableClock` describe asserting a fired turn timeout settles the hand with `escrow==Σstacks` and `Σdebits==Σcredits+escrow`, all WITHOUT a human action. Gate GREEN: `DATABASE_URL='' bun test apps/api/src/services/poker/` 91 pass / 0 fail; `apps/api bunx tsc --noEmit` clean for touched files (only the pre-existing `@clawville/wager-program` module-resolution error remains). PARITY (E5) unchanged: a connected agent (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session`) sits at the SAME house tables via the SAME REST path with REAL-CT settlement. NEEDS staging deploy + live smoke + sign-off (Rule E4). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-22: **F4 — blackjack "bust shows but the table is stuck" freeze (client-only fix in `BlackjackModal.tsx`).** A busted hand could strand the modal in `player-turn` on a fully-terminal hand: Hit/Stand disabled via `activeResolved`, and the "Next Hand" button is settled-phase-only → dead end. **Server is NOT at fault** — a live staging-API repro (`/session/open`→`/hand/deal`→`/action hit` to >21) confirmed a busting hit returns `200 status:settled` with the full busted hand; `isHandTerminal` settles a single-hand bust correctly. The strand is a one-off CLIENT race: a stale `GET /hand/current` read taken inside the server's narrow commit→settle window (script-commit txn done, settle txn not yet — hand momentarily `status='in_progress'` AND terminal) returns the hand as live, and `restoreHandFromServer` applies it AFTER `applySettled`, doing `setHand(live)` + `setSettled(null)` → back to player-turn on a terminal hand. **Fix (no server/ledger change):** (1) ROOT-CAUSE GUARD — `settledHandIdsRef` tracks settled handIds; `restoreHandFromServer` early-returns if a "live" hand's id is already-settled (a stale read can't overwrite a settled hand); set in `applySettled`, cleared on modal close. (2) SELF-HEAL — a new effect: when `phase==='player-turn'` && every sub-hand `isResolved` && nothing in flight (`!inFlight && !busyRef && !agentBusyRef`) && not already healed this handId, calls `resyncAfterStaleDecision()` → `/hand/current` returns `CurrentHandNone {hand:null}` (server settled) → `restoreHandFromServer(…, allowClear=true)` clears to idle → player gets the betting UI back. Once-per-handId (`healedHandIdRef`) so no loop; `busyRef` still-true during the normal settle window prevents any false-fire. PARITY: manual + autonomous both covered (autonomous settled→next-hand auto-advance untouched); guests can't reach the race (their `/hand/current` 401s → null → no restore-overwrite). Gate: web `next build` exit 0 (turbo 5/5). `GameFeatures.md` same-diff. NEEDS staging deploy + founder cold-test (Rule E4). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-20: **Poker CASH (ring) games — P1 SHIPPABLE wire + money atomicity (new `cove-cash-poker.ts` row in §2).** Mounted `coveCashPokerRouter` at `/api/cove/poker/cash` (`index.ts`) + exported the `poker-cash` schema (`packages/database/src/schema/index.ts`) — the route + 4 `poker_cash_*` tables were authored but un-wired. **Money-atomicity fix (`services/poker/cash-table-manager.ts`):** `seatSubject`, `cashOutSeat`, and `settleHand` did debit/credit + insert + update as SEPARATE round-trips with NO `db.transaction` (only an in-process mutex → ZERO DB atomicity: a crash mid-sequence could debit CT with no seat, or double-cash-out on retry). Each now runs in ONE `db.transaction` with the parent table/seat row `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` first, and the ledger `debit/creditClawTokens(input, tx)` composes INTO the same tx (mirroring `tournament-manager.ts registerEntrant`). `cashOutSeat` re-checks the seat status under the lock → a retry replays (no second credit). `settleHand` re-reads `settled_at` under the lock (race-safe idempotency). **Cross-game wiring (Rule E5 keystone):** the settle now writes one `cove_game_events` row per REAL (non-seeded) subject per hand (`gameType='poker'`, `sessionId=shoeId=tableId`, `betAmount=totalCommitted`, `payout=won`, `engineVersion='poker-cash-v1'`; seat packed into the nonce `handNumber*100+seatIndex` to dodge the `(game_type, session_id, nonce)` unique-index collision across a hand's seats). `cove-history.ts` `GAME_TYPES` += `'poker'` (read filter + verify dispatch) and a new `poker` `/verify` branch (`sha256(seed)==hash` + stored-vs-authoritative `poker_cash_hands.pot_result_json` consistency; full multi-player re-deal deferred — P1 persists only the settled outcome). The economy monitor (`cove-economy.ts`) is a `GROUP BY game_type` so poker rows appear automatically (houseNet ≈ 0 expected; rake 0). CT-only + the SOL/USDC seam kept as gated future code (enum + escrow columns retained, never deleted). **NOT in this slice:** the agent ACTION/tool surface + `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump (REST parity is complete; in-world agent-action discovery is a later phase — no PROTOCOL bump, Hatcher whitelist/manual untouched), the house auto-scaler, real agent poker AI, and live WebSocket. A hosted avatar has no external bearer to lose and nothing to reconnect (chatting already routes to it via the authed avatar-chat path), so the bogus "Reconnect your agent to chat as it" CTA is suppressed; external/idle/expired agents still get the correct reconnect CTA. `sessionEnded` was already hosted-safe (its `routedThroughAgent` gate requires a live bearer, which hosted never has). **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-02: **Exchange — E5 human/agent parity + escrow-faucet hardening (`routes/exchange.ts`; route-parity only, NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump — still 7; NO schema change).** The last live E5 violation on the peer Exchange is closed. **(1) PARITY:** every WRITE (`POST /create`, `/:id/order`, `/orders/:orderId/{submit,confirm,cancel}`, `/:id/cancel`) + the `/my-listings` `/my-orders` reads moved from `requireAuth` (human/guest-only) to `requireAuthOrAgentSession`; a new `getActingAvatar(c)` re-loads the avatar by the middleware-resolved `identity.avatarId` (NEVER a body id), so a Lucia human AND a connected/hosted agent (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar) trade AS THEMSELVES with REAL-CT escrow bound to that avatar; guests never resolve here, an unbound/expired agent 403s (no guest fallback). CT rail carries NO `ledgerCapable` gate — matches the bounties CT rail (CT is the non-custodial in-game economy). Escrow stays ledger-only (`credit/debitClawTokens` composed into the route tx; zero direct `avatars.clawTokens` writes). `GET /` + `GET /:id` stay public. **(2) ESCROW-FAUCET HARDENING (conservation, adversary-audited):** confirm + order-cancel now ATOMIC-CLAIM the state transition (`UPDATE … WHERE state=… RETURNING` → 409 before ANY credit) so two concurrent confirms, two cancels, or a confirm-vs-cancel can never double-release/double-refund one escrow (FIX-1); `submit` claims `WHERE state='open'` so a concurrent cancel can't resurrect a refunded order back to submitted→confirmed (FIX-11); `:id/order` takes a `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` on the listing row to serialize concurrent order placement (a NEED over-capacity could otherwise release more than escrowed) (FIX-10); NEED capacity counts `cancelled` slots as consumed (a per-order cancel already refunded that slot → re-filling it would pay a claimant with no backing escrow) (FIX-9); `:id/cancel` atomic-claims the listing (`WHERE status IN ('open','paused')`) and the NEED remaining-refund is `max(0, capacity − completed − cancelledTotal + justCancelled.length)` where `justCancelled` = the open/submitted orders THIS call cancelled (captured via RETURNING), counts taken AFTER the bulk-cancel — this returns EXACTLY the unreleased escrow (the prior `capacity − completed` form was a `cancelled`-slot FAUCET; the intermediate `− cancelledTotal` form VAPORIZED open-order escrow) (FIX-8a); confirm now locks the listing row FIRST (consistent with order/listing-cancel) to kill a cap-1-NEED confirm-vs-listing-cancel lock-order deadlock, and the one_shot auto-close is `WHERE status='open'`-guarded. Adversarial money auditor APPROVED (conservation algebraically exact; no reachable double-credit/refund/vaporization). PARITY note — human path: `POST /api/exchange/*` via Lucia cookie; agent path: same endpoints via `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar; settlement binds to `getActingAvatar → identity.avatarId`. Agent tool DISCOVERY (a formal `tools.json` declaration) is NOT added — mirrors the shipped `bounties.ts`, which also achieves E5 via route-parity only (no exchange/bounty tool is declared or announced on this base; a `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump is therefore not triggered). Gate GREEN: `apps/api` `tsc --noEmit` 0 errors on `exchange.ts` (31 baseline errors in unrelated files unchanged). NOT committed/pushed (orchestrator opens a non-merged PR → staging for founder sign-off, Rule E4/E5). See §2 (`exchange.ts` row). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-02: **Agent-metaverse P1 — RELIABILITY + money/security + tests fix pass (R1–R5, M1–M5, T1–T3; backend only, NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump — still 7).** **R1 (BLOCKING — "stuck on one boot" root cause):** ElizaOS `runtime.initialize()` (`packages/agent-runtime/src/eliza-runtime.ts`) takes the plugin-sql `pg_advisory_lock`; a hung holder made both a queued `InitMutex.acquire()` waiter AND `initialize()` itself block FOREVER, wedging the box on one boot. Now bounded by an **IN-PROCESS rejecting `Promise.race`** (`INIT_TIMEOUT_MS=90_000`) — NEVER an external kill (an external kill orphans the bun proc still squatting the lock, the documented root cause); `acquire(timeoutMs)` rejects + splices its queued resolver out; `start()` uses `let acquired` so the outer `finally` releases the mutex ONLY if it was actually held. On timeout it REJECTS → `catch` (state='error'+onError) + `finally` (release) run IN-PROCESS, so `agent-orchestrator.ensureAgentRuntime`'s `finally` clears `recoveryInProgress` (a hang previously stranded it → every future call fell into the wait-2s-return-null branch permanently). Residual (accepted, no external-kill mitigation): `Promise.race` does not cancel the losing `initialize()`, so a genuinely lock-holding init stays held in-proc until its connection closes. **R2 (warming watchdog):** `agent-autonomy-driver.ts` `warming` is now a `Map<agentId,warmingSince>`; a warm older than `WARM_WATCHDOG_MS=90s` is evicted so a fresh warm relaunches (a never-settling warm can't wedge an agent forever); `.finally` compare-and-deletes so a watchdog relaunch isn't clobbered by the old warm's late settle. **R3 (nondeterministic/duplicable platformAgentId):** the seeder's arbitrary `.find()` over openclaw-bot rows (no ORDER BY, no unique constraint) let concurrent boots insert 2 `platform_agents` rows for one `openclawBotId` and bind a different one each boot. Now `.orderBy(asc(agents.createdAt))` → binds the earliest + best-effort stop+delete of extras; NEW partial unique index `platform_agents_openclaw_bot_singleton` on `(user_id, type, (config->>'openclawBotId')) WHERE type='openclaw-bot' AND (config->>'openclawBotId') IS NOT NULL` (schema `agents.ts`, mirrors the `platform_agents_system_singleton` precedent) via idempotent `migrations-manual/2026-07-02_platform_agents_openclaw_bot_singleton.sql` (dedupe DELETE keeping earliest, THEN `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`) + `packages/database/scripts/apply-platform-agents-openclaw-bot-singleton.ts` (explicit `AGENTS_DATABASE_URL`, NOT db:push). **R4 (silent empty-decide spin):** `withTimeout` collapses timeout AND error to `''`, so a persistent empty decide (OpenAI 429/quota) span silently — now a per-entry `consecutiveEmptyDecides` counter WARNs past `EMPTY_DECIDE_WARN_THRESHOLD=3` (health SIGNAL only; drive behavior + the `''` contract + the `[AutonomyDriver][debug]` instrumentation unchanged). **R5 (sweeper belt-and-suspenders):** `agent-body-idle-sweeper.ts` house-exemption set is now the UNION of `agentAutonomyDriver.getHouseAgentIds()` and the DB `is_house` flag, so a dropped/renamed column can't silently un-exempt + reap a live house body. **Reconciler fixes (2026-07-02 — post-adversarial):** **(codex BLOCKING)** `agent-orchestrator.ensureAgentRuntime` had an `await db.query` BETWEEN the `recoveryInProgress.has()` guard and the `.add()`, so two concurrent callers (e.g. multiple agents chatting the SAME system NPC) could both pass → duplicate `startAgent`/`runtime.start()` + overwritten `runningAgents`; now `.add()` runs with NO await after the guard (atomic check-then-set) and the DB lookup moved inside the try/finally. **(adversary HIGH — same class as M1)** the `ct-` connect ticket (`ct-<base64url(24)>`, bound to the pending avatar on possession alone, polled repeatedly as `GET /api/agent/connect-status/:token` path param) was NOT covered by the redaction regex → leaked unredacted to stdout/Coolify; `BEARER_TOKEN_RE` now covers `(oc|ag|hat|ct)-` (`services/log-redact.ts` + a new `ct-` test case). **M1 (bearer leak):** `app.onError` (`index.ts`) `console.error(err)` + the `alertError` `Uncaught error on <method> <path>` message + `err.stack` carried the raw `/api/agent/oc-<bearer>/…` real-CT bearer to stdout/Coolify + Telegram — now `redactBearerTokens`'d generally in `services/alert-error.ts` (message top-level + `console.warn` fallback + `JSON.stringify(context)` block) AND at the `index.ts` `console.error`; JSON 500 shape unchanged. **M2 (CT faucet):** `/visit-building` + `/building/:buildingId/chat` credited 1 CT unconditionally → a parked ledger-bound agent could loop-farm; NEW `creditBuildingRewardOncePerDay()` gates ONLY the `creditClawTokens` call (leaderboard event untouched) with an idempotent per-(avatar, building, reason, UTC-day) dedup inside one `db.transaction` that `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`s the SAME `avatars` row the ledger locks, then existence-probes `claw_token_transactions` before crediting — no double-credit under concurrency, no legit-visit regression. **M3/M4:** `/building/:id/chat` + `/move` now use the shared `resolveBuildingCenter` own-property guard (NEW dependency-free `services/building-center.ts`) + `BUILDING_INTERACTION_RADIUS` (was bare `NPC_BUILDING_CENTERS[id]` + `CHAT_RADIUS=2000`), closing the prototype-key NaN proximity-bypass on both, matching `/visit-building`. **M5:** NEW `packages/database/scripts/apply-openclaw-is-house.ts` (explicit `AGENTS_DATABASE_URL`, NOT db:push) applies the idempotent `2026-07-01_add_openclaw_is_house.sql`. **T1/T2/T3:** F1 test now exercises the REAL exported `resolveBuildingCenter`; NEW no-money invariant test (decide→enter_building→talk emits only `agent_chat`, never a token event); stale `partner-hatcher.ts` P5-2 comment reworded to the actual `ocb-<base64url(agentId)>` body id. Gate: `apps/api`+`packages/agent-runtime`+`packages/database`+`packages/shared` `tsc --noEmit` 0; `agent-autonomy-p1.test.ts` 12/12; `log-redact.test.ts` 7/7 (+ the new `ct-` case). Adversarial: codex (R1 race + M2 idempotency) + a general-purpose adversary (M1 bearer + R2/R4/R5 + M3/M4) — both APPROVED after the two reconciler fixes above. NOT committed (orchestrator commits). Phase machine deciding→walking→arrived→talking (LLM only in deciding/arrived; walking is a cheap arrival poll). Hard LLM timeout (a slow brain never blocks the tick or another agent) + idle-throttle (backs off when `npcSimulation.getActiveHumanCount()===0`). First behavior: the agent CHOOSES a teacher by NEED (real inference from the building label/`cryptoFocus` in perception, not nearest) → `enter_building` → `talk_to_npc`. **Orchestrator exemption (`agent-orchestrator.ts`):** `RunningAgent.isHouse` + `startAgent(id, userId, {isHouse})`; `stopInactiveAgents` skips `type==='system-agent' || isHouse` (the driver drives via `useModel`, which does not bump `lastActivity`). `agent-body-idle-sweeper.ts` also EXEMPTS is_house rows. **Shared perception:** the former module-local `agent-gateway.ts buildPerception` MOVED to `npcSimulation.buildPerception(npcId)` (single source; gateway `GET /perception` + SSE `/events` + the driver all call it — no duplication/drift). **PROXIMITY GATE** in `npc-simulation.ts executeHatcherAction` `talk_to_npc` (before `injectAgentChat`): a NON-`hatcher-proxy` body must be within `BUILDING_INTERACTION_RADIUS` of the target (npc pos or `NPC_BUILDING_CENTERS[buildingId]`, `Object.hasOwn`-guarded) or the talk is DROPPED (fail-closed — an unresolvable body has no hatcher-proxy client). A `usdc` bounty escrows on-chain USDC through the EXISTING SAP Option-C gate (`services/sap/escrow-gate.ts`) instead of debiting CT — via a new thin adapter `apps/api/src/services/bounty-escrow-link.ts` (`openBountyEscrow`/`approveBountyEscrow`/`settleBountyEscrow`/`refundBountyEscrow`/`runBountyUsdcSettle`; jobId=bounty.id, depositor=creator, worker=hunter, single-call escrow = whole reward, `usdcRewardBaseUnits` = reward×10^6). Rules: USDC bounty MUST carry `acceptance_criteria` + `maxAttempts=1` (superRefine); no CT debit; escrow opened LAZILY at approve (creator-review approve IS the v1 requester-approval verdict) → `open→approve→settle` releases USDC on PASS; reject records a FAIL (no escrow opened, nothing to refund); connected agent must be ledger-capable (else 403). New admin-only `POST /:id/admin-fail-refund` (adminOnly) force-refunds an OPENED USDC escrow to the creator/depositor (the depositor-bound "fail/refund" path SAP lacked — admin can only trigger, the gate re-asserts depositor + atomic claim). **CT-FAUCET GUARD:** cancel (DELETE) + reject never credit CT for a USDC bounty (creator never debited CT). **TRIPLE-GATED OFF** (`SAP_ENABLED`/`SAP_ESCROW_ENABLED`/`SAP_USDC_ESCROW_ENABLED` default false) + `SAP_DRY_RUN=true` — a `payment_rail=usdc` create returns 503 until a founder flip; no real money moves; every existing CT bounty unchanged.
