The escrow is just escrow: a poster (human OR agent) funds USDC into escrow → a worker agent submits → the depositor approves → settle RELEASES the escrow USDC straight to the worker agent's OWN custodial ATA (NO CT/vCLAW conversion, NO fee, NO custodial float — the in-game CT economy is untouched); refund reclaims unspent USDC. **Rule E5 parity:** both sides bind to `identity.avatarId`'s own Phase-5.1 wallet via `requireAuthOrAgentSession`+`requireLedgerCapable` — a connected/hosted agent posts/works AS ITSELF, no body-supplied signer, no guest. **Gating intact:** `SAP_ENABLED`/`SAP_ESCROW_ENABLED`/`SAP_USDC_ESCROW_ENABLED` default false, `SAP_DRY_RUN=true` default (build+simulate only, never broadcast), mainnet behind the `SAP_ALLOW_MAINNET` code constant (crash-loud + live-send genesis-hash refusal). Schema applied by hand via `migrations-manual/2026-06-30_sap_escrow.sql` (NOT db:push). Needs the Solana audit + Codex before any funded-devnet broadcast. — **PRIOR (2026-06-27): Tokenomics F2 - USDC->vCLAW on-ramp re-authored onto staging + BOUGHT tagging + $0.10/coin store price (GATED, devnet, non-cashable).** Re-authored the audited x402 on-ramp (was only on the 811-commits-stale `feat/payai-x402-economy` branch) onto this F1-based branch. **Route `apps/api/src/routes/ct-topup.ts`** (mounted `/api/ct/topup` in `index.ts`): `POST /quote` issues an x402 v2 402-challenge + a PENDING `ct_topups` row; `POST /settle` verify->settles the payment through the facilitator primitive and credits vCLAW EXACTLY ONCE. **Service `services/x402-payai.ts`** = the verify->settle primitive (`buildTopupQuote`/`verifyAndSettle`/`usdToCt`/`CT_PER_USDC`) over `@x402/core` `HTTPFacilitatorClient` (Apache-2.0; ZERO `@payai/*` imports). **Service `services/x402-config.ts`** re-authored from the branch (supersedes staging's older scaffold): adds the `payai`/`mock`/`cdp` facilitator presets AND a CRASH-LOUD module-load guard that REFUSES TO BOOT if the mock facilitator is active while `CLAWVILLE_ENV==='production'` (the mock rubber-stamps settlement => would mint FREE vCLAW). **Service `services/x402-mock-facilitator.ts`** = TEST-ONLY local facilitator, mounted `/api/x402-mock` only when `X402_MOCK_FACILITATOR==='true'` and NEVER on a prod box (belt-and-suspenders second guard at the mount site). **F2 deltas vs the branch source:** (1) **store price $10 = 100 vCLAW ($0.10/coin) — `CT_PER_USDC` 100->10** (founder 2026-06-27); `usdToCt($10)=100` (NOT 1000) is unit-locked in `x402-payai.test.ts`. This is a ONE-WAY BUY price, never a peg/redeem rate. (2) **on-ramp credits are tagged BOUGHT (non-cashable V-Bucks), NOT the default SOFT** — the settle credit routes through F1's `creditClawTokens({ provenance:'bought', usdBasis })` (no parallel credit), stamping `usd_basis` = the dollars paid on the ledger row (same value as `ct_topups.usd_basis_at_receipt`). BOUGHT is non-cashable BY CONSTRUCTION (only `mintEarned` writes the cashable EARNED tag). **Ledger extension (`claw-token-ledger.ts`, additive):** `LedgerCreditInput` gains an optional `usdBasis?: string` threaded through `creditInTx`; a SOFT credit carrying a `usdBasis` THROWS (usd_basis is a BOUGHT-only accounting field). **DOUBLE-CREDIT INVARIANT** unchanged from the audited branch (one settled tx sig credits once — `ct_topups_txsig_unique` partial-unique + the settle UPDATE+credit in ONE tx + a per-`topupId` `withKeyedMutex` against the payer-loss race). **No schema change** — `ct_topups` (+ `usd_basis_at_receipt`, the unique indexes) is ALREADY on staging (`schema/land.ts`). **PARITY (Rule E5):** both routes go through `requireAuthOrAgentSession` so a connected/hosted agent (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` -> bound avatar) tops up ITS OWN avatar for REAL vCLAW; guests/unbound agents get 401/403, never a body-supplied avatarId. **GATE (nothing real-money flips on):** `/quote`+`/settle` 503 `on_ramp_unconfigured` when `CLAWVILLE_MERCHANT_WALLET_PUBKEY` is unset; the network defaults to DEVNET (`resolveTopupNetwork` never inherits the x402-config mainnet default); `X402_ENABLED` stays off; mainnet is a config flip AFTER a funded settled smoke. **Price-rescale AUDIT (REPORT ONLY, no code change):** at $0.10/coin every CT sink price set in the ~$0.01/coin era now costs 10x more real dollars (e.g. an A-tier parcel 80k CT reads ~$8,000; a Lv4->5 structure upgrade 11k CT reads ~$1,100); a per-surface old$/new$/proposed table is in the F2 implementer report for the founder to confirm BEFORE any rescale — cove bet limits ($0.50 min / $50 max) read fine and were proposed "keep". **No `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump** — the agent ACTION/tool surface is unchanged (REST parity only; in-world agent-action exposure is a later phase, separate task). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-24: **Land builder-economics Slice 1 - RENT-or-BUY acquisition + eviction sweep (engine built; ACTIVATION gated on a world expansion).** New `POST /api/land/parcels/:parcelId/rent` (`land.ts`) mirrors the buy route's exact money discipline: per-avatar `pg_advisory_xact_lock` (outer) + `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` (inner) + `claw-token-ledger.debitClawTokens` IN-TX (first week charged up front, `source:'api'`, never a raw `avatars.clawTokens` write) + `land_transactions(rent_payment)` audit row, single-acquire safety via the `available`→`owned` flip (replay → 409). Sets `tenure='rented'`, `rent_paid_through = now() + RENT_PERIOD_DAYS`. Errors: 404 `parcel_not_found`, 409 `parcel_not_available`/`parcel_cap_reached`, 400 `rent_not_available` (tier carries no `rent_ct_weekly`)/`insufficient_clawtokens`, 501 `founder_not_in_v1`. The weekly rent is the SERVER-stamped `land_parcels.rent_ct_weekly` (read from the row, never the body — same discipline as `price_ct`). **Schema (additive, `schema/land.ts`):** `land_parcels` += `tenure` (`land_tenure` enum rented|owned|starter, NULL=available), `rent_ct_weekly` int, `rent_paid_through` + `grace_until` timestamptz, partial index `land_parcels_rent_sweep_idx` on `(rent_paid_through) WHERE tenure='rented'`; `land_structures` += `status` (`land_structure_status` enum active|archived, default active); `land_transaction_kind` += `rent_payment` + `eviction` (promoted from reserved). Buy + claim-starter now stamp `tenure` ('owned'/'starter'). Both acquire paths reconcile an eviction-archived structure under the parcel lock (`reconcileArchivedStructureOnAcquire`: same-avatar re-acquire → restore, different avatar → purge); owned/structure reads exclude `status='archived'`. **NEW service `services/land-rent-sweeper.ts`** (hourly `setInterval`, started/stopped in `index.ts` like the body-idle sweeper): per due rented parcel, under the SAME advisory+row lock, charges the week (`source:'system'`, advances `rent_paid_through = now()+period`, clears grace) → on insufficient CT opens a `RENT_GRACE_DAYS` grace window → on elapsed grace EVICTS (parcel `status='available'` + owner/tenure/rent cleared, structure `status='archived'`, `land_transactions(eviction)`, `broadcastLandEvent(status:'available')` so the for-sale sign reappears live). Re-entrant + per-row try/catch (one bad row never aborts the pass); side effects (cache bust + SSE) run AFTER each parcel's tx commits. Constants (`@clawville/shared land-economy.ts`): buy ladder raised (C 2k-4k, B 10k-24k, A 40k-80k), new `LAND_RENT_LADDER` (C 50-100, B 250-550, A 1k-2.4k weekly), `RENT_PERIOD_DAYS=7`, `RENT_GRACE_DAYS=3`. **Idempotent migration `apps/api/scripts/migrate-land-tenure.ts`** (explicit `SEED_DATABASE_URL` only, never `.env.local`; `--dry-run` default-safe): creates the enums, adds the columns/index, adds the `eviction` value, reprices existing `price_ct` to the new ladder ("existing == new" founder decision) + stamps `rent_ct_weekly` + back-fills tenure. **PARITY (E5):** rent binds to `identity.avatarId` from `requireAuthOrAgentSession` (human cookie OR `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar) so a connected/hosted agent rents to its OWN avatar with REAL CT; guest → 401; no `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump (rent matches buy's deferred Phase-3 agent-action exposure). The rent engine is tier-agnostic and rents whatever tier carries a `rent_ct_weekly`; it goes LIVE only once a CT-priced tier ring is added to the world (founder decision 2026-06-24: grow the world with a new outer ring — geometry build is the follow-up slice). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-23: **Land 2.1 - cross-player LIVE land-sync over SSE.** `apps/api/src/routes/world.ts` adds a module-level `landStreamSubscribers` Set (each `/api/world/:roomId/stream` registers its SSE writer on connect, removes it on abort + failed keepalive) plus an exported `broadcastLandEvent(payload)` that fans a single named `land` SSE event to EVERY connected world-stream subscriber across ALL rooms - land is GLOBAL state, so the fan-out is cross-room, not per-room. `broadcastLandEvent` is FULLY GUARDED (outer try/catch around `JSON.stringify` + the sync part of `writeSSE`, inner per-subscriber try/catch, async `.catch` on the write) so it can NEVER throw to its caller: it runs AFTER a committed buy/claim, and a thrown notification would otherwise turn an already-settled sale into a client-facing 500. `apps/api/src/routes/land.ts` calls it fire-and-forget after the buy + claim-starter commits (right after the leaderboard event) - notification only, NO settlement/ledger/route-shape change, E5 parity intact. Client: `apps/web/src/hooks/use-world-stream.ts` adds `es.addEventListener('land', …)` that invalidates the shared `['landParcels']` query (extracted to `apps/web/src/lib/land-query-keys.ts`, a `three`-free module re-exported by `land-state-hydrator.tsx` so the SSE hook never pulls the R3F graph) so `LandStateHydrator` refetches and the in-world for-sale signs update within ~1s of another player's buy - no reload, no 60s wait. world-presence OWNS the SSE seam; land CONSUMES it (minimal additive - no snapshot/presence/tick change). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-22: **Poker CASH house-bots — adversarial-audit BLOCKING fixes (scope-leak + turn-clock settlement; no schema change).** Two HIGH money-path holes in the house auto-scaler/bots build, fixed in place. **(1) SCOPE LEAK — a user could self-serve a house-bank-funded bot table.** The create route (`cove-cash-poker.ts`) accepted `source: z.enum(['house','player-public'])` from any caller and `createTable` imposed no creator restriction, so `POST /tables {source:'house',…}` minted a user-owned table whose seats filled with house-bank-debited bots AND was self-driven by the tick — a house-bank exposure/drain vector any player could summon. FIX (both belt + braces): the route enum now allows ONLY `source:'player-public'` for the public branch (`z.literal('player-public')`), so a user POST can never reach a house table; AND `cash-table-manager.ts createTable` now rejects `source==='house'` unless the creator's avatarId IS the house-bank avatar (resolved via `houseBankAvatarProvider`) → `house_table_creation_forbidden` (403). The scaler is unaffected (it builds its creator subject from the house-bank avatar and calls the manager directly). **(2) Turn-clock auto-fold ran OUTSIDE `withTableLock` and never settled.** The sim's armed `setTimeout` fired `onTurnTimeout` directly — mutating the SimTable + advancing + resolving the hand with NO per-table lock (racing a concurrent human action at an `await` suspension point) and leaving an undrained `pendingResults` entry that no tick drains on a no-tick (player-public/private) table → escrow stranded vs Σ seat stacks indefinitely. FIX: `poker-table-sim.ts` gains a `setTurnTimeoutHook(fn)` seam; when set (the cash manager sets it in its constructor), the armed timer routes the expiry to the hook with the tableId INSTEAD of calling `onTurnTimeout` directly (WS-demo/MTT sims set no hook → unchanged inline behavior). The cash manager's new `handleTurnTimeout(tableId)` acquires the SAME `withTableLock`, then `sim.onTurnTimeout` → `settleIfComplete` → `driveSeededAgents` → `settleIfComplete` → `startAndAdvance`, so the timeout-resolved hand is auto-folded/checked AND settled in one locked critical section — never interleaving with a human action, never leaving stranded escrow on ANY table type. Belt-and-braces: `getTableState` lazily settles a surviving `pendingResults` under the lock before a public read so escrow is never reported inflated. **Conservation invariant unchanged** (timeouts move chips between seats at settle; the ONLY mint stays the single guarded boot bankroll credit; bot buy-ins still DEBIT the house bank). Tests: `cash-table-manager.test.ts` +3 cases (create-time scope-guard rejection, non-house-bank-creator rejection, fill-time faucet defense-in-depth) + a new `ControllableClock` describe asserting a fired turn timeout settles the hand with `escrow==Σstacks` and `Σdebits==Σcredits+escrow`, all WITHOUT a human action. PARITY (E5) unchanged: a connected agent (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session`) sits at the SAME house tables via the SAME REST path with REAL-CT settlement. NEEDS staging deploy + live smoke + sign-off (Rule E4). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-22: **F4 — blackjack "bust shows but the table is stuck" freeze (client-only fix in `BlackjackModal.tsx`).** A busted hand could strand the modal in `player-turn` on a fully-terminal hand: Hit/Stand disabled via `activeResolved`, and the "Next Hand" button is settled-phase-only → dead end. **Server is NOT at fault** — a live staging-API repro (`/session/open`→`/hand/deal`→`/action hit` to >21) confirmed a busting hit returns `200 status:settled` with the full busted hand; `isHandTerminal` settles a single-hand bust correctly. The strand is a one-off CLIENT race: a stale `GET /hand/current` read taken inside the server's narrow commit→settle window (script-commit txn done, settle txn not yet — hand momentarily `status='in_progress'` AND terminal) returns the hand as live, and `restoreHandFromServer` applies it AFTER `applySettled`, doing `setHand(live)` + `setSettled(null)` → back to player-turn on a terminal hand. **Fix (no server/ledger change):** (1) ROOT-CAUSE GUARD — `settledHandIdsRef` tracks settled handIds; `restoreHandFromServer` early-returns if a "live" hand's id is already-settled (a stale read can't overwrite a settled hand); set in `applySettled`, cleared on modal close. (2) SELF-HEAL — a new effect: when `phase==='player-turn'` && every sub-hand `isResolved` && nothing in flight (`!inFlight && !busyRef && !agentBusyRef`) && not already healed this handId, calls `resyncAfterStaleDecision()` → `/hand/current` returns `CurrentHandNone {hand:null}` (server settled) → `restoreHandFromServer(…, allowClear=true)` clears to idle → player gets the betting UI back. Once-per-handId (`healedHandIdRef`) so no loop; `busyRef` still-true during the normal settle window prevents any false-fire. PARITY: manual + autonomous both covered (autonomous settled→next-hand auto-advance untouched); guests can't reach the race (their `/hand/current` 401s → null → no restore-overwrite). Gate: web `next build` exit 0 (turbo 5/5). `GameFeatures.md` same-diff. NEEDS staging deploy + founder cold-test (Rule E4). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-20: **Poker CASH (ring) games — P1 SHIPPABLE wire + money atomicity (new `cove-cash-poker.ts` row in §2).** Mounted `coveCashPokerRouter` at `/api/cove/poker/cash` (`index.ts`) + exported the `poker-cash` schema (`packages/database/src/schema/index.ts`) — the route + 4 `poker_cash_*` tables were authored but un-wired. **Money-atomicity fix (`services/poker/cash-table-manager.ts`):** `seatSubject`, `cashOutSeat`, and `settleHand` did debit/credit + insert + update as SEPARATE round-trips with NO `db.transaction` (only an in-process mutex → ZERO DB atomicity: a crash mid-sequence could debit CT with no seat, or double-cash-out on retry). Each now runs in ONE `db.transaction` with the parent table/seat row `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` first, and the ledger `debit/creditClawTokens(input, tx)` composes INTO the same tx (mirroring `tournament-manager.ts registerEntrant`). `cashOutSeat` re-checks the seat status under the lock → a retry replays (no second credit). `settleHand` re-reads `settled_at` under the lock (race-safe idempotency). **Cross-game wiring (Rule E5 keystone):** the settle now writes one `cove_game_events` row per REAL (non-seeded) subject per hand (`gameType='poker'`, `sessionId=shoeId=tableId`, `betAmount=totalCommitted`, `payout=won`, `engineVersion='poker-cash-v1'`; seat packed into the nonce `handNumber*100+seatIndex` to dodge the `(game_type, session_id, nonce)` unique-index collision across a hand's seats). `cove-history.ts` `GAME_TYPES` += `'poker'` (read filter + verify dispatch) and a new `poker` `/verify` branch (`sha256(seed)==hash` + stored-vs-authoritative `poker_cash_hands.pot_result_json` consistency; full multi-player re-deal deferred — P1 persists only the settled outcome). The economy monitor (`cove-economy.ts`) is a `GROUP BY game_type` so poker rows appear automatically (houseNet ≈ 0 expected; rake 0). Lean — no admin triage UI (status column exists for later). **Plus the cove provably-fair `/verify` false-negative FIX** (`cove-verify-compat.ts` `jsonEq` → canonical key-order-insensitive `canonicalJsonEq`; jsonb reorders keys so the old raw `JSON.stringify` compare false-negatived every honest blackjack/holdem/baccarat/slots hand) **+ client Verify UI un-stub** (`ServerEventVerifier.tsx` replaces the stale "ships in Phase 6.7.1" placeholder with a trustless in-browser `sha256(seed)==hash` check + server replay verdict). All staging-first, Codex-reviewed. · 2026-06-19: **Session/auth reconciliation + multiplayer presence dedup (3 founder bugs).** **(1) Delayed login** — `apps/web/src/app/login/page.tsx` now `removeQueries(['auth-me'|'avatar'|'agent-session'])` (`purgeAuthCache`) after a successful login/signup, BEFORE `router.push`. The session cookie is set in the jar by the login response, but the shared SPA `QueryClient` (one instance via `providers.tsx`, `staleTime` 60s) held a stale pre-login `auth-me: null`; the soft-nav into `/game` read it and rendered logged-OUT until a focus refetch ~60s+ later. Evicting the entries forces a clean refetch (brief loading, never wrong-logged-out). **(2) Logout residue** — `apps/web/src/components/game/sidebar-menu.tsx handleLogout` now calls `queryClient.clear()` + `usePlayerStore.clear()` + research-store clears after `api.logout()`; `resetStore()` only reset the game Zustand slice, so the prior user's cached `auth-me`/`avatar`/`agent-session` bled into the next session ("login behavior follows as NPC after logout"). **(3) Trailing "Visitor" ghost** — identity dedup in `apps/api/src/services/room-registry.ts joinPlayer`: a FRESH join evicts any other live presence with the same non-null `userId` (reusing `leavePlayer` so the swapped NPC is restore-queued, no leaked slot); **agents are excluded** (an agent presence resolves AS its owner's `userId` — dedup-by-userId would wrongly evict a human + co-present agent, or two agents of one owner). A RECOVERY rejoin (client replayed a `roomTicket`) that finds a same-account owner is refused via a thrown `PresenceSupersededError` → `apps/api/src/routes/world.ts` `/join` maps it to 409 `{ code: 'presence_superseded' }` (ping-pong guard so two live sessions of one account can't kick each other; Codex-required). Client: `use-world-stream.ts` detects the 409, parks the stream + toasts; `stores/players.ts` tracks a capped "former selves" id set so `isLocal` filters ALL of the viewer's own ids (a lingering prior body never renders as a ghost); a `pagehide` keepalive `/leave` removes the body on reload/close/bfcache. `joinPlayer` return type unchanged (throw, not a widened union) so the ~50 existing destructuring call sites + tests stay type-safe. Codex co-reviewed the plan + revised design (Rule E3-style); the `EventSource`-can't-send-headers + /24-shared-fp-hash findings killed an earlier "send fingerprint on world calls" idea. API tsc clean for changed files; 44 room-registry tests green (5 new dedup cases); `room-ticket` 19 green; `next build` exit 0. NOT the protected Hatcher partner surface (world.ts not in the list; bearer/TTL model + `[ACTION]` whitelist + shared openclaw types untouched). NEEDS founder cold-test (Rule E4). `GameFeatures.md §11z.g` same-diff. Prior audit follows.
**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-02: **Building-interaction proximity gate — EDGE-distance, not center-distance (fixes an un-visitable-large-building bug for ALL agents; NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump).** The four agent building-interaction gates — `POST /:sid/visit-building` + `POST /:sid/building/:buildingId/chat` (`agent-gateway.ts`), `talk_to_npc` building-target (`npc-simulation.ts executeHatcherAction`), and the autonomy driver's `hasArrived` (`agent-autonomy-driver.ts`) — measured `distanceToCenter <= BUILDING_INTERACTION_RADIUS (1000 wu)`. But buildings are large (`BUILDING_EXTENTS`: memory-rag 722×723, messaging-channels / api-integrations 850×850 wu half-extents) and their collider (+`BUILDING_COLLISION_BUFFER` +the moving entity's half-width +A* padding) pushes the nearest WALKABLE approach BEYOND 1000 wu from center — so the gate was **geometrically unsatisfiable for the bigger buildings**: a connected OpenClaw agent (proven live: stalled at 1110 wu from memory-rag's center) AND the hosted house agent (latent — Coralia only "worked" at the small buildings) could never complete a visit/chat/talk there. FIX: new `buildingEdgeDistanceGamePx(x, y, buildingId)` in `packages/shared/src/constants/world-colliders-data.ts` (distance to the building's collider AABB edge in game-pixel units, 0 inside, `null` for a non-building id → callers treat as `Infinity` fail-closed), used by all four gates; `BUILDING_INTERACTION_RADIUS` now means "≤ N wu from the FOOTPRINT edge." `buildPerception().nearbyBuildings[]` gains an `edgeDistance` field (additive; `AgentPerceptionBuilding` type updated) that `hasArrived` + the agent-facing perception now key on. `/move{buildingId}` + `enter_building` destinations unchanged (they already land at the collider edge, which now satisfies the edge-gate). Rule E3 (sim/geometry) — Codex-reviewed. tsc 0; `agent-autonomy-p1.test.ts` 18/18 (+ a regression: a body ~1050 wu from a large building's center — which the OLD center-gate wrongly dropped — now passes). Same-diff: this row. NEW harness `apps/api/scripts/agent-connect/openclaw-e2e.ts` (token-bound real-CT OpenClaw connected-agent e2e) drives the fix on staging. Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-02: **Boot-crush crash-loop fix — process crash guards (+ defensive sequential warm) (`apps/api/src/index.ts` only; NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump).** A fresh Coolify boot CRASH-LOOPED (observed `restarts=2`, `Bun v1.3.14` crash footers, staging). **Accurate root cause (corrected after live verification — the initial "~11 concurrent system-agent warms" write-up was WRONG; `SYSTEM_AGENT_TEMPLATES` has exactly ONE entry, town-guide/Nori, and `ensureSystemNpcs` only seeds DB rows — the 10 teachers lazy-start on first chat, confirmed by live `warmed-count=1`):** on a COLD/CONTENDED boot — cold Supabase + the ElizaOS migration still holding the plugin-sql `pg_advisory_lock` + Coralia's driver lazy-warm racing the single town-guide boot warm — one ElizaOS runtime's `initialize()` exceeded ElizaOS's built-in bootstrap plugin's internal 30s "service registration waiting for runtime initialization" timeout (`task`/`embedding-generation`/`trajectory_logger`). That timeout REJECTED on a promise chain the app does not own → **UNHANDLED REJECTION** → with NO `process.on('unhandledRejection')` handler, Bun killed the whole API → Coolify restart → crash-loop for ~3–4 min until the boot was warm enough (migration done, DB cached) that init finished under 30s. **FIX (`index.ts` only):** **(1) PROCESS CRASH GUARDS (the actual fix)** registered BEFORE the boot IIFE, both LOG REDACTED (`redactBearerTokens` — an agent route path/stack carries a real-CT bearer, cf. M1): `unhandledRejection` → **non-fatal** (log + keep serving — that IS the crash vector; a stray optional-service reject must not down the server, the runtime lazy-restarts); `uncaughtException` → **log + `exit(1)`** (a sync throw = undefined state; resuming risks a zombie serving corrupt in-memory state — e.g. a poker/cove sim-tick on a bad table — with `/health` green so Coolify never self-heals; exiting restores crash-ONLY self-healing and does NOT re-introduce the crash-LOOP, which came from the rejection, not a sync throw). **(2) SEQUENTIAL warm (DEFENSIVE/future-proofing, NOT the fix)** — the boot warm loop over `systemAgents` is now a **detached** `void (async () => { for (…) { try { await ensureAgentRuntime(…) } catch {} } })()`; today it warms the ONE system agent, but kept sequential so that IF more system agents are added their warms funnel one-at-a-time through the init mutex instead of stacking pipelines. Detached so boot + `/health` never wait on the warm chain; per-agent errors swallowed + a warn on a null (not-ready) runtime (Codex nit). Crash-loud boot invariants UNAFFECTED (`FINGERPRINT_SECRET`, `ALLOW_TEST_PARTNER_PUBKEY`-on-prod throw at MODULE LOAD before the handlers register; the CF-worker preflight + agents-table check call `process.exit()` directly, which `uncaughtException` does not intercept). In-process only (no external kill — never orphans the plugin-sql advisory lock, per `feedback_elizaos_init_timeout_lock`/R1). Adversarial review: reliability lens APPROVE, safety lens APPROVE_WITH_NITS (`uncaughtException`→`exit(1)`, applied), Codex APPROVE_WITH_NITS (warm-log-on-null nit, applied). tsc 0; `agent-autonomy-p1.test.ts` 17/17. **VERIFIED on a real fresh Coolify deploy:** `restarts=0`, 0 `Bun` crash footers, 0 service-reg timeouts, town-guide warmed, Coralia decide→walk→arrive→talk (a `docker restart` does NOT reproduce the crush — needs a real deploy). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-02: **Autonomy driver — human-presence-INDEPENDENT decision cadence (founder design correction; backend only, NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump — still 7).** The P1 driver (`services/agent-autonomy-driver.ts`) had an idle-throttle (`IDLE_DECIDE_EVERY=4`): when `npcSimulation.getActiveHumanCount()===0` it LLM-decided only every 4th 30s tick (=120s), so a hosted agent was **4× less active when alone** — a direct contradiction of the agent-metaverse premise that hosted agents ARE the living economy and must act continuously with ongoing activity present (`docs/agent-metaverse-model.md`; CLAUDE.md "Autonomous is a FULL-SCOPE economic participant … PERSIST when the user leaves"). REMOVED entirely: the `IDLE_DECIDE_EVERY` const, the `getActiveHumanCount()`/`throttledIdle` computation in `tick()`, the `throttledIdle` param + `if (throttledIdle) return` guard in `driveOnce`, and the `humansPresent`/`throttledIdle` debug fields. The driver now runs the SAME cadence regardless of human presence; real per-agent decide rate is naturally paced by the 30s tick + walk time + the 60s talk-cooldown (~2–3 min per full decide→walk→arrive→talk→cooldown loop), so always-on cost for one agent is modest and the fleet-scale cost lever is the planned open-source-model migration (config, not code), NOT dialing agents down when unwatched. `npcSimulation.getActiveHumanCount()` remains (now unused by the driver; harmless). tsc 0; `agent-autonomy-p1.test.ts` 17/17 (no test referenced the throttle). Same-diff: `docs/agent-metaverse-p1-plan.md` ("Human-presence-independent cadence" + N1 marked superseded) + this row. Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-02: **Agent `[ACTION:]` executor — label-tolerant building resolution (dropped-decide-tick fix; backend only, NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump — still 7).** The autonomy/Hatcher perception lists each teaching building as `"<label> [<slug>]"` (e.g. `"Chum Bucket [code-development]"`); a live staging log showed Coralia occasionally emit the human LABEL as the id (`enter_building(buildingId=Chum Bucket)`) → the strict `Object.hasOwn(NPC_BUILDING_CENTERS, id)` gate DROPPED it (`postDispatch destinationBuildingId=null`) → a wasted 30s decide tick (she self-corrected the next tick, so non-fatal, but real). NEW exported `resolveBuildingId(raw)` in `services/building-center.ts` (alongside `resolveBuildingCenter`) maps a raw id to its canonical slug: (1) exact own-property slug, else (2) a case/punctuation-insensitive match against the slug then the `BUILDING_OPENCLAW_THEMES` label. The alias table is a **`Map`** (not a plain object) so a normalized `"constructor"/"tostring"/…` can NEVER hit `Object.prototype` and resolve to a truthy fn — same prototype-key CT-farm class `resolveBuildingCenter` guards (F1); it only ever returns an own-property teaching-building slug or `null`. NOT a signing/session/SSRF/money/custody change. Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-02: **Agent-metaverse P1 — RELIABILITY + money/security + tests fix pass (R1–R5, M1–M5, T1–T3; backend only, NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump — still 7).** **R1 (BLOCKING — "stuck on one boot" root cause):** ElizaOS `runtime.initialize()` (`packages/agent-runtime/src/eliza-runtime.ts`) takes the plugin-sql `pg_advisory_lock`; a hung holder made both a queued `InitMutex.acquire()` waiter AND `initialize()` itself block FOREVER, wedging the box on one boot. Now bounded by an **IN-PROCESS rejecting `Promise.race`** (`INIT_TIMEOUT_MS=90_000`) — NEVER an external kill (an external kill orphans the bun proc still squatting the lock, the documented root cause); `acquire(timeoutMs)` rejects + splices its queued resolver out; `start()` uses `let acquired` so the outer `finally` releases the mutex ONLY if it was actually held. On timeout it REJECTS → `catch` (state='error'+onError) + `finally` (release) run IN-PROCESS, so `agent-orchestrator.ensureAgentRuntime`'s `finally` clears `recoveryInProgress` (a hang previously stranded it → every future call fell into the wait-2s-return-null branch permanently). Residual (accepted, no external-kill mitigation): `Promise.race` does not cancel the losing `initialize()`, so a genuinely lock-holding init stays held in-proc until its connection closes. **R2 (warming watchdog):** `agent-autonomy-driver.ts` `warming` is now a `Map<agentId,warmingSince>`; a warm older than `WARM_WATCHDOG_MS=90s` is evicted so a fresh warm relaunches (a never-settling warm can't wedge an agent forever); `.finally` compare-and-deletes so a watchdog relaunch isn't clobbered by the old warm's late settle. **R3 (nondeterministic/duplicable platformAgentId):** the seeder's arbitrary `.find()` over openclaw-bot rows (no ORDER BY, no unique constraint) let concurrent boots insert 2 `platform_agents` rows for one `openclawBotId` and bind a different one each boot. Now `.orderBy(asc(agents.createdAt))` → binds the earliest + best-effort stop+delete of extras; NEW partial unique index `platform_agents_openclaw_bot_singleton` on `(user_id, type, (config->>'openclawBotId')) WHERE type='openclaw-bot' AND (config->>'openclawBotId') IS NOT NULL` (schema `agents.ts`, mirrors the `platform_agents_system_singleton` precedent) via idempotent `migrations-manual/2026-07-02_platform_agents_openclaw_bot_singleton.sql` (dedupe DELETE keeping earliest, THEN `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`) + `packages/database/scripts/apply-platform-agents-openclaw-bot-singleton.ts` (explicit `AGENTS_DATABASE_URL`, NOT db:push). **R4 (silent empty-decide spin):** `withTimeout` collapses timeout AND error to `''`, so a persistent empty decide (OpenAI 429/quota) span silently — now a per-entry `consecutiveEmptyDecides` counter WARNs past `EMPTY_DECIDE_WARN_THRESHOLD=3` (health SIGNAL only; drive behavior + the `''` contract + the `[AutonomyDriver][debug]` instrumentation unchanged). **R5 (sweeper belt-and-suspenders):** `agent-body-idle-sweeper.ts` house-exemption set is now the UNION of `agentAutonomyDriver.getHouseAgentIds()` and the DB `is_house` flag, so a dropped/renamed column can't silently un-exempt + reap a live house body. **Reconciler fixes (2026-07-02 — post-adversarial):** **(codex BLOCKING)** `agent-orchestrator.ensureAgentRuntime` had an `await db.query` BETWEEN the `recoveryInProgress.has()` guard and the `.add()`, so two concurrent callers (e.g. multiple agents chatting the SAME system NPC) could both pass → duplicate `startAgent`/`runtime.start()` + overwritten `runningAgents`; now `.add()` runs with NO await after the guard (atomic check-then-set) and the DB lookup moved inside the try/finally. **(adversary HIGH — same class as M1)** the `ct-` connect ticket (`ct-<base64url(24)>`, bound to the pending avatar on possession alone, polled repeatedly as `GET /api/agent/connect-status/:token` path param) was NOT covered by the redaction regex → leaked unredacted to stdout/Coolify; `BEARER_TOKEN_RE` now covers `(oc|ag|hat|ct)-` (`services/log-redact.ts` + a new `ct-` test case). **M1 (bearer leak):** `app.onError` (`index.ts`) `console.error(err)` + the `alertError` `Uncaught error on <method> <path>` message + `err.stack` carried the raw `/api/agent/oc-<bearer>/…` real-CT bearer to stdout/Coolify + Telegram — now `redactBearerTokens`'d generally in `services/alert-error.ts` (message top-level + `console.warn` fallback + `JSON.stringify(context)` block) AND at the `index.ts` `console.error`; JSON 500 shape unchanged. **M2 (CT faucet):** `/visit-building` + `/building/:buildingId/chat` credited 1 CT unconditionally → a parked ledger-bound agent could loop-farm; NEW `creditBuildingRewardOncePerDay()` gates ONLY the `creditClawTokens` call (leaderboard event untouched) with an idempotent per-(avatar, building, reason, UTC-day) dedup inside one `db.transaction` that `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`s the SAME `avatars` row the ledger locks, then existence-probes `claw_token_transactions` before crediting — no double-credit under concurrency, no legit-visit regression. **M3/M4:** `/building/:id/chat` + `/move` now use the shared `resolveBuildingCenter` own-property guard (NEW dependency-free `services/building-center.ts`) + `BUILDING_INTERACTION_RADIUS` (was bare `NPC_BUILDING_CENTERS[id]` + `CHAT_RADIUS=2000`), closing the prototype-key NaN proximity-bypass on both, matching `/visit-building`. **M5:** NEW `packages/database/scripts/apply-openclaw-is-house.ts` (explicit `AGENTS_DATABASE_URL`, NOT db:push) applies the idempotent `2026-07-01_add_openclaw_is_house.sql`. **T1/T2/T3:** F1 test now exercises the REAL exported `resolveBuildingCenter`; NEW no-money invariant test (decide→enter_building→talk emits only `agent_chat`, never a token event); stale `partner-hatcher.ts` P5-2 comment reworded to the actual `ocb-<base64url(agentId)>` body id. Gate: `apps/api`+`packages/agent-runtime`+`packages/database`+`packages/shared` `tsc --noEmit` 0; `agent-autonomy-p1.test.ts` 12/12; `log-redact.test.ts` 7/7 (+ the new `ct-` case). Adversarial: codex (R1 race + M2 idempotency) + a general-purpose adversary (M1 bearer + R2/R4/R5 + M3/M4) — both APPROVED after the two reconciler fixes above. NOT committed (orchestrator commits). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-07-01: **Agent-metaverse P1 (slices 1+3) — ONE live autonomous "house" agent (non-money loop; NO CT/leaderboard — slice 4 deferred).** Activates the first ClawVille-hosted autonomous agent end-to-end: perceive → decide (gpt-4o-mini) → act (walk to a teacher → talk), on its OWN 30s driver, separate from the 200ms NPC tick. **Schema (additive):** `openclaw_bots.is_house BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false` (`schema/claws.ts`) — INTERNAL-ONLY (marks a hosted fixture; drives the body-idle-sweeper exemption + server-side house-agent enumeration; NEVER serialized to any snapshot/`/rooms` roster/wire). Idempotent DDL `migrations-manual/2026-07-01_add_openclaw_is_house.sql` (apply by hand — NOT db:push). **NEW service `services/house-agent-seeder.ts` (`ensureHouseAgent`)** — boot-wired in `index.ts` after the system-NPC seeder; mirrors `ensureSystemAgents`: upserts the `openclaw_bots` row (is_house=true, `session_expires_at=NULL` so the 24h session sweeper skips it) + a `platform_agents` (type `openclaw-bot`, **NO gateway** → cognition on the OpenAI text provider = gpt-4o-mini, provider-SWAPPABLE for the fleet by later setting `customization.gateway`) whose runtime is warmed via `agentOrchestrator.startAgent(id, userId, {isHouse:true})` + an in-world AVATAR body via `npcSimulation.registerOpenClaw` with protocol **`nanoclaw` (NOT `hatcher-proxy`)** — the body renders via `snapshot.npcs` (the Hatcher-proven avatar-body path), NOT `autonomousAvatars`. Restart survival is free (seeder re-runs at boot; `avatarBodyId(agentId)` is deterministic). Money: NONE. **NEW service `services/agent-autonomy-driver.ts`** — own ~30s `setInterval` (started/stopped in `index.ts` boot/shutdown), enumerates registered house agents (bounded Map + in-flight Set), per agent: `npcSimulation.buildPerception(bodyId)` → `ElizaRuntime.decide` → `npcSimulation.dispatchHatcherActions(bodyId, reply)`. Phase machine deciding→walking→arrived→talking (LLM only in deciding/arrived; walking is a cheap arrival poll). Hard LLM timeout (a slow brain never blocks the tick or another agent) + idle-throttle (backs off when `npcSimulation.getActiveHumanCount()===0`). First behavior: the agent CHOOSES a teacher by NEED (real inference from the building label/`cryptoFocus` in perception, not nearest) → `enter_building` → `talk_to_npc`. **Orchestrator exemption (`agent-orchestrator.ts`):** `RunningAgent.isHouse` + `startAgent(id, userId, {isHouse})`; `stopInactiveAgents` skips `type==='system-agent' || isHouse` (the driver drives via `useModel`, which does not bump `lastActivity`). `agent-body-idle-sweeper.ts` also EXEMPTS is_house rows. **Shared perception:** the former module-local `agent-gateway.ts buildPerception` MOVED to `npcSimulation.buildPerception(npcId)` (single source; gateway `GET /perception` + SSE `/events` + the driver all call it — no duplication/drift). **PROXIMITY GATE** in `npc-simulation.ts executeHatcherAction` `talk_to_npc` (before `injectAgentChat`): a NON-`hatcher-proxy` body must be within `BUILDING_INTERACTION_RADIUS` of the target (npc pos or `NPC_BUILDING_CENTERS[buildingId]`, `Object.hasOwn`-guarded) or the talk is DROPPED (fail-closed — an unresolvable body has no hatcher-proxy client). Hatcher (`hatcher-proxy`, both register modes) stays EXEMPT (contract-locked — §3a manual + `PROTOCOL_VERSION` + harness fast-follow); predicate keys on `getOpenClawClient(npcId)?.getProtocol()`, NOT `is_house`. `BUILDING_INTERACTION_RADIUS = 1000` is ONE shared constant (`packages/shared/src/constants/npc-definitions.ts`) that ALSO replaced the ad-hoc `VISIT_RADIUS=2000` on the authed `agent-gateway.ts visit-building` path. **NEW `ElizaRuntime.decide(prompt)`** (`packages/agent-runtime/src/eliza-runtime.ts`) — single-shot `useModel(TEXT_SMALL)`, provider-swappable, no memory/action pipeline (the sim owns dispatch). **NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump** (7 unchanged — the `[ACTION:]` whitelist + SKILL.md/manual are untouched; the deferred Hatcher-gate fast-follow owns any bump). **NO money:** `claw-token-ledger` untouched; the executor never settles CT. See `docs/agent-metaverse-p1-plan.md` "What shipped". **Reconciler fixes (2026-07-01 — punch list B1 + N1–N4):** **(B1, blocking)** the house body's `avatarConfig.autonomyMode` is now **`self-managed`** (was `server-managed`), so `planNpcBehaviors` (which only skips `isOpenClaw && autonomyMode==='self-managed'`) no longer wander-hijacks it out from under the 30s driver — the body is now moved EXCLUSIVELY by `agentAutonomyDriver` (path movement in `moveNpcs` is mode-independent; only the autonomous PLANNER is mode-gated). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-22: **Poker CASH house-bots — adversarial-audit BLOCKING fixes (scope-leak + turn-clock settlement; no schema change).** Two HIGH money-path holes in the house auto-scaler/bots build, fixed in place. **(1) SCOPE LEAK — a user could self-serve a house-bank-funded bot table.** The create route (`cove-cash-poker.ts`) accepted `source: z.enum(['house','player-public'])` from any caller and `createTable` imposed no creator restriction, so `POST /tables {source:'house',…}` minted a user-owned table whose seats filled with house-bank-debited bots AND was self-driven by the tick — a house-bank exposure/drain vector any player could summon. FIX (both belt + braces): the route enum now allows ONLY `source:'player-public'` for the public branch (`z.literal('player-public')`), so a user POST can never reach a house table; AND `cash-table-manager.ts createTable` now rejects `source==='house'` unless the creator's avatarId IS the house-bank avatar (resolved via `houseBankAvatarProvider`) → `house_table_creation_forbidden` (403). The scaler is unaffected (it builds its creator subject from the house-bank avatar and calls the manager directly). **(2) Turn-clock auto-fold ran OUTSIDE `withTableLock` and never settled.** The sim's armed `setTimeout` fired `onTurnTimeout` directly — mutating the SimTable + advancing + resolving the hand with NO per-table lock (racing a concurrent human action at an `await` suspension point) and leaving an undrained `pendingResults` entry that no tick drains on a no-tick (player-public/private) table → escrow stranded vs Σ seat stacks indefinitely. FIX: `poker-table-sim.ts` gains a `setTurnTimeoutHook(fn)` seam; when set (the cash manager sets it in its constructor), the armed timer routes the expiry to the hook with the tableId INSTEAD of calling `onTurnTimeout` directly (WS-demo/MTT sims set no hook → unchanged inline behavior). The cash manager's new `handleTurnTimeout(tableId)` acquires the SAME `withTableLock`, then `sim.onTurnTimeout` → `settleIfComplete` → `driveSeededAgents` → `settleIfComplete` → `startAndAdvance`, so the timeout-resolved hand is auto-folded/checked AND settled in one locked critical section — never interleaving with a human action, never leaving stranded escrow on ANY table type. Belt-and-braces: `getTableState` lazily settles a surviving `pendingResults` under the lock before a public read so escrow is never reported inflated. **Conservation invariant unchanged** (timeouts move chips between seats at settle; the ONLY mint stays the single guarded boot bankroll credit; bot buy-ins still DEBIT the house bank). Tests: `cash-table-manager.test.ts` +3 cases (create-time scope-guard rejection, non-house-bank-creator rejection, fill-time faucet defense-in-depth) + a new `ControllableClock` describe asserting a fired turn timeout settles the hand with `escrow==Σstacks` and `Σdebits==Σcredits+escrow`, all WITHOUT a human action. Gate GREEN: `DATABASE_URL='' bun test apps/api/src/services/poker/` 91 pass / 0 fail; `apps/api bunx tsc --noEmit` clean for touched files (only the pre-existing `@clawville/wager-program` module-resolution error remains). PARITY (E5) unchanged: a connected agent (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session`) sits at the SAME house tables via the SAME REST path with REAL-CT settlement. NEEDS staging deploy + live smoke + sign-off (Rule E4). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-22: **F4 — blackjack "bust shows but the table is stuck" freeze (client-only fix in `BlackjackModal.tsx`).** A busted hand could strand the modal in `player-turn` on a fully-terminal hand: Hit/Stand disabled via `activeResolved`, and the "Next Hand" button is settled-phase-only → dead end. **Server is NOT at fault** — a live staging-API repro (`/session/open`→`/hand/deal`→`/action hit` to >21) confirmed a busting hit returns `200 status:settled` with the full busted hand; `isHandTerminal` settles a single-hand bust correctly. The strand is a one-off CLIENT race: a stale `GET /hand/current` read taken inside the server's narrow commit→settle window (script-commit txn done, settle txn not yet — hand momentarily `status='in_progress'` AND terminal) returns the hand as live, and `restoreHandFromServer` applies it AFTER `applySettled`, doing `setHand(live)` + `setSettled(null)` → back to player-turn on a terminal hand. **Fix (no server/ledger change):** (1) ROOT-CAUSE GUARD — `settledHandIdsRef` tracks settled handIds; `restoreHandFromServer` early-returns if a "live" hand's id is already-settled (a stale read can't overwrite a settled hand); set in `applySettled`, cleared on modal close. (2) SELF-HEAL — a new effect: when `phase==='player-turn'` && every sub-hand `isResolved` && nothing in flight (`!inFlight && !busyRef && !agentBusyRef`) && not already healed this handId, calls `resyncAfterStaleDecision()` → `/hand/current` returns `CurrentHandNone {hand:null}` (server settled) → `restoreHandFromServer(…, allowClear=true)` clears to idle → player gets the betting UI back. Once-per-handId (`healedHandIdRef`) so no loop; `busyRef` still-true during the normal settle window prevents any false-fire. PARITY: manual + autonomous both covered (autonomous settled→next-hand auto-advance untouched); guests can't reach the race (their `/hand/current` 401s → null → no restore-overwrite). Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-20: **Poker CASH (ring) games — P1 SHIPPABLE wire + money atomicity (new `cove-cash-poker.ts` row in §2).** Mounted `coveCashPokerRouter` at `/api/cove/poker/cash` (`index.ts`) + exported the `poker-cash` schema (`packages/database/src/schema/index.ts`) — the route + 4 `poker_cash_*` tables were authored but un-wired. **Money-atomicity fix (`services/poker/cash-table-manager.ts`):** `seatSubject`, `cashOutSeat`, and `settleHand` did debit/credit + insert + update as SEPARATE round-trips with NO `db.transaction` (only an in-process mutex → ZERO DB atomicity: a crash mid-sequence could debit CT with no seat, or double-cash-out on retry). Each now runs in ONE `db.transaction` with the parent table/seat row `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` first, and the ledger `debit/creditClawTokens(input, tx)` composes INTO the same tx (mirroring `tournament-manager.ts registerEntrant`). `cashOutSeat` re-checks the seat status under the lock → a retry replays (no second credit). `settleHand` re-reads `settled_at` under the lock (race-safe idempotency). **Cross-game wiring (Rule E5 keystone):** the settle now writes one `cove_game_events` row per REAL (non-seeded) subject per hand (`gameType='poker'`, `sessionId=shoeId=tableId`, `betAmount=totalCommitted`, `payout=won`, `engineVersion='poker-cash-v1'`; seat packed into the nonce `handNumber*100+seatIndex` to dodge the `(game_type, session_id, nonce)` unique-index collision across a hand's seats). `cove-history.ts` `GAME_TYPES` += `'poker'` (read filter + verify dispatch) and a new `poker` `/verify` branch (`sha256(seed)==hash` + stored-vs-authoritative `poker_cash_hands.pot_result_json` consistency; full multi-player re-deal deferred — P1 persists only the settled outcome). The economy monitor (`cove-economy.ts`) is a `GROUP BY game_type` so poker rows appear automatically (houseNet ≈ 0 expected; rake 0). CT-only + the SOL/USDC seam kept as gated future code (enum + escrow columns retained, never deleted). **NOT in this slice:** the agent ACTION/tool surface + `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump (REST parity is complete; in-world agent-action discovery is a later phase — no PROTOCOL bump, Hatcher whitelist/manual untouched), the house auto-scaler, real agent poker AI, and live WebSocket. Gates GREEN: `bun test apps/api/src/services/poker/` 74 pass; `apps/api bunx tsc --noEmit` 0 errors; web `bun run build` ✓ Compiled. Migration `migrations-manual/2026-06-20_poker_cash_tables.sql` stays additive idempotent (apply by hand, NOT db:push). `GameFeatures.md §18a.m` same-diff. Prior audit follows. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-21 batch-2: **Chat brevity (F3) + hosted-agent reconnect (F2).** **(F3 — building teacher + Nori replies too long)** `packages/agent-runtime/src/eliza-runtime.ts`: `convertToElizaCharacter` now appends a module-const `CONCISE_CHAT_DIRECTIVE` (STRICT "reply in 1-3 short sentences, plain prose, no greetings/lists/monologues, expand only on an explicit go-deep request") to the resolved `system` for EVERY location + system agent — applied AFTER the persona prompt (whether `customization.system` verbatim or synthesized) so a per-template prompt can't drop it. `processMessage` gained a `conversational?: boolean` context flag; when set it caps the reply at `CHAT_RESPONSE_MAX_TOKENS` (=200), else keeps the 1000 default. (A live staging probe drove the final numbers: the first cut — soft "aim for 2-3" wording + a 320 cap — still let gpt-4o-mini ramble to ~6 sentences on a normal question; the strict directive + 200 cap is what actually lands replies at ~1-3 sentences.) The building-teacher (`chat.ts:291`) + system-agent/Nori (`chat.ts:102`) routes pass `conversational: true`; avatar-chat (`avatars.ts`) and connected-agent gateway (`agent-gateway.ts`) paths are unchanged (1000). **We deliberately did NOT gate on `providerState.services`** — services are injected whenever the VISITOR has an avatar (i.e. for nearly every real logged-in chat), so that gate would have left the founder's case uncapped (the exact miss caught at tsc/probe time). Regression test (`eliza-runtime.persona.test.ts`) asserts the directive is present whether the system came from customization OR was template-synthesized. **Also fixed in the same batch (latent F5 chibi debt surfaced by `tsc`):** `AgentConfigExport.agentCategory` (`packages/database/src/schema/agent-configs.ts`, the hardcoded mirror of `AGENT_CATEGORIES`) + the agent-import Zod enum (`agent-setup.ts:177`) were missing `'chibi'`, so `agent-setup.ts` failed to typecheck and an exported chibi/hatcher agent would 400 on re-import — both extended to the full 6-category set. **(F2 — hosted agent shows "reconnect" on chat)** `/api/auth/me/agent-session` (`auth.ts`) already returns `mode` (`'hosted'` = a Milady/Hermes ElizaOS runtime that IS the avatar via `HOSTED_HARNESSES` + `platformAgentId`; vs `external-active|idle|expired`). The client just wasn't consuming it: `api.getAgentSession`'s return type now includes the `mode` union, and `apps/web/src/components/game/avatar-chat-bar.tsx` reads the SAME cached `['agent-session']` query (enabled:false → pure cache subscription, no extra fetch) to derive `agentHosted = connected && mode==='hosted'`, then gates `pairedNoBearer = agentPaired && !agentSessionId && !agentHosted`. A hosted avatar has no external bearer to lose and nothing to reconnect (chatting already routes to it via the authed avatar-chat path), so the bogus "Reconnect your agent to chat as it" CTA is suppressed; external/idle/expired agents still get the correct reconnect CTA. `sessionEnded` was already hosted-safe (its `routedThroughAgent` gate requires a live bearer, which hosted never has). No server change, no DB/env change. Web `next build` exit 0; agent-runtime tsc clean; persona+brevity tests 5/5. NEEDS live staging verify + founder cold-test (Rule E4). All staging-first, Codex-reviewed. · 2026-06-19: **Session/auth reconciliation + multiplayer presence dedup (3 founder bugs).** **(1) Delayed login** — `apps/web/src/app/login/page.tsx` now `removeQueries(['auth-me'|'avatar'|'agent-session'])` (`purgeAuthCache`) after a successful login/signup, BEFORE `router.push`. The session cookie is set in the jar by the login response, but the shared SPA `QueryClient` (one instance via `providers.tsx`, `staleTime` 60s) held a stale pre-login `auth-me: null`; the soft-nav into `/game` read it and rendered logged-OUT until a focus refetch ~60s+ later. Evicting the entries forces a clean refetch (brief loading, never wrong-logged-out). **(2) Logout residue** — `apps/web/src/components/game/sidebar-menu.tsx handleLogout` now calls `queryClient.clear()` + `usePlayerStore.clear()` + research-store clears after `api.logout()`; `resetStore()` only reset the game Zustand slice, so the prior user's cached `auth-me`/`avatar`/`agent-session` bled into the next session ("login behavior follows as NPC after logout"). **(3) Trailing "Visitor" ghost** — identity dedup in `apps/api/src/services/room-registry.ts joinPlayer`: a FRESH join evicts any other live presence with the same non-null `userId` (reusing `leavePlayer` so the swapped NPC is restore-queued, no leaked slot); **agents are excluded** (an agent presence resolves AS its owner's `userId` — dedup-by-userId would wrongly evict a human + co-present agent, or two agents of one owner). A RECOVERY rejoin (client replayed a `roomTicket`) that finds a same-account owner is refused via a thrown `PresenceSupersededError` → `apps/api/src/routes/world.ts` `/join` maps it to 409 `{ code: 'presence_superseded' }` (ping-pong guard so two live sessions of one account can't kick each other; Codex-required). Client: `use-world-stream.ts` detects the 409, parks the stream + toasts; `stores/players.ts` tracks a capped "former selves" id set so `isLocal` filters ALL of the viewer's own ids (a lingering prior body never renders as a ghost); a `pagehide` keepalive `/leave` removes the body on reload/close/bfcache. `joinPlayer` return type unchanged (throw, not a widened union) so the ~50 existing destructuring call sites + tests stay type-safe. Codex co-reviewed the plan + revised design (Rule E3-style); the `EventSource`-can't-send-headers + /24-shared-fp-hash findings killed an earlier "send fingerprint on world calls" idea. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-18: **i18n translation UX rework (lag / switch-back / language count).** Client `game-language-control.tsx` reworked: (1) **incremental processing** — the MutationObserver now processes only changed/added subtrees (`collectRecordsFromNode`) via a debounced `flushPending` queue instead of a FULL `.game-container` TreeWalker re-walk on every mutation (the old lag source); full walk runs only on initial pass + language switch. (2) **fast-reapply** — when React reverts a translated node back to English, the observer re-applies the cached translation SYNCHRONOUSLY (no debounce, no API) so it never flashes English ("randomly switches back"); the crude global `suppressObserverUntil` is replaced by per-node/per-attr self-write detection (`current===applied` ⇒ our write). (3) **dynamic-string skip** (`isDynamicStatusText`) — high-churn counters/timers ("6 / 12 visited", "1,250", "LV 1 · 0 SKILLS") are not translated, so they stop thrashing the cache and tripping the limit. (4) **~43-language menu** (was 10; server already supports any BCP-47 locale). (5) **client 429 backoff** (~12s, applies cached-only) + a generation/seq model (full pass bumps `requestSeq`, incremental reads it; post-await bail on stale). **Server:** `i18n.ts` rate limit 30→60/min/IP (headroom for a real first-page translate now that the client makes far fewer calls). Recapture-bug fix (`getTextOriginal`/`getAttrOriginal` now recapture a tracked-but-untranslated node React fills with new text) caught by Codex review (Rule E3). Web `next build` exit 0; `apps/api` tsc clean for `i18n.ts`. No DB schema/env change. `GameFeatures.md` updated same-diff. Prior audit follows.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-15: **Portal outbound SSRF guards + CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_* boot preflight (security; no schema change).** Two HIGH partner-protected-surface hardenings. (1) `apps/api/src/routes/portal.ts` — both outbound partner session-issue `fetch()`es now route through the SAME SSRF guards the cognition path uses: the Hatcher fetch (`POST /api/portal/hatcher`) through `validateHatcherProxyUrlResolved` (hatcher: host allowlist + DNS private-IP reject) and the 'scape fetch (`POST /api/portal/scape`) through `validateOutboundUrlResolved` (DNS private-IP reject, no host allowlist — 'scape is not a Hatcher host). Both fail CLOSED (503 + `*.cross_failed` event with `reason:'ssrf_blocked'`) and set `redirect:'error'` so a 3xx rebind can't re-leak the signed issuer payload to an internal host. These are server-configured URLs (defense-in-depth + the "all outbound cognition/webhook/launch SSRF-guarded" invariant), not live attacker input. (2) `apps/api/src/index.ts` — explicit BOOT preflight asserts `CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_URL` + `CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_BEARER` are set at startup (`console.error` + `process.exit(1)`), making the documented "hard-required on boot" guarantee TRUE instead of a lazy first-wallet-op failure inside `keypair-vault.ts` (whose lazy `requireWorkerEnv` check stays as defense-in-depth). Mirrors the `FINGERPRINT_SECRET` crash-loud pattern. Gates: `apps/api` `bunx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (zero new errors). §7 + §13 same-diff. Prior audit follows.

Partner relay items R1-R8 (Hatcher must confirm/change) tracked in `.hatcher-ref/CONTRACT.md`. **Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-12: **Codex ROUND-6 re-audit (2 MAJOR backend; no schema change).** A sixth independent Codex pass (`docs/codex-review-findings-2026-06-12.md` ROUND 6) ACCEPTED the P5-1 not-a-bug argument (commit-first ordering, single-replica + process-local Map) and found 0 BLOCKING + 2 MAJOR. **P6-1 (MAJOR) — `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bumped 4 → 5.** `skill-protocol.ts` is the single source of the connection-protocol version; the prior round had pinned it at 4 with a "stays at 4 / error-response doc only" comment. That judgment was correct for the override error-contract change IN ISOLATION but WRONG cumulatively: across this session the protocol manual (`buildProtocolManual`) gained multiple MATERIAL contract additions a partner is TOLD it can rely on — `sessionExpiresAt` surfacing, the idle-body-despawn two-clock model, the Hatcher `session.ended` expiry webhook, the per-partner daily registration cap, AND the override-mode target-availability error contract. Partners keying on `orientation.version` (not just the content-hash) must get an EAGER re-embed signal, so the version moves to 5. The bump propagates through the single import with NO hardcoded literals anywhere: `skills.ts` (`manifest.json` + `X-Skill-Version` + served `skill.md`), `agent-substrate-client.ts` (the `clawville.orientation.version` on the cognition body), and `partner-hatcher.ts` (the `protocol` pointer on register/patch) all read the constant; the manual renders `version: ${PROTOCOL_VERSION}` dynamically. Verb/whitelist parity (G4) is unaffected — these are lifecycle/error docs, not new verbs. The stale `@ v2` / `PROTOCOL_VERSION = 2` doc comments in `selftest-e2e.ts` (assertions already tracked the imported constant dynamically) were corrected. THREE-SURFACE SYNC: the surface-#1 orientation knowledge `packages/shared/src/constants/orientation-skill.ts:144` carried a LIVE RAG-embedded string pinning the manual at "protocol_version 2" (a present-tense, agent-facing claim, stale by 3) plus the same stale parenthetical in two comments (`orientation-skill.ts:127`, `town-guide.ts:123`). The live string now tells the agent to re-read the manifest `protocol.version` + `contentHash` on connect rather than assuming a fixed version, and the comments de-pin to "single-source PROTOCOL_VERSION" — drift-proof, matching the already-correct `town-guide.ts:132` live string that never pinned a version. Surface #3 (hosted-runtime protocol-knowledge) is `buildProtocolManual` rendered dynamically from the bumped constant, so it is correct at v5 with no literal. `packages/shared` + `packages/agent-templates` were rebuilt so the API serves the corrected string. **P6-2 (MAJOR) — failed override PATCH no longer leaves the row on the failed target.** `PATCH /api/partner/hatcher/agents/:id` commits the new `mode`/`targetNpcId`/cognition-token to the row INSIDE the db.transaction (commit-first), THEN spawns post-commit. On an OVERRIDE re-register failure (target NPC occupied) the prior in-memory body is restored from `restoreSnapshots` (P5-2, no orphan) — but the persisted ROW still described the FAILED new override target, so a restart / idle-despawn restore would re-attempt the failed target (throws → null, NPC stays held), or a later restore would "succeed" into a PATCH the partner was told 409-failed — stats + restore both dishonest. FIX: the tx now SNAPSHOTS the prior body-defining row fields (`mode`, `targetNpcId`, `name`, `species`, `color`, `metadata`, `proxyUrl`, `proxyTokenEnc/Iv/Tag`) from the locked-read `existing` BEFORE the `.update()`; on an override spawn-failure a COMPENSATING `db.update` (keyed by `row.id`) writes those prior fields back so the persisted row MATCHES the restored prior live body. The compensation does NOT touch `sessionExpiresAt`, and reverts `sessionKeyHash` ONLY in the minted sub-case: when the failed override PATCH MINTED a new bearer (no live session existed to preserve, so the spawn left no live body and the minted id is never surfaced — the 409 carries no sessionId), the compensation also RESTORES the prior `sessionKeyHash` / `sessionExpiresAt` / `sessionSweptAt` from the snapshot (Codex pass-7 correction — the earlier null was WRONG: the mint step overwrote the prior hash, so nulling bricked the partner’s pre-PATCH restorable bearer). A failed override PATCH is now a FULL session no-op; DELETE / expiry / sweep all enforce (no committed hash for a session that never lived). This is consistency/honesty, not security — the dangling hash is inert (the id never entered the sim Map, nobody holds it). The PRESERVED-bearer case (`minted === false`) keeps its hash: the restored prior body is live under it and the PATCH never rewrote it. Fail-closed-ish: the response is already a 409/503 (`override_target_unavailable` / `propagation_failed`), so if the compensating write itself throws we KEEP that failure and log loudly — never upgrade to a success on a compensation failure. The AVATAR-PATCH path is unaffected (no override target). TESTS: `partner-hatcher-p5-handler.test.ts` grew two P6-2 cases — (1) PRESERVED: register an avatar body → occupy an NPC with a blocker → PATCH override to it → 409 `override_target_unavailable`, no sessionId, AND the row's `mode`/`targetNpcId`/`proxyTokenEnc` read the PRIOR body (not the failed target) AND `sessionKeyHash` UNCHANGED, prior body restored; (2) MINTED: register an override agent on a free NPC → despawn its body → occupy a different target → PATCH override → 409, row compensated to the prior free target, AND `sessionKeyHash` RESTORED to the prior bearer (a failed override PATCH is a full session no-op so the partner keeps their pre-PATCH restorable session), no live body. Gates: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api` exit 0; selftest-e2e G1/C2/G3/G4 version-and-parity gates PASS at v5; P5+P6 handler tests 4/4 + keyed-mutex 6/6 (the selftest-e2e H9/H10 fails + the 8 full-suite fails are the SAME PRE-EXISTING env-gated artifacts — H9/H10 assume a dummy `DATABASE_URL` makes the register tx fail but this environment has a reachable Supabase, so the register succeeds 200; identical on the pre-P6 baseline). PARITY: both touch the connected/hosted-agent partner surface (manual version + override row honesty on the shared bearer/restore path); no human path or settlement-math change. Prior audit follows.
**Last Audited:** 2026-06-15: **Cove agent-parity completed (Rule E5).** `cove-slots.ts`, `cove-baccarat.ts`, and `cove-holdem.ts` now resolve a connected/hosted agent session (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → `resolveAgentSession`, `require-auth-or-agent.ts`) to its bound avatar on the real-CT settlement path, matching the already-audited `cove-blackjack.ts`. `getSubject` is async, precedence Lucia human → agent session → guest; `user` + `agent` are both ledger subjects (real CT on the bound avatar wallet), guests stay demo-CT, non-ledger/unbound agents get 403 (never a guest demotion). The slots route-catalog row (this §) had a stale `casino-slots.ts` / `/api/casino/slots/*` name — corrected to the live `cove-slots.ts` / `/api/cove/slots`. Slots `requireAuth` gameplay routes (`/session/close`, `/session/current`, `/session/:id`, `/session/:id/spins`) converted to subject-resolved (ownership still binds via `ownerMatches(userId)`).
