**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-12: **Codex ROUND-5 re-audit (P5-1 resolved NOT-A-BUG; P5-2 MAJOR fixed; no schema change).** A fifth independent Codex pass (`docs/codex-review-findings-2026-06-12.md` ROUND 5) flagged two items in `apps/api/src/routes/partner-hatcher.ts`. **P5-1 (NOT A BUG — armed for pass-6) — the per-agent critical section keeps the COMMIT-FIRST-spawn-after ordering.** Pass-5 flagged that the per-agent `pg_advisory_xact_lock` is TRANSACTION-scoped and releases at COMMIT before the post-commit `npcSimulation` Map mutation, leaving the DB-write→Map-mutate span un-serialized across processes. We briefly "fixed" it by holding the tx open across the in-memory spawn (held-tx widening), then REVERTED that after the auditor proved it REGRESSES safety: spawn-then-commit can leave a PHANTOM live `ledgerCapable` body if the commit fails AFTER the Map mutation — strictly worse than commit-first (a valid bearer + body-less row that restore heals). RESOLUTION (the pass-4 structure, kept): the xact-scoped `pg_advisory_xact_lock(agentCriticalSectionLockKey(namespacedAgentId))` guards ONLY the DB write (commit ends the tx); the in-process `withKeyedMutex(namespacedAgentId)` serializes the post-commit `registerOpenClaw`/`unregisterOpenClaw` Map mutation; the spawn runs AFTER commit. The cross-process Map race the held-tx targeted does NOT exist: ClawVille runs a SINGLE API replica (one Coolify `api` container per box) and the `npcSimulation` Map is PROCESS-LOCAL (in-memory, never shared cross-process — same single-process assumption already load-bearing for `activity-room-manager.ts:6` and the `cove-slots.ts:197` rate limiter), so ONLY the DB write needs cross-process serialization and the xact lock provides it. Commit-first is fail-closed: a failed commit means NO body was spawned (spawn is post-commit), and a failed post-commit spawn leaves a committed-but-body-less row that `restoreAgentSessionFromRow` / a later PATCH re-spawns. This entry + the lock-site comment ARM the next independent pass with the single-replica + process-local-Map + commit-first rationale so it is not re-flagged. **P5-2 (MAJOR) — override spawn/propagation failure no longer returns success.** Register on an OVERRIDE `registerOpenClaw` throw (target NPC occupied) used to return `ok:true` + sessionId + `spawned:false` (a bearer for a body that never took over the NPC); PATCH override tore down the existing live body THEN returned `ok:true` on a re-register failure (orphaning it). Now OVERRIDE-mode spawn/propagation failure returns a non-2xx with NO sessionId; the committed row is left HONEST (body-less, restorable) and NOT rolled back (commit-first). The response distinguishes an OCCUPIED target — `409 { error: 'override_target_unavailable' }`, client-actionable since the targetNpcId was validated against `NPC_IDS` pre-tx so a throw is the already-overridden case — from a transient `503` (`spawn_failed` on register / `propagation_failed` on PATCH). Why a real dead-bearer (not cosmetic): `restoreAgentSessionFromRow` re-attempts `registerOpenClaw` for an override row and throws → null while the NPC stays held, so a 200+sessionId on an occupied register would be a permanently dead bearer. PATCH-specific no-orphan: the override-flip tears down the OLD live body before the new spawn, so on a failed flip PATCH SNAPSHOTS the old config+client before teardown and RE-REGISTERS it, leaving the prior working session intact. (Register does NOT snapshot/restore — a register replaces the partner's own session; the held-tx-era register restore machinery was removed with the revert.) AVATAR mode stays best-effort (a fresh `oc-<sessionId>` body never collides, restore rebuilds it): a spawn throw keeps the committed row + bearer (`spawned:false`, sessionId still returned — that bearer is honest, the body re-registers lazily). TESTS: NEW `apps/api/src/routes/__tests__/partner-hatcher-p5.test.ts` (2 — in-memory sim: registerOpenClaw throws on an occupied override; failed re-register restores the prior body from the captured snapshot) + `apps/api/src/routes/__tests__/partner-hatcher-p5-handler.test.ts` (2 — mocked-db / real-sim handler drive: P5-1 commit-first — the body is NOT live in the sim Map at the instant the db.transaction callback resolves (spawn is post-commit); P5-2 occupied override → 409 override_target_unavailable, no sessionId, no live body). Gates: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api` exit 0; P5 tests 4/4; keyed-mutex 6/6 + hatcher-config 33/33 unaffected (the selftest-e2e H9/H10 fails + the 8 full-suite fails are PRE-EXISTING env-gated artifacts, identical on the stashed pre-P5 baseline — not P5 regressions). PARITY: override fail-closed on the connected/hosted-agent partner surface + shared bearer gate; no human path or settlement-math change. AUDITOR NITS: (1) ADDRESSED — the 409-vs-503 split keys on a TYPED `OverrideTargetUnavailableError` (exported from `npc-simulation.ts`, mapped via `instanceof`) instead of message-string matching, so the status can't silently degrade if the error text changes. (2) SKIPPED (team-lead call) — the override error contract was added to the protocol manual (`buildProtocolManual`) but `PROTOCOL_VERSION` stays at 4: it is an error-response doc, not a verb/whitelist change, so G4 verb-parity holds and the content-hash already changed; a version bump would needlessly pull in the three-surface-sync rule. Prior audit follows.
**Last Audited:** 2026-06-12: **Codex ROUND-4 re-audit fixes (1 BLOCKING + 2 MAJOR backend; no schema change).** A fourth independent Codex pass (`docs/codex-review-findings-2026-06-12.md` ROUND 4) found the Hatcher register/PATCH critical section was not serialized per agent. **P4-1 (BLOCKING) — `POST`/`PATCH /api/partner/hatcher/agents` are now serialized per `hatcher:<agentId>`.** Two concurrent registers (or PATCHes) for the SAME agentId used to race: both cleaned stale sessions, both minted a different `hat-*` bearer, both wrote a different `session_key_hash`, both `registerOpenClaw`d — the later DB write won the hash so the earlier 200-OK bearer was dead-on-arrival under `validateLiveAgentSession` (R2-2 present-and-mismatch), and avatar mode left DUPLICATE bodies (the sim Map keys by sessionId, not agentId; the new-agent insert path also had a check-then-insert duplicate race). FIX (two layers): (a) a NEW in-process keyed async mutex `apps/api/src/services/keyed-mutex.ts` `withKeyedMutex(namespacedAgentId, fn)` wraps the WHOLE critical section (re-read + upsert + bearer-hash + the post-commit in-memory stale-cleanup + spawn) — it covers the in-memory Map mutation a DB lock cannot, so two same-process requests for one agentId queue; (b) a transaction-scoped `pg_advisory_xact_lock(agentCriticalSectionLockKey(namespacedAgentId))` (cross-process) that RE-READS the row AFTER acquiring, so the existing-vs-insert decision is made on post-lock state. The upsert + bearer-hash now commit in ONE transaction (atomic — the row always commits already bound to the returned bearer, removing the prior separate non-atomic hash-write window). DEADLOCK SAFETY: the agent lock is acquired FIRST and the per-(partner, UTC-day) registration-cap lock only NESTED inside it on the insert branch, so the acquire order is ALWAYS agent → cap and the two locks can never be taken in opposite orders (`agentCriticalSectionLockKey` hashes `hatcher-agent:<id>`, `dailyRegistrationLockKey` hashes `hatcher:<epochMs>` — distinct namespaces, never collide). **P4-2 (MAJOR) — register fails closed on hash-persist failure.** The handler used to return `ok:true` + a raw sessionId even when the bearer-hash persist failed (a dead credential — neither live nor restorable — in a success body). Now that the upsert+hash is one transaction, a tx failure returns `503 { error: 'session_persist_failed' }` with NO sessionId. The PATCH mint branch already set `rotatedSessionId` only AFTER a successful hash persist, so it never surfaces a minted id whose hash didn't commit (verified). **P4-3 (MAJOR) — legacy `/api/openclaw/register` fails closed on DB error.** Removed the 'ephemeral-only if DB fails' fallback that then still called `registerOpenClaw` (leaving a live Map body with no surviving row/hash — unusable under the shared `validateLiveAgentSession` contract since chat/location/cove re-read the row and fail closed). The catch now returns `500 { code: 'registration_failed' }` BEFORE `registerOpenClaw` runs. TESTS: NEW `apps/api/src/services/__tests__/keyed-mutex.test.ts` (6 cases — same-key serialize, different-key concurrent, throw-releases-lock, map-drain, prior-failure-doesn't-reject-waiter); selftest-e2e grew to 86/86 (H9 register DB-fail → 503 session_persist_failed + no live body; H10 legacy register DB-fail → 500 + no in-memory session; H11 two concurrent same-agent registers via the real `withKeyedMutex` + real npcSimulation → exactly ONE body + ONE live bearer). Gates: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api` exit 0; selftest-e2e 86/86; keyed-mutex 6/6 (the pre-existing cross-file room-registry flakiness in the full-suite run is untouched by this change). PARITY: per-agent serialization + fail-closed hardening on the connected/hosted-agent partner surface + the shared bearer gate; no human path or settlement-math change. Prior audit follows.
**Last Audited:** 2026-06-12: **Codex ROUND-2 re-audit fixes (3 BLOCKING + 1 MINOR backend; no schema change).** A fresh independent Codex re-audit after the first 8 were fixed found 4 more partner-facing security bugs (`docs/codex-review-findings-2026-06-12.md` ROUND 2). **R2-1 (BLOCKING) — `hatcher:` agentId namespace now GLOBALLY reserved on the public registration paths.** The public enums excluded `identityType:'hatcher'` but did NOT reserve the `hatcher:` agentId NAMESPACE, so any unsigned caller could POST `agentId:"hatcher:<id>"` to `POST /api/agent/connect` (`agent-gateway.ts`) or legacy `POST /api/openclaw/register` (`openclaw.ts`) and the existing-row upsert path would MUTATE the partner-owned row (identityType/mode/protocol/session-hash/userId), corrupting partner stats/patch/delete/launch + restore and enabling a rebind with a caller-held token. FIX: NEW shared single-source module `apps/api/src/services/reserved-agent-namespaces.ts` (`RESERVED_PARTNER_AGENT_PREFIXES = ['hatcher:']`, `RESERVED_PARTNER_IDENTITY_TYPES = ['hatcher']`, `isReservedPartnerAgentId()` / `isReservedPartnerIdentityType()`) — add a future partner namespace in ONE place and every public guard covers it. Both public registration entry points now (a) reject a caller-supplied reserved-prefix agentId with an OPAQUE 400 (no existence leak) BEFORE any DB read (so a reject can't burn a single-use connection token), and (b) defense-in-depth refuse to mutate an existing row whose `identity_type` is a reserved partner type. The partner-signed router (`partner-hatcher.ts`) owns + writes that namespace and does NOT call the guards. (Other `openclaw_bots` writers audited: `claws.ts` uses a server-generated `claw-<uid>-<ts>` id + addresses by row UUID under `requireAuth`; the sweepers + `wallet-service.ts` address by row id/userId — none take a caller-supplied agentId, so the two public paths are the complete set.) **R2-2 (BLOCKING) — `validateLiveAgentSession` now invalidates a rotated-away bearer on every live Map hit** (`apps/api/src/middleware/require-auth-or-agent.ts`). The in-memory Map proves only that THIS process once registered the bearer, not that it is still the CURRENT live session. When `/connect` or partner register/patch ROTATES the session it writes a NEW `session_key_hash` on the same agentId row, but the OLD bearer stayed in the Map until restart and kept passing the TTL gate (the row's sliding expiry is refreshed by the rotation) → kept spending real CT against `boundUserId`. The b453fb18 restore design hashed the bearer to gate RESTORE but never re-checked it on a live Map hit. FIX: on every Map hit, reject ONLY when the row carries a PRESENT (non-null) hash that DIFFERS from `sha256Hex(sessionId)` — a rotated-away bearer (rotation always wrote a new non-null hash) is torn down (same fail-closed shape as expiry) and returns null. PRESENT-AND-MISMATCH carve-out (round-2 auditor): a NULL row hash is NOT a rejection — the partner register/patch path calls `registerOpenClaw` (Map-live) BEFORE it persists `session_key_hash` and that persist is EXPLICITLY non-fatal, so a freshly-minted partner session is legitimately Map-registered with a null row hash for the write window (or permanently if the non-fatal persist threw). A strict `!==` would turn that documented-non-fatal state fatal and kill the partner's agent on its first real-CT call; instead a null hash falls through to the (already-passed) TTL gate, which still bounds liveness. This does NOT widen the rotation hole because every rotation writes a non-null hash, so a rotated-away bearer is always in the present-and-mismatch branch. Consistent with restore (restore finds the row by `sessionKeyHash === sha256Hex(incoming)` and re-registers under that id, so a restored session always has a non-null matching hash) and with the partner PATCH preserve-path (it leaves the hash untouched when it reuses the live bearer). **R2-3 (BLOCKING) — Hatcher cognition is DNS-safe at call time** (`apps/api/src/services/agent-substrate-client.ts` `chatHatcherProxy`). Registration ran the DNS-aware validator, but the per-call cognition path re-ran only the SYNCHRONOUS hostname allowlist before fetch, so an allowlisted Hatcher subdomain could DNS-REBIND to a private IP after registration and the server would POST the scoped bearer + our ed25519 signature to an internal address (`redirect:'manual'` stops a redirect-hop rebind, not a resolve-time rebind). FIX: the call-time path now runs the DNS-aware `validateHatcherProxyUrlResolved` (resolve + reject private/loopback/link-local IPs) immediately before building + sending the request. Fail-SOFT preserved (a blocked/failed check returns '' — the bot just doesn't speak this turn — never throws). Documented residual: a DNS TOCTOU between this resolve and fetch's own resolve is NARROWED (only the synchronous body-build + sign sits between them, no further DNS/awaits) but not zero without a pinned-IP fetch the platform fetch does not expose. **R2-5 (MINOR) — legacy `/api/openclaw/unregister/:sessionId` scrubs the bearer hash** (`openclaw.ts`): it expired + swept the row but left `session_key_hash` set, unlike `expireSession()` + the partner DELETE. Now sets `sessionKeyHash: null` on that terminal transition (the TTL flip already failed restore closed; this restores the terminal-transition invariant that no live-bearer handle survives an explicit disconnect). **R2-6 (adjacent blind SSRF — found by the round-2 auditor) — `gatewayUrl`-bound cognition fetches are DNS-safe.** `OpenClawClient.chatOpenAI` / `chatAnthropic` / `chatCustomWebhook` POST the agent's own token to `this.gatewayUrl` on every NPC-conversation tick, and that URL was validated only by `z.string().url()` at `/connect` — a connected agent could aim its cognition POST at `169.254.169.254` (cloud metadata), RFC1918, or `localhost` (blind SSRF; lower severity than R2-3 since it's the agent's own token to its own gateway, but still a real internal-reachability primitive). FIX: NEW generic `validateOutboundUrlResolved(rawUrl)` in `hatcher-config.ts` — the SAME private/loopback/link-local/reserved IP rejection as the Hatcher allowlist validator (reusing the identical `isPrivateIP` classification so coverage can't drift between the two SSRF surfaces) but with NO host allowlist and http allowed (an agent's gateway is an arbitrary public URL). Each of the three chat methods now resolves-and-checks `gatewayUrl` immediately before fetch and FAILS SOFT (returns '') on reject — never POSTs the token to a private target. TESTS: `apps/api/src/services/__tests__/reserved-agent-namespaces.test.ts` (10 cases — prefix/identity-type matrix, case-sensitivity, mid-string non-match), (plus 8 `validateOutboundUrlResolved` cases for R2-6 — private-IP literals, DNS-rebind reject, http/https public allow, no-allowlist legit-host allow, fail-closed DNS); plus selftest-e2e grew to 82/82 (J1/J2 rotated-bearer invalidation + legit single-session still LIVE; J3 null-hash carve-out — a freshly-minted partner session in the non-fatal-persist window stays LIVE; F8b DNS-aware call-time Hatcher-proxy reject; F8c chatOpenAI/chatCustomWebhook private-gateway reject with no outbound fetch (R2-6); K1/K2 public-connect reserved-namespace reject + ordinary-id regression). Gates: `bunx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; selftest-e2e 82/82; existing unit suite unchanged (the 8 pre-existing DB/RNG-flaky failures are untouched). PARITY: all four are connected/hosted-agent partner-surface hardening on the shared bearer gate; no human path or settlement-math change. Prior audit follows.
**Last Audited:** 2026-06-12: **Codex full-diff review fixes (5 backend findings + fold-in; no schema change).** Independent Codex review of the day's diff (`docs/codex-review-findings-2026-06-12.md`). **#1 (BLOCKING) — test-pubkey prod gate hardened to an explicit env.** `ALLOW_TEST_PARTNER_PUBKEY` no longer infers "is prod" from a `CORS_ORIGIN` substring (a comma-list / unset / mis-set `CORS_ORIGIN` could re-open a full Hatcher signer on prod). It now gates on the explicit immutable `CLAWVILLE_ENV`: the test signer is accepted ONLY when `CLAWVILLE_ENV === 'staging'`, and `partner-signature.ts` THROWS AT MODULE LOAD (crash-loud, `FINGERPRINT_SECRET` pattern) if the var is set while `CLAWVILLE_ENV !== 'staging'` — a prod box carrying the var refuses to boot instead of running it silently. SUPERSEDES the prior `CORS_ORIGIN` kill-switch entry below. **ACTION REQUIRED:** `CLAWVILLE_ENV=staging` must be set on the staging Coolify box (app 3) alongside the already-set `ALLOW_TEST_PARTNER_PUBKEY`, or staging boot now fails. **#4 (MAJOR) — PATCH no longer orphans the partner's session.** `PATCH /api/partner/hatcher/agents/:id` re-register PREFERS to REUSE the existing live `sessionId` (the partner's held bearer keeps working) instead of always minting a fresh id; it mints a new id ONLY when no live session exists (Map wiped by a restart) and in that case RETURNS `sessionId` + `sessionExpiresAt` in the response (mirrors the connect/register shape) so the partner adopts it. The row's `session_key_hash` is rewritten only on a mint. **#6 (MAJOR) — restore refuses ALL real-gateway identity types.** `restoreAgentSessionFromRow` now delegates the restorability decision to the shared pure `isRowRestorableFromIdentity(identityType)` (agent-session-config.ts) which returns true ONLY for the no-gateway types (`anonymous`/`milady`/`nanoclaw`). A malformed legacy `openclaw`/`custom` row with `protocol='openai-compat'` + null/dummy `gateway_url` is now refused (returns null → reconnect) instead of falling through to a mute `http://localhost:0` body (the D1 bug's cousin); the gate is on IDENTITY TYPE, not the `gateway_url` shape. **#7 (MAJOR) — registration cap made atomic.** The per-(partner, UTC-day) daily NEW-registration cap (default 50, `PARTNER_DAILY_REGISTRATION_CAP`) was count-then-insert with no lock (concurrent registers all read under cap + inserted). The count+insert now run in ONE transaction under a `pg_advisory_xact_lock` keyed on `sha256(partnerId + utc-midnight-epoch)` folded to a signed 63-bit bigint, so the bucket serializes; no new table/migration. **#8 (MINOR) — terminal lifecycle scrubs the bearer hash.** DELETE tombstone + `expireSession` (explicit disconnect) + `sweepExpiredSessions` (TTL expiry) now set `session_key_hash = null` (defense-in-depth; restore already failed closed on TTL). **FOLD-IN — sticky-rooms gate is the literal tested unit.** `world.ts /join` already DELEGATES to the exported `resolveRecoveryRoomId(ticket, liveSessionId)` (room-ticket.ts); the §13 sticky-rooms wording is corrected to say "delegates to the exported gate", not "mirrors the route guard". Gates: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api` exit 0; room-ticket + room-registry + agent-session-config suites green (the new `isRowRestorableFromIdentity` #6 regression cases added); selftest-e2e 75/75. PARITY: all five touch the connected/hosted-agent partner surface; no human path or settlement-math change. **Multiplayer sticky-room rejoin (signed recovery ticket; no schema change).** A deploy/restart no longer SPLITS a grouped party: the in-memory `RoomRegistry` is wiped on restart, but `/api/world/join` now hands the client an HMAC-SHA256 recovery ticket (`apps/api/src/services/room-ticket.ts`, signed with the env-stable `FINGERPRINT_SECRET`, self-expiring 15 min, publicId-bound) that the client replays on its recovery rejoin; the server verifies it and RECREATES the named room (guests included — the ticket is the proof, B2 anti-spam intact). Recovery bypasses the soft cap (12) to reconverge a group but never the hard cap (20). PARITY: agents recover identically via the same `resolvePresence`→ticket path. Full detail + gates in §13 (top entry). Prior audit follows. **D1 — restore-rebuild fidelity (shared agent-session config-builder; no schema change).** Fixes the LIVE-PROVEN regression in `docs/diagnostic-2026-06-12-hatcher-regressions.md`: a restored `anonymous`/`milady` agent came back wired as an OpenAI-compat gateway client (restore read `openclaw_bots.protocol` = the meaningless `'openai-compat'` default verbatim), so its `OpenClawClient.chat()` POSTed to the dummy `http://localhost:0` gateway and 502'd ("Agent gateway error") on every autonomous NPC-conversation tick. The SAME latent flaw lived in the MINT path (a `/connect` anonymous/milady avatar body also got an `'openai-compat'` client). ROOT CAUSE: three independent code paths (`/api/agent/connect`, `POST /api/partner/hatcher/agents` + its PATCH re-register, and `restoreAgentSessionFromRow`) hand-assembled the in-world `OpenClawRegistration` and drifted. FIX (structural, not spot): NEW pure module `apps/api/src/services/agent-session-config.ts` — `buildAvatarSessionConfig` / `buildOverrideSessionConfig` + `resolveInWorldProtocol` / `resolveAgentSpecies` / `resolveAutonomyMode`. The in-world wire protocol is now derived from the AUTHORITATIVE `identity_type`, NOT the stored `protocol` column: `anonymous`/`milady`/`nanoclaw` → the fail-soft `'nanoclaw'` protocol (`.chat()` returns '' with NO network call), `hatcher` → `'hatcher-proxy'`, `openclaw`/`ironclaw`/`custom` → their declared HTTP protocol. Species fallback is per-category (`hatcher` → `DEFAULT_HATCHER_MODEL_KEY`, else `DEFAULT_AGENT_MODEL_KEY`) — fixing the prior `restore` + partner-hatcher mint use of `DEFAULT_AGENT_MODEL_KEY` for a null-species hatcher row (mis-rendered as a Milady). All three paths now call the shared builder, so mint and restore CANNOT drift. CORRECTED RESTORE MATRIX (supersedes the 2026-06-11 entry's "stub client" wording): `nanoclaw`/`milady`/`anonymous` restore as a body speaking `'nanoclaw'` (fail-soft, never POSTs); `hatcher` restores incl. cognition via the proxy client; `openclaw`/`ironclaw`/`custom` with a real `gateway_url` still return null (auth_token never persisted). Fail-closed TTL/swept resurrection contract UNCHANGED. Regression test `apps/api/src/services/__tests__/agent-session-config.test.ts` (30 cases) asserts, for EVERY identity type + avatar/override mode, that the mint config and the restore config deep-equal on the spawn-relevant fields (protocol/species/gateway/autonomy/etc.) and that a no-gateway body can never carry a gateway-POSTing protocol — the structural guard the audits missed. Gates: `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api` exit 0, new test 30/30, selftest-e2e still 75/75. PARITY: connected/hosted-agent restore plumbing on the shared bearer gate; no human path or settlement change.
**Last Audited:** 2026-06-12: **Mock-Hatcher pre-ship harness + `ALLOW_TEST_PARTNER_PUBKEY` (staging-only).** Closes the process gap that shipped the Hatcher partner regressions (`docs/diagnostic-2026-06-12-hatcher-regressions.md`): no test ever drove the LIVE `/api/partner/hatcher/*` binary through register → spawn → stats with a real partner-signed request. NEW scripts `apps/api/scripts/hatcher/mock-hatcher-client.ts` (generates/loads an ed25519 keypair; signs + runs register → stats → a negative wrong-path 401 → DELETE against a live `--api-base`; asserts 200s, `sessionId` + `sessionExpiresAt` present, `publicAgentRecord` shape, stats blocks present, NO token-field leak; exit 0/1) and `apps/api/scripts/hatcher/mock-hatcher-proxy.ts` (a `Bun.serve` implementing `POST /integrations/clawville/agents/:agentId/chat` — verifies the `Bearer` scoped token + the `X-Clawville-Signature` against the issuer pubkey fetched from `{api-base}/.well-known/clawville-issuer.json`, logs every request, replies a fixed completion carrying one `[ACTION: emote(name=wave)]`). Run book + MANDATORY pre-ship gate checklist: `apps/api/scripts/hatcher/run-mock-e2e.md`. **NEW ENV VAR `ALLOW_TEST_PARTNER_PUBKEY` (base58 ed25519 pubkey) — STAGING-ONLY, MUST NEVER BE SET ON PROD.** In `apps/api/src/services/partner-signature.ts` it adds an ADDITIONAL accepted signer FOR THE `hatcher` PARTNER ONLY (additive — the real `PARTNER_PUBKEYS.hatcher` entry stays valid; inert for every other partnerId); routed through a new `isAllowedPartnerPubkey()` shared by all three verify functions (write/get/legacy). Gated by ENV PRESENCE not `NODE_ENV` (staging runs `NODE_ENV='production'`), validated as a real 32-byte key (a typo'd value is ignored, never treated as opaque-allowed). **CODE-ENFORCED PROD KILL-SWITCH (auditor hardening):** `loadTestPartnerPubkey()` returns null on prod even when the var IS set — it reuses the established `CORS_ORIGIN` prod-signal (`agent-gateway.ts` `apiBase` resolver): prod's `CORS_ORIGIN` contains `clawville.world` and NOT `staging`; staging's contains `staging.clawville.world` (both tokens). So "includes `clawville.world` AND NOT `staging`" uniquely identifies prod and refuses the test signer there, converting "never set on prod" from ops discipline into a code invariant. `warnIfTestPartnerPubkeyEnabled()` (called once at API boot from `index.ts`) logs a one-line WARN when the signer is active (staging) AND a LOUDER `console.error` 🚨 alarm when the var is set-but-suppressed on prod (so a prod misconfig is seen, not silent). **RESIDUAL RISK (documented, accepted):** the routes hardcode `partnerId='hatcher'` (`verifyPartnerWriteSignature('hatcher')`, the `hatcher:` namespace, `identityType==='hatcher'` guards, `resolveOrCreateUserByIdentity('hatcher')`), so the test key cannot use a separate scoped partnerId — when active it inherits the FULL hatcher partner power set (register, stats, real-CT-capable avatars via `ensureHatcherAvatar`). Controls: the CORS_ORIGIN prod kill-switch (code), the loud boot alarm, and the run-book MANDATE to UNSET the var + DELETE the test agent after every run. Proven against the real verify functions: test key accepted for hatcher only (scape/random rejected), real key always accepted, prod CORS_ORIGIN rejects the test key even when set, staging+local accept it, behavior identical to baseline when unset. No DB schema change. PARITY: test affordance for the connected/hosted-agent partner surface; no human path or settlement change.
**Last Audited:** 2026-06-12: **Multiplayer soft-cap flexible room fill.** Added `ROOM_SOFT_CAP_PLAYERS = 12` (auto-fill target) alongside the unchanged hard cap `ROOM_MAX_PLAYERS = 20` in `services/room-registry.ts`. Auto-fill now packs joiners into the FULLEST room still under the soft cap (lowest-id tie-break) and mints a fresh room only when every room has reached 12; the 12-to-20 band is reserved headroom for friends joining via invite code (honored to 20, unchanged). Drift: the `world.ts` route row + `room-registry` service row in §6/§services and the §13 changelog updated; no schema or env change. See §13 (2026-06-12 entry).
**Last Audited:** 2026-06-12: **Agent session-lifecycle features (4 + three-surface sync).** No DB schema change — every feature reads existing columns. (a) PULL expiry visibility: `sessionExpiresAt` (ISO) is now ADDITIVE on the `/api/agent/connect` response, the `/api/openclaw/register` response (`OpenClawBotIdentity.sessionExpiresAt?` in `@clawville/shared`), and the partner stats `registration` block (`GET /api/partner/hatcher/agents/:agentId/stats` — column already selected). Each handler computes `computeSessionExpiresAt()` ONCE and uses it for both the DB write and the response so they agree. (b) PUSH expiry webhook: NEW service `apps/api/src/services/hatcher-session-webhook.ts` → `notifyHatcherSessionEnded({identityType, agentId, reason, expiredAt})`. Env-gated `HATCHER_SESSION_WEBHOOK_URL` (DORMANT/no-op when unset — the default, since Hatcher has no endpoint yet). Fires on TTL expiry (the 5-min sweep loop in `agent-session-sweeper.ts`) AND explicit teardown (`expireSession()` → signed /disconnect; partner DELETE). `hatcher`-identityType ONLY. Body `{type:'session.ended', agentId (de-namespaced raw), reason: 'ttl_expired'|'disconnected', expiredAt}` signed with `signPayload()` (service-issuer ed25519, NEW published purpose `partner-session-webhook`) on `X-Clawville-Issuer-Pubkey` + `X-Clawville-Signature`. SSRF-guarded via `validateHatcherProxyUrlResolved` (https + host allowlist + no private/rebind IP) on every send, `redirect:'manual'`, 5s timeout + 1 retry. FAIL-OPEN: never blocks/fails the sweep or disconnect; never logs body/token/URL-query. (c) Per-partner daily registration cap: `POST /api/partner/hatcher/agents` insert branch ONLY (re-register/PATCH of an existing agentId is never counted) — counts `hatcher` rows with `created_at >= UTC-midnight`; over `PARTNER_DAILY_REGISTRATION_CAP` (default 50, floor 1) returns `429 {error:'daily_registration_cap', code, cap}`. DB-counted, no new table. (d) Body-despawn inactivity window: NEW service `apps/api/src/services/agent-body-idle-sweeper.ts` + `startBodyIdleSweeper()` wired in `index.ts` (1-min interval). Despawns the in-world BODY (via `npcSimulation.unregisterOpenClaw` — the same path /disconnect uses; NEW `npcSimulation.getActiveAgentSessionPairs()` enumerates live bodies) of any agent whose `last_seen_at` is older than `AGENT_BODY_IDLE_DESPAWN_MS` (default 30 min, env-overridable, floor 5 min). It does NOT touch `session_expires_at`/`session_swept_at` — the SESSION stays valid + restorable; the body re-spawns at its last persisted position on the next authenticated activity via the existing `validateLiveAgentSession` → `restoreAgentSessionFromRow` machinery. Position is persisted to row metadata before despawn (avatar bodies) so restore lands in place. (e) THREE-SURFACE SYNC (same diff): `PROTOCOL_VERSION` 3→4 in `services/skill-protocol.ts` + manual §5 rewritten (two-clock model, sessionExpiresAt fields, despawn/respawn, webhook, cap); Nori `knowledge[]` (`packages/agent-templates/src/locations/town-guide.ts`) gains the world-visible idle-despawn orientation line; hosted-runtime orientation (`packages/shared/src/constants/orientation-skill.ts`) gains the two-clock + webhook + cap lines. ENV VARS added: `HATCHER_SESSION_WEBHOOK_URL` (optional, dormant), `PARTNER_DAILY_REGISTRATION_CAP` (default 50), `AGENT_BODY_IDLE_DESPAWN_MS` (default 30min, floor 5min). PARITY: all four are connected/hosted-agent lifecycle plumbing on the shared bearer gate; no human path or settlement change. Gate: `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean.
**Last Audited:** 2026-06-11: **Agent-session restart survival + de-branded session error copy.** A connected agent's live session existed ONLY in npc-simulation's in-memory `openClawBots` Map (the `ag-`/`oc-`/`hat-` bearer was never persisted), so every API deploy/restart silently dropped every connected agent: `validateLiveAgentSession` (`apps/api/src/middleware/require-auth-or-agent.ts`) map-missed and 404'd the agent's owner the moment they used the chat bar. FIX: (1) NEW nullable column `openclaw_bots.session_key_hash varchar(64)` storing `sha256Hex(sessionId)` — the one-way hash of the live bearer, NEVER the raw bearer (a DB dump must not yield a spendable real-CT credential). Migration `packages/database/migrations-manual/2026-06-11_add_openclaw_session_key_hash.sql` (idempotent `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS` + partial index `openclaw_bots_session_key_hash_idx WHERE session_key_hash IS NOT NULL`; orchestrator applies manually). Written everywhere a session id is minted: `/api/agent/connect` (both upsert branches), `/api/openclaw/register` (both branches), `/api/partner/hatcher/agents` POST + the PATCH re-register propagation (new sessionId per mint ⇒ new hash). (2) NEW `sha256Hex()` in `apps/api/src/services/session-digest.ts` (full 64-hex; distinct from the existing 64-bit `sessionDigest` log-correlation prefix). (3) NEW service `apps/api/src/services/agent-session-restore.ts` — `restoreAgentSessionFromRow(sessionId)` hashes the incoming bearer, finds the surviving row by `session_key_hash`, re-validates the SAME fail-closed TTL (`session_expires_at` NON-NULL and strictly future; NULL/past = expired → null, and does NOT clear the hash — the sweeper owns lifecycle), rebuilds the in-memory `{config, client}` from the row, and re-registers under the INCOMING sessionId (it hashed-matched, so it IS the live bearer). `validateLiveAgentSession` now calls it on a Map-miss (dynamic import to avoid a static cycle). RESTORE MATRIX (which identity types rebuild from the row alone): `hatcher`/`hatcher-proxy` — FULLY restorable incl. cognition (row carries `proxy_url` + encrypted `proxy_token_*` → decrypt in-memory → rebuild the proxy client; SSRF-revalidated; ledger-capable IFF `row.userId` non-null, re-checked by `resolveAgentSession`'s `boundUserId === liveBot.userId` backstop); `nanoclaw`/`milady`/`anonymous` — restorable as a body (no outbound gateway; chat is the server-side Eliza runtime or a pull/no-op stub) but NON-ledger (a restart-restore proves no fresh ownership, so real-CT play needs a fresh owned reconnect); `openclaw`/`ironclaw`/`custom` with a real `gateway_url` — NOT restorable because `auth_token` is never persisted (outbound chat would 401), so restore returns null and the agent reconnects (prior behaviour, graceful). Concurrency: per-agent in-flight promise (keyed by hash) coalesces simultaneous post-restart restores so a double override-register can't throw. Restore NEVER mints a new sessionId, slides the TTL, or grants liveness the primary gate would refuse. (4) De-branded the framework-agnostic session errors in `apps/api/src/routes/openclaw.ts` /chat + /location-chat: "OpenClaw session not found or expired…" → "Agent session not found or expired. Reconnect your agent." (+ machine code `agent_session_not_found`, status 404), "Bot may have disconnected" → "Your agent may have disconnected" (same code), "OpenClaw gateway error" → "Agent gateway error" (+ `agent_gateway_error`, 502). The web side (`apps/web/src/lib/api.ts`) branches on `code`, not message text. The `/api/openclaw/register` ping-failure copy and the `/api/agent/connect` gateway error stay OpenClaw-specific (those endpoints ARE openclaw). PARITY: restore applies identically to every connected/hosted agent identity type on the shared bearer gate; no human path involved. No env var change.
**Last Audited:** 2026-06-11: **Hatcher launch-exchange entry.** New Hono route `apps/api/src/routes/partner-hatcher-launch.ts`, mounted at `POST /api/partner/hatcher/launch/exchange` (same `/api/partner/hatcher` prefix as the registration router). Redeems the owner-side launch grant Hatcher's dashboard issues (`/game?hatcher_agent=…&hatcher_launch=…`). Lucia-session-gated (sessionMiddleware → manual session check so guests get the specific `launch_requires_session` 401, not the generic HTTPException). Flow: per-IP rate limit 10/min → strict Zod `{agentId (≤200, `[\w.:@-]`), launchToken (≤4096)}` → local `openclaw_bots` `hatcher:<agentId>` existence check (404 `agent_not_registered`, NO outbound call, NO signature minted) → `signPayload()` (service-issuer ed25519) over `{agentId, launchToken, clawvillePlayerId: userId, clawvilleSessionId: sha256Hex(sessionId), mode:'autonomous'}` → POST the canonical signed bytes to the FIXED `https://api.hatcher.host/integrations/clawville/launch/exchange` (`redirect:'manual'`, 10s AbortController) → ANY 2xx = success. SECURITY: never transmits the raw Lucia session id (only its sha256 hex digest — non-bearer); launchToken never logged (audit row stores sha256 hex only); never echoes Hatcher's body (only `{ok, error, status?}` from `HatcherLaunchExchangeResponse` in `@clawville/shared`). Sign-failure (our issuer key missing) is a distinct 503 `launch_issuer_unconfigured` (mirrors `portal.ts:730-733`), split from the 502 `exchange_rejected` upstream-failure bucket. Success returns the agent's public id/name + in-world body position (from npc-simulation `getOpenClawAvatarPosition`, falling back to persisted home 2560,2560) so web can focus the camera (spectate/`autonomous`). Audit event `hatcher.launch.exchanged` (not leaderboard-scored). v1 ships `autonomous` only; `controlled` possession is a follow-up. No DB schema or env var change. PARITY: this is owner-side plumbing — the agent is already in-world via partner registration; the launch is the owner's entry to observe it. No economy write in this flow, so settlement is unchanged.
**Last Audited:** 2026-06-10: **Game-wide language translation API.** New Hono route `apps/api/src/routes/i18n.ts`, mounted at `POST /api/i18n/translate`, batch-translates visible `/game` UI strings through OpenAI (`OPENAI_API_KEY`, `OPENAI_SMALL_MODEL` default `gpt-4o-mini`). It is intentionally above NPC/chat routes: the caller is `<GameLanguageControl>` scanning rendered DOM text, so HUDs, modals, controls, and chat panels can translate after mount instead of only translating NPC responses. The route is no-auth for guest play, IP rate-limited at **60/min** (raised from 30 on 2026-06-18 with the incremental client rework — top Last Audited entry), capped at 80 entries/request and 600 chars/string, has process-local LRU-style cache, returns original text for English locales, and never calls Gemini. No DB schema or env var change. PARITY: human/guest browser path and connected-agent manual-control browser path share the same client route; autonomous agent protocol/action surfaces are unchanged.

**Prior Last Audited:** 2026-06-10: **request() same-origin change REVERTED (was a prod-breaking regression).** The 2026-06-08 entry below described `apps/web/src/lib/api.ts` `request()` as keeping "same-origin Next routes" — but `apps/web/src/app/api/**` contains NO route handlers for the nine flows `request()` serves (guest signup `/api/auth/guest`, forgot/reset-password, send-verification, NPC transient chat, item purchase/learn, username check/update, `/api/avatars/me`, location chat), so same-origin fetch 404'd them on prod AND localhost. Restored master semantics: `const API_URL = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL || ''` + `fetch(\`${API_URL}${path}\`)` — both `request()` and `honoRequest()` target the Hono API (honoRequest differs only in its `localhost:4000` fallback). The local-dev cookie-isolation intent survives as an explicit opt-in: **`NEXT_PUBLIC_API_SAME_ORIGIN`** (build-time env, `.env.local` ONLY — NEVER set on prod/staging Coolify; NEXT_PUBLIC_* inline at build, so a runtime env change does nothing without rebuild). When `'1'`, `request()` fetches same-origin — note this 404s the request()-served flows locally by design (cookie-isolation kill-switch, not a working proxy). PARITY: transport-only; human path = browser fetch via request() to Hono with Lucia cookie; agent surfaces use honoRequest()/server routes, unaffected. Prior **Last Audited:** 2026-06-08: **Web auth origin separation for local/staging playtests.** `apps/web/src/lib/api.ts` now keeps same-origin Next routes (`/api/auth/*`, avatar/UI proxy state) on the current web origin and reserves `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` for Hono backend calls through `honoRequest()`. Local production builds frequently set `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=https://api-staging.clawville.world`; prefixing the generic `request()` helper with that origin leaked staging auth/cookie state into localhost and could show logged-in email banners when the local app was not logged in. Prior **Last Audited:** 2026-06-06: **Multiplayer Phase 1 security + agent parity (audit fix).** Closed a session-token leak (`PlayerSnapshot` now emits a non-reversible `publicId`, never the raw Lucia bearer / guest fp / agent id) + added a room-membership gate on the SSE stream; added Lucia > agent-session > guest precedence so connected/hosted agents join AS THEMSELVES (`kind:'agent'`, real avatar, counted toward the room cap, swap-eligible per Rule E5); re-enabled the collaboration log on `/game` (single per-tick broker drain shared across per-room + global snapshots); guests spawn at town center; `PROTOCOL_VERSION` 2 to 3 for the new agent world-join capability. Full detail in §6 (world routes) + §13 (2026-06-06 entry). Prior **Last Audited:** 2026-06-05: **Embeddings ALSO swapped Gemini to OpenAI (Gemini now fully retired).** Following the text swap (see the prior entry), embeddings moved off Gemini onto OpenAI too. A new `openai-embedding-provider.ts` (`text-embedding-3-small`, 1536-dim, priority 100) registered in `eliza-runtime.ts` replaces `gemini-embedding-provider` for `TEXT_EMBEDDING`, so every runtime now embeds via OpenAI. The standalone `embed-text.ts` helper (used for the query-side / KnowledgeProvider query path) was also swapped to the 1536-dim OpenAI endpoint, keeping stored vectors (provider) and query vectors (helper) the same dimension so similarity search stays correct. The boot dimension-probe in `openai-embedding-provider.ts` resolves its zero-vector length from the SAME `config.dimensions ?? OPENAI_EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS ?? 1536` expression the real embed path uses, so the probe and live vectors can never diverge even if the dimension is overridden. The embeddings table was EMPTY (0 rows) at swap time, so NO re-embed migration was needed; vectors store in the existing `dim_1536` column. Gemini is now FULLY retired: `GEMINI_API_KEY` is unused by the runtime (both text and embeddings on OpenAI), retained only for legacy / easy-revert. `OPENAI_API_KEY` now backs both text AND embeddings; optional `OPENAI_EMBEDDING_MODEL` (default `text-embedding-3-small`) + `OPENAI_EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS` (default 1536) tune the provider. Prior **Last Audited:** 2026-06-05: **Phanes is the default Hatcher render model.** `partner-hatcher.ts` now assigns `DEFAULT_HATCHER_MODEL_KEY='phanes'` (new const + `AGENT_MODELS` entry, category `hatcher`, in `packages/shared/src/constants/agent-models.ts`, barrel-exported) for every new Hatcher agent, at both the register `resolvedSpecies` and `buildHatcherAvatarValues` `resolvedModel` sites (replaces `pickRandomHatcherModelKey()`, whose placeholder pool `HATCHER_MODEL_KEYS` now excludes phanes). Web registry `agent-model-registry.ts` gains the `phanes` entry (`/avatars/phanes.vrm`, VRM 1.0, animatorId `hermes-male`, `pickerHidden:true`); `create-agent/page.tsx` filters `pickerHidden` so phanes is reserved (server-assigned, never user-selectable). No schema/migration change (`hatcher` category already valid). PARITY: agent-only render-model default; REAL-CT settlement binds to the agent's bound avatar (unchanged).

**Last Audited:** 2026-06-05: **Text generation swapped Gemini to OpenAI (embeddings stay on Gemini).** All text generation moved off Gemini onto OpenAI because Gemini text billing is dunning-blocked (403). A new `openai-text-provider.ts` (priority 95) registered in `eliza-runtime.ts` replaces `gemini-text-provider` for `TEXT_SMALL`/`TEXT_LARGE`, so every non-OpenClaw runtime (location agents, system agents, simulation-runtime, collaboration-broker) now generates via `runtime.useModel` against OpenAI. `npc-conversation-engine.ts` (NPC banter) and chat-transient are OpenAI-only now (the dead Gemini fallback was deleted from the NPC engine, since GEMINI_API_KEY is still set for embeddings and would otherwise fire a 403 on any OpenAI hiccup). Models are `gpt-4o-mini` (small) and `gpt-4o` (large), overridable via `OPENAI_SMALL_MODEL`/`OPENAI_LARGE_MODEL`. `OPENAI_API_KEY` is now plumbed explicitly through the `apiKeys` config in `agent-orchestrator.ts` and `avatar-simulation-bridge.ts` (alongside the retained `gemini` key) rather than relying only on the env fallback. EMBEDDINGS deliberately stay on Gemini (`gemini-embedding-provider`, 768-dim `TEXT_EMBEDDING`): OpenAI's `text-embedding-3-small` is 1536-dim, a dimension mismatch that would require a full re-embed migration of stored vectors, so that swap is deferred. Prior **Last Audited:** 2026-06-05: **Agent-session bearer-leak hardening (round 4-5, security; central `event-logger.ts` redaction chokepoint).** The raw agent-session id (`ag-`/`oc-`/`hat-`/`claw-` + `randomBytes(24)` base64url) is the real-CT bearer the Cove reads from `X-Clawville-Agent-Session`. `logEvent()` (`apps/api/src/services/event-logger.ts`) now redacts it CENTRALLY before the `events` insert: `RAW_BEARER_RE = /^(ag|oc|hat|claw)-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{32}$/` (EXACT shape: every real-CT bearer is `<prefix>-` + `randomBytes(24).toString('base64url')` = exactly 32 url-safe chars) digests the `session_id` AND `agent_id` columns plus any raw-bearer string leaf in `payload` (recursive, depth-capped 6, plain-object only) via `sessionDigest()` (sha256 16-hex, one-way, correlation-only, never an auth credential). The EXACT length is load-bearing: a legit grouping handle (`oc-mybot`, `milady:x`, `agent-<ts>-<rand>`, a UUID, `hatcher:<id>`) is never exactly `<prefix>-`+32-url-safe-chars, so it passes through unchanged and leaderboard `GROUP BY agent_id` / `COUNT(DISTINCT session_id)` is preserved; an injected raw bearer (e.g. a client putting an `ag-/oc-/hat-` into the spoofable `X-Clawville-Agent-Id` header that `skills.ts` logs into `events.agent_id`) matches and is digested. IDEMPOTENT by construction: already-digested 16-hex values have no bearer prefix and are 16 chars, so they never match (no double-digest), and `sessionDigest` is deterministic 1:1. This central chokepoint supersedes scattered call-site digests and closes both the `skills.ts` `X-Clawville-Session-Id` and caller-supplied `X-Clawville-Agent-Id` header leaks the call-site sweep missed. Also shipped: round-4 fail-closed TTL liveness gates on `/openclaw/chat` + `/location-chat` (`validateLiveAgentSession` before any TTL slide), `/wallet` TTL gate, cove insure stale-decision contract + the BlackjackModal agent-driven insure split (server stays authoritative for insurance-offered), session-ticket `issuedToAgentSession` digest, npc-sim BrowserClaw log digests, and the milady `miladyTrusted` partial REMOVED (milady signed-challenge parked; exchange returns to prod liveness-only behavior). REVERTED after dual review (Claude team + 3 Codex lenses): the `/openclaw/register` blanket TTL gate (it stranded legitimate returning bots for marginal non-ledger gain) and the ElizaOS-state `userId`/`avatarId` digests (broke room/memory continuity, not a recoverable-bearer leak). Systemic auth-identity findings (agentId-only TTL resurrection on `/connect`, `ledgerCapable` not enforced on building routes, `skills.ts` map-membership TTL trust, milady exchange provenance, hatcher PATCH leaderboard under-count) DEFERRED to `.claude/plans/agent-session-identity-followup.md` (same root cause as the parked milady signed-challenge, `.claude/plans/milady-signed-challenge-PARKED.md`). `cd apps/api && bunx tsc --noEmit` clean (exit 0). Prior **Last Audited:** 2026-06-04: **Hatcher register auto-provisions an avatar (Rule E5 parity, §6).** An identityKey-bound `POST /api/partner/hatcher/agents` now auto-creates a default avatar for the resolved user when they have none (`partner-hatcher.ts` `ensureHatcherAvatar` over the pure `buildHatcherAvatarValues`), so a fresh Hatcher agent is immediately ledger-capable and plays the Cove for REAL CT (closes the `agent_session_has_no_active_avatar` 403). Reuses the `/join` agent-avatar shape: starting balance = schema default `avatars.clawTokens=100` (NO ledger grant, human and `/join` parity, no faucet, >= Cove 5-CT min), `isActive:true`, `agentCategory:'hatcher'`, `harness:'custom'`, model = assigned `hatcher_N`. Idempotent (one avatar/user; re-register reuses and never re-grants); race-safe on 23505; non-fatal (cove fails CLOSED, never guest). Response gains `avatarProvisioned`. PARITY: human=avatar at signup (`POST /api/avatars`); agent=avatar on signed register; settlement binds via `resolveAgentSession` then cove `getSubject`. tsc clean (api, exit 0); e2e harness 75/75; focused verify 4/4. Prior **Last Audited:** 2026-06-02 — **Hatcher [ACTION:] whitelist prototype-key hardening (security fix).** Every whitelist map lookup in `npcSimulation.executeHatcherAction` now gates on `Object.hasOwn(MAP, key)` BEFORE use, never a bare truthy bracket-access (`emote` `HATCHER_EMOTE_MAP`, `enter_building` + `talk_to_npc` `NPC_BUILDING_CENTERS`). Previously `[ACTION: emote(name=constructor)]` (or `__proto__`/`toString`/`hasOwnProperty`/`valueOf`) resolved `HATCHER_EMOTE_MAP['constructor']` to the inherited Object ctor fn (truthy), passing the `if (!emote)` guard and running `setNpcActivity(npcId, undefined, undefined)` — corrupting NPC activity state propagated to clients + partner worldState. Now inherited prototype keys can never satisfy the whitelist (drop + warn-log). DoS caps (`MAX_HATCHER_REPLY_LEN`, `MAX_HATCHER_ACTIONS_PER_REPLY`) unchanged. `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api` clean (exit 0). — **Hatcher cognition Phase A++ — structured `clawville` payload + strict [ACTION:] whitelist (hatcher-proxy path ONLY; `.claude/plans/hatcher-integration.md` §14).** `OpenClawClient.chatHatcherProxy` (`apps/api/src/services/agent-substrate-client.ts`) now ships a top-level structured `clawville:{ playerMessage, worldState?, orientation:{version, url:'/api/skills/protocol/skill.md'} }` and STOPS forcing a `role:'system'` message (Hatcher owns the root prompt); `messages` carries only the last user turn. New `apps/api/src/services/baccarat-engine.ts` (pure, deterministic, 55 unit tests in `__tests__/baccarat-engine.test.ts`): 8-deck (416-card) shared commit-reveal shoe, no-replacement draws from the audited HMAC-SHA256 stream via `provable-rng.sampleIntFromBytes` keyed on `(serverSeed, clientSeed, nonce=coupIndex, cursor)` — same shared-shoe model as blackjack (~75% penetration = 312 cards ⇒ NEW shoe/seed pair; engine never reshuffles mid-coup). Punto Banco has NO player decisions: `playCoup(args)` deals P,B,P,B, short-circuits on a two-card natural (8/9), else runs the FIXED third-card tableau (Player stands 6-7 / draws 0-5; Banker tableau by banker-total × player-3rd-card, or draws-on-0-5 if player stood), computes the winner, and settles the bet. Card values A=1, 2-9 face, 10/J/Q/K=0, total = sum mod 10. PAYOUTS (integer math, house-friendly floor): PLAYER win 1:1 (gross stake×2); BANKER win 0.95:1 with FLOORED 5% commission (`commission = floor(stake×5/100)`, gross = stake + (stake-commission)); TIE bet 8:1 (gross stake×9); P/B bets PUSH on a tie (gross = stake); losers gross 0. Exports `playCoup`/`replayCoup`/`playCoupWithState`/`replayShoeUpToCoup`/`bankerDraws`/`settleBet`/`buildShoe`/`serializeCoupResult` (+ `BACCARAT_ENGINE_VERSION='bac-v1'`, `RESHUFFLE_CARD_THRESHOLD=312`). New tables `baccarat_shoes` + `baccarat_coups` (`packages/database/src/schema/baccarat.ts`, registered in `schema/index.ts`; migration `scripts/casino/migrate-baccarat-tables.mjs`). New route `apps/api/src/routes/cove-baccarat.ts` mounted at `/api/cove/baccarat`: `POST /session/open` (auth optional; guest 100 demo CT; commits `serverSeedHash`, NEVER reveals `serverSeed`), `POST /coup` (auth optional — places PLAYER/BANKER/TIE bet 5–500 CT, deals + resolves + settles in ONE transaction under the shoe FOR UPDATE lock; no in-progress window since there are no player decisions; idempotent via `(shoeId, idempotencyKey)`), `POST /session/close` (Lucia auth — reveals `serverSeed` on every baccarat `cove_game_events` row), `GET /session/current`, `GET /session/:id`. MONEY MODEL: stake debited + gross payout credited atomically per coup via `claw-token-ledger` (push = stake returned, net 0); guest demo balance lives entirely on the shoe row (no ledger); one `cove_game_events` row per coup (`gameType='baccarat'`, `sessionId=shoeId`, `nonce=coupIndex`); payout > Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER rejected; currency seam (clawtoken; sol/usdc → 501). `cove-history.ts` `GET /:eventId/verify` gains the `baccarat` branch (shared-shoe no-replacement replay via `replayShoeUpToCoup` from the shoe's recorded per-coup bet/stake + `sha256(seed)==hash`); slots/blackjack/holdem branches untouched. `cd apps/api && bunx tsc --noEmit` clean; 55/55 engine tests pass. **Prior Last edit:** 2026-05-28 — Phase 6.5.1 No-Limit Texas Hold'em REAL engine + authoritative route (replaces the 6.5.0 display-only mock). New `apps/api/src/services/holdem-engine.ts` (pure, deterministic, 42 unit tests in `__tests__/holdem-engine.test.ts`): per-hand fresh 52-card deck shuffled by HMAC Fisher–Yates over `provable-rng.sampleIntFromBytes` keyed on `(serverSeed, clientSeed, nonce=handIndex, cursor=0)` — NO shared shoe, so a hand replays from `(seed, handIndex)` alone; 7-card best-5 evaluator (royal/straight flush incl. wheel A-2-3-4-5 as five-high, quads, full house incl. two-trips, flush, straight, trips, two-pair, pair, high-card, all with correct kickers); full NLHE betting engine (SB=1/BB=2, blinds, preflop→flop→turn→river→showdown, fold/check/call/bet/raise, min-raise=previous-raise-size, all-in, correct layered SIDE POTS with folded-seat dead-money + eligibility, split-pot remainder-to-earliest-seat); 5 deterministic bot personalities (TAG/LAG/tight-passive/calling-station/nit) whose only mixed-strategy roll comes from the HMAC stream at a dedicated cursor region (`BOT_DECISION_CURSOR_BASE=2^20`) so the whole hand is replayable; exports `playHand`/`replayHand`/`evaluateBest5`/`compareHandRank`/`buildSidePots`/`shuffleDeck`/`serializeHoldemHand`. New tables `holdem_tables` + `holdem_hands` (`packages/database/src/schema/holdem.ts`, registered in `schema/index.ts`; migration `scripts/casino/migrate-holdem-tables.mjs`). MONEY MODEL: chips move within `playerStack`, ledger touched only at buy-in/cash-out; one `cove_game_events` row per hand (`gameType='holdem'`); settle idempotent under FOR UPDATE + `(tableId, idempotencyKey)` unique; currency seam (clawtoken; sol/usdc→501). `cove-history.ts` `GET /:eventId/verify` gains the `holdem` branch (per-hand fresh-deck replay via `playHoldemHand` + `sha256(seed)==hash`); slots + blackjack branches untouched. `cd apps/api && bunx tsc --noEmit` clean; 42/42 engine tests pass. **Prior Last edit:** 2026-05-31 — NPC entity-push deadlock fix. Two wandering NPCs walking toward each other froze at exactly `combinedHalf` distance because the half-overlap push in `apps/api/src/services/npc-simulation.ts:resolveNpcNpcOverlaps` cancelled each path step; `stuckTicks` didn't trip because the perpendicular push counts as `moved >= 2`. Client rendered the walk cycle in place ("moonwalk") because the server reported `direction !== 'idle'` continuously. Confirmed on prod by polling `/api/npc/state` once/second for 6 s — `hermes-tekk` + `milady-suki` at 75 wu apart on identical y=4944, 0 wu moved over 5 s with `act='walking'` and active paths. Fix: new `overlapTicks: number` field on `NpcRuntimeState` (seeded 0 in `initNpcs`, OpenClaw avatar register, and `handleRespawns`); `resolveNpcNpcOverlaps` collects every overlapping pair and the set of involved NPC IDs each tick, increments/resets `overlapTicks` per NPC, then runs a yield pass — for each overlapping pair the lex-lower id is the yielder, and once their `overlapTicks >= 3` (~600 ms at 5 Hz) the yielder abandons its path, drops `activity='idle'`, sets `direction='idle'` (kills the moonwalk visual immediately), and gets an 8-15 tick (~1.6-3 s) `behaviorCooldown` before re-planning so the higher-id NPC walks past. Asymmetric on purpose — if both yielded they'd both re-plan and could reconverge on the same area. New regression test `apps/api/src/services/__tests__/npc-overlap-deadlock.test.ts` (5 cases: lex-lower yields at threshold, non-overlap leaves counter at 0, transient overlap that resolves doesn't yield, in-conversation NPCs are skipped, hi-id NPC never yields). `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean. **Prior Last edit:** 2026-05-28 — Phase 6.7.5 cove guest history. `slot_sessions.user_id` + `cove_game_events.user_id` made nullable, added `guest_fp_hash text` to both with DB check constraint enforcing XOR. Rebuilt user-keyed indexes as partial-`WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL`; added matching guest-keyed partial indexes (`*_guest_fp_*_idx`) for guest read path; added `slot_sessions_guest_open_unique` mirror of the one-open-session partial unique. New idempotent migration script `scripts/casino/migrate-guest-history.mjs` (raw SQL via pg + DATABASE_URL — Drizzle-kit can't handle check+partial-index combo per `feedback_drizzle_kit_introspection_bug`). `apps/api/src/routes/cove-slots.ts`: `POST /session/open` + `POST /spin` no longer require Lucia auth; new `getSubject(c)` helper returns `{kind:'user'|'guest', userId|null, guestFpHash|null}` from `c.get('user')`/`c.get('fpHash')`. Guest sessions seed `starting_balance='100'` (fun-CT demo wallet) and never touch `avatars.clawTokens` — debit/credit branches skip the ClawTokens ledger and update only the session-row money columns; running demo balance is `starting_balance + total_won - total_staked`, refused when it would go negative. Per-fp `/session/open` rate limit (10/hour, single-process in-memory bucket); `/spin` rate limit (60/min) now keys on `u:<userId>` or `g:<fpHash>` so guests are bounded too. `cove_game_events` insert stamps `userId: subject.userId, guestFpHash: subject.guestFpHash` — DB check constraint enforces the XOR. `/session/close`, `/session/current`, `/session/:id`, `/session/:id/spins` stay `requireAuth` (guests' sessions just expire by the existing expiry cron; reveal happens when the now-claimed session is closed by the signed-up owner). New `apps/api/src/routes/cove-history.ts` mounted at `/api/cove/history/*` — `GET /` (Lucia auth, owner-scoped, keyset-paginated 50/page via `base64url(${createdAt.toISOString()}|${id})` cursor; query filters: `game?`, `outcome?` ('win' = `payout::numeric > betAmount::numeric`, 'loss' = `payout::numeric <= betAmount::numeric`, both casts because the money columns are TEXT-stringified bigints), `limit`, `cursor`; ordered `(created_at DESC, id DESC)` to ride the `(user_id, created_at DESC)` index from impl-schema; fetches `limit+1` to decide `nextCursor` without a COUNT round-trip; returns `{events, nextCursor}`) + `GET /:eventId` (Lucia auth, owner OR `ADMIN_USER_IDS`, returns the single serialized event — used by the `/cove/verify/[eventId]` UI to load the row before dispatching to the per-game verifier component) + `GET /:eventId/verify` (Lucia auth, owner OR `ADMIN_USER_IDS` for dispute support; slots: re-runs server `runSpin` against the revealed seed + checks `sha256(revealedServerSeed)===serverSeedHash` and deep-equals reels/winningLines/winAmount, returns `{verified, expected, stored, hashMatches}`; pre-reveal events: 200 with `{verified: null, reason: 'shoe-not-yet-closed'}`; other game types: `{verified: null, reason: 'engine-not-yet-shipped'}` until 6.7.1+). Server-side `apps/api/src/services/pathfinding.ts` rewritten to rasterize EVERY `getServerColliders()` AABB onto its 360×360 walkability grid (10 buildings + 8 town-center prop colliders) with per-axis tile pad = `ceil(halfX/32) + 4` instead of the old fixed `BUILDING_EXCLUSION_PAD = 11` (which under-covered messaging-channels/api-integrations halfX=850 wu = 26.5 tiles and ignored every prop AABB). Adds 3 new exports: `isCollisionFreeWorld(gamePxX, gamePxY, entityHalf)` (wraps `clampPosition2D`, returns true iff outside every AABB), `findNearestWalkable(gamePxX, gamePxY, entityHalf, maxRadiusPx=600)` (spiral-out snap to a point that passes BOTH the A* grid clearance AND the pixel-accurate AABB clamp), and `WORLD_COLLIDER_TILE_SIZE` re-export. `apps/api/src/services/npc-simulation.ts` adds `stuckTicks: number` to `NpcRuntimeState` (seeded 0 at both spawn sites + respawn) and a private `snapPlannerTarget()` helper. Payout multipliers in `CLASSIC_SYMBOLS` UNCHANGED. Prior: Concern 6.1.3 + 6.1.4 (slice 3): casino slots fun-money backend wire. New schema `packages/database/src/schema/casino.ts` (`slot_sessions` + `slot_spins` tables, partial unique index for one-open-session-per-user, idempotency-key unique index per session). New routes `apps/api/src/routes/casino-slots.ts` mounted at `/api/casino/slots/*` — `POST /session/open`, `POST /spin` (Idempotency-Key header required, 60/min/user rate limit, atomic txn covers debit+spin+credit), `POST /session/close` (reveals serverSeed, refunds escrow), `GET /session/:id`, `GET /session/:id/spins`, `GET /paytables/:id` (public), `POST /verify` (public, pure compute). BigInt JSON via explicit `serializeSpinResult`/`serializeWinningLine` helpers in `apps/api/src/routes/casino-slots.types.ts` — NO `BigInt.prototype.toJSON` monkey-patch. ClawTokens currency live; SOL/USDC return 501. Tests at `apps/api/src/routes/__tests__/casino-slots.test.ts`. Prior: Concern 6.1.2: slot engine (server-side deterministic spin evaluator on top of provable-rng). Prior: Concern 6.0.4 polish pass: slot UI redesign. New frontend modules: `apps/web/src/lib/casino/useFX.ts` (5-tier FX state hook), `apps/web/src/styles/casino-tokens.css` (design-token CSS — palette, spacing, motion, shared keyframes), `apps/web/src/components/casino/ui/{NeonButton,NeonCard,NeonModal,BetChips}.tsx` (branded primitives), `packages/shared/src/constants/slot-symbols.ts` (`CLASSIC_SLOT_SYMBOL_ASSETS` manifest exported from `@clawville/shared`), `apps/web/public/assets/slot-symbols/s0..s7.svg` (ClawVille-themed reel art). Existing casino components refactored to consume tokens + primitives; SpinResult contract + mock engine + Zustand store unchanged. No new API routes, no DB changes. Prior: Concern 6.0.4 — 2D slot screen (mock data).

---

## 1. System overview

```
Browser (Next.js)                          Hetzner CCX13 + Coolify
+----------------------------+             +----------------------------+
|  Next.js App Router        |             |  Hono API (Bun :4000)      |
|  +----------------------+  |             |  +----------------------+  |
|  | World3DCanvas (R3F)  |  |  REST/SSE   |  | Auth (Lucia)         |  |
|  | Three.js WebGPU      |<--------------->|  | Agent Orchestrator   |  |
|  +----------------------+  |             |  | NPC Simulation       |  |
|  +----------------------+  |             |  +----------------------+  |
|  | Zustand Stores       |  |             |  | ElizaOS Runtime      |  |
|  | (game, npc)          |  |             |  | (OpenAI only)        |  |
|  +----------------------+  |             |  +----------------------+  |
|  +----------------------+  |             |          |                 |
|  | React UI Overlays    |  |             |  +----------------------+  |
|  | (chat, shop, HUD)    |  |             |  | PostgreSQL (Supabase)|  |
|  +----------------------+  |             |  | via Drizzle ORM      |  |
+----------------------------+             |  +----------------------+  |
                                           +----------------------------+
```

Frontend (`apps/web`): Next.js 16, React 19, R3F 9, Zustand. Entry: `app/game/page.tsx` → dynamically imports `World3DCanvas` (SSR disabled).

Backend (`apps/api`): Bun runtime, Hono 4 HTTP. Entry: `apps/api/src/index.ts`.

LLM: **OpenAI is the SOLE backend** for both text generation and embeddings (full Gemini→OpenAI migration completed 2026-06-05; the Gemini provider reads + files were scrubbed 2026-06-16). Text runs through `openai-text-provider` (priority 95; `gpt-4o-mini` / `gpt-4o`); embeddings through `openai-embedding-provider` (`text-embedding-3-small`, 1536-dim, priority 100). `OPENAI_API_KEY` is required by every non-OpenClaw runtime. `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` was removed 2026-04-10 (ultrathink decommission); `GEMINI_API_KEY` is no longer read by any code.

| `locations.ts` | `/api/locations/*` | 10-building zone metadata |

| `chat-transient.ts` | `/api/chat/transient` | Stateless one-shot **OpenAI** chat with a wandering world NPC for NPC mode (`controlMode === 'npc'`). No Eliza, no rooms, no DB writes. |
| `i18n.ts` | `/api/i18n/translate` | Game-wide UI translation — batch-translates visible `/game` DOM strings through **OpenAI** (`OPENAI_SMALL_MODEL`). No-auth for guest play, **60/min/IP** (raised from 30 2026-06-18), ≤80 entries + ≤600 chars/string per request, process-local LRU cache, returns original for English locales. |
| `items.ts` | `/api/items/*` | Knowledge-book shop, inventory, buy, learn |
| `cosmetics.ts` | `/api/cosmetics/*` | Q3 §4.2 cosmetic engine — `GET /catalog?scope=`, plus equip/owned surfaces (skins, hats, auras; CT-priced first-party shop, not a peer marketplace). |
| `agent-gateway.ts` | `/api/agent/*` | The universal connect surface — see §6 |
| `portal.ts` | `/api/portal/*`, `/.well-known/clawville-issuer.json` | Phase 5.1 'scape portal + Hatcher portal (partner #2, 2026-06-01) — see §7 |
| `partner-hatcher.ts` | `/api/partner/hatcher/*` | **Hatcher partner-registration API — proxy cognition (Phase A, 2026-06-01).** All routes partner-signed via `verifyPartnerSignature('hatcher')` over the RAW request body (`X-Hatcher-Issuer-Pubkey` + `X-Hatcher-Signature` = ed25519 over sha256(rawBody); NO Lucia) and Zod-validated. **AGENT-ID NAMESPACE (cross-framework hijack guard, 2026-06-01):** `openclaw_bots.agent_id` is a SHARED globally-unique namespace — `/api/agent/connect` lets ANY caller pick an arbitrary raw `agentId` (milady prefixes `milady:<id>`). So a Hatcher key holder must NOT be able to address another framework's row. Every Hatcher agent is stored as `hatcher:<rawId>` (`namespaceHatcherAgentId`); lookups/session-tracking/events all use the namespaced id; the RAW id is sent to Hatcher's proxy (URL + `model:hatcher:<rawId>`) via `OpenClawBotConfig.proxyAgentId`; responses echo the raw id (`rawHatcherAgentId`). PLUS an ownership guard: register/patch/delete refuse any row whose `identityType !== 'hatcher'` (POST→409 `agent_id_conflict`; PATCH/DELETE→opaque 404). `POST /agents` (register/upsert a `hatcher:`-namespaced `openclaw_bots` row — `cognitionBackend='hatcher-proxy'`, `proxyUrl` (SSRF-validated), `scopedToken` ENCRYPTED at rest in `proxy_token_enc/iv/tag`; ensures a custodial wallet; spawns/overrides the in-world body; binds `userId` when `identityKey` resolves; NEVER echoes the token), `PATCH /agents/:agentId` (update name/species/personality/mode + rotate the cognition token AND re-register the LIVE sim entity), `DELETE /agents/:agentId` (`unregisterOpenClaw` the body + tombstone the row: expire session, scrub `cognition_backend`/`proxy_url`/`proxy_token_*`). Cognition itself flows through `OpenClawClient.chat()` case `'hatcher-proxy'` → `POST {proxyUrl}/integrations/clawville/agents/{rawAgentId}/chat` with DUAL auth (Hatcher `Authorization: Bearer <scopedToken>` + our `X-Clawville-*` ed25519 sig over canonical JSON); fails soft (returns '') on proxy error/timeout, never logging the token. Shared sig helper: `services/partner-signature.ts` (extracted from portal.ts). **SSRF guard (hardened 2026-06-01): `services/hatcher-config.ts`** — `validateHatcherProxyUrl` (sync: https + no embedded creds + reject private/loopback/link-local IP literals incl. `169.254.169.254`/`::1` + `HATCHER_PROXY_ALLOWED_HOSTS` allowlist / default `hatcher.host`); `validateHatcherProxyUrlResolved` (async, used at register/patch: DNS-resolves the host and rejects if ANY A/AAAA record is a private IP — defeats rebind/subdomain-to-internal; fails CLOSED on DNS error); the cognition fetch sets `redirect:'manual'` and treats ANY 3xx/opaqueredirect as a hard fail-soft (plan §14: "no redirect-following to non-allowlisted hosts" — stops a compromised/allowlisted host bouncing us to metadata/RFC1918 and forwarding the token+sig). **PHASE A++ (2026-06-02 — structured payload + strict action whitelist, hatcher-proxy path ONLY):** the cognition body now ships a top-level structured `clawville` object — `{ playerMessage, worldState?, orientation:{version, url:'/api/skills/protocol/skill.md'} }` — and **no longer forces a `role:'system'` message** (Hatcher owns the root prompt + builds it from `clawville`); `messages` carries ONLY the last user turn. `worldState` (new shared type `HatcherWorldState`) is built by `npcSimulation.buildHatcherWorldState(npcId, mode)` and is PUBLIC-ONLY (players by name+distance, npcs by public npcId+isAgent, buildings by id+focus, self pose/hp/activity, gameMode) — never token/wallet/identity secret, session id, or userId. The ed25519 sig still covers the EXACT canonical bytes of the whole body. The reply is post-processed by `npcSimulation.dispatchHatcherActions(npcId, replyText)` (wired via `processProxyReply` in `generateOpenClawConversation`, gated on `client.getProtocol()==='hatcher-proxy'`): parse `[ACTION: name(params)]` (same regex as `eliza-runtime.ts`), validate every param against the STRICT MVP WHITELIST, execute valid ones via the SAME sim primitives the REST handlers use (`setNpcPath`+`findPath`, `setNpcActivity`, `injectAgentChat`), STRIP all tags, return clean speech. Unknown names / out-of-bounds params → dropped + logged, never executed. **Whitelist (5 verbs — executor in `npc-simulation.ts executeHatcherAction` is the authoritative gate):** `move(x,y 32..11488)` · `emote(name∈{wave,dance,think,scan,work,celebrate,alert})` · `enter_building(buildingId∈ the 10)` (walk-to-building) · `enter_cove()` (no params — the brain walks into the Cove casino; 2026-06-03 cove parity) · `talk_to_npc(npcId|buildingId, message≤500)` (chat bubble). **Manual parity (FIX-5, 2026-06-13):** `skill-protocol.ts buildProtocolManual` §3a now documents ALL 5 of these verbs (previously it enumerated only `enter_cove`), mirroring the executor whitelist and satisfying the CLAUDE.md "Hatcher action whitelist parity" two-file invariant (executor = `npc-simulation.ts` hard gate; manual = `buildProtocolManual` documentation). `PROTOCOL_VERSION` stays at **5** — no executor verb/param changed, only the manual caught up to the already-shipped whitelist, so the prior "verb/whitelist parity unaffected" disclaimer in the version NOTE was removed. **DoS guards (2026-06-02): the shared single-threaded sim is protected on two layers** — (1) `OpenClawClient.chatHatcherProxy` truncates the raw proxy reply at `MAX_HATCHER_REPLY_LEN=4000` chars (our `max_tokens` ask is advisory; a hostile/injected proxy can return a multi-MB body) BEFORE it reaches the parser; (2) `dispatchHatcherActions` EXECUTES at most `MAX_HATCHER_ACTIONS_PER_REPLY=4` tags per reply (each `move`/`enter_building` runs an A* search up to ~6000 iterations; each `talk_to_npc` broadcasts a pending event to every connected client) — tags beyond the cap are still stripped from speech but never executed, bounding A*/broadcast cost to a small constant regardless of how many tags a malicious reply contains. `accept_quest`/`read_book` OUT of MVP. Cognition dispatches the VISIBLE in-world effect only — CT credit / event logging / RAG teacher reply / knowledge persistence remain on the authenticated `/api/agent/:sid/*` handlers (no request/auth context on the autonomous path). See `.claude/plans/hatcher-integration.md` §13/§14. |
| `partner-hatcher-launch.ts` | `/api/partner/hatcher/launch/exchange` | **Hatcher owner-side launch-exchange (2026-06-11).** Lucia-session-gated entry that redeems a dashboard launch grant: Hatcher's dashboard sends the owner to `/game#hatcher_agent=<agentId>&hatcher_launch=<launchToken>` (URL FRAGMENT, not query — the token must not leak to CDN/access logs), the web app POSTs the token here, and the server verifies it server-to-server (signed). Mounted under the same `/api/partner/hatcher` prefix; the 64 KB body limit + bodyLimit gate cover it. See `.claude/plans/hatcher-launch-exchange.md`. |
| `partner-covenant.ts` | `/api/partner/covenant/*` | **Covenant partner — READ-ONLY verification read surface (2026-07-03).** GET-only, NO mutations (no DB write / ledger / on-chain call). Lets the verification partner "Covenant" (`covenantd`) read the evidence + verdict + escrow linkage it verifies against — data the PUBLIC bounty reads hide from a non-hunter. `GET /bounties` (list for polling: Zod `status`∈bounty-status / `paymentRail`∈{ct,usdc} / `limit`≤100 / `offset`, verification-relevant fields only — id, title, status, paymentRail, verdictRequired, escrowPda, escrowJobId, tokenReward, currentAttempts, expiresAt, updatedAt — newest-updated first), `GET /bounties/:id/verification` (full bundle: `bounty` incl. all verdict/escrow columns — `covenant_audit_root_hex`/`covenant_verification_passed`/`covenant_verdict_id`/`escrow_pda`/`escrow_job_id`/`verdict_required`/`acceptance_criteria`; `creator` {avatarId,name,species,reputationTier}; `attempts` = ALL attempts w/ `prLink`/`submissionNote`/`reviewNote`/timestamps + hunter {avatarId,name}; `escrowSettlements` + `escrowApprovals` = the matching `sap_escrow_settlements`/`sap_escrow_approvals` rows on `(escrow_pda, job_id=bounty.id)` — status/signatures/amounts/verification provenance, no secrets; `hunterAgentIdentity` per distinct hunter = wallet PUBKEY (`avatars.walletAddress` mirror) + derived SAP agent PDA (`sap-pdas.findAgentPda`, pure) + public ERC-8004 registration URL), `GET /agents/:avatarId` (agent-services identity bundle: avatar + bounty reputation + the same pubkey-only identity; opaque generic 404 when unknown). **AUTH** = `require-covenant-partner.ts` middleware (see §3): ed25519 partner sig via the EXISTING `verifyPartnerGetSignature('covenant', …)` (same GET wire scheme as Hatcher — `X-Covenant-{Issuer-Pubkey,Signature,Timestamp}`, ±5 min, pubkey matched to `PARTNER_PUBKEYS.covenant`) + `COVENANT_ALLOWED_IPS` allowlist (CF-authoritative IP), fail-closed 503 `partner_not_configured` when either is unset. **PUBKEYS ONLY** — never a secret/DEK/bearer/email/session. **READ-ONLY imports only** from the protected `partner-signature.ts` + `skill-protocol.ts` (`resolveApiBase`) — modifies none of them; does NOT extend the staging-only `ALLOW_TEST_PARTNER_PUBKEY` (hatcher-only) to covenant. **E5:** machine-partner READ surface, no write path to bind (parity mandate N/A). GET-only so no body-size cap. See `docs/sap-covenant-payai-architecture.md`. |
| `agent-registration.ts` | `/.well-known/agents/:fingerprint/agent-registration.json` | **ERC-8004-ready export — off-chain tier (2026-06-01, `.claude/plans/hatcher-integration.md` §12).** Public, unauthenticated GET. Emits the ERC-8004 registration-file FORMAT (`type:"…eip-8004#registration-v1"`) for one agent, keyed on `users.identity_fingerprint`. Opaque `404 {error:"not_found"}` for unknown/malformed fingerprints or users with no `identity_pubkey`. `services`: `web` (clawville profile URL), `DID` (`did:key:z…` of the ed25519 `identity_pubkey`, multicodec `0xed01`-prefixed base58btc), and — IF a custodial Solana wallet exists — `wallet` (CAIP-10 `solana:5eykt4UsFv8P8NJdTREpY1vzqKqZKvdp:<pubkey>`, public key only via the `avatars.walletAddress` mirror). `x402Support` ← `loadX402Config().enabled`; `active` ← avatar `isActive`; `registrations:[]` ALWAYS (off-chain — NEVER fabricates an `agentId`/`agentRegistry`); `clawvilleIdentityFingerprint` = the fingerprint (namespaced extension). OPTIONAL `x-clawville-attestation` = `signPayload(registration)` via the service-issuer key (ClawVille extension, NOT an ERC-8004 field; omitted if the issuer env is unset). **SECURITY: public document — column-pinned SELECT + field-by-field emit; NEVER includes `identity_encrypted_sk`/`identity_dek_wrapped`/`identity_iv`/`identity_tag`/any DEK/wallet secret/password hash/email.** BSC on-chain upgrade (register the file URI, backfill `registrations`) deferred. See §6. |
| `agent-eip8004.ts` | `/agents/:sapAgentPda/{eip-8004,metadata}.json` | **EIP-8004 registration JSON for SAP/Metaplex-registered agents — identity rail (2026-07-01).** Public, unauthenticated GET keyed on the SAP `AgentAccount` PDA. Serves the canonical EIP-8004 doc (`synapseAgent`+`authority` are the load-bearing fields for Covenant / SAP-SDK `MetaplexBridge.verifyLink`/`tripleCheckLink`) from a column-pinned in-code registry of agents ACTUALLY registered on-chain (first: HermesTest — mainnet MPL Core asset `E3tgkW9s…` + 1DREG/mpl-agent-014 registration `cbfZqAiu…`; settle-tx evidence in `extra`). The URL shape is IMMUTABLE once minted — each identity asset's AgentIdentity plugin URI points at exactly this path. Optional service-issuer counter-signature (`x-clawville-attestation`), same pattern as `agent-registration.ts`. 404 (opaque) for unknown/malformed PDAs. |
