Builds a portable, content-addressed, ed25519-signed manifest: `mesh{uri,sha256,format,kBytes}` (body bytes fetched over the web origin + SHA-256'd), `skeleton`, `owner{CAIP-2 chain, base58 PUBKEY}` (via `avatars.walletAddress` mirror — no secret), `createdAt`, equipped `cosmetics[]` (best-effort, omitted on any error), and `clawville.{identity.pubkey, character (buildCharacterExport), skillPack (buildSkillPack), provenance}`. Signed via the **reused** `service-issuer.signPayload` (import only — that file is protected partner-surface and is NOT edited) over `sha256(canonicalize(manifest \ signature))`; signature block carries `signer` (issuer pubkey, published at `/.well-known/clawville-issuer.json`) + `purpose:'avatar-manifest'`. **INVARIANT: pubkeys only — no secret ever appears.** Pure code in `@clawville/shared/avatar-manifest` (types + `canonicalize` byte-clone of the issuer's `canonicalJson` + zod schema) + `services/avatar-manifest-core.ts` (DB-free assemble/sign/verify) + `services/avatar-manifest-service.ts` (DB + body fetch). Body-URL mirror: `@clawville/shared` `AGENT_MODEL_BODY_PATHS` (paths-only, coverage-tested vs `AGENT_MODEL_KEYS`). Verifier CLI: `apps/api/scripts/avatar-manifest/verify-manifest.ts`. Human-only for now; agent-parity (resolve `X-Clawville-Agent-Session`) is a follow-up phase. See `.claude/plans/agent-export-portability.md`. |
| `agent-setup.ts` | `/api/agent/setup/*` | Multi-agent roster + loadout (`MAX_AGENTS=1`) |
| `agent-v2.ts` | `/api/v2/agent` | Experimental alt-shape gateway |
| `ct-topup.ts` | `/api/ct/topup/*` | **Tokenomics F2 — USDC->vCLAW on-ramp (Phase A, GATED/devnet, 2026-06-27).** `POST /quote` (x402 v2 402-challenge + a PENDING `ct_topups` row) · `POST /settle` (verify->settle via `services/x402-payai.ts` over `@x402/core`, then credit vCLAW EXACTLY ONCE). Store price **$1 = 100 vCLAW ($0.01/coin, `CT_PER_USDC=100` since the A3 ¢-peg redenomination 2026-07-07; was $0.10/coin at `CT_PER_USDC=10`)** — a ONE-WAY buy price, never a peg. Credits are tagged **BOUGHT** (non-cashable V-Bucks) via F1's `creditClawTokens({provenance:'bought', usdBasis})`, stamping `usd_basis` = the dollars paid; BOUGHT can never cash out (only `mintEarned` writes the cashable EARNED tag). DOUBLE-CREDIT guard: `ct_topups_txsig_unique` partial-unique + settle-UPDATE+credit in ONE tx + per-`topupId` `withKeyedMutex`. **PARITY (E5):** both routes through `requireAuthOrAgentSession` — a connected/hosted agent (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` -> bound avatar) tops up its OWN vCLAW for REAL credit; guests/unbound 401/403. **GATE:** 503 `on_ramp_unconfigured` until `CLAWVILLE_MERCHANT_WALLET_PUBKEY` is set; network defaults DEVNET; `X402_ENABLED` off; mainnet is a config flip after a funded settled smoke. The TEST-ONLY `services/x402-mock-facilitator.ts` mounts at `/api/x402-mock` ONLY when `X402_MOCK_FACILITATOR==='true'` and crashes the boot on a prod box (mock rubber-stamps => would mint free vCLAW). |
| `x402-checkout.ts` | `/api/x402/checkout/*` | **Tokenomics C — generic x402 USDC checkout (GATED/devnet, 2026-07-07).** ANY vCLAW-priced thing settles as a REAL USDC payment; vCLAW is the QUOTE unit only (¢-peg `usdCents === priceVclaw`, tripwired against `CT_PER_USDC` at module load) and the buyer's internal vCLAW is NEVER debited. `POST /quote` (kind-specific SERVER-SIDE pricing — cosmetic price from the SKU row, rent-prepay amount caller-chosen 1..1M like deposit-topup — then a PENDING `x402_checkouts` row + 402 challenge) · `POST /settle` (ct-topup machinery verbatim in `services/x402-checkout.ts`: per-checkout `withKeyedMutex` → fast idem replay → caller-bound row → server-side requirement re-derivation → `verifyAndSettle` → ONE tx { flip pending→settled claiming `tx_signature` under `x402_checkouts_txsig_unique` + the kind FULFILLER's writes } → 23505 ⇒ replay). **Fulfiller registry** (`registerFulfiller`/`getFulfiller` — the STABLE contract the marketplace stage imports; kinds `rent_payment`/`cosmetic_purchase`/`marketplace_purchase`/`tournament_entry`): unknown kind refuses at quote AND pre-facilitator at settle (never take USDC we can't fulfill); read-only preflights re-check preconditions pre-facilitator; an in-tx `CheckoutFulfillmentRefusal` rolls back and records a LOUD failed row claiming the signature (manual-refund trail). Shipped fulfillers: **cosmetic_purchase** (shared `grantSkinInTx`/`checkSkuPurchasable` from `routes/cosmetics.ts`, `acquiredVia:'shop_usdc'`, MINTS ZERO internal vCLAW — no buyer debit, no treasury-avatar credit; the treasury revenue is `enqueueClvBuy('checkout_cosmetic')` same-tx) + **rent_payment** (deposit-tenure escrow prepay: advisory(owner)→parcel `FOR UPDATE`, owner+tenure guards mirroring deposit-topup, `deposit_remaining_ct += priceVclaw` with NO avatar debit — the documented ESCROW-CONSERVATION EXTENSION, backed by `land_transactions.kind='land_deposit_prepay_usdc'` stamped with usd_basis; `decideDepositSweep` reused READ-ONLY for grace-clear; `enqueueClvBuy('checkout_rent_prepay')` same-tx) + **marketplace_purchase** (C4, 2026-07-07 — the P2P marketplace buyer leg; FLAG-GATED OFF via `MARKETPLACE_SETTLE_ENABLED`: quote resolver AND preflight AND the in-tx fulfiller all refuse `marketplace_settle_disabled` while unset, so no 402 is even issuable; when enabled it claims the listing active→pending_settlement→settled under `FOR UPDATE`, enqueues the FULL settled USDC (`enqueueClvBuy('marketplace_purchase')`) same-tx, and records the 444-bps rake + 9556-bps seller-payout INTENTS on `market_settlements` (`payout_status='pending_review'`); see the `market.ts` route row + §8). **PARITY (E5):** both routes `requireAuthOrAgentSession`+`requireNonGuestIdentity`; an agent settles for ITS OWN `identity.avatarId`; non-ledger agent sessions 403 `agent_not_ledger_capable` (cove convention, stricter than ct-topup). **GATE:** 503 until `CLAWVILLE_MERCHANT_WALLET_PUBKEY`; network shared with ct-topup (`X402_TOPUP_NETWORK`, devnet-first); no flag flips. Migration `0016_x402_checkouts.sql`. Tests: `services/__tests__/x402-checkout.test.ts` 22/22. |
| `market.ts` | `/api/market/*` | **P2P marketplace v1 (Tokenomics C4, GATED, 2026-07-07).** Sellers list what they own; buyers settle through the x402 checkout above (`itemKind:'marketplace_purchase'`, `itemRef`=listingId) — there is NO parallel buy route. `POST /listings` (Zod `.strict()`; **CLV Resident license**: hold ≥ `MARKET_SELLER_MIN_CLV` (default 50,000) CLV uiAmount via `services/linked-wallet-clv-balance` — human→linked self-custody wallet, agent→its custodial `avatars.wallet_address`; FAIL-SOFT `available:false` ⇒ REFUSE 503 `clv_balance_unavailable`, never fail-open; env retunes but never disables. `earned_bundle` ⇒ 409 `earned_not_available` (EARNED doesn't exist yet — v1 lists `land_deed` only); land_deed create runs ONE tx under the land lock order (advisory(seller) OUTER → parcel `FOR UPDATE` INNER) asserting ownership + transferable tenure (`owned` ONLY, narrowed 2026-07-08 Codex re-review; `hold` ⇒ 409 `hold_transfer_not_supported` — its OWN typed refusal, because the deed-flip normalizes tenure=`owned` + NULLs the hold cols, so a sold HOLD parcel would escape the CLV-hold obligation AND the tenure sweep; buyer-inherits-obligation is the designed follow-up, FEATURE_GATE `market_hold_deed_transfer` in `market-listings.ts`; `rented`/`deposit`/`starter` ⇒ 409 `not_transferable_tenure`), INSERTs the listing (status `active`, `escrow_state 'deed_locked'`, license wallet stamped as payout destination) + the `market_deed_locks` row — a lock conflict or the live-item partial-UNIQUE 23505 aborts the whole tx ⇒ 409 `parcel_already_listed`) · `GET /listings` (PUBLIC browse — active + unexpired, limit ≤100) · `GET /listings/mine` (auth; every status) · `POST /listings/:id/cancel` (seller-only, only from `active`; flips `cancelled` + NULLs `escrow_state` + DELETEs the deed lock in one tx; the listing `FOR UPDATE` serializes cancel against the settle fulfiller). **Service layer:** `services/market-listings.ts` (license + state machine) + `services/checkout-fulfillers/marketplace-purchase.ts` (fulfiller + `splitMarketplaceUsd` — the exact ¢-peg µUSD 4.44/95.56 split). **CODEX-GATED SEAMS (marked in-code):** on-chain CLV payout+rake execution (reads `pending_review` settlement rows) and the deed `owner_avatar_id` flip (land-domain review; the deed lock stays HELD through `settled` until it runs; land.ts deliberately untouched — land-side release/evict races are refused by the preflight + under-lock owner re-check instead). **PARITY (E5):** every write `requireAuthOrAgentSession`+`requireNonGuestIdentity`; non-ledger agent sessions 403. **LEDGER-ONLY:** no route/fulfiller touches `avatars.clawTokens`. Migration `0017_market_p2p.sql`. Tests: `services/__tests__/market.test.ts` 42/42. |

Commit-reveal RNG + session escrow over `slot_sessions`/`slot_spins`. Surfaces: `POST /session/open` (subject-resolved — debits `predict` ClawTokens for a ledger subject, returns `serverSeedHash` + `clientSeed`, NEVER reveals `serverSeed`), `POST /spin` (subject-resolved, `Idempotency-Key` header required, 60 spins/min/subject — atomic txn debits predict + inserts spin row + updates session counters + credits winnings, idempotency replay short-circuits BEFORE engine), `POST /session/close` (subject-resolved, ledger subjects only — reveals `serverSeed`, refunds remaining `escrowAmount`), `GET /session/current` + `/session/:id` + `/spins` (subject-resolved, ledger subjects only, owner-only, redacts `serverSeed` while `status='open'`), `GET /paytables/:id` (public, fed by `@clawville/shared` `CLASSIC_*` constants), `POST /verify` (public, pure compute — runs `runSpin` and returns serialized result). Events emitted: `cove.slots.session.opened/resumed`, `cove.slots.spin.executed`, `cove.slots.session.closed`. **Phase 6.7.0 (2026-05-27)** — `POST /spin` and `POST /session/close` now ALSO write to `cove_game_events` in the same transaction (history mirror + commit-reveal seed-reveal on close). Slots is the first game wired into the cross-game history store. **Agent parity (Rule E5, 2026-06-15):** `getSubject` is now async and resolves **Lucia human → agent session (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → `resolveAgentSession`) → guest**, matching `cove-blackjack.ts`. `user` + `agent` are both REAL-CT ledger subjects bound to the avatar's `userId` (`isLedgerSubject` collapses them so the audited debit/credit path is reused verbatim); a guest stays demo-CT-only; a non-ledger-capable or unbound agent gets 403, NEVER a guest demotion. Gameplay routes that were `requireAuth` (`/session/close`, `/session/current`, `/session/:id`, `/session/:id/spins`) are now subject-resolved so a connected agent can drive + restore + inspect its own session; ownership still binds via `ownerMatches(userId)`. No `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump (settlement resolver only; agent ACTION whitelist unchanged). |
| `cove-history.ts` | `/api/cove/history/*` | **Phase 6.7.0** (2026-05-27) — cross-game unified history + provable-fair verifier. Surfaces: `GET /api/cove/history?game=&outcome=&limit=&cursor=` (Lucia auth, owner-only, keyset-paginated default 50/max 200 via `base64url(${createdAt.toISOString()}|${id})` cursor; `outcome` filter is `'win'` (payout > betAmount, strict net-positive) or `'loss'` (payout <= betAmount, includes break-even/push); both compared as `::numeric` casts since the underlying columns are TEXT-stringified bigints; returns `{events, nextCursor}` where each event is `{id, userId, gameType, sessionId, shoeId, betAmount, payout, outcomeJson, serverSeedHash, revealedServerSeed, clientSeed, nonce, txSignature, engineVersion, createdAt}`) · `GET /api/cove/history/:eventId` (Lucia auth, owner OR `ADMIN_USER_IDS`, returns the single serialized event — used by the `/cove/verify/[eventId]` UI to load the row before dispatching to the per-game verifier component) · `GET /api/cove/history/:eventId/verify` (Lucia auth, owner OR `ADMIN_USER_IDS` for dispute support; slots: runs server-side `runSpin` replay against the revealed seed + sha256(seed)==hash check, returns `{verified, expected, stored, hashMatches}`; pre-reveal events: returns `{verified: null, reason: 'shoe-not-yet-closed', stored, hashMatches: null}`; blackjack/holdem/**baccarat** (6.6.1) branches all SHIPPED — each replays its engine against the revealed seed; **poker** (cash, 2026-06-20) branch verifies `sha256(revealedServerSeed)==serverSeedHash` AND that the stored per-subject outcome (`totalCommitted`/`won`/`net` + the row's `betAmount`/`payout`) is consistent with the AUTHORITATIVE `poker_cash_hands.pot_result_json` for the decoded `(handNumber, seatIndex)` — full multi-player deal/side-pot RE-DEAL is deferred (P1 persists only the settled outcome, not the per-hand action log), `reason: 'engine-replay-deferred; verified = seed-commit + authoritative-consistency'`; any remaining game type: `{verified: null, reason: 'engine-not-yet-shipped'}`). `GAME_TYPES` (read filter + verify dispatch) = `['slots','blackjack','holdem','baccarat','poker']`. Server verifier is the dispute fallback; client-side WebCrypto port stays the canonical surface per plan §0 #5. Slots `outcomeJson` shape: `{paytableId, reels, winningLines, winAmount, isFreeSpin, wildMultipliers, scatterPayout, cursorBefore, cursorAfter, predict, nonce, paytableVersion}` — the last five are engine-replay inputs embedded so the browser verifier can run without a second round-trip to `slot_spins`. |
| `cove-holdem.ts` | `/api/cove/holdem/*` | **Phase 6.5.1 No-Limit Texas Hold'em AUTHORITATIVE route** (2026-05-28) — replaces the 6.5.0 display-only `POST /play-mock-hand` shell. 6-max: 1 human/agent seat (seat 0) + 5 house BOTS. Commit-reveal provably-fair over `holdem_tables`/`holdem_hands`. Each hand shuffles a FRESH 52-card deck from `(serverSeed, clientSeed, nonce=handIndex, cursor=0)` (HMAC Fisher–Yates via `provable-rng`) — NO shared shoe, so a hand replays from `(seed, handIndex)` alone. Surfaces: `POST /session/open` (auth optional — buy in 20–500 CT, default 100; authed: debits buy-in from `avatars.clawTokens` into the table's `playerStack`; guest: 100 demo CT on the table row; commits `serverSeedHash`, NEVER reveals `serverSeed`), `POST /hand/deal` (auth optional — rotates button `handIndex % 6`, posts SB=1/BB=2, deals, runs bots forward to the first human decision or settles immediately if the human never acts), `POST /action` (auth optional — ONE human decision `fold|check|call|bet|raise` + total-street `amount`; server appends under the hand FOR UPDATE lock, re-validates legality via the engine, runs bots to the next human turn or showdown; idempotent re-POST on a settled hand replays), `POST /session/close` (Lucia auth — reveals `serverSeed` on every `cove_game_events` row for the session AND cashes out the remaining `playerStack` to the avatar), `GET /session/current` + `GET /session/:id` (Lucia auth, owner-only, `serverSeed` redacted while `status='open'`). MONEY MODEL (poker, distinct from blackjack's per-hand stake): chips move WITHIN `playerStack`; the ledger is touched ONLY at buy-in (open) and cash-out (close). Per hand: `newPlayerStack = startingStack + humanNet`. One `cove_game_events` row per hand (`gameType='holdem'`, `sessionId=shoeId=tableId`, `nonce=handIndex`, `betAmount=humanBet`, `payout=humanPayout`). Settle is idempotent under the table FOR UPDATE lock + `(tableId, idempotencyKey)` partial unique. Currency seam: `currency:'sol'\|'usdc'` returns 501 `CURRENCY_COMING_SOON`. Server FULLY authoritative — client sends only the human's action. Engine: `apps/api/src/services/holdem-engine.ts` (pure, deterministic, 42 unit tests; 7-card best-5 evaluator with wheel + kickers, full NLHE betting w/ min-raise + all-in + correct SIDE POTS + split-pot remainder-to-earliest-seat, 5 deterministic bot personalities TAG/LAG/tight-passive/calling-station/nit with HMAC-derived mixed-strategy rolls at a dedicated cursor region so the whole hand replays). Verifier: `cove-history.ts` `GET /:eventId/verify` `holdem` branch replays via `playHoldemHand(seed, handIndex, buttonSeat, startingStack, recorded actions)` + `sha256(seed)==hash`. Events: `cove.holdem.table.opened/closed`, `cove.holdem.hand.settled`. Migration: `scripts/casino/migrate-holdem-tables.mjs`. **Agent parity (Rule E5, 2026-06-15):** `getSubject` resolves a connected/hosted agent session (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → `resolveAgentSession`) to its bound avatar on the buy-in/cash-out ledger path, matching `cove-blackjack.ts`; guests stay demo; non-ledger/unbound agents get 403. No `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump (settlement resolver only). |
| `cove-baccarat.ts` | `/api/cove/baccarat/*` | **Phase 6.6.1 Baccarat (Punto Banco) AUTHORITATIVE route** (2026-05-28) — the FIRST playable baccarat backend (no prior route/modal; only a cove sign placeholder existed). 8-deck shared commit-reveal shoe over `baccarat_shoes`/`baccarat_coups`. Punto Banco has NO player decisions, so each coup is dealt + resolved + settled atomically in ONE `db.transaction` under the shoe FOR UPDATE lock — there is no in-progress window. The engine (`apps/api/src/services/baccarat-engine.ts`) re-derives every card from `(serverSeed, clientSeed, nonce=coupIndex, cursor)` (no-replacement draws from the HMAC stream); for `coupIndex > 0` the route reconstructs the remaining-shoe state by replaying prior settled coups (`reconstructShoeState`, O(prior coups)) and asserts it matches the live shoe counters. Surfaces: `POST /session/open` (auth optional — authed snapshots `avatars.clawTokens`; guest 100 demo CT on the shoe row; commits `serverSeedHash`, NEVER reveals `serverSeed`; resumes an existing open shoe idempotently), `POST /coup` (auth optional — `{shoeId, bet:'player'\|'banker'\|'tie', stake:5–500}`; 75% penetration ⇒ 409 `reshuffled` so the client opens a fresh shoe; debits stake + credits gross payout via `claw-token-ledger` in the same tx; idempotent via `Idempotency-Key` + `(shoeId, idempotencyKey)` partial unique — a reused key replays the stored coup, never deals twice or double-credits; payout > Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER → 500), `POST /session/close` (Lucia auth — reveals `serverSeed` on every `cove_game_events` row for the shoe), `GET /session/current` + `GET /session/:id` (Lucia auth, owner-only, `serverSeed` redacted while `status='open'`). PAYOUTS (integer, house-friendly floor): PLAYER 1:1; BANKER 0.95:1 w/ floored 5% commission; TIE 8:1; P/B PUSH on a tie. MONEY MODEL: stake + gross payout move per coup (a push returns the stake, net 0); guest demo balance = `startingBalance + totalPayout − totalBet` on the shoe row (no ledger). One `cove_game_events` row per coup (`gameType='baccarat'`, `sessionId=shoeId=shoeId`, `nonce=coupIndex`, `betAmount=stake`, `payout=gross`). Currency seam: `currency:'sol'\|'usdc'` → 501 `CURRENCY_COMING_SOON`. Server FULLY authoritative — client sends only its bet + stake. Verifier: `cove-history.ts` `GET /:eventId/verify` `baccarat` branch replays via `replayShoeUpToCoup(seed, targetNonce, recorded per-coup bet/stake)` + `sha256(seed)==hash`. Events: `cove.baccarat.shoe.opened/closed`, `cove.baccarat.coup.settled`. Migration: `scripts/casino/migrate-baccarat-tables.mjs`. Rate limits: 120 coups/min/subject; 10 guest shoes/hour (best-effort, per-process). **Agent parity (Rule E5, 2026-06-15):** `getSubject` resolves a connected/hosted agent session (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → `resolveAgentSession`) to its bound avatar on the coup stake/payout ledger path, matching `cove-blackjack.ts`; guests stay demo; non-ledger/unbound agents get 403. No `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump (settlement resolver only). |

Real-CT buy-in / prize via `claw-token-ledger`. Full lobby UI is a later phase; this is the backend seam. |
| `cove-cash-poker.ts` | `/api/cove/poker/cash/*` | **Poker CASH (ring) games (P1, 2026-06-20)** — classic fixed-blind ring tables; **chips == CT 1:1** (a SEPARATE product from MTT's play-money chips). Tables: `poker_cash_tables`/`_seats`/`_hands`/`_ledger_events` (migration `migrations-manual/2026-06-20_poker_cash_tables.sql`, idempotent `CREATE … IF NOT EXISTS` — apply by hand, NOT `db:push`). Service: `services/poker/cash-table-manager.ts` (`CashTableManager` over the shared `PokerTableSim` via the dedicated `cashTableSim` singleton). Surfaces: `GET /tables` (PUBLIC — open PUBLIC tables only, never private), `POST /tables` (user OR agent — create a fixed-tier `low/mid/high` public table or a private custom-stakes table; per-creator concurrent-open cap 3, per-IP 10/min), `POST /tables/join-by-code` (user OR agent — private tables are JOIN-CODE-gated; a direct `/sit` to a private UUID is 403), `POST /tables/:id/sit` (user OR agent — DEBIT the buy-in → chips, idempotent re-sit returns the existing seat), `POST /tables/:id/leave` (user OR agent — CREDIT the seat's current stack; between hands → immediate (200), mid-hand → queued `sitting_out` + cashed at the next boundary (202)), `POST /tables/:id/action` (user OR agent — ONE betting action `fold/check/call/bet/raise`), `GET /tables/:id/state-for-agent` (own seat view + own hole cards, NO leak), `GET /tables/:id` (PUBLIC table state — config + seats + live snapshot, no hole cards, never the join code). **MONEY MODEL (P1):** the ledger crosses on exactly two flows — SIT/REBUY `debitClawTokens('poker_cash_buy_in')` subject→`table_escrow_ct`, LEAVE `creditClawTokens('poker_cash_cash_out')` escrow→subject. **RAKE = 0** (`rake_bps`/`rake_cap_ct`/`rake_taken_ct` columns reserved). Per-hand settle moves chips BETWEEN seats only — escrow UNCHANGED. **CONSERVATION:** at rest `table_escrow_ct == Σ seat.current_stack_ct`; across the lifecycle `Σ buy_in == Σ cash_out + escrow + rake`. **ATOMICITY (2026-06-20):** `seatSubject`, `cashOutSeat`, and `settleHand` each run inside ONE `db.transaction` with the parent table/seat row `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` first — the ledger debit/credit composes INTO the same tx (`debit/creditClawTokens(input, tx)`), so a crash mid-sequence rolls EVERYTHING back (no CT debited with no seat; cash-out re-checks the seat status under the lock so a retry replays instead of double-paying). The hand `settled_at` is the idempotency anchor, re-read under the lock. **Seeded agents (P1 stub):** empty seats may be filled by a seeded agent (`subject_type='agent'`, `is_seeded='true'`, trivial check/call/fold stub) so a single human can play; seeded chips are REAL-CT-backed by a house-bank debit (`poker_cash_house_seed`, reclaimed `poker_cash_house_reclaim`) — a seeded provider WITHOUT a `houseBankAvatarProvider` THROWS `seeded_agent_requires_house_bank` (explicit anti-faucet). Both providers are UNSET in the P1 singleton (no agent fill on prod yet). **HISTORY:** the settle writes one `cove_game_events` row per REAL (non-seeded) subject per hand (`gameType='poker'`, `sessionId=shoeId=tableId`, `betAmount=totalCommitted`, `payout=won`, `engineVersion='poker-cash-v1'`). To avoid the `(game_type, session_id, nonce)` unique-index collision across a hand's multiple seats, the seat is PACKED into the nonce: `nonce = handNumber*100 + seatIndex` (`SEAT_NONCE_STRIDE=100`; verify recovers `handNumber=floor(nonce/100)`, `seatIndex=nonce%100`). **Agent parity (Rule E5):** built in from the start — `resolveSubject` (mirrors `cove-poker-mtt`) resolves Lucia human OR `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar; settlement binds to that avatarId. NO guest tier (a CT ring table has no demo mode; unauth economy write = 401). NOT YET wired: the agent ACTION/tool surface (agent-gateway tools / `[ACTION:]` whitelist / skill-protocol) — REST parity is complete but in-world agent-action discovery + `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump are a later phase (no PROTOCOL bump in this diff — settlement resolver + history only). |

Wraps the deployed `clawville_wager` Anchor program (`HgQhHVYV2C5Mw8K81kEnADkqsuS5YQRmGJDUR5wnZVuG` on devnet, config PDA `AbvtPhFtbQNQ9oT8vQumPEWDowRXibtPeLpmDvTz5i2a`, rake_bps=500). Surfaces: `POST /lobbies` (create — Lucia auth), `GET /lobbies` (list — public, filters: activityId, roomId, state, mine), `GET /lobbies/:idOrInviteCode` (single + players), `POST /lobbies/:id/join` (deposit + join, Lucia auth), `POST /lobbies/:id/lock` (admin/match-server), `POST /lobbies/:id/settle` (admin/match-server — body `{winnerAvatarId}`), `POST /lobbies/:id/cancel` (creator if `state='open'`; admin if in `('open','locked')`), `POST /lobbies/:id/refund` (per-player after cancel). Modes: `multiplayer` (real on-chain escrow), `solo-bots` (no escrow). Visibility: `public` / `private` / `friends` (latter two require `invite_code`). Rake snapshotted at create-time. FEATURE_GATE: `wager-spl-lobbies` (SPL routes refuse), `wager-mainnet-paid` (devnet-only RPC). |
| `land.ts` | `/api/land/*` | **Land Economy — Phase 1 (Slice A free claim + Slice B priced buy / structures / upgrades)** (Slice A 2026-06-16; Slice B 2026-06-17). User-facing surface over the Phase 0 `land_parcels`/`land_structures`/`land_transactions` schema. **Slice A (free claim + reads):** `GET /parcels?tier=&status=` (PUBLIC, 60/min/IP, 60s in-memory cache; Zod-validated `tier` ∈ 5 land tiers + `status` ∈ 4 statuses, both optional, default `status='available'`; one indexed query on `land_parcels_tier_status_idx`; returns a flat `LandParcelDTO[]`), `GET /owned/:avatarId` (PUBLIC, 60/min/IP, cached; the multiplayer render seam — ⚠ **SHAPE CHANGE (Slice B, 2026-06-17):** was a flat `LandParcelDTO[]`, now an OBJECT `{parcels: LandParcelDTO[], structures: LandStructureDTO[]}` so the render seam gets parcels + their structures in one call — callers must read `.parcels`), `GET /me` (auth: `sessionMiddleware` + `requireAuthOrAgentSession` → `identity.avatarId`; ⚠ **now `{avatarId, parcels: LandParcelDTO[], structures: LandStructureDTO[]}`** — added `structures`; cache-bypassed so a just-completed buy/place/upgrade in the same session is reflected immediately), `POST /claim-starter` (auth, PARITY-BOUND, idempotent atomic `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` grant + `land_transactions(parcel_purchase, amount_ct=0)` audit row, emits `land.parcel.purchased` weight-5 event; the 5-cap `MAX_PARCELS_PER_AVATAR` is NOT applied to the free starter — one-per-avatar via the ownership idempotency check). **Slice B (priced sale + structures, all PARITY-BOUND via `requireAuthOrAgentSession`, all CT settled through `claw-token-ledger.debitClawTokens` — never a raw `avatars.clawTokens` write):** `GET /parcels/:parcelId/structure` (PUBLIC → `{structure: LandStructureDTO \| null}`), `GET /catalog?tier=` (PUBLIC → with tier `{tier, maxLevel, premium, homeSkus:[{key,label}], shopSkus:[{key,label}], upgradeCosts:number[]}`; no tier → `{tiers: Record<LandTier,{maxLevel,premium,homeSkus,shopSkus}>, upgradeCosts}` — tier-gated catalog sourced from `@clawville/shared TIER_STRUCTURE_RULES`), `POST /parcels/:parcelId/buy` (priced primary sale → `{parcel: LandParcelDTO, amountCt}`; `amountCt` = the SERVER-read `land_parcels.price_ct` debited, NO client price reaches the debit; single-charge safety = status flip `available`→`owned` under the per-avatar advisory lock + `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`, a replay sees `owned`→409; cap `MAX_PARCELS_PER_AVATAR=5` counted under the lock; Founder tier is auction/USDC-only → 501 `founder_not_in_v1`; emits `land.parcel.purchased` weight-5), `POST /parcels/:parcelId/rent` (weekly-rent acquire, builder-economics 2026-06-24, surfaced in the UI 2026-06-27 → `{parcel: LandParcelDTO, amountCt, rentPaidThrough}`; charges the FIRST week immediately from the SERVER-stamped `land_parcels.rent_ct_weekly` — empty body, NO client price; rentable = c-tier only (starter/founder carry `rent_ct_weekly` NULL → 400 `rent_not_available`; founder also → 501); flips `available`→`owned` with `tenure='rented'` + `rent_paid_through = now()+RENT_PERIOD_DAYS` under the same per-avatar advisory lock + `FOR UPDATE` as `/buy`; same `MAX_PARCELS_PER_AVATAR` cap; `land-rent-sweeper` charges each subsequent week, applies the grace window on a failed charge, evicts after grace; emits `land.parcel.purchased` weight-5 once on acquire — weekly renewals emit NO leaderboard event), `POST /parcels/:parcelId/structure` (free Lv1 placement → `{structure: LandStructureDTO}`; body `{structureType:'home'\|'shop', catalogKey}`; validates `catalogKey ∈ STRUCTURE_CATALOG` for the type AND `isSkuAllowedForTier` for the parcel's tier; one structure per parcel UNIQUE → 409 `structure_exists`; emits `land.structure.placed` weight-3), `POST /structures/:structureId/upgrade` (priced level-up → `{structure: LandStructureDTO, costCt, idempotencyReplay?}`; body `{idempotencyKey:1..64}` **REQUIRED** — Codex BLOCK HIGH: a keyless retry would be charged AGAIN as a fresh Lv+1 upgrade (paid double-charge), so an absent/empty key → 400 `idempotency_key_required` and an unparseable body → 400 `invalid_body`, both BEFORE any lock/debit (no `.catch(()=>({}))` into a keyless path); `costCt`=SERVER-derived `STRUCTURE_UPGRADE_COSTS[level+1]`; tier gate `target > getTierMaxLevel(parcel.tier)`→409 `tier_max_level`, global ceiling `target > MAX_STRUCTURE_LEVEL`(5)→409 `max_level_reached`, `idempotencyKey` already spent on a DIFFERENT structure→409 `idempotency_key_conflict` (the `land_upgrades.idempotency_key` index is GLOBAL, not per-structure, so a client must use a FRESH key per upgrade — ownership is asserted BEFORE any idempotency replay so a key can't read/replay another avatar's structure, audit-1 security fix); ownership is checked against the AUTHORITATIVE `land_parcels.owner_avatar_id` with BOTH the structure and its parent parcel row-locked (`FOR UPDATE OF s, p`) so a stale denorm `land_structures.owner_avatar_id` can't pay to upgrade a parcel it no longer owns (drift→409 `ownership_desync`) — Codex BLOCK MED; emits `land.structure.upgraded` weight-5). `LandParcelDTO = {id, parcelCode, tier, status, gridX, gridY, priceCt, ownerAvatarId, rentCtWeekly, tenure}` (`rentCtWeekly: number\|null` = server-stamped `land_parcels.rent_ct_weekly`, non-null only on rentable c-tier rows; `tenure: 'rented'\|'owned'\|'starter'\|null` = HOW the parcel is held, null when available — both added 2026-06-27 so the Land Office UI can gate its Rent action + show the weekly cost; additive/read-only, surfaced on EVERY parcel-DTO site: `GET /parcels`, `GET /owned/:avatarId`, `GET /me`, the `/buy` + `/rent` + `/claim-starter` response parcels); `LandStructureDTO = {id, parcelId, ownerAvatarId, structureType, catalogKey, level}`. **Tier ladder (each tier a SUPERSET of the one below):** starter maxLevel 2 (homes shack/cottage, shops stall/shopfront), c maxLevel 3 (+house/+market), b maxLevel 4 (+villa/+emporium), a maxLevel 5 (+mansion/+grand-bazaar), founder maxLevel 5 + premium (ALL SKUs incl founder-only `home-founders-estate`/`shop-founders-exchange`). **Agent parity (Rule E5):** every write binds to `identity.avatarId` from `requireAuthOrAgentSession` — a REAL avatar for BOTH a Lucia human AND a connected/hosted agent (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar); no guest fallback, no client value reaches any debit. **Leaderboard:** the 3 land events are scored in `leaderboard.ts` (see §5b). PHASE 3: expose buy/structure/upgrade on the agent `tools.json` + `[ACTION:]` whitelist (settlement already parity-bound; Phase 3 is discovery-surface only — do not touch the partner/hatcher surface or `skill-protocol.ts` in that diff). **Slice 4 — Run-a-store / land services (P3, 2026-07-05, BUILT — pending staging e2e + founder sign-off, not yet claimed live):** 6 new routes over `service_listings`/`service_purchases` (see §8). `POST /structures/:structureId/services` (auth, PARITY-BOUND — list a peer CT service on an OWNED/RENTED, ACTIVE `'shop'` structure; body `{title:1..80, description?:0..500, priceCt:int 0..1_000_000}` `.strict()`; ownership checked against the AUTHORITATIVE `land_parcels.owner_avatar_id`, same discipline as `/upgrade`; 400 `not_a_shop` (structureType≠'shop'); 403 `not_structure_owner`; 404 `structure_not_found`; 409 `structure_archived` \| `ownership_desync` \| `listing_cap_reached` (cap **6** simultaneously-active listings per structure, `MAX_ACTIVE_LISTINGS_PER_STRUCTURE`, COUNT under the per-avatar advisory lock) → `{listing: ServiceListingDTO}`). `PATCH /services/:listingId` (auth, PARITY-BOUND, own-listing-only — update/deactivate; body `{title?, description?, priceCt?, status?:'active'\|'paused'\|'delisted'}` `.strict()` + refine ≥1 field; 403 `not_listing_owner`; 404 `listing_not_found` → `{listing: ServiceListingDTO}`). `GET /structures/:structureId/services` (PUBLIC, 60s cache, 60/min/IP — active listings for one structure → `{listings: ServiceListingDTO[]}`). `GET /services?page=&limit=` (PUBLIC, cached, rate-limited, paged — ALL active listings newest-first, `limit` clamped 1..50 default 20 → `{listings, nextPage?}`). `GET /services/mine` (auth, PARITY-BOUND, owner-scoped, UNCACHED — the caller's OWN listings across ALL statuses incl paused/delisted, so the store-manage UI can re-activate a paused listing the public browse can't show; owner-scoped by `identity.avatarId`, newest-first, no leak → `{listings: ServiceListingDTO[]}`; distinct literal path from `GET /services`, no route shadow). `POST /services/:listingId/buy` (auth, PARITY-BOUND, atomic — body `{idempotencyKey: string 8..64}` **REQUIRED** (Codex money-safety pattern, mirrors `/upgrade`'s keyless-replay guard); price is ALWAYS server-read from the locked listing row, never the body; **MONEY MODEL: full CT transfer buyer→seller, NO rake in v1** (`platformFeeBps` reserved at 0) — composed via `debitClawTokens(buyer)` + `creditClawTokens(seller, provenance:'soft')` in ONE tx with the `service_purchases` insert (conservation: buyer −P, seller +P, sum unchanged); the idempotency-key lookup runs BEFORE any ledger touch so a same-key retry replays the cached purchase (`cached:true`, no double charge, no second event) and a key already spent on a DIFFERENT listing/buyer → 409 `idempotency_key_conflict`; self-purchase asserted before any debit → 409 `self_purchase`; low balance → 400 `insufficient_clawtokens`; inactive listing → 409 `listing_not_active`; unknown → 404 `listing_not_found`; **adversarial-audit hardening (2026-07-05, all BEFORE any ledger touch):** the listing's shop is RE-READ UNDER LOCK (`SELECT s.status, p.owner_avatar_id … FOR UPDATE OF s, p`, lock order buyer-advisory→listing→structure→parcel, s-before-p — no new deadlock edge) and a defunct/transferred shop — structure archived by a rent-lapse eviction (the sweeper does NOT cascade-delist its `service_listings`) OR the parcel changed owner — → 409 `structure_unavailable`; a non-CT (USDC `partner`) listing can never settle in CT → 409 `not_a_peer_listing`; and because this is the first land route to credit a SECOND avatar in-tx, a mutual-buy deadlock (Postgres 40P01 — A-buys-B while B-buys-A inverts the ledger-row lock order) is fully rolled back by Postgres and mapped to a retryable → 409 `concurrent_retry` (client retries with the SAME idempotencyKey) → `{purchase: ServicePurchaseDTO, priceCt, cached}`). Emits `land.service.sold` (weight 40, cap 50/day — see §5a) EXACTLY ONCE per fresh (non-replayed) purchase, keyed to the **SELLER** (avatarId/agentId/userId all resolved to the listing owner, not the buyer — a run-a-store sale credits the seller's leaderboard contribution). **Agent parity (Rule E5):** inherited verbatim — every write resolves `identity.avatarId` from the SAME `requireAuthOrAgentSession` every other land route uses; no new resolver, no guest fallback. **UI:** `apps/web/src/components/game/land/land-office-modal.tsx` new "🛍️ Services" tab — Browse (paged, public) + Manage (owned shops only: list/edit/pause/delist, cap counter); query-key namespace `['landServices', 'browse'\|'structure', ...]`. **Tests:** `apps/api/src/routes/__tests__/land-services.test.ts` (zod-schema mirrors + routing-integrity, deterministic; money-path conservation/idempotency/cap/ownership tests DB-backed via `describeIfDb()`, same pattern as `cove-blackjack.test.ts`). No `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump (no agent `[ACTION:]`/tools.json surface added this slice — same deferred Phase-3-discovery note as buy/structure/upgrade above). |

Admin-only `POST /:id/admin-fail-refund` force-refunds an opened USDC escrow to the depositor. |

Emits `skill_md.fetched`; partner-key fetches tag `via:'partner-import'` (leaderboard-excluded). |

| `sap.ts` | `/api/sap/*` | **SAP (Synapse Agent Protocol) — on-chain agent identity/reputation/tool/discovery (Phase 1) + SOL/USDC escrow money rail (Phase 2)**, GATED OFF + devnet + dry-run by default (`SAP_ENABLED`/`SAP_ESCROW_ENABLED`/`SAP_USDC_ESCROW_ENABLED` all default false, `SAP_DRY_RUN=true`; mainnet is a CODE gate `SAP_ALLOW_MAINNET` in `sap-config.ts`, not env). Reads: `GET /status`, `/agents`, `/agent/:pubkey` (public, 60/min/IP). Writes (`requireAuthOrAgentSession` + `requireLedgerCapable`, 20/min/IP): identity `/register`, `/tools/publish`, `/feedback`, `/attestation`(+`/revoke`); SOL escrow `/escrow/{stake,deposit-stake,create,deposit,settle,withdraw,close}`; **Option C USDC escrow gate** `/escrow/usdc/{open,submit,approve,settle,refund}` — verify-before-release: depositor funds → worker submits → DEPOSITOR-ONLY persisted approval → server-built verification → worker settles (release vault→worker ATA) ONLY on a passing verdict, clamped server-side to the approved/funded ceiling; refund reclaims unspent USDC. **Rule E5 parity:** every write binds the acting party to `identity.avatarId`'s OWN Phase-5.1 custodial wallet (depositor on open/approve/refund, worker on settle) — human cookie OR agent session both reach the same real path; no body-supplied signer; no guest. The escrow USDC release goes straight to the worker agent's own ATA (NO fee, NO CT, NO vCLAW conversion). |

## 3. Middleware (`apps/api/src/middleware/`)

| File | Applied | What it does |
|---|---|---|
| `auth.ts` | Global `sessionMiddleware` on `app.ts` · per-route `requireAuth` | Resolves Lucia cookie → `c.get('user') / c.get('session')`. `requireAuth` throws HTTPException(401) when no session. **Cookie domain** (2026-05-22): both Hono (`apps/api/src/lib/auth.ts`) and Next.js (`apps/web/src/lib/auth.ts`) set `domain: '.clawville.world'` in production via `resolveSessionCookieDomain()`; override with `SESSION_COOKIE_DOMAIN` env var for staging. Dev keeps host-only. This eliminates the split-brain that 401'd `clawville.world/api/auth/me` for sessions minted by `api.clawville.world`. Recovery playbook in `docs/auth-security-recovery.md`. |
| `rate-limit.ts` | `/connect` (10/min/IP) · `/export-character` (CF-aware IP via `cf-connecting-ip`) · `/api/auth/guest` (5/min/IP) · `/api/leaderboard/agents` (60/min/IP) · `/api/leaderboard/reef-race/daily-best-lap` (60/min/IP — separate limiter) | `createRateLimiter` + `getClientIp` helpers. **IP trust order (FIX-18, 2026-06-13):** `getClientIp` no longer trusts `x-real-ip`; order is now `cf-connecting-ip` → last `x-forwarded-for` entry → `'unknown'`. `x-real-ip` was client-spoofable on any non-Cloudflare path (Traefik doesn't overwrite it), letting a caller rotate the rate-limit key to defeat the partner register/stats per-IP caps; `cf-connecting-ip` is always present on the CF-fronted prod/staging path, so the change is a no-op on prod and closes the non-CF gap. NOTE: `auth.ts` has a SEPARATE independent `x-real-ip` read (not via `getClientIp`) — flagged for a follow-up review if direct-to-Traefik exposure is possible there. |
| `admin-only.ts` | `/api/dashboard/*` | Reads `ADMIN_USER_IDS` env at module load. 401 if no user, 403 if not on allowlist. Must run AFTER `sessionMiddleware`. |
| `fingerprint.ts` | Every request | Populates `fp_hash` + `ip_prefix_hash` from sha256 of `FINGERPRINT_SECRET || raw_browser_fingerprint`. Module load throws if `FINGERPRINT_SECRET` is missing or <32 chars — crashes API boot rather than silently emitting unsalted hashes. |
| `partner-key.ts` | `requirePartnerKey(scope)` on partner skill reads (`/api/skills/manifest.json`, `/protocol/skill.md`, `/:buildingId/skill.md`) · `partnerRateLimit(opts)` (per-partner bucket) | `requirePartnerKey` validates `Authorization: Bearer` → `sha256` → `partner_api_keys` row lookup (revoked/scope-missing/missing → opaque 401, fails closed on DB error); sets `partnerId`+`partnerScopes` on context + non-blocking `last_used_at` bump. `partnerRateLimit` keys the bucket on the validated `partnerId` (not IP — a partner egresses all agents from one IP). Pod-local; Redis swap flagged for multi-pod. |
| `require-covenant-partner.ts` | `requireCovenantPartner` on `/api/partner/covenant/*` (READ-only Covenant verification surface) | Two-layer fail-closed gate for the Covenant read routes. **(1) ed25519 partner sig (primary):** consumes the EXISTING `verifyPartnerGetSignature('covenant', …)` (`services/partner-signature.ts`) — same GET wire scheme as Hatcher (`X-Covenant-Issuer-Pubkey`/`-Signature`/`-Timestamp`, ±5 min, pubkey matched to `PARTNER_PUBKEYS.covenant`). **(2) IP allowlist (defense-in-depth):** env `COVENANT_ALLOWED_IPS` (comma-separated exact IPs) matched against the CF-authoritative `getClientIp` (reused from `rate-limit.ts`). Order: per-IP 60/min limiter → config (missing pubkey OR empty allowlist ⇒ 503 `partner_not_configured`) → IP (403 generic) → sig (401 generic). Never echoes the presented pubkey/sig. Exports pure helpers (`parseCovenantAllowedIps`, `isCovenantConfigured`, `covenantConfigStatus`) so the config/parse logic is unit-tested. Does NOT extend the staging-only `ALLOW_TEST_PARTNER_PUBKEY` (hatcher-only). |

## 4. Service layer (`apps/api/src/services/`)

| Service | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `agent-collaboration` | Agent ↔ agent consultation helper. Emits `agent.collaboration.turn` per consulted expert. |
| `agent-orchestrator` | Lazy-starts ElizaOS runtimes on first chat, auto-stops after 30 min idle. System agents (singletons) are exempt from the sweep. |
| `alert-error` | Itachi-debug Telegram bot. 1/60s collapse per `${source}::${message}` with suppressed-count suffix. Required env: `ITACHI_DEBUG_BOT_TOKEN`, `ITACHI_DEBUG_CHAT_ID`. Degrades to `console.warn` when creds missing. |
| `article-scraper` | Pulls + normalizes external articles into `research_articles`. |
| `auth-challenge` | Phase 5.1 in-memory nonce store for signed-challenge reconnect. 60s TTL, single-use, 10k-entry spam cap. Migrate to Redis when multi-pod. |
| `claw-token-ledger` | Canonical write path for `claw_token_transactions`. `transferClawTokens()` does the atomic 2-avatar transfer and emits `tokens.settled` on success. **Never bypass — never write `avatars.clawTokens` directly.** |
| `house-treasury-seeder` | **Tokenomics T0 (2026-07-07)** — idempotent boot provision of the named HOUSE-TREASURY fee-sink subject: system user `house-treasury@house.clawville.internal` + a 0-CT avatar (`clawTokens:0`+`softBalance:0` mirrored for the vclaw sum CHECK, `isActive:false`) + the `treasury_subjects` registry row (`purpose='house-fees'`, ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING + re-find; the registry row is authoritative). **NO bankroll mint** — pure revenue SINK (contrast cash-house-seeder’s one-time bankroll); it only accumulates the T0 `house_fee_*` credits and NEVER pays players. Exposes `houseTreasuryAvatarId()` (sync, throws pre-ensure) + `getHouseTreasuryAvatarId()` (async fee-site resolver: cached fast path; lazily re-runs `ensure()` if the boot seed failed; returns null — never throws — when unavailable so a fee degrades to the pre-T0 burn instead of aborting a player settlement). Boot-wired in `index.ts` beside the cash-house block, non-fatal. |
| `clv-price-oracle` | **Tokenomics T0 (2026-07-07)** — READ-ONLY on-chain CLV price feed. Polls the ClawVille token's USD price (mint `Epht7Fw4…ELiZA`, Token-2022) every ~60s (env `CLV_ORACLE_POLL_MS`, default 60000/floor 15000): Helius DAS `getAsset` price primary (env `HELIUS_API_KEY`; local-dev fallback key only when unset), keyless DexScreener fallback (highest-liquidity `pairs[].priceUsd`). Writes one `clv_price_snapshots` row per SUCCESSFUL fetch + keeps an in-memory spot + 30-min TWAP cache (seeded from the DB on boot). `getClvPrice()` (sync) → house-favorable `min(spot, twap30m)`, REFUSING (quoteUsd null, `available:false`) any snapshot > `CLV_ORACLE_MAX_STALE_MS` (default 10 min); degrade Helius→DexScreener→last-known(`stale:true`). `getRecentSnapshots(N)` backs the read route's `history`. `startClvPriceOracle()` boot-wired in `index.ts` (non-fatal), `stopClvPriceOracle()` in shutdown. **Never touches `avatars.clawTokens` or the ledger** — all values are USD decimals. |
| `clv-swap-executor` | **Tokenomics C3 (2026-07-07; GoLive upsert 2026-07-07)** — the shared CLV buy-queue seam: `enqueueClvBuy()` (idempotent UPSERT on the `(reason, source_ref)` partial UNIQUE — a settle replay returns the EXISTING queueId, never a throw; HARD $10k max-notional cap mirroring `CHECKOUT_MAX_PRICE_VCLAW`), pure `planClips()` impact math, the DRY-RUN worker (the ONLY boot-wired behavior), and the `CLV_SWAP_EXECUTE` module-load HARD GATE. |
| `clv-swap-custody` | **Tokenomics GoLive executors (2026-07-07)** — SHARED treasury-keypair custody for the on-chain economy executors. PINNED exports (payout/deed executors import these exactly): `loadClvSwapKeypair(): Promise<Keypair>` (purpose=`clv-swap`, decrypt -> pubkey-match vs the row column AND `getClvSwapWalletPubkey()`), `loadX402MerchantKeypair(): Promise<Keypair>` (purpose=`x402-merchant`, match vs the env-pinned `CLAWVILLE_MERCHANT_WALLET_PUBKEY`; no pin = REFUSE), `getClvMainnetConnection(): Connection` (Helius mainnet / public mainnet-beta fallback — never devnet). Mismatch = refuse-to-sign; key bytes never logged/persisted; memoized (rotation = restart). |
| `clv-swap-live` | **Tokenomics GoLive executors (2026-07-07; Codex re-review fixes 2026-07-08) — DARK, structurally unreachable** (`CLV_SWAP_EXECUTE` module-load throw + default-OFF per-entrypoint gate + mainnet/real-facilitator network guard). **Boot wiring in index.ts is CONDITIONAL on `CLV_SWAP_EXECUTE='true'`** (2026-07-08): flag unset ⇒ the dry-run worker; `'true'` ⇒ `startClvSwapLiveWorker()` — a DEAD branch while the module-load throw stands, so the go-live diff is removing ONLY that throw (the old "one-line un-gate onto unchanged boot wiring" claim was FALSE — it would have crash-looped the API on the dry-run worker's gate). The live C3 path: funding sweep (merchant USDC -> swap wallet, `clv_swap_funding` exactly-once trail, capture-before-send, ambiguous = reconcile-never-retry), atomic claim `planned->executing` before any decrypt/sign/send, per-clip oracle re-fetch + hard-stop, Jupiter USDC->CLV clips with the ORACLE-derived min-out floor, BigInt conservation. Hardenings (2026-07-08): `CLV_SWAP_JUPITER_BASE_URL` is HOST-ALLOWLISTED (`lite-api.jup.ag` default + `api.jup.ag` only; anything else warns + falls back — hatcher-config allowlist pattern) and the live tick pages ops (`alertError` warning) on any `clv_buy_queue` row stuck `'executing'` past `CLV_SWAP_EXECUTING_STALE_MS` (default 300000, floor 180000) — ALERT-ONLY, never auto-resumed. All I/O behind injectable deps (unit-tested without chain/DB). FEATURE_GATE `clv_swap_live_execution` in-file. |
| `market-deed-transfer-executor` | **Tokenomics GoLive executors (2026-07-07) — DARK, default-OFF `MARKET_DEED_TRANSFER_ENABLED`; NOT wired into index.ts.** Pure-DB (no custody/chain/ledger): flips `land_parcels.owner_avatar_id` seller->buyer for SETTLED `land_deed` settlements (`deed_transferred_at IS NULL`). ONE tx per settlement: claim (settlement `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` + pending predicates; `deed_transfer_claim_id` = audit) -> land lock order (advisory(seller) OUTER, parcel `FOR UPDATE` INNER) -> under-lock re-verify (seller still owns; live deposit-escrow refused — conservation) -> fresh-owned flip (tenure `'owned'`, deposit/hold/grace cols reset, `rent_ct_weekly` kept; FLAGGED for land review) -> denormalized `land_structures.owner_avatar_id` -> buyer as-is (FLAGGED for land review) -> stamp `deed_transferred_at` -> DELETE the `market_deed_locks` row. Conflict = TERMINAL `deed_transfer_failure_reason` (never a forced flip; deed lock stays HELD for ops). Crash = full rollback, resumable. Writes land via its OWN SQL — `routes/land.ts`/`land-rent-sweeper.ts` untouched; land's release/evict paths still don't consult the market lock (land-domain seam) — the under-lock re-verify is the interim guard. FEATURE_GATE `market_deed_transfer_executor` in-file. |
| `market-payout-executor` | **Tokenomics GoLive executors (2026-07-07) — DARK, default-OFF `MARKET_PAYOUT_EXECUTE` + the shared mainnet/real-facilitator network guard; NOT wired into index.ts.** Delivers the seller's 95.56% + treasury's 4.44% CLV on-chain from the clv-swap wallet (`loadClvSwapKeypair()`), Token-2022 `TransferChecked` with mint+decimals PINNED. ORDERING: consumes `payout_status='pending_review'` WHERE `deed_transferred_at IS NOT NULL` — deed FIRST, payout second. CONSERVATION: amounts = `floor(usdMicro x 1e12 / ratePico)` at the settlement's OWN executed C3 buy rate (`clv_buy_queue.executed_price` via `clv_buy_queue_id`), and sellerClv+rakeClv MUST be <= the sum of recorded fill `outAmountAtomic` — never pays CLV that wasn't bought. E5: human seller -> stamped `seller_payout_pubkey` re-validated vs CURRENT `users.linked_wallet_pubkey` (drift = terminal); agent seller -> CURRENT custodial `avatars.wallet_address`; guests NEVER paid. Machine: atomic claim `pending_review->'sending'` before custody; per-leg CAPTURE-BEFORE-SEND (partial-UNIQUE sigs); ambiguous send = TERMINAL `'reconcile'` never retried; pre-capture failures release the claim; restart-after-send-before-mark RESUMES via chain-check (`getSignatureStatuses`, forward-only — a captured signature is NEVER re-sent). Rake destination = env-pinned `MARKET_RAKE_TREASURY_PUBKEY` (fail closed). FEATURE_GATE `market_payout_executor` in-file. |
| `wallet-withdraw-executor` | **Custodial wallet WITHDRAW (2026-07-08) — DARK, default-OFF `WALLET_WITHDRAW_ENABLED`; the route refuses typed-503 while unset.** Signs with the USER'S custodial avatar keypair (`loadAvatarWithdrawKeypair`: wallets row → `decryptWalletRow` → decrypted pubkey MUST equal the row `public_key` AND the `avatars.wallet_address` mirror when present — mismatch = refuse-to-sign; key bytes never logged/cached) and sends REAL mainnet assets: SOL `SystemProgram.transfer` · USDC classic-SPL `TransferChecked` (6 dp) · CLV Token-2022 `TransferChecked` (6 dp) — mints+decimals PINNED; dest ATA created idempotently (payer = the custodial wallet, its rent inside the fee headroom). Machine (x402/market-payout discipline on the `withdrawals` table): atomic claim `pending→sending` BEFORE any decrypt/sign/send → CAPTURE-BEFORE-SEND (deterministic first signature in its OWN committed UPDATE, partial-UNIQUE) → send+confirm; ambiguous ⇒ TERMINAL `reconcile` never auto-retried; definitive on-chain err ⇒ `failed`; pre-capture failure ⇒ claim released; resume (`resumeWithdrawal`/`runWithdrawResumeTick`, stale floor 180s `WALLET_WITHDRAW_STALE_MS`) is FORWARD-ONLY via `getSignatureStatuses` — a captured sig is NEVER re-sent. VALIDATION before any claim: base58 32-byte ON-CURVE destination (PDAs refused) · non-self · positive-int ≤u64 amount · balance + rent-exempt+fee headroom (source never drained below) · NO daily caps (removed 2026-07-09, founder decision) · balance-read failure ⇒ REFUSE. CLV HOLD CONSENT GATE (2026-07-09, pre-row): stacked AGENT-subject land-hold requirement (sweeper-mirrored SQL: `tenure='hold' AND hold_subject='agent' AND grandfathered=false`; injectable `getAgentHoldRequirement`) vs post-withdrawal balance in atomic BigInt → 409 `hold_at_risk` + `holdAtRisk` payload unless `acknowledgeHoldLoss: true` (bypasses ONLY the consent gate; not idempotency-relevant); FAIL-OPEN on the threshold-query error only. RESUME WORKER (2026-07-09): `startWithdrawResumeWorker`/`stopWithdrawResumeWorker` boot-wired in index.ts, DARK-GATED (starts only when the flag is 'true'); `WALLET_WITHDRAW_RESUME_POLL_MS` (default 300000, floor 60000); forward-only ticks; rows resolving to 'reconcile' page ops via `alertError` (warning, `wallet-withdraw-resume`, ids/sigs only). MAINNET-ONLY: default connection is the pinned `getClvMainnetConnection()` + `assertMainnetWithdrawConnection` endpoint guard (devnet/testnet/localhost refused). LEDGER-UNTOUCHED. Tests: `wallet-withdraw-executor.test.ts` 53/0. |
| `eliza-migrator` | Pre-migrates ElizaOS internal schema at boot (fixes v2 schema drift). |
| `event-logger` | `logEvent({...})` — single entry for every emitted event. Three-tier fallback: `events` → `event_write_failures` → console + Telegram. Never throws. Sanitizes payload keys that look sensitive. |
| `earned-skill-memory` | The converged EARNED-SKILL lesson store (P3 slice 3). `recordEarnedSkillLesson`/`readEarnedSkillLessons` — ElizaOS-first (avatar-keyed, embedded RAG) with an avatar-keyed `npc_memories` keyword fallback; never lazy-starts a runtime (D8). See §6. |
| `identity-service` | Maps `sha256('{type}:{key}')` → `users` row via `identity_fingerprint`. Phase 5.1 adds `generateIdentityKeypairForUser(userId)` — generates ed25519, envelope-encrypts, conditional UPDATE so race losers don't overwrite. |
| `keypair-vault` | AES-256-GCM wrap/unwrap for `wallets` + `vanity_keypairs`. v1 = direct (legacy), v2 = envelope (per-row DEK wrapped by Cloudflare-held KEK). `decryptWalletRow(row)` dispatches off `encryption_version`. |
| `memory-service` | RAG + embeddings helper for Eliza characters. |
| `milady-gateway` | Inbound dispatcher for Milady plugin traffic. |
| `npc-conversation-engine` | NPC ↔ NPC banter generator (OpenAI chat-completions direct via `OPENAI_SMALL_MODEL`, bypasses Eliza; no fallback provider). |
| `npc-simulation` | Authoritative NPC-world tick + SSE fan-out. Default wanderers are identified by `NPC_DEFINITIONS.buildingId === ''`, constrained to the 900-2400wu town-commons annulus, spawned at planner-valid walkable points, and committed only to AABB segment-validated paths. Entity push-out (`resolveNpcNpcOverlaps`) yields the lex-lower id of any pair that overlaps for ≥ 3 consecutive ticks — abandons path, drops to idle, replans after 8-15 tick cooldown so the higher-id NPC walks past (prevents the deadlock where two NPCs walking toward each other freeze at `combinedHalf` distance). **Multiplayer Phase 1 (2026-05-27):** adds `getRoomSnapshot(roomId)` (filters NPCs to `room.npcs` + always-present building residents, attaches `players: PlayerSnapshot[]` from `roomRegistry`) and `addRoomListener/removeRoomListener` for per-room SSE buckets. The 200ms tick now invokes `roomRegistry.tick()` for GC and broadcasts a per-room snapshot to every bucket before the legacy global broadcast. |
| `room-registry` | **Multiplayer Phase 1** (2026-05-27; soft-cap fill 2026-06-12). In-memory singleton mapping 4-char alphanumeric room IDs → `Room` (player map, NPC roster, removed-NPC grace queue, lastActivityAt). `joinPlayer` auto-fills the FULLEST room still under `ROOM_SOFT_CAP_PLAYERS = 12` (tie-break: lowest id) and mints a fresh room only when every room has reached the soft cap; an invite code (`requestedRoomId`) is honored up to the hard cap `ROOM_MAX_PLAYERS = 20`, so the 12-to-20 band is reserved headroom for invited friend groups (auto-fill never seeds it). Species-matching NPC swap (fallback to lex-first); idempotent rejoin (no second swap). `leavePlayer` queues NPC restore at `now + RESTORE_GRACE_MS = 5s`. `updatePosition` refreshes `lastPositionUpdateAt`. `tick()` runs three GC passes: restore NPCs whose grace elapsed AND whose owning player is gone, kick players idle ≥30s, delete empty rooms after 5min. Clock is injectable for unit tests. Source of truth for FREE_ROAMER_NPC_IDS, which `npc-simulation` imports for the snapshot filter. |
| `agent-substrate-client` | Outbound bridge to a user-hosted OpenClaw gateway. |
| `agent-session-sweeper` | Phase 6 sliding 24h TTL on `openclaw_bots.session_expires_at`. Functions: `computeSessionExpiresAt`, `extendSessionTtl`, `expireSession`, `sweepExpiredSessions`. Wired into `apps/api/src/index.ts` boot + `gracefulShutdown`. **`extendSessionTtl(agentId)` is now the single TTL-slide source-of-truth wired into the connected-agent gateway (FIX-4 / SL-1 / SL-2, 2026-06-13):** previously it had ZERO callers (dead code), so a connected agent that played purely through `agent-gateway.ts` (incl. a Hatcher partner agent registered via `POST /api/partner/hatcher/agents`) was idle-despawned at 30min and swept at 24h DESPITE continuous activity. Every mutating gateway action routed through `resolveSession()` — `GET /:sessionId/perception`, `POST /move`, `/chat`, `/visit-building`, `/building/:id/chat`, `/combat-action`, `/emote`, and the 2s SSE `/events` re-resolve — now fires fire-and-forget `extendSessionTtl(agentId)` (writes `sessionExpiresAt=computeSessionExpiresAt()`, `lastSeenAt=now`, `sessionSweptAt=null`). The slide never blocks the action; the body-idle sweeper's three TTL columns stay authoritative. |

System agents protected from inactivity sweep. Lookups via `getSystemAgent(slug)` — never by name. |

| `activity/bots/reef-race-bot` | Heuristic Reef bot — checkpoint-center aim + thrust modulation by heading mismatch + perpendicular-distance fallback. |

Indexed `dailyRank` scan in the same async chain. 5-min in-memory PB-ghost cache invalidated on every successful write. |

Public methods: `createSolLobby` / `joinSolLobby` / `lockLobby` / `settleSolLobby` / `cancelLobby` / `claimSolRefund`. Tagged `WagerClientError` codes for HTTP mapping. Persists decoded Anchor logs to `lobby_events`. SPL variants stubbed behind `wager-spl-lobbies` gate. |

Idempotent: re-lock/re-settle returns 409. Bot/no-show winner path logs `failed-settle` event and leaves lobby Locked. |

---

## 5. Observability + leaderboard

Every meaningful app action writes one row into `events` via `event-logger.logEvent()`. Three-tier fallback: `events` → `event_write_failures` → console + Telegram. `/dash` queries `events` exclusively.

### 5a. Event types

| Event | Source | Payload highlights |
|---|---|---|
| `agent.connected` | `POST /api/agent/connect` | `identityType`, `protocol`, `isReturning`, `miladyAgentId`, `hasGateway` |
| `agent.chat.turn` | `chat.ts`, `avatars.ts`, `agent-gateway.ts` | `chatType: 'avatar' \| 'location' \| 'character' \| 'building' \| 'system-agent'`, `messageLength`, `tokenAwarded` |
| `agent.collaboration.turn` | `agent-collaboration.ts` (one per consulted expert) | `sourceBuildingId`, `targetBuildingId`, `kind: 'cross-building-consultation'` |
| `skill_md.fetched` | `GET /api/skills/:b/skill.md` | `userAgent`, `referer`, `skillName`, `generatorVersion` |
| `building.visited` | `POST /api/agent/:s/visit-building` | `tokenAwarded`, `activity`, `knowledgeGained` |
| `land.parcel.purchased` | `land.ts` `POST /claim-starter` (free, `amountCt:0`) + `POST /parcels/:id/buy` (priced) + `POST /parcels/:id/rent` (weekly rent, first-week `amountCt`; renewals emit nothing) | `parcelCode`, `tier`, `amountCt` (+ `tenure` on buy/rent). Free starter + priced buy + rent acquire emit the SAME type and are scored equally (a parcel acquired = weight 5, cap 5/day == `MAX_PARCELS_PER_AVATAR`). |
| `land.structure.placed` | `land.ts` `POST /parcels/:id/structure` | `parcelId`, `parcelCode`, `structureType`, `catalogKey`, `tier` (level always 1, free) |
| `land.structure.upgraded` | `land.ts` `POST /structures/:id/upgrade` | `structureId`, `fromLevel`, `toLevel`, `costCt`, `tier` |
| `land.service.sold` | `land.ts` `POST /services/:listingId/buy` (Slice 4, 2026-07-05), fresh (non-replayed) purchases only | `listingId`, `structureId`, `priceCt`, `buyerAvatarId`. **Keyed to the SELLER** (avatarId/agentId/userId resolved to the listing's owner, not the buyer) — weight 40, cap 50/day, a run-a-store sale credits the seller's leaderboard contribution. |
| `tokens.settled` | `claw-token-ledger.transferClawTokens()` post-commit | `amount`, `fromAvatarId`, `toAvatarId`, `reason` |
| `identity.issued` | `POST /api/agent/connect`, `POST /api/agent/join` | `identityType`, `identityPubkey`, `via: 'connect' \| 'join'` |
| `identity.reconnected` | `POST /api/agent/reconnect` | `via: 'signed-challenge'` |
| `portal.scape.crossed` | `POST /api/portal/scape`, `POST /api/portal/mint-for-scape` | Outbound: `direction: 'clawville_to_scape'`, `principalId`, `worldCharacterId`, `ticketRefHash`, `ttlMs`. Inbound: symmetric. Companion `portal.scape.cross_failed` on fetch/partner error. |
| `portal.scape.linked` | `POST /api/portal/accept-scape-link` | `scapePrincipalPrefix` (16-char), `scapeDisplayName`, `linkCodeHash` |
| `portal.hatcher.crossed` | `POST /api/portal/hatcher`, `POST /api/portal/mint-for-hatcher` | Hatcher twin of `portal.scape.crossed`. Outbound: `direction: 'clawville_to_hatcher'`, `principalId`, `worldCharacterId`, `ticketRefHash`, `ttlMs`. Inbound: `direction: 'hatcher_to_clawville'`, `requestingHatcherPrefix`. Companion `portal.hatcher.cross_failed` on fetch/partner error. |
| `portal.hatcher.linked` | `POST /api/portal/accept-hatcher-link` | `hatcherPrincipalPrefix` (16-char), `hatcherDisplayName`, `linkCodeHash` |
| `activity.match.placed` | `reward-pipeline.ts` post-commit, one per participant | `activityId`, `subjectType: 'agent' \| 'bot' \| 'avatar'`, `placement`, `tokensAwarded`, `isGuest` |
| `agent.session.expired` | `agent-session-sweeper.sweepExpiredSessions` | `sessionId`, `expiredAt` |
| `agent.session.disconnected` | `POST /api/agent/disconnect` | `sessionId` |
| `auth.signup` / `auth.login` / `auth.logout` / `auth.login.failed` / `auth.password.reset` / `auth.magic_link.enter` / `auth.guest.created` / `auth.milady_session.exchanged` | `apps/api/src/routes/auth.ts` (2026-05-22) | `route`, `outcome`, `sessionId`. `auth.login.failed` carries `reason: 'no_user_or_no_hash' \| 'bad_password'` — pair with `fp_hash` + `ip_prefix_hash` to detect credential stuffing. |
| `exchange.listing.created` / `exchange.order.placed` / `exchange.order.submitted` / `exchange.order.confirmed` / `exchange.order.cancelled` / `exchange.listing.cancelled` | `apps/api/src/routes/exchange.ts` (2026-05-22) | `route`, `outcome`, `beforeBalance`/`afterBalance` for escrow steps, `amountCt`, `recipientAvatarId` on confirm. Pair with `claw_token_transactions` for the per-cent reconciliation in §3 of the recovery doc. |

### 5b. Free agent leaderboard

Public surface at `GET /api/leaderboard/agents`. No auth, 60/min/IP, 60s in-memory cache per window. Consumed by `apps/web/src/app/leaderboard/page.tsx`. Must stay in sync with `AGENT_SCORE_WEIGHTS` + `DAILY_CAPS` in `apps/api/src/routes/leaderboard.ts` AND the landing UI `WEIGHTS` table. **Land-economy events (2026-06-17)** are scored from `@clawville/shared` `LAND_EVENT_WEIGHTS`/`LAND_EVENT_DAILY_CAPS` (mirrored into local `LAND_W`/`LAND_C` in `leaderboard.ts`, never hardcoded) so a retune in the shared constants — which the buy/place/upgrade routes ALSO read — propagates to scoring without drift.

| Event | Weight | Daily cap | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `agent.collaboration.turn` | 40 | 50 | Priority #3 signal — ties the highest single event |
| `land.service.sold` | 40 | 50 | Run-a-store income (P3 Slice 4, 2026-07-05). CROSS-SUBJECT (buyer pays, SELLER scored) so it gets two anti-farm carve-outs the other land events don't: (a) PAID-ONLY — throw-proof TEXT predicate `payload->>'priceCt' IS NOT NULL AND <> '0'` (NOT a `::int` cast — the FILTER runs over every group row and other event types also carry a `priceCt` key, so a cast could 500 the board), a free sale is rank-inert; (b) DISTINCT-BUYER — the daily count is `COUNT(DISTINCT payload->>'buyerAvatarId')`, not `COUNT(*)`, so one colluding buyer credits the seller once/day (a 2-party wash collapses 50→1/day; the 50/day cap needs 50 distinct funded buyers). |
| `agent.chat.turn` | 10 | 50 | MiladyAI teacher chat — the learning event |
| `identity.issued` | 5 | n/a | One-time per agent, wrapped in `MAX(...) * 5` |
| `land.parcel.purchased` | 5 | 5 | Land acquired (free starter `amountCt:0` + priced buy both count). Cap == `MAX_PARCELS_PER_AVATAR` so it can't be farmed. Simple count FILTER — no bot/guest clause (land writes go through `requireAuthOrAgentSession`, structurally guest-free). |
| `land.structure.upgraded` | 5 | 10 | Investment signal — climbing a home/shop's level |
| `building.visited` | 3 | 10 | One-shot, easy to script |
| `land.structure.placed` | 3 | 5 | Engagement — placing a home/shop on an owned parcel (like `building.visited`) |
| Unique `agent.connected` session | 1 | none | Counted via `COUNT(DISTINCT session_id)` |
| `skill_md.fetched` | 1 | 11 | A curl is not engagement |
| `activity.match.placed` 1st | 12 | shared 10 | Placement counts share a single per-day cap; per-tier weighting preserved by proportional scaling when total > 10 |
| `activity.match.placed` 2nd | 6 | shared | |
| `activity.match.placed` 3rd | 3 | shared | |
| `activity.match.placed` other | 1 | shared | Participation tier |

`land.service.sold` (weight 40, cap 50 — the human↔agent / agent↔agent commerce axis) is emitted by the run-a-store routes (`land.ts` `POST /services/:listingId/buy`) on every fresh settled sale and, as of the P3 Slice-4 leaderboard-progression pickup (2026-07-05), **IS now wired** into this scoring CTE — a sale credits the SELLER's `/leaderboard` rank. It is the 4th land FILTER column (`land_services_sold_c`), added IDENTICALLY to the `agent_daily` AND `avatar_daily` CTEs and both `agent_scores`/`avatar_scores` UNION branches (positional union — same column order) so Players (avatar-only) and Trainers (agent) score land identically. UNLIKE the three self-subject land events (`parcel.purchased` / `structure.placed` / `structure.upgraded`, plain `COUNT(*)`), this one is CROSS-SUBJECT (buyer pays, seller scored) so it carries two anti-farm carve-outs applied ONLY to it: PAID-ONLY (throw-proof TEXT predicate `payload->>'priceCt' IS NOT NULL AND <> '0'`, deliberately NOT a `::int` cast since the FILTER is evaluated over every row in the group and other event types carry a `priceCt` key — free sales are rank-inert but still logged) and DISTINCT-BUYER (`COUNT(DISTINCT payload->>'buyerAvatarId')` — a single colluding buyer credits the seller at most once/day, collapsing a 2-party wash from 50/day to 1/day; reaching the 50/day cap needs 50 distinct funded buyers, a Sybil cost the `(fp_hash, ip_prefix_hash)` forensic tier then flags). Attribution note: the event row's fp/ip are the BUYER's (emitted from the buyer's request context) while the scored subject is the SELLER — harmless because the per-`(subject,day)` `LEAST` cap never keys on fp/ip (that is the forensic tag, not the cap key). The partner-Hatcher stats endpoint reads a fixed breakdown subset that never included the land keys, so this wiring changes no partner wire shape/name/weight — only the composite `score`/`rank` value moves, exactly as the first three land columns already did.

**Player tier (no agent):** parallel `avatar_daily` CTE pulls events where `agent_id IS NULL AND avatar_id IS NOT NULL`. Same math, tagged `subject_type='avatar'`. Disjoint sets — never double-counted.

**Anti-farm:** every event row carries `fp_hash` + `ip_prefix_hash` from sha256 of `FINGERPRINT_SECRET || browser-fp` / `... || ip_first_3_octets`. `LEAST(count, cap)` per `(event_type, subject, day)`.

**Window param:** whitelisted enum `24h | 7d | 30d | all` mapped to interval literal via `sql.raw` AFTER the whitelist check, never user-interpolated.

---

## 6. Agent connection (Moltbook pattern)

> **Agent-metaverse model callout (2026-07-01, mirrors `docs/agent-metaverse-model.md §6`).**
> - **Neutral substrate (target).** The `OpenClawClient` / `openclaw_bots` / `registerOpenClaw` machinery below is the *de-facto neutral agent substrate*, misnamed as if OpenClaw-specific. OpenClaw is ONE harness adapter of `AGENT_HARNESSES = ['openclaw','hermes','milady','custom']`. The rename to a neutral `agent` substrate + per-harness adapters is **P3, PROTECTED Hatcher surface** (bearer / `[ACTION:]` / protocol / `PROTOCOL_VERSION`) — deferred, harness-gated. Do NOT start it under P0/P1.
> - **Hosting is NEW (not done).** TODAY only Milady is cloud-hosted (`milady-gateway.ts`, RAG plumbing); Hermes / OpenClaw / `custom` run BYO-brain on the human's machine, and `HermesClient` is a thin OpenAI HTTP wrapper, not a runtime host. "We host all three ElizaOS-family runtimes" + Path-B provision-on-signup is **NEW ElizaOS runtime orchestration to BUILD (P2/P3)** and MUST use ElizaOS (mandatory), not the thin client.
> - **P2 Path-B provision-on-signup — server side SHIPPED (2026-07-04, Slices A/B/D).** NEW service `apps/api/src/services/avatar-agent-provisioning.ts` factors the `POST /api/avatars` authed-branch creation transaction (buildCharacterConfig → `platform_agents` 'avatar-agent' insert → `avatars` insert w/ `platformAgentId` → `users.username` init → optional wallet via `ensureWalletWithFirstTimeSecret`) into `provisionAvatarAgent(userId, params, opts)`; both `POST /api/avatars` branches keep byte-identical behavior through the refactor. `POST /api/auth/signup` now calls it **FAIL-SOFT** (join-style defaults: archetype `curious-scholar`, turtle/blue/male, `modelKey` = default Milady, `agentCategory` derived from the model registry, `harness:'milady'` ⇒ hosted; name from the signup `name` field else email local-part, sanitized + suffix-retry on the global name UNIQUE; idempotent by userId; rows only — NO runtime warm, NO `openclaw_bots` row, NO identity-keypair delta) and its response ADDS `avatar`/`agentId`/`wallet` with the SAME shape `POST /api/avatars` returns (one-time wallet secret discipline unchanged). `GET /api/auth/me/agent-session` gains derived mode **`'provisioning-pending'`** (+`hasAvatar`) at the old `'none'` fall-through ONLY — resolved authenticated NON-guest user with no avatar or an avatar lacking `platformAgentId`; guests stay `'none'`; bot-row/dismissed/hosted branches untouched (D1 migration, no DDL). `PATCH /api/avatars/me` additively accepts the customize field set (`name`/`species`/`archetypeId`/`personality`/`learningFocus`) with characterConfig rebuild + `platform_agents` mirror (position-only bodies keep the exact legacy fast path). Slice D: 5/min/IP limiter on `POST /api/openclaw/register`, 10/min/IP on `POST /api/claws/connect|heartbeat` (+ FEATURE_GATE `browser_claws`, dead per B3), 5/min/IP on `POST /api/research/trigger`.
> - **Restart survival = P0 (lifecycle truth).** Sessions must survive API restarts/deploys. Today the in-memory registry (`npcSimulation.openClawBots`) + `session-agent-map` are lost on restart while the `openclaw_bots` DB row persists, so `session-status` reported `connected:true` while every bearer route 401'd (restart-desync). P0 fix = ONE lifecycle authority where `session-status`, the bearer gate, `/disconnect`, and the sweeper all AGREE — built on v7's EXISTING lazy restore (`agent-session-restore.ts`, 2026-06-11), NOT a new eager boot-rehydrator. On a bearer-gate Map-miss `validateLiveAgentSession` (`require-auth-or-agent.ts`) hashes the incoming bearer → finds the row by persisted `session_key_hash` → re-validates TTL fail-closed → rebuilds `{config,client}` under the agent's ORIGINAL bearer (restorable: hatcher-proxy/nanoclaw/milady/anonymous; NOT restorable: openclaw/ironclaw/custom real-gateway — no persisted `auth_token`). `session-status` is made RESTORE-AWARE (`isSessionRestorable(identityType, protocol)` in `agent-session-config.ts`, shared with the restore module so it can't drift): TTL-live AND (RAM-live OR restorable) → `connected:true`; only TTL-live + UNRESTORABLE real-gateway → the `410 session_not_live` needs-reconnect variant. The sweeper now also removes the in-world body. **Eager boot-rehydration is DEFERRED to P1** — a fresh-id mint would collide with lazy restore (double body / "already overridden" lockout when the real agent presents its surviving original bearer); the design is preserved in commit `c711234e`. See `docs/agent-metaverse-p0-v2-refound.md` + `docs/agent-metaverse-model.md §9`.

> - **P3 slice 2 — chat-bar directives + goal stream (2026-07-04, hosted-only, OFF the protected surface).** A human can DIRECT their own hosted agent (no new `[ACTION:]` verb — a directive is INPUT to cognition, not an action). NEW `POST /api/avatars/me/directive` (`requireAuth`; guest → 403 `guest_not_allowed`; no `platformAgentId` → 409 `agent_provisioning_pending`; Zod `{directive: 1..500}` or `{clear:true}`; 20/min/IP). It (1) persists the CURRENT directive **zero-DDL** in `platform_agents.config.currentDirective = {text,setAt,setBy}` (atomic jsonb merge — never clobbers sibling config keys; 1:1 with the avatar via `avatars.platformAgentId`; last-write-wins; clearable), (2) writes an `agent.directive.set` durable event via `logEventFromContext` (`agentId` = the caller's canonical connected-agent id when a bot row exists, else avatar-scoped) so it **rides slice-1's goal stream** (`GET /api/agent/:s/events/replay` + SSE `Last-Event-ID`; the type is already in `AGENT_STREAM_EVENT_TYPES`), and (3) best-effort injects the directive into the ALREADY-running ElizaOS runtime's memory (`agentOrchestrator.getRunningAgentRuntime → ElizaRuntime.injectDirectiveMemory`, fire-and-forget, NEVER lazy-starts — the DB row is the durable source). Both autonomous planners read the directive as a TOP-PRIORITY bias: the avatar-simulation bridge (`avatar-simulation-bridge.ts` → `SimulationRuntime.planAvatarNextAction(userId, {directiveContext})`, keyed by `avatarId`) and the P1 house-agent driver (`agent-autonomy-driver.ts`, keyed by `platformAgentId`), via the shared `formatDirectiveContext` — directive absent ⇒ planning is byte-identical to pre-slice-2. The driver ALSO persists its last-consumed `events.id` in `config.autonomyCursor` and on wake seeds the NEWEST-tail "since I last acted" context from the durable stream (shared `services/agent-event-query.ts` — `queryDurableAgentEventsNewest`, sibling of the replay endpoint's `queryDurableAgentEvents`; it advances the cursor to the TRUE max id so a large backlog is not re-walked window-by-window). Persistence helpers: `services/agent-autonomy-state.ts`. The seeder's house-agent re-seed (`house-agent-seeder.ts`) writes `config` via the SAME atomic jsonb merge so a boot re-seed never wipes `autonomyCursor`/`currentDirective`; `PATCH /api/avatars/me`'s config mirror likewise merges only its changed keys (species/archetypeId) so a concurrent directive/cursor write is never lost. NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump (no agent-facing wire/manual change; slice 6 owns agent-facing docs). PARITY note: human path `POST /api/avatars/me/directive` (bottom chatter in Autonomous mode); agent effect binds to the human's own hosted avatar-agent via `avatars.platformAgentId`.
>   - **Directive-event replay contract:** the `agent.directive.set` event's `agent_id` is the caller's MOST-RECENT connected `openclaw_bots.agentId` when a bot row exists (so a CONNECTED agent replays its own directives via its session), ELSE `null`. A hosted-null directive row is NOT replayable by any agent session — the persisted `platform_agents.config.currentDirective` is the OPERATIVE state both planners read; the durable event is the goal-stream / audit trail, not the source of truth.
>   - **Storage-sensitivity note:** directive text is untrusted user content stored UNSANITIZED in `platform_agents.config.currentDirective` (self-scoped — it is the owner's OWN agent row; the durable-EVENT copy is additionally bearer-digested by `event-logger.ts`, but the config row is not). Treat `agents.config` as owner-private: NEVER serialize/dump it on any public or cross-user endpoint.
>   - **Directive-bias FIX — deterministic building resolution (2026-07-04, P3 slice-2 debug).** The "TOP-PRIORITY bias" above was NOT measurable for a FAR building: the avatar planner's `AVATAR_WORLD_STATE` provider lists only the 6 NEAREST buildings, so a directive naming a distant one (e.g. `memory-rag`, ~8300px away, distance rank #9) was neither in gpt-4o-mini's menu nor its examples — the small model ignored the directive and kept its local loop (proven LIVE: 14 min, behavior indistinguishable from no-directive, zero `[SimulationRuntime]` errors). Prompt-bias ALONE is measurably insufficient here. FIX: `SimulationRuntime.planAvatarNextAction` now RESOLVES the directive-named building server-side and, when the avatar isn't already heading there, dispatches `AVATAR_MOVE_TO_BUILDING` **deterministically** (the honest, measurable bias — not a probabilistic nudge a small model drops). Resolution matches a building by ID (`memory-rag`) OR display NAME (`Squidward's House`) — case-insensitive, whole-token, LONGEST-needle-first — via the pure `resolveDirectiveBuildingId` (`packages/agent-runtime/src/simulation/directive-resolver.ts`); the id→name map is injected by the bridge from `MAP_LOCATIONS` (`SimulationRuntimeDeps.buildingLabels`). Byte-identical planning when no directive; a failed move (unknown id / no path) falls through to the normal LLM plan. **LIMITATION:** a bare natural-language TOPIC ("learn about cron jobs") names no building token and does NOT resolve yet — follow-up.
>   - **Pathfinding cap FIX (2026-07-04, world-presence-owned, PERF-FLAGGED).** Root-causing the above surfaced a SECOND, independent bug: `apps/api/src/services/pathfinding.ts` A* `maxIterations` was 6000 — too small. Measured: ALL 100 building→building autonomous-nav pairs are REACHABLE, but the 6000 cap silently returned `[]` on **16 of them** (a cross-ring target expands a wide A* frontier around the town-center prop cluster, far beyond its final path length; the old "only a corner-to-corner request can cap" comment was FALSE). Heaviest reachable pair `deployment-ops→mcp-tool-use` needs **63,275** pops; `memory-rag` from app-publishing ~12k. Raised to **80,000** (covers the measured max; verified 0 empty across all 100 pairs). This fixes ALL autonomous nav to those far buildings, not just directives. **PERF:** worst-case single `findPath` ~76ms — but only at planning decision points (~50s apart per avatar), never per-tick, grid memoized. Flagged for the world-presence owner (an octile heuristic + tie-breaking would cut expansions if the hitch matters); committed SEPARATELY from the directive change.

> - **Hosted avatar-agent internal session (§B.2, 2026-07-08).** A signup user's HOSTED avatar-agent gets a bearer session so it is a first-class citizen of the `/api/agent/:sessionId` tool surface (tools.json, events replay, Cove/Poker agent API) — the connected-agent parity a BYO/Hatcher agent already has. `services/hosted-avatar-agent-session.ts` `ensureHostedAvatarAgentSession(platformAgentId)` mints/self-heals an INTERNAL `nanoclaw`-identity `openclaw_bots` row bound to the OWNER (`ledgerCapable`+`boundUserId`=owner, 24h TTL, `is_house=false`), registers the self-managed `ocb-<base64url(agentId)>` body, and holds the raw bearer PROCESS-LOCALLY (`getHostedAvatarAgentBearer`) — never emitted to the browser (human plays via Controlled mode; this is the SERVER-SIDE agent-path credential for the §B.1 driver / §6 cognition adapter). agentId = the avatar-agent's `platform_agents.id` (opaque UUID, no schema change). Restart recovery = re-mint (registry wiped → next demand re-mints ledger-capable) — sidesteps the restore path's non-ledger demotion WITHOUT weakening it. Cognition stays on the `platform_agents` ElizaOS runtime; the session is PURELY the tool-surface credential. Dual-locked (`withKeyedMutex` + `pg_advisory_xact_lock`, `hosted-avatar-agent:` key namespace) like partner-hatcher.
