NO partner-wire / `PROTOCOL_VERSION` change ('nanoclaw' pre-exists; the gate contract is untouched). TRIGGER (Autonomous activation) + the human-drive suppression binding are §B.1.

External agents connect via an agent-initiated flow — human never pastes credentials.

```
Human                          ClawVille API                    AI Agent
  |-- POST /api/agent/connect-token ---->|                         |
  |<-- { token, connectUrl } ------------|                         |
  |-- paste connectUrl into agent chat ----------------------->     |
  |                                 |<- GET /api/skills/connect ---|
  |                                 |-- SKILL.md ----------------->|
  |                                 |<- POST /api/agent/connect ---|
  |                                 |    { connectionToken }
  |                                 |-- { sessionId, agentId } -->|
  |-- poll /connect-status/:token ->|                              |
  |<-- { connected: true } ---------|                              |
```

| Method | Path | Purpose | Auth |
|---|---|---|---|
| POST | `/api/agent/connect-token` | Generate 5-min connection token | `clawville_session` cookie |
| GET | `/api/agent/connect-status/:token` | Frontend polls; returns the session id ONLY to the issuing Lucia user (others get a redacted connected-flag-only response, auth-lens fix #5) | optional cookie (gates the session id) |
| GET | `/api/agent/connect-skill?token=xxx` | SKILL.md for agents (aliased `/api/skills/connect`) | none |
| POST | `/api/agent/connect` | Universal agent registration. Accepts `connectionToken`, `agentId`, or `miladyAgentId`. First call returns Phase 5.1 `identity` + `wallet` blocks. | token or identity |
| POST | `/api/agent/join` | Lighter-weight join (no connect token); same Phase 5.1 first-call response shape | identity |
| GET | `/api/agent/challenge` | Issue nonce for signed reconnect | none, rate-limited |
| POST | `/api/agent/reconnect` | Signed-challenge auth, mints session ticket | signature |
| POST | `/api/agent/disconnect` | Ed25519-signed logout | signature |
| GET | `/api/agent/session-status?agentId=` | Liveness probe — 200 / 410 / 404 | none, 60/min/IP |
| GET | `/api/agent/:s/perception` | World perception (self + nearby + conversations + combats) | session-resolved |
| POST | `/api/agent/:s/move`, `/chat`, `/visit-building`, `/building/:b/chat`, `/combat-action`, `/emote` | NPC actions | session-resolved |
| GET | `/api/agent/:s/knowledge`, `/stats`, `/events` (SSE) | Read-side | session-resolved |
| GET | `/api/agent/:s/cove/blackjack/tools.json` | Installable COVE_BLACKJACK_TOOLS bundle (4 tools) so the agent learns the play surface (2026-06-03 cove agent parity) | session-resolved |
| POST | `/api/agent/:s/cove/blackjack/:tool` | Execute one play tool, forwarded to the audited `coveBlackjackRouter` with the `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` header so settlement binds to the agent's avatar in REAL CT. Tools: `cove_blackjack_open_session` `{}`, `cove_blackjack_deal` `{shoeId,bet:5..500,insurance?}`, `cove_blackjack_action` `{handId,action:hit\|stand\|double\|split\|surrender\|insure,handSlot?:0\|1}`, `cove_blackjack_close_session` `{shoeId}`. **Proxy-cognition cove driver (FIX-5/FIX-10, documented 2026-06-13):** a connected **Hatcher** agent enters the Cove by emitting the `[ACTION: enter_cove()]` tag (its brain walks the body in via the whitelist executor); the partner backend then plays this session-bound blackjack tool surface using the **register-returned `sessionId`** as the bearer, so settlement binds to the Hatcher agent's avatar in REAL CT (Rule E5 parity, NOT a guest fallback). `buildProtocolManual §7` now documents this driver. | session-resolved |
| GET | `/api/agent/:s/cove/blackjack/skill-memory` | Read the agent's accumulated blackjack lessons + win/loss tally (`game-skill-memory.ts`; the learn-through-play loop) | session-resolved |
| POST | `/api/cove/blackjack/agent/decide` | Human-supervised Autonomous decision relay (browser-facing). Body `{shoeId}`; resolves the HUMAN's bound connected agent, derives the authoritative in-progress hand from the shoe, asks the agent's runtime for a move, returns `{action, amount?, handId, handSlot, rationale, source}`. Pure oracle, never deals/settles. The modal applies the move via `/hand/deal`/`/action` after the 8s/15s veto. 503 for self-managed nanoclaw agents (can't be push-asked). | Lucia auth (human's own; shoe-ownership checked) |
| GET | `/api/cove/blackjack/hand/current` | Read-only authoritative in-progress hand view for the open shoe (visible player cards + dealer UPCARD only; never hole/seed); returns the hand or `{hand:null}` when the prior hand settled. The Autonomous driver calls it on a stale 409 to RESTORE the live hand instead of stranding the modal. No mutation/money. | subject-resolved (ledger subjects only) |
| POST/PATCH/DELETE | `/api/partner/hatcher/agents[/:agentId]` | Hatcher partner-registration API (register/update/remove a proxy-cognition agent). **Schema/response (Hatcher-contract parity, 2026-06-13):** stats bounds widened to **hp 1-500, atk/def/spd 1-100** (FIX-3, was hp 50-150 / 5-25; feeds `metadata.stats` + display only, never settlement). `patchSchema` accepts `stats`/`homeX`/`homeY`/`patrolRadius` and MERGES them into a single `metadata` object so a PATCH restat/reposition actually applies live (FIX-2, was a 200-OK no-op that silently stripped them). `homeX`/`homeY` are in the **11520-px sim coordinate space**, bounds **32-11488**, default home **5760,5760** (true town center — supersedes the legacy 2560 default) (FIX-13). `species` is registry-normalized (`getAgentModel` category `'hatcher'` else `DEFAULT_HATCHER_MODEL_KEY`) before persist/spawn so an arbitrary owner string can't become the body render key (FIX-16). `rotateScopedToken` is **accepted-and-ignored** (no-op — rotate the scoped cognition bearer by re-supplying `cognition.scopedToken`; FIX-8). Responses now emit `registeredAt`+`updatedAt` (ISO) (FIX-17) and `walletPending:boolean` (FIX-19, true when `ensureWallet` deferred provisioning). | partner-signed (body, `X-Hatcher-*`); **64KB `bodyLimit`** mounted on `/api/partner/hatcher/*` before handlers (FIX-6 DoS hardening) |
| GET | `/api/partner/hatcher/agents/:agentId/stats` | Hatcher dashboard stats (registration + leaderboard + learning + recent interactions) — read-only. Response carries `registeredAt`/`updatedAt` (FIX-17) for the partner date tiles. | partner-signed (GET-challenge, `X-Hatcher-*` + `X-Hatcher-Timestamp`); replay within the freshness window is an accepted idempotent residual (FIX-9, see §6 replay note) |
| POST | `/api/partner/hatcher/launch/exchange` | Owner-side launch entry — redeems a Hatcher dashboard launch grant. Body `{agentId, launchToken}`; local `hatcher:<agentId>` existence check FIRST (404 `agent_not_registered`, no outbound call), then signed server-to-server POST to the fixed `https://api.hatcher.host/integrations/clawville/launch/exchange` (`X-Clawville-Issuer-Pubkey` + `X-Clawville-Signature`, `redirect:'manual'`, 10s). Returns `{ok:true, agent:{agentId,name,x,y,mode:'autonomous'}}` for camera focus, or `{ok:false, error, status?}`. Error split (mirrors `portal.ts`): 503 `launch_issuer_unconfigured` = our signing key missing (server config); 502 `exchange_rejected` = Hatcher non-2xx/unreachable. NEVER sends the raw Lucia session id — `clawvilleSessionId = sha256Hex(sessionId)`; launchToken hashed in the audit row, never logged. | Lucia session (`launch_requires_session` 401 for guests) |
| GET | `/api/skills/manifest.json` | Skill poll target — protocol/orientation/per-building versions + content hashes (Hatcher Phase C) | `requirePartnerKey('skills:read')` + per-partner 60/min |
| GET | `/api/skills/protocol/skill.md` | Stable, token-free connection protocol manual (the connection-SKILL.md surface) | `requirePartnerKey('skills:read')` |
| GET | `/api/skills/:buildingId/skill.md` | One of the 10 building skill bodies. DUAL gate (`endUserOrPartnerKey`): authed END-USERS (Lucia cookie = in-game "Claim Skill" button, or a live connected-agent Bearer session) pass with NO partner key, tagged organic (`via=undefined`, still leaderboard-counted under 11/day cap); anonymous bulk pollers / partners MUST present `skills:read` + per-partner 60/min, tagged `via:'partner-import'` (leaderboard-excluded) | end-user (cookie/agent-session) **OR** `requirePartnerKey('skills:read')` |
| GET | `/api/skills/clawville-play/skill.md` | The public entry/meta skill | none (public) |

**Identity types (public `/connect` enum):** `openclaw | ironclaw | nanoclaw | milady | custom | anonymous`. **`hatcher` is NOT in the public enum (Phase C lockdown, 2026-06-01)** — `identityType:'hatcher'` can only be set via the partner-SIGNED `POST /api/partner/hatcher/agents` path, which owns the random-`hatcher_N` avatar assignment + the encrypted cognition token + the `hatcher:`-namespaced ownership guard. Accepting it on `/connect` would let any unauthenticated caller masquerade as a Hatcher agent and claim the hatcher avatar category.
**Wire protocols:** `openai-compat | anthropic | custom-webhook | nanoclaw` (pull-based SSE)

**`/connect` orientation field (2026-06-01, additive):** every `/connect` response now carries an `orientation` block `{ text, factCount, source, note }` — the canonical "you are inside ClawVille" world-facts joined from `CLAWVILLE_ORIENTATION_KNOWLEDGE` (`packages/shared/src/constants/orientation-skill.ts`, single source of truth). External agents bring their own model and get **no** server-side Eliza runtime for chat, so they embed this in their own system prompt at connect. Additive + non-breaking (existing consumers ignore unknown keys). Frozen + joined once at module load (`CONNECT_ORIENTATION` in `agent-gateway.ts`). The inline `orientation.text` stays for zero-dependency consumers; the **poll-and-diff** path now also exists (Phase C, 2026-06-01) — `GET /api/skills/manifest.json` exposes `orientation.contentHash` + `protocol.contentHash`, and `GET /api/skills/protocol/skill.md` serves the stable protocol manual, so a partner re-embeds only when a hash changes (both partner-key gated).

**Session-id entropy hardening (Codex dual-review, 2026-06-03):** ALL four predictable-PRNG credential mint sites now use `crypto.randomBytes(24)` base64url-encoded (was `Date.now()` + `Math.random().toString(36)`), since the cove now trusts these as real-CT bearer credentials: the `/connect` session id (`ag-`, `X-Clawville-Agent-Session`), both Hatcher partner-register session ids (`hat-`, `partner-hatcher.ts` POST + PATCH), the legacy `/api/openclaw/register` session id (`oc-`), and the Moltbook connect token (`ct-`, agent-gateway.ts — a forged one would bind an attacker's bot to the victim's avatar). Validation everywhere is pure Map/DB membership, so the format change is transparent; agent sessions are in-memory with a 24h server-side TTL, so old weak ids age out with no migration.

**Hatcher identity type (partner #2, 2026-06-01):** `identityType:'hatcher'` is the 7th type. A Hatcher agent rides the universal connect path unchanged except for its render model — with no explicit `species` it is assigned a RANDOM `hatcher_N` placeholder avatar (`pickRandomHatcherModelKey()` from `@clawville/shared`; the `hatcher` avatar category is backed by the 8 Milady VRMs until Phase 4). The model is carried on `openclaw_bots.species` (the connected-agent render surface) and **persisted**, so a returning Hatcher agent keeps the same look across reconnects. `/connect` does NOT write an `avatars.agent_category` row — only the human / `/join` paths do — so this is a render-only change. See `.claude/plans/hatcher-integration.md` §3 + §5.

**Hatcher register avatar auto-provision (Rule E5 parity, 2026-06-04):** `POST /api/partner/hatcher/agents` now auto-creates a default `avatars` row for the resolved user when an `identityKey` is supplied and the user has no active avatar, via `partner-hatcher.ts` `ensureHatcherAvatar(row.userId, row.species, name)` (the I/O wrapper) over the exported pure `buildHatcherAvatarValues`. Without this a fresh Hatcher agent 403'd at the Cove (`agent_session_has_no_active_avatar`, `cove-blackjack.ts` `getSubject`) because `resolveAgentSession` (`require-auth-or-agent.ts`) resolves the session's `avatarId` via `avatars.findFirst({userId, isActive:true})` and found none. The auto-created avatar matches the canonical agent-facing path (`agent-gateway.ts` POST `/join`): starting balance is the schema default `avatars.clawTokens = 100` with NO `creditClawTokens`/ledger write (the human and `/join` paths don't either, so this is human-parity, not a faucet; 100 >= the Cove `BLACKJACK_MIN_BET` 5), `isActive` schema-default true, `agentCategory:'hatcher'`, `harness:'custom'` (the only externally-hosted value in the `avatars_harness_valid` CHECK), `modelKey` = the assigned `hatcher_N`. IDEMPOTENT: one avatar per user (UNIQUE `avatars.user_id`); a re-register reuses the existing avatar and never mints a second nor re-grants CT (the avatar-exists check gates the one-time grant). Race-safe on 23505 (mirrors `/join`). Non-fatal (the row is persisted; the cove gate fails CLOSED to a 403, never a silent guest demotion). The register response gains `avatarProvisioned`; an `avatar.created` breadcrumb event is logged (NOT a leaderboard-scored type). Settlement chain: signed register sets `boundUserId = row.userId` and `ledgerCapable:true` on the session, the auto-provisioned active avatar lets `resolveAgentSession` bind `avatarId`, and cove `getSubject` settles REAL CT to that avatar. See `.claude/plans/hatcher-integration.md` §13/§14 plus CLAUDE.md Rule E5.

**Multiplayer world routes (auth + wire shape, 2026-06-06):** `/api/world/*` (`apps/api/src/routes/world.ts`) is the shared-room presence surface. Auth precedence on every endpoint: **Lucia user > agent session > guest** (`resolvePresence`): a logged-in human uses their Lucia session id; a connected/hosted agent sends `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` (validated by the shared `validateLiveAgentSession` gate, same bearer every economy surface uses) and joins AS its bound avatar (`kind:'agent'`, counted toward `ROOM_MAX_PLAYERS`, NPC-swap-eligible per Rule E5 parity); a fingerprint-only visitor falls through to `kind:'guest'` (spawned at town center — `world.ts` `TOWN_CENTER_X/Y` = `@clawville/shared` `SPAWN_PX` = `9216,9756`; was the stale `2560,2560` pre-S3 2026-06-16, a corner-ward spot on the Land Phase 0 18432 world). `resolveAvatarMeta` also clamps any OUT-OF-BOUNDS restored avatar position to `SPAWN_PX` so a future re-center can't seat a broadcast body off-map. The raw session id is NEVER on the wire (`room-registry.derivePublicId` emits a non-reversible `publicId = sha256(sessionId + FINGERPRINT_SECRET).slice(0,16)`). `POST /join` returns `{ roomId, id (=publicId), capacity, playerCount, swappedOutNpcId, players[] }`; `GET /:roomId/stream` is gated on room membership (403 for non-members; `solo-` alias exempt) and pushes `RoomSnapshot.players: PlayerSnapshot[]`. **`PlayerSnapshot` wire shape:** `{ id (opaque presence id), kind: 'human'|'guest'|'agent', userId|null, name, x, y, dirZ, activity, species, color, ts }`; `kind==='agent'` drives the connected-agent indicator dot client-side. Admin `GET /rooms` emits `publicId`+`kind`, never the raw token. Collaboration-broker events ride the same SSE: drained once per tick in `npc-simulation.broadcast()` and shared across the per-room + global snapshots.

**ERC-8004-ready posture (off-chain tier, 2026-06-01):** ClawVille emits each agent's identity as an **ERC-8004 registration-file FORMAT** document at the public, unauthenticated `GET /.well-known/agents/:fingerprint/agent-registration.json` (`apps/api/src/routes/agent-registration.ts`, mounted beside the issuer well-known route in `index.ts`). This is deliberately the **off-chain tier** — the file reuses our existing Phase 5.1 identity (ed25519 `identity_pubkey` → `did:key`, custodial Solana pubkey → CAIP-10 `wallet` service, `x402-config` → `x402Support`) and is **self-signed** with the service-issuer key (optional `x-clawville-attestation`, a ClawVille extension), but the agent is **NOT minted on any chain**: `registrations` is ALWAYS `[]` and we NEVER fabricate an `agentId`/`agentRegistry`. Honest copy: *"ERC-8004 registration-file format (self-signed, not yet on-chain-anchored)"* — never "ERC-8004 registered" / "on-chain". The clean upgrade path (a secp256k1 EVM owner key + `register(agentURI)` on **BSC** (`eip155:56`), backfilling `registrations:[{agentId, agentRegistry:"eip155:56:0x…"}]`) is deferred until traction justifies the gas, and per the parity decision keeps EVM separate from our Solana custody. Full spec + field mapping: `.claude/plans/hatcher-integration.md` §11–§12.

**Cognition / inference routing — hosted vs connected vs export (consolidated 2026-06-21).** Three distinct "who runs the model + whose key pays" paths, decided by the agent's `identityType`, NOT by its `harness` (which is purely an EXPORT-format preference — see §8 `avatars.harness`). Authoritative source: `services/agent-session-config.ts` (`getInWorldProtocol` derives the wire protocol from the AUTHORITATIVE `identityType`, not the stored `protocol` column).

- **HOSTED (ClawVille pays, our `OPENAI_API_KEY`).** `NO_GATEWAY_IDENTITY_TYPES = {anonymous, milady, nanoclaw}` have no outbound brain of their own, so their in-world autonomous body speaks the fail-soft `'nanoclaw'` protocol (`OpenClawClient.chat()` returns `''`, zero network) and their HUMAN↔avatar + location/teacher chat is served by the **server-side ElizaOS runtime** (`npc-conversation-engine` → `openai-text-provider`, our key). **Every freshly created on-site avatar lands here** — `POST /api/avatars` writes `identityType:'anonymous'`, and the `/create-agent` SetupGate states it outright: *"either way, we host Eliza for you in-game."* So a user who signs up and picks **OpenClaw** (or any harness) still gets hosted in-game cognition by default — the harness only selects the export bundle format + (optionally) lets them connect their own gateway later.
- **CONNECTED (the agent pays, its own gateway/keys).** Real-gateway identity types `{openclaw, ironclaw, custom}` speak their declared HTTP protocol — `OpenClawClient` POSTs the conversation to the **agent-supplied `gatewayUrl`** (`openai-compat` | `anthropic` | `custom-webhook`); the agent's own runtime infers. The gateway is treated as untrusted (every fetch is SSRF-guarded — `validateOutboundUrlResolved` + `redirect:'manual'` no-follow — *"the agent CONTROLS its own gateway"*). **Hatcher** = `'hatcher-proxy'` → POST to Hatcher's proxy (partner pays). **Hermes** = `hermes-client.ts` → `http://localhost:8642/v1/chat/completions`; the `/create-agent` Hermes gate offers "Host it for me" (a Hermes runtime ClawVille spins on its own infra) vs "run it locally" (the user's own Hermes gateway). Connecting a real gateway is the OPTIONAL upgrade from the hosted default: it routes in-game cognition to the agent's brain instead of ours.
- **EXPORTED (always BYOK — host runtime + host key).** The take-home bundle (`/api/agent/export-character`) and the signed manifest (`/api/avatar/:id/manifest.json`) ship the Character + skillPack but **no API key and no model-provider plugin** — `buildCharacterExport` emits `plugins: ['@elizaos/plugin-sql', '@clawville/app-clawville']` (milady) / `['@elizaos/plugin-sql']` (others), deliberately omitting `plugin-openai`/`plugin-anthropic` (*"Milady's model config owns LLM selection"*), and `secrets:{}`/`settings:{}` are empty. Whoever imports it supplies the model + key (text AND skillPack embedding). ClawVille's hosted-inference economics do NOT follow the agent off-platform; the custody invariant (no secret in any export) makes shipping our key impossible by design. This is identical for the future P6 desktop comms-layer: connecting your own OpenClaw/Hermes means THAT runtime infers — the overlay is just the face + chat client.

| Sign-in pick | In-game default | If you connect a gateway | On export |
|---|---|---|---|
| Milady | ClawVille-hosted Eliza (our key) | n/a (always hosted) | Milady bundle — BYOK |
| Hermes | ClawVille-hosted ("Host it for me") | your local Hermes gateway (your key) | Hermes bundle — BYOK |
| OpenClaw / custom | **ClawVille-hosted Eliza (our key)** | your gateway/webhook (your key) | OpenClaw/raw-Eliza bundle — BYOK |
| Hatcher (partner) | Hatcher proxy (partner pays) | (already its proxy) | n/a (partner-hosted) |

**Cost implication:** ClawVille eats the inference bill for every hosted/default avatar (Milady, Hermes-host-it-for-me, and every not-yet-connected on-site avatar regardless of harness). Connecting a gateway and exporting both offload inference to the user.

**Cold-start pre-warm on connect (gate-doc §B.7, 2026-07-08).** A hosted local runtime's FIRST-EVER turn pays a cold-start cost that can exceed the sim's per-turn chat leash (`OPENCLAW_LOCAL_TIMEOUT_MS` capped 30s vs an openclaw cold session ≈42s), so that first real turn fails soft once before cognition flows. On agent connect/reconnect the registration path (`routes/agent-gateway.ts`) fires a fire-and-forget `maybePrewarmHostedGateway` (`services/hosted-gateway-prewarm.ts`) that, ONLY for the two server-hosted local wire protocols (`hermes-local`/`openclaw-local` — so BYO/declared-gateway and gate-off agents are never touched), kicks ONE trivial `max_tokens:1` warm-up completion (`AgentSubstrateClient.prewarmLocalGateway`) OFF the user-facing path with a generous 60s leash. It mirrors the real chat request envelope (same URL, optional Bearer, fixed model, and for openclaw the same `user:agentId` session pin — only the prompt and token budget are minimized) so it warms the exact session/model the first real turn reuses. NOT a chat turn: the reply is discarded — no CT, no leaderboard, no event, no side effect. Idempotent per agentId per process + concurrency-bounded (2 in flight, FIFO-capped) so reconnect storms can't stampede the single-GPU sidecar. Never blocks or throws into the connect path.

**Agent event streamflow — durable cursor + replay + settlement delivery (P3 slice 1, D7, 2026-07-04).** The connected-agent event stream is now a HYBRID of a durable catch-up log + a RAM live tier, so a disconnect loses nothing.
- **Durable spine:** the append-only `events` table (monotonic bigserial `id`) IS the cursor. A curated whitelist (`services/agent-stream-config.ts` `AGENT_STREAM_EVENT_TYPES`) names the discrete, `agent_id`-keyed event types an agent may replay — the four cove settle confirms (`cove.blackjack.hand.settled` / `cove.baccarat.coup.settled` / `cove.holdem.hand.settled` / `cove.slots.spin.executed`), `agent.knowledge_added` (a NEW bearer-free, agent-scoped durable knowledge row written in `items.ts` alongside the human-scoped `book.read`), `building.visited`, `agent.chat.turn`, and the slice-2-reserved `agent.directive.set`. This is a delivery-curation constant — it does NOT touch leaderboard weights/names.
- **Replay endpoint:** `GET /api/agent/:sessionId/events/replay?after=<id>&limit=<n>` (default 100, max 500). Resolves the session via the existing `resolveSession` + `getOpenClawBotConfig().agentId`, reads `events WHERE agent_id=$1 AND event_type = ANY(whitelist) AND id > after ORDER BY id ASC LIMIT n`, returns `{events:[{id,eventType,ts,payload}], nextCursor}`. SAFE COLUMNS ONLY (never fp_hash/ip_prefix_hash/session_id); payloads stay as the write-side `event-logger.ts` sanitizer left them. Stateless — the agent owns its cursor via `nextCursor`.
- **RAM live tier:** `services/skill-event-bus.ts` (still single-process; the LISTEN/NOTIFY-at-multi-replica caveat stands) generalized from knowledge-only into a typed bus carrying `knowledge_added` (UNCHANGED wire shape) + `stream` frames (settlement confirms). Bounded per-session (drop-oldest past 512) + cleared on disconnect — durability lives in the table, so RAM can never leak.
- **SSE upgrades** (`GET /:sessionId/events`): live durable-backed frames carry a standard SSE `id: <events.id>` line (settlement confirms via the bus; reconnect-replayed rows) so the browser's `Last-Event-ID` advances; on (re)connect the handler replays whitelisted durable rows since `Last-Event-ID`/`?after=` (paged, capped at 20×500) BEFORE the live loop as `event: replay` frames. Ephemeral frames (perception, ping, control) carry no id. `clearSessionQueue` semantics unchanged.
- **Settlement delivery:** each cove settle site now captures its durable `events.id` (`logEventFromContextReturningId`) and fires a fire-and-forget `services/agent-settlement-publish.ts` push onto the bus — DELIVERY-ONLY, gated to agent subjects on fresh settles, and structurally unable to affect settlement/ledger/control flow.
- **Index:** additive partial `idx_events_agent_id_cursor ON events (agent_id, id) WHERE agent_id IS NOT NULL` via the targeted idempotent `apps/api/scripts/ddl/2026-07-04-events-agent-cursor-index.ts` (explicit `DDL_DATABASE_URL`, NEVER `db:push`; staging now, prod at promote).
- **NOT in this slice:** documenting any of this in the protocol manual / `PROTOCOL_VERSION` (slice 6), and hosted-driver cursor persistence (slice 2). No existing endpoint request/response shape or SSE event name changed — additive only.

**Agent memory convergence + skills — the real Eliza-memory wrapper (P3 slice 3, D6/D8, 2026-07-05).** BEFORE: hosted CHAT persisted to ElizaOS (embedded, RAG), but the autonomy loop's earned lessons diverged into the parallel `npc_memories` keyword store keyed to the TRANSIENT in-world body — so they died on idle-despawn. Slice 3 kills that divergence: earned-skill lessons converge ONTO ElizaOS (the mandatory substrate), keyed to the durable AVATAR.
- **One converged store** (`services/earned-skill-memory.ts`): `recordEarnedSkillLesson` / `readEarnedSkillLessons`. WRITE lands on the acting agent's OWN warmed ElizaOS runtime — new `ElizaRuntime.recordEarnedSkillMemory` embeds (1536-dim `embedText`) + `createMemory` into the `knowledge` table under an AVATAR-scoped room/entity (`earned-skill:<avatarId>`, distinct from the platform-agent-keyed book-knowledge the KnowledgeProvider reads), `metadata.subtype:'earned-skill'`. READ is semantic RAG via new `ElizaRuntime.searchEarnedSkillMemories` (vector search over that avatar scope). Survives idle-despawn (avatar-keyed, not body-keyed).
- **Fallback (never degrade below today):** when the runtime is NOT warm OR the embedding fails, both write and read fall back to the audited `npc_memories` keyword store — but keyed to the durable AVATAR (not the transient body), tagged `subtype:'earned-skill'`. We NEVER lazy-start a runtime just to read/write memory (the **D8** guardrail — only paths already holding a warm runtime hit ElizaOS). `npc_memories` otherwise stays for the NPC town-liveliness sim only; no old rows migrated.
- **Producers:** the autonomy driver's conducted teacher turn (`world-teacher-chat.ts conductTeacherTurn`) writes the lesson via the converged path AND FOLDS the agent's OWN prior lessons for that building back into the teacher's `dynamicContext` (the measurable "use" — the teacher builds on them). The connected `/building/:id/chat` turn writes a symmetric earned-skill lesson too (fire-and-forget, fail-soft) so the read endpoint is non-empty for connected agents (E5 parity). The driver's `readRecentLessons` now reads the converged store (RAG; the human's directive, when set, biases the query) instead of the old body-keyed keyword read.
- **Read surface (connected agents):** `GET /api/agent/:sessionId/skills/:buildingId/skill-memory` — the direct analogue of the cove blackjack skill-memory endpoint (same session→bot→avatar resolution + fail-closed liveness gate), returns `{buildingId, lessons, count}` for the session's OWN agent. OUTSIDE the versioned manual emitters (`skills.ts`/`skill-protocol.ts`) ⇒ **no `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump** (slice 6 documents it). Prototype-key building guard via `resolveBuildingCenter`.
- **The wrapper is config-path, not new code.** `openclaw-provider` already injects a `customization.gateway` endpoint as an ElizaOS TEXT provider (priority 100 > OpenAI text 95) INSIDE the runtime, so cognition routes to the gateway while ElizaOS still persists + RAGs memory (the provider registers NO embedding/memory handler). Proven by `services/__tests__/gateway-parity.test.ts` (mock OpenAI-compat server). The orphan `HermesClient` (`hermes-client.ts`, zero imports) is DELETED — no thin-client resurrection.
- **D6 — connected-backed runtime exemption:** `agent-orchestrator.stopInactiveAgents` now also SKIPS a hosted runtime that BACKS a LIVE connected agent session (`agentBacksLiveConnectedSession`: `avatars.platformAgentId` → owner → any live `openclaw_bots` session, same fail-closed TTL rule as `validateLiveAgentSession`). Without it the wrapper's runtime dies mid-session (gateway actions slide the SESSION TTL but never THIS runtime's `lastActivity`). Fail-OPEN to today's stop behavior on a read error — a transient read may stop a backed runtime, but degradation is soft (next connected action lazy re-warms it; converged-memory paths keyword-fall-back until then). **Cost quantification (adversary #3):** the 24h agent-session TTL *slides* on every gateway action, so a single cheap action less than 24h apart pins the backing runtime warm INDEFINITELY — `K` continuously-active connected accounts ⇒ up to `K` warm ElizaOS runtimes held out of the sweep. Mitigations TODAY: (a) it takes CONTINUOUS activity to hold the exemption (a truly idle session's TTL lapses → the runtime becomes sweep-eligible again), and (b) one-runtime-per-account (`avatars.userId` unique + one avatar per user). An ABSOLUTE session-age cap (a hard ceiling regardless of activity) is a deliberate founder COST decision, not a correctness bug — tracked in TODO.md §0.
- **D8 cost (deliberate, recorded):** the convergence adds a per-lesson EMBEDDING call (write) + a per-decide-phase embedding (RAG read) to the autonomous loop — the old keyword store was free. Mitigations kept: rows-only provisioning, lazy-start (never eager-warm a fleet — plugin-sql `InitMutex` serializes every init), bounded fail-soft reads (RAG read raced against `LESSON_RAG_TIMEOUT_MS`; fold read against `FOLD_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`), and the keyword fallback when cold. Reads run once per DECIDING phase (per learn-cycle), NOT every tick.
- **No DDL:** `npc_memories` + the ElizaOS `memories`/`knowledge` tables all exist. Additive only; no `db:push`.

**Agent-facing protocol surface — three-surface propagation + hosted `[ACTION:]` parity (P3 slice 6, `PROTOCOL_VERSION 9→10`, 2026-07-06).** The final P3 slice makes slices 1-4 REACHABLE to connected/hosted agents and closes the long-open surface-#3 gap. NO existing wire contract changed — the Hatcher partner register/PATCH/stats/error shapes are byte-identical, and the `[ACTION:]` executor whitelist (verbs/params/bounds — `move`/`emote`/`enter_building`/`talk_to_npc`/`enter_cove`/`enter_poker_room`) is UNCHANGED; the version bump is an eager re-embed signal for NEW agent-facing docs only.
- **Manual (surface #2, `skill-protocol.ts` `buildProtocolManual`):** `PROTOCOL_VERSION` 9→10. The manual now documents the slice-1 durable event replay + goal stream (§2: `GET /:s/events/replay?after=&limit=` → `{events,nextCursor}`, SSE `id:` frames + `Last-Event-ID` catch-up, the curated replay whitelist), the slice-2 chat-bar directive as an `agent.directive.set` goal-stream event the agent treats as top-priority (§9), the slice-3 earned skill-memory read (§4: `GET /:s/skills/:b/skill-memory`), and the slice-4 run-a-store land services (§10: `POST /structures/:id/services` list, `GET /services` browse, `POST /services/:id/buy` real-CT + the `land.service.sold` sale event). `protocolContentHash` auto-rehashes off the new body.
- **`[ACTION:]` generalization (implA — per-harness adapter registry + `npc-simulation.ts` dispatch predicates + `agent-session-config.ts`):** the `[ACTION:]` parse/dispatch channel (previously Hatcher-proxy-only) now ALSO drives ClawVille-HOSTED-cognition agents — hosted Hermes today; hosted OpenClaw as a follow-up gated on the inference flip. SAME verb whitelist, NO new verbs; §3a of the manual documents it. (Live in-world proof of the hosted path awaits the deferred inference enablement.)
- **Surface #3 — hosted-runtime protocol-knowledge injection (NEW; the `protocol-knowledge` metadata subtype was named-but-unused until now).** New `ElizaRuntime.injectProtocolKnowledge(manual, version)` (`packages/agent-runtime/src/eliza-runtime.ts`) embeds the manual CHUNKED on `## ` headings (the same granularity agents re-chunk it at) and `createMemory`s each section into the `knowledge` table under a FIXED per-agent ISOLATED `protocol-knowledge` room/entity — distinct from BOTH the KnowledgeProvider's platform-agent-keyed book-knowledge room AND the `earned-skill:<avatarId>` scope, so the manual never bleeds into teacher/human-chat RAG. Deduped by `protocol-knowledge:v<version>:<index>` + `unique:true`. Mirrors `recordEarnedSkillMemory`: embed-first, fail-soft per chunk, NEVER lazy-starts, never throws, no secret. **Caller:** `agent-orchestrator.startAgent` runs `injectProtocolKnowledgeIntoRuntime(runtime)` fire-and-forget AFTER `runtime.start()`, gated to PLAYER runtimes (`agentType==='avatar-agent' || isHouseAgent`) — teacher `location-agent`s + Nori `system-agent` don't play in-world, so injecting there would be RAG noise. A `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump is picked up on the next runtime start (dedupe-by-version keeps it idempotent); no out-of-band `stopAgent` is needed because `knowledge` rows are read live at query time. Wiring point chosen: the orchestrator start hook (NOT `system-npc-seeder.ensureSystemAgents`, which only upserts DB `knowledge[]` and starts no runtime).
- **Nori (surface #1, `packages/agent-templates/src/locations/town-guide.ts` `knowledge[]`):** slice-6 entries added for the goal stream, the Autonomous-mode chat-bar directive, run-a-store, and hosted `[ACTION:]` — each pointing agents at `/api/skills/manifest.json` for the exact contract ("point at the manual, don't replace it").
- **No DDL, no new endpoint:** every endpoint documented at v10 already shipped in slices 1-4; slice 6 is docs (surfaces #1/#2) + the surface-#3 injection code. **PARITY:** human path = the existing UIs (bottom chatter directive / Land Office services tab); agent path = the now-documented `:sessionId` / `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` endpoints; settlement binds to the session's bound avatar exactly as before.

---

## 7. Auth phases (5, 5.1, 6)

### Phase 5 — agent-issued magic link (`b527636`)

Agent mints a one-time login URL for its human operator without password/OAuth.

- Table `agent_session_tickets`: 32-byte token, 5-min TTL, `consumed_at`.
- Service `session-ticket-service.ts`.
- Exchanger `GET /api/auth/enter?t=<ticket>` (`auth.ts:188-229`). Validates → marks consumed → mints Lucia cookie → 302 to `/game`. Expired/consumed → 302 with `?error=expired-link` → `ExpiredLinkBanner` on landing.

### Phase 5.1 — wallet identity + 'scape portal

Three keypair roles per user, all ed25519:

```
users.id (UUID PK, stable)                  ← identity handle; never rotates
├── identity keypair
│    pub: users.identity_pubkey             ← rotatable
│    priv (primary): agent config           clawville:identity:<userId>
│    priv (backup):  users.identity_encrypted_sk (envelope-encrypted, support recovery only)
│    purpose: sign reconnect challenges; derives portal principalId
│
└── avatar wallet keypair (Solana)
     row: wallets{subject_type='avatar', subject_id=avatar.id}
     pub: wallets.public_key                ← mirrored to avatars.walletAddress
     priv (server): wallets.encrypted_secret_key  (envelope-encrypted under CF KEK)
     priv (human):  shown ONCE in first-connect response
     priv (agent):  never holds
     purpose: holds $CLAWVILLE; server signs custodially
```

Plus a **service issuer keypair** (singleton). Private key in Cloudflare Secrets Store; public published at `GET /.well-known/clawville-issuer.json`. Signs outbound partner calls. **Published purposes:** `partner-portal-issue`, `partner-portal-accept-link`, `partner-cognition-callback`, `partner-session-webhook`, and **`partner-launch-exchange`** (FIX-14, 2026-06-13 — signs the Hatcher owner-launch server-to-server exchange body in `partner-hatcher-launch.ts`). Relay note R8: `partner-launch-exchange` is only load-bearing if Hatcher's exchange verifier enforces signed-purpose scoping; today verification succeeds on pubkey match regardless of purpose, so this is a parity-safety add, not a fix to a current break.

**Reconnect (signed-challenge):** `POST /api/agent/challenge` → nonce → `POST /api/agent/reconnect { userId, nonce, signature: ed25519.sign(nonce, identityPrivKey) }` → mint session ticket. Wallet key never involved.

**Atomic identity bootstrap:** `UPDATE users SET identity_pubkey=$1, ... WHERE id=$2 AND identity_pubkey IS NULL RETURNING`. Race losers get `needsHumanReauth: true`.

**Envelope encryption (`keypair-vault.ts`):** each encrypted secret has a random 32-byte DEK; DEK is wrapped by Cloudflare-held KEK via the Worker at `CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_URL` (POST `/wrap`, `/unwrap`, bearer-authed). Hetzner never sees plaintext KEK. **Boot preflight (2026-06-15):** `CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_URL` + `CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_BEARER` are HARD-REQUIRED at startup — `apps/api/src/index.ts` asserts both are set on boot and `process.exit(1)`s (crash-loud, mirroring `FINGERPRINT_SECRET`) if either is missing, so a misconfigured box refuses to start rather than booting fine and failing on the first wallet op. The lazy `requireWorkerEnv` check inside `keypair-vault.ts` stays as defense-in-depth.

**Portal (ClawVille → 'scape):** `POST /api/portal/scape` → server builds canonical-JSON body → signs `sha256(body)` with service issuer SK → **SSRF-guards `SCAPE_HOSTED_SESSION_URL` via `validateOutboundUrlResolved` (DNS private-IP reject, no host allowlist — 'scape is not a Hatcher host; fail-closed 503 + `portal.scape.cross_failed{reason:'ssrf_blocked'}`)** → POSTs (`redirect:'error'`) to the validated URL with `X-Clawville-Issuer-Pubkey` + `X-Clawville-Signature` → emits `portal.scape.crossed` → returns `{ redirectUrl }`.

**Portal ('scape → ClawVille):** `POST /api/portal/mint-for-scape` — verifies `X-Scape-*` against `PARTNER_PUBKEYS.scape` allowlist env var, mints Phase 5 magic-link ticket.

**Account link (existing 'scape user):** `POST /api/portal/scape-link-code` (Lucia-authed) → user pastes in 'scape → `POST /api/portal/accept-scape-link` → consumes `pending_account_links` row atomically.

**Partner generalization (2026-06-01):** the inbound signature verifier is `verifyPartnerSignature(partnerId, …)` — `partnerId` selects the `PARTNER_PUBKEYS[partnerId]` allowlist entry; the ed25519-over-`sha256(rawBody)` crypto is partner-agnostic. Scape callers pass `'scape'`, Hatcher callers pass `'hatcher'`. `PARTNER_PUBKEYS` is a JSON map keyed by partner id, so adding a partner is one env key + four mirrored endpoints — no crypto change. **Cross-partner redemption fix (same diff):** both `accept-*-link` handlers now require `pending_account_links.remote_world` to equal the partner the endpoint serves, so a code minted for one partner can't be redeemed via another's endpoint; a mismatch returns the same opaque `link_expired_or_invalid` (404) as a missing code.

**Replay residual (FIX-9, 2026-06-13 — ACCEPTED, not a coded cache):** within the ±300s freshness window the same partner-signed bytes verify more than once (ed25519 over identical bytes verifies forever). This is a **documented, accepted residual**, not a hole: every partner write is **idempotent-by-agentId** (register/PATCH upsert the same row, DELETE no-ops once gone) so a replay yields no NEW effect, and the window matches Hatcher's agreed `authNonceExpirySecs:300` skew ceiling. A seen-signature cache was **prototyped and then REVERTED** (Codex review 2026-06-13): consuming a signature inside the verifier — before the route's durable outcome is known — would falsely `401 replayed_signature` a LEGITIMATE client retry after a 400/5xx/dropped response, a worse failure than the benign replay it blocks. **Future rule:** any NON-idempotent partner verb added later MUST carry proper replay protection at that time — a partner-supplied nonce bound into the signed challenge and consumed delete-on-read (mirror `consumeNonce` in `auth-challenge.ts`), gated on the route's durable success so a retry of a failed request is never mistaken for a replay. The verifier `verifyPartnerSignature` stays pure.

**Hatcher portal (partner #2, 2026-06-01 — `.claude/plans/hatcher-integration.md` §2):** a faithful mirror of the four scape endpoints. `POST /api/portal/hatcher` (Lucia, CV→Hatcher; signs + **SSRF-guards `HATCHER_HOSTED_SESSION_URL` via `validateHatcherProxyUrlResolved` — the SAME hatcher: host-allowlist + DNS private-IP guard the cognition path uses; fail-closed 503 + `portal.hatcher.cross_failed{reason:'ssrf_blocked'}`** — then POSTs (`redirect:'error'`) to the validated URL, relays the returned `sessionToken` into a `HATCHER_WEB_ORIGIN` redirect). `POST /api/portal/mint-for-hatcher` (Hatcher sig via `X-Hatcher-*`, same `principal:clawville:<uuid>` anti-enumeration regex, mints a `identityType:'portal-hatcher'` magic-link ticket). `POST /api/portal/accept-hatcher-link` (Hatcher sig; requires `remote_world='hatcher'`; writes `linked_hatcher_*` atomically). `POST /api/portal/hatcher-link-code` (Lucia; mints a pending link with `remote_world:'hatcher'`, 10-min TTL). Reuses `CLAWVILLE_SERVICE_ISSUER_SK/_PUBKEY` unchanged (one outbound keypair serves all partners). Per-IP rate limiters: portal 10/min, link-code 3/min. The outbound "Cross to Hatcher" frontend UI is deferred until Hatcher ships their session-issue endpoint.

### Phase 6 — per-user building-character memory isolation (`51e97cb`)

Every user gets an isolated memory partition with each building character. One ElizaOS runtime per character; partitioned rooms per `(userId, locationId)`.

- Primitive `characterRoomId(locationId, userId) → UUIDv5` in `packages/agent-runtime/src/room-scoping.ts`. Namespace `8f3b1b27-5f2a-4a8d-9c1d-2e7b4d1f6a9c`.
- Read/write gate: `processMessage` in `@clawville/agent-runtime` keys every memory lookup on the derived `roomId`. Legacy string `roomId`s are ignored.

---

## 8. Database schema (`packages/database/src/schema/`)

All schema files re-exported from `schema/index.ts`. **Single source of truth for column types — this table is a summary.**

| Table | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `users` / `sessions` | Lucia auth. Phase 5: `identity_fingerprint`. Phase 5.1: `identity_pubkey/encrypted_sk/iv/tag/dek_wrapped/encryption_version`, plus 'scape `scape_principal_id / scape_world_character_id / linked_scape_*`. **Hatcher (partner #2, 2026-06-01):** 6 mirror columns `hatcher_principal_id` (UNIQUE) / `hatcher_world_character_id` (UNIQUE) / `linked_hatcher_principal_id` (UNIQUE) / `linked_hatcher_world_character_id` (UNIQUE) / `linked_hatcher_display_name` / `linked_hatcher_at` — same types/constraints as the scape columns. Added via `migrations-manual/2026-06-01_add_user_hatcher_portal_columns.sql` (idempotent raw `ALTER … ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`, applied manually against shared Supabase). Guest auto-create: `is_guest`, `guest_expires_at`. **Username (2026-05-19):** `username VARCHAR(20) UNIQUE`, check constraint `users_username_format = '^[a-zA-Z0-9_]{3,20}$'`. Seeded from `avatar.name` on first `POST /api/avatars`; editable via `PATCH /api/users/me/username`. Independent from `avatar.name` thereafter. See `GameFeatures.md §14a.bis`. |
| `agent_session_tickets` | Phase 5 magic-link store. 32-byte token, 5-min TTL, `consumed_at`. |
| `avatars` | One per user. Identity/species/color/archetype/stats/position. Phase 2: `model_key`, `agent_category`, `harness` (NOT NULL + CHECK). **CHECK `avatars_agent_category_valid` gained `'hatcher'` (partner #2, 2026-06-01)** — mirrors the shared `AGENT_CATEGORIES` tuple (the avatars route validates with `z.enum(AGENT_CATEGORIES)` + writes `agent_category`, so the CHECK must stay in lockstep). Connected Hatcher agents render via `openclaw_bots.species`, NOT an avatars row; the CHECK add is defense-in-depth for the human/direct-API write path. Applied via `migrations-manual/2026-06-01_add_hatcher_agent_category.sql` (idempotent drop+recreate; new predicate is a superset so no existing row is rejected; NOT auto-run — db:push flaky on `avatars`). Guest: `is_guest`. **Tokenomics F1 vCLAW provenance (2026-06-27):** `claw_tokens` stays the ONE displayed TOTAL; three new NOT-NULL per-tag balance columns `soft_balance` (default **100**, mirrors `claw_tokens`'s default so a bare INSERT satisfies the sum CHECK) / `bought_balance` / `earned_balance` (default 0) split it by CASHABILITY (O(1) read for the spend allocator, vs deriving from ledger rows). HARD INVARIANT, maintained atomically by `claw-token-ledger.ts` in the same FOR-UPDATE tx as every balance change + enforced by the new DB CHECK `avatars_vclaw_balance_sum` (`claw_tokens = soft + bought + earned`). Backfill folds legacy `claw_tokens` entirely into `soft_balance` (legacy CT was never paid for ⇒ SOFT). Added via `migrations-manual/2026-06-27_vclaw_provenance.sql` (idempotent; apply via `packages/database/scripts/apply-vclaw-provenance.ts` with an explicit `TOKENOMICS_DATABASE_URL`, NOT db:push). |
| `avatar_inventory` | Knowledge books owned per avatar (quantity). |
| `map_locations` | 10 static building zones (seeded). |
| `location_agents` | Per-user agent config at each location. |
| `platform_agents` | ElizaOS agent records. System agents use `type='system-agent' + customization.slug=<slug>`. Partial unique index `platform_agents_system_singleton`. |
| `platform_agent_logs` | Agent activity logs. |
| `openclaw_bots` | External agent identity, gateway, learned knowledge, session count, `session_expires_at` (Phase 6 sliding 24h TTL). **Hatcher proxy-cognition (partner #2, Phase A — 2026-06-01):** `cognition_backend` (null \| `'hatcher-proxy'`), `proxy_url` (Hatcher per-agent proxy base URL, SSRF-allowlisted at call time), and the scoped bearer token ENCRYPTED AT REST in `proxy_token_enc` / `proxy_token_iv` / `proxy_token_tag` (AES-256-GCM under `VANITY_ENCRYPTION_KEY`, same envelope shape as identity/treasury secrets — NEVER plaintext, NEVER logged; decrypted in-memory only at callback time). Added via `migrations-manual/2026-06-01_add_openclaw_cognition_proxy.sql` (idempotent `ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS`, applied manually). Set by `POST /api/partner/hatcher/agents`; scrubbed by `DELETE` (tombstone). **Agent-metaverse P1 (2026-07-01):** `is_house BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false` marks a ClawVille-HOSTED autonomous "house" agent (first fleet member, seeded by `house-agent-seeder.ts`). INTERNAL-ONLY — drives the body-idle-sweeper EXEMPTION + server-side house-agent enumeration; NEVER serialized to any snapshot / `/rooms` roster / wire (a house agent is indistinguishable from any other agent to outsiders). A house row carries `session_expires_at = NULL` (never expires — the 24h session sweeper skips it). Added via `migrations-manual/2026-07-01_add_openclaw_is_house.sql` (idempotent, applied manually). |
| `agent_configs` | Export/import bundles. |
| `building_skills` | Compiled SKILL.md cache keyed by `buildingId`. Served from `/api/skills/:b/skill.md`; rebuilt by `skill-generator`. **`content_hash varchar(64)` (Hatcher Phase C, 2026-06-01)** — sha256 of the served body, backfilled by `scripts/generate-building-skills.ts` so the manifest can diff per-skill (nullable; the manifest also computes it live, so the column is an optimization not a dependency). Added via `migrations-manual/2026-06-01_add_partner_api_keys_and_skill_content_hash.sql` (NOT applied). |
| `partner_api_keys` | **(Hatcher Phase C, 2026-06-01)** Scoped, revocable read tokens for partner integrations. Cols: `id`, `partner_id`, `key_hash` (sha256 of the bearer, UNIQUE — the request-time lookup key; raw token NEVER stored), `key_prefix` (non-secret display chars), `scopes text[]`, `label`, `created_at`, `last_used_at`, `revoked_at`. Minted by `scripts/mint-partner-key.ts` (show-once `hk_` token, hash-only persist — no recovery path). Validated by `requirePartnerKey(scope)` middleware gating the high-volume skill-read surfaces; revoke via `revoked_at` without touching the ed25519 `PARTNER_PUBKEYS` allowlist. Same migration as `building_skills.content_hash` above (NOT applied). |
| `npc_memories` | NPC convo memory for `npc-conversation-engine`. |
| `activity_log` | Append-only feed for sidebar. |
| `research_articles` | Cached external scrapes. |
| `wallets` | Unified `subject_type ∈ {avatar, agent, treasury}`. Encrypted Solana keypairs. Phase 5.1 envelope: `dek_wrapped`, `encryption_version` (1=legacy, 2=envelope). Dispatcher in `keypair-vault.decryptWalletRow`. |
| `withdrawals` | **Custodial wallet WITHDRAW ledger (2026-07-08, DARK behind `WALLET_WITHDRAW_ENABLED`)** — exactly-once state machine for moving deposited on-chain SOL/USDC/CLV out of the custodial avatar wallet: `subject_type ('user'|'agent', CHECK)` · `avatar_id FK` (the signing wallet's owner, middleware-resolved) · `asset ('SOL'|'USDC'|'CLV', CHECK)` · `amount_atomic numeric(30,0) CHECK > 0` (on-chain base units, NEVER ClawTokens) · `destination` · `status withdrawal_status` (`pending→sending→sent`; terminal `failed`/`reconcile` — reconcile NEVER auto-retried) · `tx_signature` (captured BEFORE send; partial-UNIQUE `withdrawals_txsig_unique`) · `claim_id`/`claimed_at` (atomic claim) · `idempotency_key` (partial-UNIQUE `withdrawals_idem_unique (subject_type, avatar_id, idempotency_key)` — retry = replay, never a 2nd send) · CHECK `sent ⇒ tx_signature NOT NULL`. Migration `0021_wallet_withdrawals.sql` (guarded CREATE TYPE — brand-new enum so single-txn-safe for migrate-ci; CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS; apply via migrate-ci — NEVER db:push). |
| `treasury_wallets` | Phase 4 x402 merchant supply + vanity set. Coexists with `wallets` for legacy rows. `treasury_purpose` enum extended 2026-05-13 to include `'wager-settlement-authority'` — the ed25519 keypair the API uses to sign wager lobby lock/settle/authority-cancel txs against the `clawville_wager` program. |
| `vanity_keypairs` | Pre-generated vanity public keys, encrypted at rest. |
| `treasury_subjects` | **Tokenomics T0 (2026-07-07)** — first-class registry NAMING the singleton house-treasury CT subject(s): `id uuid PK`, `purpose text NOT NULL UNIQUE` (T0 singleton `'house-fees'`), `avatar_id uuid NOT NULL FK→avatars(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT` (the balance-bearing system avatar — the ledger + the `avatars_vclaw_balance_sum` CHECK can only hold a balance on an avatars row), `notes`, `created_at`. Seeded idempotently on boot by `services/house-treasury-seeder.ts`; every routed fee site credits this subject’s avatar via `creditClawTokens(..., tx)` composed into the settle/purchase tx (reasons `house_fee_*`, `source='system'`, SOFT). Distinct from `treasury_wallets` (Solana keypair custody). Migration `packages/database/migrations/0007_treasury_subjects.sql` (idempotent `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS`, additive-only, drizzle-convention constraint names `treasury_subjects_purpose_unique` / `treasury_subjects_avatar_id_avatars_id_fk`; apply by hand/CI — NEVER db:push). |
| `clv_price_snapshots` | **Tokenomics T0 (2026-07-07)** — durable price history behind the READ-ONLY CLV price oracle (`services/clv-price-oracle.ts`): `id uuid PK`, `price_usd numeric(20,12) NOT NULL` (spot USD, full precision for a sub-cent token — a USD DECIMAL, NEVER a ClawToken amount), `source text NOT NULL` (`'helius'`/`'dexscreener'`; `'last_known'` is a quote-level label, never a stored row), `created_at timestamptz` (indexed `clv_price_snapshots_created_at_idx` for the 30-min TWAP window + `history` ordering). One row per successful poll; seeds the in-memory TWAP across restarts. NEVER references `avatars` / the ledger. Migration `packages/database/migrations/0008_clv_price_snapshots.sql` (idempotent `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` + `CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS`, additive-only, no enum changes; apply by hand/CI — NEVER db:push). |
| `clv_buy_queue` | **Tokenomics C3 (0014) + GoLive executor columns (0019/0019a, 2026-07-07)** — one row per owed CLV buy (USD decimals, NEVER vCLAW): `amount_usdc numeric(20,6)`, `quoted_price`, `max_slippage`, `status clv_buy_status` (`planned`->`executing`->`executed`, `skipped`; `executing` = the live executor's atomic claim), `reason`, `source_ref`, GoLive cols `claim_id uuid`/`claimed_at`/`executed_at`/`executed_price numeric(20,12)`/`tx_signatures jsonb` (per-clip fills, captured BEFORE each send)/`skipped_reason`. Partial UNIQUE `clv_buy_queue_reason_source_ref_uniq (reason, source_ref) WHERE source_ref IS NOT NULL` = enqueue idempotency (replay = existing queueId). |
| `clv_swap_funding` | **Tokenomics GoLive executors (0019, 2026-07-07)** — exactly-once trail for the merchant->swap-wallet USDC FUNDING SWEEP (dark until the `CLV_SWAP_EXECUTE` seam opens): `source_ref text UNIQUE` (double-sweep guard), `checkout_id` (amounts tied to SETTLED checkouts only), `amount_usdc numeric(20,6)`, `status text` (`pending`->`sweeping`->`swept`; terminal `failed`/`reconcile` — reconcile is NEVER auto-retried), `sweep_tx_signature` (captured BEFORE send; partial-UNIQUE `clv_swap_funding_sweep_sig_uniq`), `claim_id`/`claimed_at`/`swept_at`/`failure_reason`. Never touches `avatars.clawTokens`. |
| `token_launches` | Per-agent token launches (Phase 4). |
| `claw_token_transactions` | **Canonical ClawToken (vCLAW) ledger** — append-only. Every credit/debit goes through `claw-token-ledger.ts` (`creditClawTokens` / `debitClawTokens` / `transferClawTokens` / `mintEarned`). **Tokenomics F1 vCLAW provenance (2026-06-27):** 4 new columns — `provenance` (enum `claw_token_provenance` = `soft\|bought\|earned`, nullable for pre-F1 rows; the CASHABILITY tag for this row's delta), `usd_basis numeric(20,6)` (set for `bought` receipts + `earned` mints, the payer-set real-dollar backing), `fp_hash` / `ip_prefix_hash text` (anti-abuse scaffolding stored by `mintEarned`; enforcement is a LATER feature). New index `claw_token_tx_provenance_idx (provenance, created_at)`. **EARNED CHOKEPOINT (plan §3.1 — laundering defense):** `earned` is written in EXACTLY ONE place, `mintEarned()`; the public `creditClawTokens` provenance param type is `'soft' \| 'bought'` (earned UNrepresentable) + a private capability token + a runtime throw enforce it 3 ways. `transferClawTokens` ALWAYS credits the receiver `soft` (internal recirculation can never become cashable). **Spend order SOFT→BOUGHT→EARNED** (burn non-cashable first; preserve the cashable balance); a multi-tag debit emits ONE ledger row per tag burned. F1 is ledger-only / GATED-OFF — nothing is cashable yet (no cash-out path). See `migrations-manual/2026-06-27_vclaw_provenance.sql` + `.claude/plans/tokenomics-vclaw-v1.md §3/§5/§6`. |
| ~~`bazaar_listings`, `bazaar_transactions`, `bazaar_reviews`, `published_skills`, `skill_upvotes`, `auctions`, `auction_bids`, `auction_agent_configs`~~ | **PEER SKILL COMMERCE DELETED 2026-07-02** (prompt-injection risk). Schema files + barrel exports + relations removed. The 8 tables are **LEFT ORPHANED in the DB** — no destructive DROP migration was written; the founder drops them as a separate approved op. `quests.skill_reward_id`, `quest_rewards.skill_id`, `bounty_rewards.skill_id` FK columns were also removed from the schema code (orphaned in DB) since they referenced `published_skills`. |
| `quests`, `quest_submissions`, `quest_rewards` | Quest board + submissions + payouts (links to `claw_token_transactions`). |
| `bounties`, `bounty_rewards`, `bounty_attempts`, `bounty_reputation` | Community bounties + payouts + attempts + reputation rollup. `bounties` carries the Phase-1 USDC-rail columns (additive, 2026-06-30): `payment_rail` (`bounty_payment_rail` enum 'ct'/'usdc', default 'ct'), `acceptance_criteria`, `escrow_pda`, `escrow_job_id`, `covenant_audit_root_hex`, `covenant_verification_passed`, `covenant_verdict_id`, `verdict_required`. A 'usdc' bounty binds to a `sap_escrow_settlements` row (jobId=bounty.id) instead of a CT ledger debit; the escrow settlement/approval ledger stays in `sap_escrow_settlements`/`sap_escrow_approvals`. |
| `events` | **Metrics spine.** Append-only. Cols: `id bigserial, ts, event_type, user_id FK, agent_id, avatar_id FK, building_id, session_id, payload jsonb, fp_hash, ip_prefix_hash`. Indexes on `(event_type, ts)`, `(agent_id, ts)`, `(avatar_id, ts)`, `(building_id, ts)`, and the partial `(agent_id, id) WHERE agent_id IS NOT NULL` (`idx_events_agent_id_cursor`, P3 slice-1 agent event-replay cursor; DDL `apps/api/scripts/ddl/2026-07-04-events-agent-cursor-index.ts`). |
| `event_write_failures` | Safety net for the metrics spine. Persists attempted row + error on primary insert failure. Partial index on unretried rows. |
| `pending_account_links` | Phase 5.1 one-time codes for existing partner-user linking (`remote_world ∈ {'scape','hatcher'}`). 10-min TTL. Consumed atomically by `POST /api/portal/accept-<partner>-link`; the accept handler now requires `remote_world` to match the partner endpoint (cross-partner redemption fix, 2026-06-01). |
| `reef_race_personal_bests` | One row per `(avatarId, activityId)`. Cols: `bestLapMs`, `bestLapRecordedAt`, `sourceRoomId`, `ghostReplayData jsonb`. Composite index on `(best_lap_recorded_at DESC, best_lap_ms ASC) WHERE activity_id='reef-race'`. |
| `activity_results` | Per-participant match outcome. Cols include `matchBestStreak`, `matchPbDailyRank`, `acknowledged_at`. |
| `activity_seasons` | Q2-Q3 season catalogue. UNIQUE on `name`. |
| `activity_rooms` | One row per match. Persisted on PENDING → COUNTDOWN. `status: 'countdown' \| 'live' \| 'completed' \| 'aborted' \| 'aborted_crash'`. `started_at` / `ended_at` set on the LIVE / RESULTS transitions. |
| `activity_room_participants` | Per-participant rows for an `activity_rooms` row. Holds `avatar_id`, `subject_type`, joined/left timestamps, the per-room loadout snapshot. |
| `activity_queue_entries` | Matchmaking queue rows. `activity_id`, `avatar_id`, `subject_type`, `queued_at`. Cleared on match or leave-queue. |
| `activity_parties` | Pre-formed parties (e.g. group queue with friends). Drives matchmaker grouping. |
| `activity_replays` | Recorded match frames for post-game playback (currently used for Reef Race ghost replays). |
| `lobbies` | Per-match wager lobby. `lobby_id bigint UNIQUE` mirrors the on-chain seed (sequence `wager_lobby_id_seq`). State machine: `'open' → 'locked' → 'settled' | 'cancelled'` enforced via CHECK constraint. `activity_id` + `room_id` link to existing activity infra. `visibility` `'public' | 'private' | 'friends'`; `'private'` / `'friends'` use `invite_code`. `mode` `'multiplayer' | 'solo-bots'` (solo-bots skips escrow entirely). `wager_amount_lamports = 0` = free lobby. On-chain tx sigs tracked per state transition (`on_chain_{create,lock,settle,cancel}_sig`). |
| `lobby_players` | One row per joiner per lobby. UNIQUE `(lobby_id, user_id)` mirrors on-chain Player PDA. Tracks `deposit_amount_lamports`, `refunded` flag, `on_chain_join_sig` + `on_chain_refund_sig`. |
| `lobby_events` | Audit log keyed off `(lobby_id, occurred_at desc)`. `kind` enum: `created | joined | locked | settled | cancelled | refunded | cleanup`. `raw_event_json` holds decoded Anchor event when available. |
| `cosmetic_skus` / `cosmetic_variants` / `avatar_skins` | Q3 Phase 3.3 cosmetic engine. `cosmetic_skus` is the catalog, `cosmetic_variants` holds per-rig assets (sunglasses-on-Milady ≠ sunglasses-on-lobster), `avatar_skins` is the ownership ledger + equipped flag. Scope-aware (`scope='avatar' \| 'all' \| 'activity:reef-race'`). License-attribution columns for CC-BY assets. |
| `dashboard_phases` | Q3 plan §10 dashboard phase tracking — which phases of the gamification rollout are live / staged / gated. |
| `tutorial_quest_claims` | Append-only ledger of which avatars claimed which tutorial quests + when. Source for §13b progression analytics on `/dash`. |
| `slot_sessions` | **Phase 6.1 slice 3** (2026-05-19) — one row per "sit down at a slot machine." Holds commit-reveal pair (`server_seed` redacted while `status='open'`, `server_seed_hash` published at open), engine cursor (`nonce_counter`, monotone `cursor_counter`), money accounting (`starting_balance`, `current_balance`, `escrow_amount`, `total_staked`, `total_won` — all `text` so bigint precision survives), state machine (`status: open\|closed\|expired`), bonus-mode state (`mode: base\|free-spin`, `free_spins_remaining` integer; LIVE since 6.1.5). **Phase 6.7.5** (2026-05-28) — `user_id` now nullable + new nullable `guest_fp_hash text` for unauth NPC-mode guest plays; DB check constraint `slot_sessions_subject_check` enforces XOR ((`user_id IS NOT NULL`) XOR (`guest_fp_hash IS NOT NULL`)). Partial unique indexes: `slot_sessions_user_open_unique` (where `status='open' AND user_id IS NOT NULL`) for authed one-open-session-per-user race-safety, mirrored by `slot_sessions_guest_open_unique` (where `status='open' AND guest_fp_hash IS NOT NULL`) for guests. Guest `starting_balance` is a 100-fun-CT demo wallet (NOT the per-spin predict snapshot the authed flow uses); the spin handler reads `starting_balance + total_won - total_staked` to derive the live demo balance and refuses spins that would go negative. No `avatars.clawTokens` debit/credit ever fires on guest spins — fun-money lives entirely on this row. Claimed by `POST /api/cove/history/claim` on signup (re-stamps `user_id` + nulls `guest_fp_hash`). |
| `slot_spins` | **Phase 6.1 slice 3** — every spin within a session, full audit trail for the public verifier. RNG inputs (`nonce`, `cursor_before`, `cursor_after`) + outputs (`reels`, `winning_lines` jsonb, `win_amount` text) + idempotency (`idempotency_key`). Unique index `(session_id, idempotency_key)` is the race-safe backstop for retried `POST /spin`; duplicates throw 23505 and the route falls back to the cached row. |
| `cove_game_events` | **Phase 6.7.0** (2026-05-27) — cross-game unified history store backing the four cove games (slots, blackjack, hold'em, baccarat). **Phase 6.7.5** (2026-05-28) — `user_id` nullable + new nullable `guest_fp_hash text` so unauth guest plays survive; DB check constraint `cove_game_events_subject_check` enforces XOR ((`user_id IS NOT NULL`) XOR (`guest_fp_hash IS NOT NULL`)). Authed indexes `cove_game_events_user_created_at_idx` + `cove_game_events_user_game_created_at_idx` are now partial `WHERE user_id IS NOT NULL`; new partial `cove_game_events_guest_fp_created_at_idx` on `(guest_fp_hash, created_at DESC) WHERE guest_fp_hash IS NOT NULL` powers the guest read path. Guest plays do NOT count toward `/dash` daily caps — `cove_game_events` is a player-facing history table, NOT a leaderboard event source. Claim path (`POST /api/cove/history/claim`) re-stamps `user_id` + nulls `guest_fp_hash` after signup so post-claim rows are indistinguishable from natively-authed rows. Migration: `scripts/casino/migrate-guest-history.mjs` (idempotent raw-SQL via pg + DATABASE_URL — apply via `docker exec <api-container> bun /tmp/migrate-guest-history.mjs` per CLAUDE.md Drizzle-kit fallback recipe). One row per atomic gameplay unit (slots: per spin; blackjack/baccarat: per hand/coup; hold'em: per HAND with action sequence inside `outcome_json`). Cols: `id uuid PK, user_id uuid FK→users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NULLABLE, guest_fp_hash text NULLABLE, game_type text ('slots'\|'blackjack'\|'holdem'\|'baccarat'), session_id text, shoe_id text NOT NULL (slots: shoe_id===session_id), bet_amount text (stringified bigint — matches slot money-column convention so lamport/µUSDC precision survives), payout text (stringified bigint), outcome_json jsonb (discriminated union by game_type — types in @clawville/shared/types/cove-history.ts, Zod-validated on write+render per plan §9), server_seed_hash text, revealed_server_seed text nullable (NULL until parent session/shoe closes — commit-reveal contract per plan §0 decision 3), client_seed text, nonce bigint mode 'number' (per-session monotone — slots: spin index; blackjack: hand index within shoe), tx_signature text nullable (Phase 6.7.4 real-money tier), engine_version text default 'v1' (pins replay code so old rows verify post-engine-bump — mirrors slot_spins.paytable_version), created_at`. Indexes: `(user_id, created_at DESC)`, `(shoe_id)`, `(user_id, game_type, created_at DESC)`. **Unique:** `(game_type, session_id, nonce)` — the idempotency key for the backfill script's `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` AND for live-route safety against double-write retries. Writes happen in the SAME transaction as the per-game source-of-truth table (slots: `slot_spins` insert; blackjack/hold'em/baccarat join in 6.7.1/2/3). Session close updates every event row for the session with the revealed seed so client-side verifier can replay deterministically. Routes: `GET /api/cove/history?game=&limit=&cursor=` (owner-only, keyset paginated 50/page) + `GET /api/cove/history/:eventId/verify` (owner OR ADMIN_USER_IDS — slots runs server-side `runSpin` replay + sha256 hash check; other games stub 'engine-not-yet-shipped' until 6.7.1+). Backfill: `scripts/casino/backfill-slot-history.ts` (idempotent — `ON CONFLICT (game_type, session_id, nonce) DO NOTHING`; spins from currently-OPEN slot_sessions land with NULL revealed_server_seed and become verifiable on session close). Drizzle-kit fallback SQL: `packages/database/migrations-manual/2026-05-27_add_cove_game_events.sql`. |
| `holdem_tables` | **Phase 6.5.1** (2026-05-28) — one row per Texas Hold'em commit-reveal table SESSION. Mirrors `blackjack_shoes`. Cols: `id uuid PK, user_id uuid FK→users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NULLABLE, guest_fp_hash text NULLABLE` (XOR via `holdem_tables_subject_check`), `currency text default 'clawtoken'`, `server_seed text` (redacted while `status='open'`), `server_seed_hash text` (committed at open), `client_seed text`, `hand_counter integer` (RNG nonce, pre-increment), `buy_in_stack text` + `player_stack text` (authoritative human bankroll, stringified bigint — chips move WITHIN player_stack; ledger only at buy-in/cash-out), `starting_balance text`, `total_bet`/`total_payout text`, `status text ('open'\|'closed')`, `hands_played integer`, `engine_version text default 'th-v1'`, timestamps. Partial unique indexes `holdem_tables_user_open_unique` / `holdem_tables_guest_open_unique` (one open table per subject). UNLIKE blackjack there is NO shared shoe — each hand shuffles a fresh 52-card deck keyed on `(seed, handIndex)`, so no `cursor_counter`/`dealt_count`. **Increment 1b** (2026-07-03) adds `cash_out text` nullable — the stringified-bigint amount cashed out at `POST /session/close`, persisted so a close-replay (table already `'closed'`, same owner re-POSTs) can reconstruct the original response without re-crediting (`playerStack` is zeroed at close, so that column alone can't recover the figure). Migration: `packages/database/migrations-manual/2026-07-03_holdem_1b_columns.sql` (+ `packages/database/scripts/apply-holdem-1b-columns.ts`), NOT db:push. |
| `holdem_hands` | **Phase 6.5.1** (2026-05-28) — one row per HAND. Mirrors `blackjack_hands`. Cols: `id uuid PK, table_id uuid FK→holdem_tables(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, hand_index integer` (RNG nonce + per-hand deck key), `button_seat integer` (0–5, rotates `handIndex % 6`; blinds/action-order derive from it), `starting_stack text` (human stack at hand start), `actions jsonb` (recorded human decisions `{type,amount?}[]` — the engine replays them, running bots between each), `status text ('in_progress'\|'settled')` (terminal flip under FOR UPDATE = idempotency), `outcome_json jsonb` (serializeHoldemHand shape), `bet_amount`/`payout`/`net`/`ending_stack text`, `idempotency_key text`, timestamps. Unique `(table_id, hand_index)` + partial unique `(table_id, idempotency_key) WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL`. Migration: `scripts/casino/migrate-holdem-tables.mjs`. **Increment 1b** (2026-07-03) adds `deal_idempotency_key text` nullable + partial unique `(table_id, deal_idempotency_key) WHERE deal_idempotency_key IS NOT NULL` — DELIBERATELY separate from `idempotency_key` (the settle-replay anchor, overwritten at settle) so a lost-response `POST /hand/deal` retry with the same key replays the SAME hand without coupling to the terminal-settle machinery. Migration: `packages/database/migrations-manual/2026-07-03_holdem_1b_columns.sql` (+ `packages/database/scripts/apply-holdem-1b-columns.ts`), NOT db:push. |
| `baccarat_shoes` | **Phase 6.6.1** (2026-05-28) — one row per Baccarat commit-reveal SHOE. Mirrors `blackjack_shoes` (8-deck shared no-replacement shoe). Cols: `id uuid PK, user_id uuid FK→users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NULLABLE, guest_fp_hash text NULLABLE` (XOR via `baccarat_shoes_subject_check`), `currency text default 'clawtoken'`, `server_seed text` (redacted while `status='open'`), `server_seed_hash text` (committed at open), `client_seed text`, `coup_counter integer` (RNG nonce, pre-increment), `cursor_counter integer` (byte offset where the NEXT coup's draw starts), `dealt_count integer` (cards dealt; crosses 312 = 75% of 416 ⇒ shoe rolls), `starting_balance text`, `current_balance`/`total_bet`/`total_payout text` (stringified bigints), `status text ('open'\|'closed')`, `coups_played integer`, `engine_version text default 'bac-v1'`, timestamps (`last_coup_at`, `closed_at`). Partial unique indexes `baccarat_shoes_user_open_unique` / `baccarat_shoes_guest_open_unique` (one open shoe per subject). |
| `baccarat_coups` | **Phase 6.6.1** (2026-05-28) — one row per COUP. Mirrors `blackjack_hands` but SIMPLER: Punto Banco has no player decisions, so a coup is dealt + resolved atomically and the row is created already-`settled` under the shoe FOR UPDATE lock (no in-progress decision machine). Cols: `id uuid PK, shoe_id uuid FK→baccarat_shoes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, coup_index integer` (RNG nonce), `cursor_before integer` + `cursor_after integer` + `dealt_before integer` + `dealt_after integer` (shoe-global no-replacement counters for cheap reconstruction), `bet text ('player'\|'banker'\|'tie')`, `stake text` (stringified bigint), `status text ('in_progress'\|'settled')`, `outcome_json jsonb` (`serializeCoupResult` shape — player/banker hands + totals + winner + payout + commission), `payout`/`net text`, `idempotency_key text`, timestamps. Unique `(shoe_id, coup_index)` + partial unique `(shoe_id, idempotency_key) WHERE idempotency_key IS NOT NULL`. Migration: `scripts/casino/migrate-baccarat-tables.mjs`. |
| `sap_escrow_settlements` | **SAP Option C** (re-authored 2026-06-30; schema `sap-escrow.ts`) — the backend at-most-once-settle ledger for the on-chain USDC escrow gate (gated OFF; no row until flip-to-live). One row per `(escrow_pda, job_id)`; the `(escrow_pda, job_id)` UNIQUE index is the at-most-once-settle lock that REPLACES the missing on-chain receipt the deployed 0.18.0 program lacks. Cols: `id uuid PK, escrow_pda varchar(64) NOT NULL, job_id varchar(128) NOT NULL, depositor_avatar_id`/`worker_avatar_id uuid FK→avatars(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` (Rule E5 — both parties are avatars), `worker_wallet_pubkey`/`depositor_wallet_pubkey`/`token_mint varchar(64)`, `price_per_call varchar(32) NOT NULL`, the per-job + escrow-wide funds ledger `max_calls`/`funded_amount`/`calls_settled`/`released_amount`/`refunded_amount varchar(32)` (enforce sum(released)+sum(refunded) ≤ sum(funded) across the nonce-less shared vault), verification provenance `verification_provider varchar(64)`/`verification_passed boolean`/`audit_root_hex varchar(64)`/`verification_detail text`, `status sap_escrow_settlement_status (open\|submitted\|settling\|settled\|refunding\|refunded\|failed\|funding_unknown)`, `settle_signature`/`funding_signature varchar(128)`, `dry_run boolean default true`, `metadata jsonb`, timestamps + `settled_at`. Lifecycle: open→submit→approve→verify→settle (release vault→worker ATA) \| refund. The `refunding`/`funding_unknown` states are the #4 (refund-vs-settle TOCTOU) + #5 (broadcast-but-unconfirmed open) recovery claims, never auto-deleted. Apply by hand via `migrations-manual/2026-06-30_sap_escrow.sql` (+ `scripts/apply-sap-escrow.ts`), NOT db:push. |
| `sap_escrow_approvals` | **SAP Option C** (2026-06-30) — the depositor's PERSISTED, authenticated approval (the #1 fix: the ONLY thing that authorizes a settle; a worker can no longer forge a request-body approval to self-release). One row per `(escrow_pda, job_id)` (unique). Cols: `id uuid PK, escrow_pda varchar(64), job_id varchar(128), approver_avatar_id`/`worker_avatar_id uuid FK→avatars(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, approved_calls varchar(32)` (optional cap; NULL ⇒ full maxCalls), `approved_at timestamptz`. Written ONLY by the recorded depositor (`POST /escrow/usdc/approve`, `identity.avatarId === depositor` asserted); `settleJob` READS it to build the verification signal SERVER-SIDE. Same migration as `sap_escrow_settlements`. |
| `land_parcels` / `land_structures` / `land_upgrades` / `land_transactions` | **Land Economy (Phase 0/1, `packages/database/src/schema/land.ts`)** — was missing from this table; backfilled 2026-07-05 alongside the Slice-4 services rows below. `land_parcels`: one pre-seeded row per parcel (fixed supply), `tier` enum (`starter\|c\|b\|a\|founder`), `status` (`available\|owned\|reserved\|retired`), `tenure` (`rented\|owned\|starter`, null=unsold), server-stamped `price_ct`/`rent_ct_weekly`, `owner_avatar_id FK→avatars`. `land_structures`: one ACTIVE structure per parcel (UNIQUE `parcel_id`), `structure_type` (`home\|shop`), `catalog_key` (validated against `STRUCTURE_CATALOG`), `level` 1..5, `status` (`active\|archived` — an eviction archives rather than deletes so a re-acquire can restore the build). `land_upgrades` / `land_transactions`: append-only audit spines (upgrade ladder + the cross-domain buyer/seller/parcel/structure trail respectively); see `land.ts` route doc above for the full money-model narrative. |
| `service_listings` / `service_purchases` | **Run-a-store — P3 Slice 4** (`land.ts` schema, 2026-07-05). `service_listings`: one row per peer CT service an avatar lists on an owned/rented ACTIVE `'shop'` structure — `structure_id FK→land_structures`, `owner_avatar_id FK→avatars`, `kind` (`peer\|partner`, only `peer` written in v1), `title varchar(120)`, `description text`, `price_ct integer CHECK >= 0`, `status` (`active\|paused\|delisted`), `platform_fee_bps` (RESERVED at 0 — v1 is a full transfer to seller, no rake). `service_purchases`: one row per settled buy — `listing_id`/`buyer_avatar_id`/`seller_avatar_id FK`s, `price_ct`, `land_transaction_id` (cross-ref into `land_transactions`), `idempotency_key` (partial-unique — the buy route's replay-not-double-charge guard). Settlement composes `debitClawTokens(buyer)` + `creditClawTokens(seller, provenance:'soft')` + the `service_purchases` insert in ONE tx (conservation). |
| `partner_storefronts` / `ct_topups` | **Reserved / on-ramp (`land.ts` schema).** `partner_storefronts`: vetted-partner USDC storefront tier — RESERVED, `fulfillment_enabled` defaults false, admin-flip only, no v1 route writes it (see `partner-storefront.ts` §2 row). `ct_topups`: the CT-buy on-ramp ledger (Phase 4 x402/USDC rail; `stripe`/`clv` reserved) — `tx_signature` UNIQUE (partial, ignores NULL) is the double-credit guard on a settled payment. |
| `market_listings` / `market_deed_locks` / `market_settlements` | **P2P marketplace v1 (Tokenomics C4, `market.ts` schema, migration `0017_market_p2p.sql` — 2026-07-07).** SETTLEMENT FLAG-GATED OFF (`MARKETPLACE_SETTLE_ENABLED`). `market_listings`: one row per peer listing — `seller_avatar_id FK→avatars` (E5: human or agent), `item_kind` (`land_deed\|earned_bundle` — earned REFUSED in v1), `item_ref` (parcelId), `price_vclaw CHECK>0` (¢-peg QUOTE unit, never debited), `status` (`active\|pending_settlement\|settled\|cancelled\|expired` — full machine in the schema header; `expired` is a v1 PREDICATE, not a flip), `escrow_state`, `seller_wallet_pubkey` (the license-gate wallet = default payout destination), `buyer_avatar_id`/`settlement_checkout_id` set at settlement; partial-UNIQUE `(item_kind, item_ref) WHERE status IN ('active','pending_settlement')` = the double-list guard. `market_deed_locks`: MARKET-OWNED parcel transferability lock (PK `parcel_id FK→land_parcels`) — taken with the listing in one tx, released on cancel, HELD through `settled` until the Codex+land-gated deed-transfer executor runs; chosen over an ALTER on `land_parcels` so the land schema stays untouched + db:push-safe. `market_settlements`: ONE row per settled checkout (`checkout_id` UNIQUE FK→x402_checkouts — the exactly-once settlement key): tx signature + `usd_basis`, the funded C3 buy (`clv_buy_queue_id FK`), `rake_bps`=444 + `rake_usd` (4.44% treasury CLV-rake INTENT) + `seller_payout_usd` (95.56% seller CLV-payout INTENT, `payout_status` default + only-v1-written value `'pending_review'`; `approved\|rejected\|paid` RESERVED for the Codex-gated payout executor), `deed_transferred_at` stamped ONLY by the (dark) deed-transfer executor; CHECK `rake_usd + seller_payout_usd = usd_basis` (exact — the ¢-peg µUSD split has zero rounding). **GoLive executor trail (migration `0020_market_payout_deed_executors.sql` + `0020a`/`0020b`, 2026-07-07):** `market_settlements` += deed cols (`deed_transfer_claim_id`/`deed_transfer_started_at` audit + TERMINAL `deed_transfer_failure_reason`) and payout cols (`payout_claim_id`/`payout_claimed_at` atomic claim, `payout_seller_tx_signature`/`payout_rake_tx_signature` captured-before-send + partial-UNIQUE each, `payout_clv_atomic` + `payout_executed_rate` exact-integer stamps, `payout_executed_at`, `payout_failure_reason`), partial index `market_settlements_deed_pending_idx` (deed-scan hot path); `market_payout_status` += `'sending'` (0020a) + `'reconcile'` (0020b), each ALONE per the migrate-ci ALTER TYPE rule — the executor machine is `pending_review → sending → paid \| reconcile(TERMINAL)` with pre-capture failures releasing back to `pending_review`; `approved`/`rejected` stay RESERVED for a human-review layer. LEDGER-ONLY: no market table references a CT balance. |

`avatars.characterConfig` (JSONB) stores resolved archetype + learned knowledge.

## 10. Control modes

| Mode | WASD | Camera | Use case |
|---|---|---|---|
| Explore | Pan camera | Free orbit | Browse world without an avatar |
| Player | Move avatar | Follows avatar | Normal gameplay (requires agent) |
| NPC | Move possessed NPC | Follows NPC | Control any NPC (no agent required) |
| Autonomous | Disabled | Follows agent | Watch AI agent play |

Toggle via `<ControlModeToggle>`. Without agent → Explore/NPC. With agent → Player/Autonomous.

- Client-side wander (`stores/npc.ts`) takes over when disconnected.
