## 12. Deployment

Two Hetzner VPS hosts since the 2026-05-23 migration. Both orchestrated by Coolify, Cloudflare in front for DNS/CDN/DDoS. Real IPs, SSH keys, and app UUIDs in `scripts/deploy/.env.deploy` (gitignored — `PROD_VPS_IP=…` + `STAGING_VPS_IP=…`).

| Env | App | Coolify App ID | Git branch | Port | Domain |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| prod    | web | 3 | `master`  | 3000 | `clawville.world` (+ `new.clawville.world`) |
| prod    | api | 2 | `master`  | 4000 | `api.clawville.world` (+ `api-new.clawville.world`) |
| staging | web | 4 | `staging` | 3000 | `staging.clawville.world` |
| staging | api | 3 | `staging` | 4000 | `api-staging.clawville.world` |

SSH keys: PROD = `~/.ssh/clawville_hillsboro` (passphrase — load into Windows ssh-agent once), STAGING = `~/.ssh/clawville_deploy`. Coolify admin UIs: prod `https://coolify-new.clawville.world`, staging `https://coolify-staging.clawville.world`.

DB: Supabase Postgres (external, paid tier — endpoint in env). **Single instance shared across prod + staging** — any staging write touches prod data. Wallets / encryption keys / fingerprint secret are byte-identical between environments by design (makes staging a 30s DNS-swap rollback target).

**Push flow (set 2026-05-24):** `git push origin staging` → `.github/workflows/deploy-staging.yml` → STAGING Coolify (apps 3+4). Verify on staging URLs. Open PR `staging → master` via `gh pr create --base master --head staging`. Merging triggers `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` → PROD Coolify (apps 2+3). **Direct push to `master` is forbidden** unless the commit message contains the literal phrase `direct to master` (case-insensitive hotfix override, CI-logged). Manual redeploys via `php artisan tinker` queue inside the Coolify container — pattern in `CLAUDE.md` "Manual redeploy" section. Full playbook + emergency access in `DEPLOY-HETZNER.md`.

**Migrations:** Coolify does NOT run them. Run `bun run db:push` from root before deploy if you touched `packages/database/src/schema/*.ts`. Destructive migrations require `ELIZA_ALLOW_DESTRUCTIVE_MIGRATIONS=true`.

**Testing rule:** never `bun run dev` locally — Three.js/WebGPU crashes Intel Iris Xe and requires a PC restart. Always push → Coolify deploys → test on prod URL.

**Curl note:** Git Bash on Windows uses schannel and rejects CRLs — always pass `--ssl-no-revoke`.

## § i18n

UI locale layer landed 2026-05-22 (Phase 1+2). English + Simplified Chinese ship; ja/ko are allow-listed but the JSON files have not been authored yet.

- **Allowlist:** `LOCALES = ['en','zh-CN','ja','ko']` exported from `packages/shared/src/constants/locales.ts` and mirrored at `apps/web/src/i18n/locales.ts` (client-bundle copy — same constants, identical `isLocale` type guard). Source-of-truth note in both files instructs same-diff edits.
- **Cookie:** `cv_locale` — `sameSite=lax`, `httpOnly=false` (client needs to read it for the language switcher and to ship it with chat fetches), `max-age=1y`, `path=/`. No `/[locale]/` URL prefix — the cookie is the sole locale signal so SEO-affecting URL shape stays stable.
- **Middleware:** `apps/web/middleware.ts` — runs on everything except `/api/*`, `/_next/static`, `/_next/image`, `favicon.ico`, and file requests. If the cookie is unset, parses `Accept-Language` (q-sorted, exact match before language-prefix fallback) and seeds the cookie. The middleware never rewrites the URL.
- **Request config:** `apps/web/src/i18n/request.ts` is wired via `createNextIntlPlugin('./src/i18n/request.ts')` in `apps/web/next.config.mjs`. It reads the cookie via `await cookies()` (Next 16 async API) and validates with the shared `isLocale()` guard before falling back to `DEFAULT_LOCALE='en'`. Messages load dynamically as `(await import(\`../../messages/${locale}.json\`)).default`.
- **Source dictionary:** the namespaced message dictionary (`common`, `lobby`, `nori`, `sidebar`, `tutorial`, `chat`, `loading`; ~158 keys at shipping time). NOTE (corrected 2026-06-16): the historical batch-regenerator script `scripts/i18n/translate-with-gemini.ts` (and a static `apps/web/messages/en.json`) are NOT in the repo. The LIVE translation path is the runtime route `POST /api/i18n/translate` (`apps/api/src/routes/i18n.ts`), which batch-translates visible `/game` DOM strings through **OpenAI** (`OPENAI_SMALL_MODEL`, default `gpt-4o-mini`) — never Gemini. It enforces a brand-term + ICU-placeholder validator and fails closed on violation.
- **Chat-route locale plumbing:** Phase 2 added an optional `locale` field to the Zod input of every system-prompt-emitting chat route — `apps/api/src/routes/{chat.ts,chat-transient.ts,agent-gateway.ts,openclaw.ts}`. Each route calls a per-file `buildLocaleAddendum(locale)` helper (English baseline returns `""`; non-English emits "respond in <language>" + a brand-term carve-out covering ClawVille, ClawTokens, Nori, OpenClaw, Hermes, Milady, Moltbook, Reef Race, Bumper Shells, and the 10 building names). The addendum is appended to `dynamicContext` (chat.ts, agent-gateway.ts, openclaw.ts) or to the inline `systemPrompt` string (chat-transient.ts).
- **Prompt-injection guard:** the Zod schema refines `locale` via shared `isLocale(v)`. Anything outside the allowlist (including `'pirate'`, `'en; ignore previous instructions'`, etc.) is rejected before the prompt is built.
- **Client cookie reader:** `apps/web/src/lib/api.ts::readClientLocale()` parses `document.cookie` and validates against `LOCALES`. The 4 chat-mutating fetches (`sendChat`, `sendSystemChat`, `openclawChat`, `openclawLocationChat`) all wrap their bodies with `{ ...body, locale: readClientLocale() }`.
- **Language switcher:** `apps/web/src/components/game/sidebar-menu.tsx` adds a `LanguageRow` in the SYSTEM category. On change the row rewrites the `cv_locale` cookie with the new value and calls `window.location.reload()` (full reload required so the server-side `getRequestConfig` re-runs against the new cookie).
- **Brand glossary:** `ClawVille, ClawTokens, Nori, Milady, Moltbook, Reef Race, Bumper Shells, OpenClaw, Hermes, ElizaOS, The Depths, SOL`, the 10 building names + 2 Phase-6 props (`Predictive Gaming Cove`, `Patrick's Rock`). Lives both in the translation script (post-translation validator) and in the chat-route addendum string.
- **Town Guide:** orientation bullet under `packages/agent-templates/src/locations/town-guide.ts` knowledge[]. Future locales add another orientation bullet, not a new section.

## 13. Recent material changes

Compact log. Single line per change with commit hash + one-line summary. When the change is described in detail in the body above, no need to repeat it here.

- 2026-07-09. **WITHDRAW REVISIONS (founder-requested; worktree `feat/wallet-withdraw`, still fully DARK — `WALLET_WITHDRAW_ENABLED` default-OFF, no flag flips).** (1) **Daily caps REMOVED entirely** (founder decision): `resolveWithdrawDailyCapAtomic`/`parseUiAmountToAtomic`/`utcDayStart` deleted, `WALLET_WITHDRAW_DAILY_CAP_{SOL,USDC,CLV}` no longer read, `daily_cap_exceeded` + its 429 mapping deleted, the cap SUM (`sumSinceAtomic`) + `markPendingFailed` DB methods deleted, cap tests dropped — the balance + fee/rent guards are the only per-request limits; idempotency machine + every exactly-once guard byte-identical. (2) **CLV HOLD CONSENT GATE** (`hold_at_risk`, informed consent — enforcement stays with the land rent sweeper): in `requestWithdrawal`, after the CLV balance read and BEFORE any row insert/claim, the avatar's stacked AGENT-subject hold requirement (`SUM(hold_threshold_ct)` over `tenure='hold' AND hold_subject='agent' AND grandfathered=false` — exactly the sweeper's stacked semantics; user-subject holds check the LINKED wallet and are unaffected; `hold_threshold_ct` is CLV uiAmount) is compared in atomic BigInt (6dp) against the post-withdrawal balance; short ⇒ **409 `hold_at_risk`** with `holdAtRisk {requiredUiAmount, postUiAmount (6dp decimal string), parcels[{parcelCode, holdThresholdCt}]}` and NO row inserted; body flag `acknowledgeHoldLoss: true` (optional boolean, `.strict()` Zod) bypasses ONLY this consent gate (never the balance/fee/on-curve/self-send/exactly-once guards) and is NOT part of `idempotencyBodyMatches` — the acked retry reuses the same Idempotency-Key. FAIL-OPEN on the threshold-query error ONLY (warn + proceed; the sweeper's grace window catches an unbacked hold next pass). Injectable dep `WalletWithdrawDb.getAgentHoldRequirement`. (3) **RESUME WORKER wired** (`startWithdrawResumeWorker`/`stopWithdrawResumeWorker` in the executor; boot-wired in index.ts beside the market-listing-expiry sweeper + graceful-shutdown stop): DARK-GATED — index.ts starts it ONLY when `WALLET_WITHDRAW_ENABLED==='true'` AND the start re-asserts the flag internally (no worker while dark; none needed — 'sending' rows can only exist once the flag has been on); idempotent interval on `WALLET_WITHDRAW_RESUME_POLL_MS` (default 300000, floor 60000); each pass runs the FORWARD-ONLY `runWithdrawResumeTick` (a captured signature is NEVER re-signed/re-sent — `resumeWithdrawal` logic untouched) and pages ops via `alertError({severity:'warning', source:'wallet-withdraw-resume'})` for every row resolving to 'reconcile' (context = withdrawalId + captured txSignature + detail — NEVER key material). Same-diff docs: this file (§2 route row, §4 executor row, Last-Audited), GameFeatures.md §5e, `.env.example` (cap knobs removed, `WALLET_WITHDRAW_RESUME_POLL_MS` added, duplicate market-sweeper block deduped), CLAUDE.md env row; web `wallet-panel.tsx` dead `daily_cap_exceeded` case removed (hold-at-risk consent UI = web follow-up). Tests `wallet-withdraw-executor.test.ts` 53/0; `tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (apps/api + packages/database).
- 2026-07-08. **CUSTODIAL WALLET WITHDRAW, SHIPPED DARK (worktree `feat/wallet-withdraw`; default-OFF `WALLET_WITHDRAW_ENABLED` — the write path refuses typed 503 `withdraw_disabled`; NO flag flips).** Users move their OWN deposited on-chain SOL/USDC/CLV out of their custodial avatar wallet to a self-custody destination. NEW `services/wallet-withdraw-executor.ts` (signs SERVER-SIDE with the avatar's custodial keypair — `decryptWalletRow` + dual pubkey verification vs the row column AND the `avatars.wallet_address` mirror (present-and-mismatched refuses; NULL mirror doesn't lock out, per the fail-closed-null-init rule); key bytes never logged/cached/returned — the one-approved-export-channel doctrine stands, withdraw never hands out the key). Exactly-once machine on NEW `withdrawals` table (migration `0021_wallet_withdrawals.sql`, guarded CREATE TYPE `withdrawal_status` — brand-new enum, single-txn-safe, no ALTER TYPE ADD VALUE): atomic claim `pending→sending` BEFORE any decrypt/sign/send → CAPTURE-BEFORE-SEND (deterministic first sig in its OWN committed UPDATE; partial-UNIQUE tx_signature) → send+confirm; ambiguous send/confirm ⇒ TERMINAL `reconcile` NEVER auto-retried (a double-send is a real double-withdrawal); definitive on-chain err ⇒ `failed`; pre-capture failure ⇒ claim released (guarded `tx_signature IS NULL`); resume FORWARD-ONLY (confirmed→sent / err→failed / not_found→reconcile; a captured sig is NEVER re-sent; stale floor 180s). Idempotency: `Idempotency-Key` header REQUIRED on `POST /api/wallet/withdraw`; `(subject_type, avatar_id, idempotency_key)` partial-UNIQUE ⇒ a retry replays the existing row (a released 'pending' replay re-executes the SAME row). Sends: SOL `SystemProgram.transfer` · USDC classic-SPL `TransferChecked` · CLV Token-2022 `TransferChecked` (mints + 6 dp pinned in code); idempotent dest-ATA create (payer = custodial wallet; ATA rent inside the fee headroom). VALIDATION before any claim/sign: ON-CURVE 32-byte base58 destination (PDAs refused) · non-self · positive-integer ≤u64 amount · amount ≤ on-chain balance · source keeps rent-exempt-min + tx fee (+ dest-ATA rent) of SOL · optional per-asset daily caps (`WALLET_WITHDRAW_DAILY_CAP_{SOL,USDC,CLV}`, UI units, post-insert fail-closed sum over the UTC day) · balance-read failure ⇒ REFUSE (never fail-open). MAINNET-ONLY: pinned `getClvMainnetConnection()` + `assertMainnetWithdrawConnection` endpoint guard (devnet/testnet/localhost structurally refused). NEW `routes/wallet-withdraw.ts` at `/api/wallet` beside wallet-link (`POST /withdraw` + read-only `GET /balances`, live regardless of the flag). E5 PARITY: `requireAuthOrAgentSession`+`requireNonGuestIdentity`; `resolveWithdrawSubject` refuses non-ledger agent sessions (403) — human AND agent withdraw from THEIR OWN avatar wallet; guests 403; NO KYC. LEDGER-UNTOUCHED: nothing imports `claw-token-ledger` or writes `avatars.clawTokens` (source-asserted in tests) — NOT a cash-out. NOTE for founder: `wallets.ts`/`wallet-service.ts` carry the 'no meaningful on-chain value until legal review' custody caveat — this IS the withdrawal flow that caveat anticipates, built dark; the flag flip is gated on that legal review + the money-review chain. Tests file-by-file: wallet-withdraw-executor **39/0** (gates + network guard, off-curve/self/over-balance/zero/u64 validation, SOL rent+fee headroom, dest-ATA rent headroom + creation, fail-closed balance reads, daily cap, idempotency replay, double-claim, capture-lost, sig-conflict release, capture-before-send ordering, ambiguous send/confirm → reconcile + no-re-send, definitive on-chain failure, custody refusal, pre-capture release + same-key completion, resume forward-only x5, tick, E5 subject resolution, agent parity, route-wiring + ledger-untouched source asserts, balances read); regression: market-payout-executor 30/0 · x402-checkout 28/0 · clv-swap-custody 11/0 · market 43/0 · guest-guard coverage 60/0; `tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (apps/api + packages/database). Same-diff docs: this file (§2 route row, §4 service row, §8 table row, header), `GameFeatures.md §5e`, `.env.example`, `CLAUDE.md` env section. PARITY note — human path: `POST /api/wallet/withdraw` (Lucia); agent path: SAME endpoint via `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` (ledger-capable) → its bound avatar; settlement binds to the middleware-resolved avatar's custodial wallet — never body-supplied, never a guest.
- 2026-07-08. **Guest-denial latent hardening (branch `fix/guest-guards-latent`).** EVERY `sap.ts` write route (`/register`, `/tools/publish`, `/feedback`, `/attestation`, `/attestation/revoke`, all `/escrow/*` incl. `/escrow/usdc/*`) and `partner-storefront.ts` `/quote` + `/settle` now carry `requireNonGuestIdentity` inline after `requireAuthOrAgentSession` (each does a custodial on-chain sign / settles USDC from the caller's own wallet — a fully-demo, wallet-less guest must never reach them; Codex round-1 confirmed register/publish/feedback are custodial on-chain writes too, so nothing is carved out). - 2026-07-08. **Marketplace LISTING-EXPIRY sweeper (task D, worktree `feat/econ-golive-executors`) — closes the squatting hole.** `POST /api/market/listings` is LIVE (not gated); v1 treated `expires_at` as a predicate only, so an expired listing stayed `active` holding its `market_deed_locks` row forever — and once the land deed-lock guard (`c7826f69`) shipped (land pool-revert PARKS while a lock exists), that = indefinite rent-free squatting. NEW `services/market-listing-expiry-sweeper.ts` (boot-wired hourly in index.ts beside the land-rent sweeper; graceful-shutdown stop; env `MARKET_LISTING_EXPIRY_SWEEP_PERIOD_MS`, default 1h floor 5min): `sweepExpiredListings()` reads `status='active' AND expires_at <= now()` candidates (cap 2000/pass), and `processExpiredListing()` — per-listing tx — locks in the FULFILLER order (listing `FOR UPDATE` → advisory(seller) OUTER → parcel `FOR UPDATE` INNER, so the lock DELETE is atomic w.r.t. land's `parcelHasLiveDeedLock` read), re-verifies expiry UNDER the lock, flips `active`→`expired` + `escrow_state=NULL`, and DELETEs the `market_deed_locks` row (land's next rent-sweep pass then proceeds). Touches ONLY `active` — never `pending_settlement`/`settled` (those locks belong to the deed-transfer executor, which deletes them on ownership flip). NOT a money path (no CT/CLV/USDC; no claw-token-ledger import; runs live). Idempotent (0-row flip / already-gone lock = clean no-op). `schema/market.ts` state-machine doc updated (expiry is now flipped, not just a predicate). Tests: `market-listing-expiry-sweeper.test.ts` 13/0 (state machine incl. settled/pending_settlement/not-expired/null-expiry no-ops, lock order + atomic release, non-deed skips locks, concurrent-flip-lost no lock delete, batch aggregate + per-listing failure isolation); tsc exit 0. PARITY: infra sweeper, no user endpoint.
- 2026-07-07. **Tokenomics GoLive executors — MARKET DEED-TRANSFER + PAYOUT EXECUTORS, SHIPPED DARK (worktree `feat/econ-golive-executors`, same slice as the CLV swap live path below; BOTH default-OFF flags, index.ts untouched).** NEW `services/market-deed-transfer-executor.ts` (`MARKET_DEED_TRANSFER_ENABLED`, pure DB): consumes SETTLED `land_deed` settlements with `deed_transferred_at IS NULL` — ONE tx per row: claim via settlement `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` (+ `deed_transfer_claim_id` audit) → land lock order advisory(seller) OUTER → parcel `FOR UPDATE` INNER → UNDER-LOCK RE-VERIFY the seller still owns (land release/evict race ⇒ TERMINAL `deed_transfer_conflict`, NEVER a forced flip; live `deposit_remaining_ct > 0` ⇒ TERMINAL `deed_transfer_escrow_present` — the land escrow-conservation invariant is never vaporized) → fresh-owned flip via OWN SQL (`owner_avatar_id`=buyer, `tenure='owned'` [FLAG for land review], deposit/hold/rent-tenancy/grace cols reset, `rent_ct_weekly` KEPT) → `land_structures.owner_avatar_id`→buyer AS-IS [FLAG for land review] → stamp `deed_transferred_at` → DELETE `market_deed_locks`; crash = full rollback = resumable; conflict keeps the deed lock HELD (house-favorable freeze). NEW `services/market-payout-executor.ts` (`MARKET_PAYOUT_EXECUTE` + the SAME `assertMainnetRealMoneyContext()` network guard as the swap path — devnet/mock can never reach a real send; imports the PINNED `loadClvSwapKeypair()`/`getClvMainnetConnection()` custody): consumes `payout_status='pending_review'` WHERE `deed_transferred_at IS NOT NULL` (**ORDERING, Codex BLOCKING: funds secured at settle → deed flips FIRST → payout second; a conflicted deed is structurally unpayable; crash-between is resumable — never a lost deed, never a lost payout**). CONSERVATION keystone: sellerClv/rakeClv = `floor(usdMicro×1e12/ratePico)` (pure-BigInt, house-favorable) at THIS settlement's OWN executed C3 buy rate (`clv_buy_queue.executed_price` via `clv_buy_queue_id`, row must be `executed`), and sellerClv+rakeClv ≤ Σ recorded fill `outAmountAtomic` — violation = TERMINAL `conservation_violated`, zero sends, NEVER pays CLV that wasn't bought. E5 PARITY: human seller → stamped `seller_payout_pubkey` re-validated against CURRENT `users.linked_wallet_pubkey` (drift/unlink = terminal mismatch); agent seller → CURRENT custodial `avatars.wallet_address` (cross-checked vs the stamp); guest (avatar OR user) = TERMINAL `guest_seller_refused`. Machine: atomic claim `pending_review→'sending'` BEFORE custody; Token-2022 `TransferChecked` (CLV mint + 6 dp pinned) per leg with CAPTURE-BEFORE-SEND in its own committed UPDATE (partial-UNIQUE per sig); ambiguous send/confirm = TERMINAL `'reconcile'` (never auto-retried); definitive on-chain failure = `'reconcile'` with a definitive reason; pre-capture failures (custody/balance/unpinned rake) RELEASE the claim (guarded `payout_seller_tx_signature IS NULL`); RESTART-AFTER-SEND-BEFORE-MARK resumes forward-only via `getSignatureStatuses` chain-check — a captured signature is NEVER re-sent (confirmed ⇒ next leg/paid; unprovable ⇒ reconcile; nothing captured ⇒ clean release); stale-claim takeover floor 180s (`MARKET_PAYOUT_STALE_MS`). Rake destination = env-pinned `MARKET_RAKE_TREASURY_PUBKEY` (FAIL CLOSED — unset ⇒ refuses before anything signs). Migrations: `0020_market_payout_deed_executors.sql` (executor trail cols + capture-sig partial-UNIQUEs + deed-pending partial index; idempotent/additive/no-DROP) + `0020a_market_payout_status_sending.sql` + `0020b_market_payout_status_reconcile.sql` (each `ALTER TYPE … ADD VALUE` ALONE per the migrate-ci rule — one per file); `schema/market.ts` mirrors all of it (enum values appended LAST to match physical order). Tests file-by-file: market-deed-transfer-executor **11/0** (gate, one-tx ordering, idempotent replay, SKIP-LOCKED double-claim, seller-no-longer-owns TERMINAL + lock-held, escrow guard, parcel-missing, non-deed refusal, crash-rollback-then-resume), market-payout-executor **30/0** (gates + network guard, deed precondition, agent + human destination branches, mismatch/guest/subject-unresolvable terminals, exact-integer floor math, conservation violation with zero custody, buy-not-executed release, zero-rake single-leg, insufficient-CLV + unpinned-rake releases, ambiguous-send terminal + no-retry, definitive on-chain failure, capture-lost no-send, resume: rake-only-send / zero-send / unprovable-reconcile / nothing-captured-release / live-claim-respected / transient chain-check, tick); regression: market 42/0, clv-swap-executor 20/0, clv-swap-custody 11/0, clv-swap-live 24/0; `tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (api + database). LEDGER-ONLY: neither executor imports claw-token-ledger; no `avatars.clawTokens` write anywhere. PARITY note — human path: settlement rows created by `/api/x402/checkout/*` settles pay out to the human's proven linked wallet; agent path: SAME settlement rows pay out to the agent's custodial avatar wallet; settlement binds to the fulfiller's middleware-resolved seller/buyer avatars — never a guest.

- 2026-07-07. **Land deed-lock guard (branch `fix/land-deed-lock-release-guard`)** — land's two pool-revert paths (`POST /parcels/:id/release` + the rent sweeper's rented/deposit/hold lapse-eviction reverts) now consult the marketplace deed escrow lock via NEW exported `parcelHasLiveDeedLock` (`routes/land.ts`; raw-SQL EXISTS on `market_deed_locks` OR a live land_deed `market_listings` row, under the already-held advisory+`FOR UPDATE` locks; 42P01 → unlocked for migration-order safety). Release refuses 409 `deed_locked_by_listing` (+ the route's error JSON gains a `code` field); the sweeper PARKS instead of evicting (`{kind:'parked'}` — grace untouched, no forfeit, loud warn + `alertError`, `parked` count in the pass summary), so a listed deed can never be double-sold out from under a settling buyer once `MARKETPLACE_SETTLE_ENABLED` flips. Closes the C4 "SCOPE HONESTY" land-side follow-up seam. Tests: `land-deed-lock-guard.test.ts` (structural + DB-gated E2E w/ `to_regclass` probe); details in Last Audited.
- 2026-07-07. **Tokenomics C — P2P MARKETPLACE v1 (worktree `feat/tokenomics-treasury`, stage 3/C4 — final stage of the C-phase pipeline; settlement FLAG-GATED OFF, no flag flips, nothing on-chain).** Sellers list; buyers settle via the C1 checkout; every CLV movement is a QUEUED Codex-gated INTENT. NEW `market_listings` + `market_deed_locks` + `market_settlements` + `market_item_kind`/`market_listing_status`/`market_payout_status` enums (`packages/database/src/schema/market.ts` — the listing STATE MACHINE (active → pending_settlement → settled | cancelled | expired, expiry-as-predicate in v1) + escrow-lock lifecycle documented in the file header; migration `0017_market_p2p.sql`, idempotent + additive, per-statement autocommit — NEVER db:push). **Deed escrow-lock is MARKET-OWNED** (`market_deed_locks`, PK parcel_id = the DB double-list guard, backed up by the live-item partial-UNIQUE on the listings table): taken in the same tx as the listing INSERT, released on cancel, HELD through `settled` until the deed-transfer executor runs; chosen over an `ALTER TABLE land_parcels` so `land.ts`/`schema/land.ts` stay untouched (C4 constraint) AND a stale-branch `db:push --force` can never silently drop a live land column. SCOPE HONESTY: land-side release/evict paths don't consult the lock — those races are REFUSED by the checkout preflight + the fulfiller's under-lock owner re-check (money never settles against a parcel the seller no longer owns); wiring land's paths to the lock is a documented land-domain follow-up seam. NEW `services/market-listings.ts`: `checkSellerLicense` (**Resident license** ≥ `MARKET_SELLER_MIN_CLV` (default 50,000, env retunes but 0/garbage falls back — never disables) CLV uiAmount via the shared A1 balance service; E5 split — human→`getLinkedWalletClvBalance(userId)`, agent→`getWalletClvBalance(avatars.wallet_address)`; **FAIL-SOFT ⇒ REFUSE** `clv_balance_unavailable` — an RPC outage can never mint unlicensed sellers) + `createMarketListing` (`earned_bundle` ⇒ `earned_not_available` before any I/O — EARNED provenance doesn't exist yet; land_deed: land lock order advisory(seller)→parcel `FOR UPDATE`, ownership + transferable-tenure (`owned`/`hold` only) guards, listing + deed-lock in ONE tx, conflict ⇒ whole-tx abort `parcel_already_listed`) + `cancelMarketListing` (seller-only, `active` only; flips `cancelled` + releases the lock; listing `FOR UPDATE` serializes against the settle fulfiller) + public/mine reads. NEW `routes/market.ts` mounted at `/api/market` (`POST /listings`, `GET /listings` public, `GET /listings/mine`, `POST /listings/:id/cancel`; Zod `.strict()`; `requireAuthOrAgentSession`+`requireNonGuestIdentity` on every write; non-ledger agent sessions 403 — the cove/checkout convention). NEW `services/checkout-fulfillers/marketplace-purchase.ts` registered against the PINNED C1 registry (`registerFulfiller('marketplace_purchase')` + preflight; the checkout route gains the `marketplace_purchase` quote arm pricing SERVER-SIDE from the listing row): **`MARKETPLACE_SETTLE_ENABLED` (default OFF) refuses at THREE layers** — quote (`marketplace_settle_disabled`, so no 402/pending row is ever issued while gated), preflight (row stays pending, facilitator never called, NO USDC moves), and the in-tx hard gate (`CheckoutFulfillmentRefusal` ⇒ engine records the loud manual-refund terminal state; nothing half-completes). When (later) enabled the fulfiller — all writes on `ctx.tx` — re-checks everything under the listing `FOR UPDATE` (+ advisory(seller)→parcel `FOR UPDATE` for deeds: owner re-check + deed-lock presence; self-buy `own_listing`; `price_mismatch` tripwire), claims active→pending_settlement→settled bound to the checkout, enqueues the **FULL settled USDC** as the C3 CLV buy (`enqueueClvBuy('marketplace_purchase', sourceRef: checkoutId)` same-tx), and INSERTs the `market_settlements` INTENT row: **4.44% (444 bps) treasury CLV rake + 95.56% seller CLV payout, `payout_status='pending_review'`** — ¢-peg-EXACT µUSD split (`usd_cents×444` + `usd_cents×9556` are integers summing to `usd_cents×10000`; zero rounding; DB conservation CHECK `rake_usd + seller_payout_usd = usd_basis`), seller payout destination = the license-gate wallet stamped on the listing (review re-validates). **EXACTLY-ONCE:** the engine's per-signature guarantee + `checkout_id` UNIQUE + an in-fulfiller prior-settlement read that replays as a NO-OP (never double-pays the seller, never double-queues the buy). **CONSERVATION / LEDGER-ONLY:** the marketplace mints/debits ZERO internal vCLAW (`avatars.clawTokens` untouched everywhere; the fulfiller imports nothing from claw-token-ledger); the buyer's dollars settle underneath; seller/treasury value exists only as on-chain-CLV INTENTS. **CODEX-GATED SEAMS (marked in-code):** (1) on-chain CLV payout + rake execution off `pending_review` rows; (2) the deed `owner_avatar_id` flip (land-domain review; `deed_transferred_at` RESERVED; the lock stays held). NEW `apps/api/src/services/clv-swap-executor.ts`: **`enqueueClvBuy({amountUsdc, reason, sourceRef, metadata?}, tx?) → {queueId}`** inserts one `status='planned'` row stamping the oracle quote (`quoted_price`, nullable on oracle outage — recording intent never blocks a settle), composable into a settle transaction via the now-EXPORTED **`LedgerTx`** type from `claw-token-ledger.ts` (type-only export, zero runtime change; re-exported by the executor); guards non-positive/NaN/malformed amounts + empty reason/sourceRef BEFORE any DB touch. PURE **`planClips({amountUsdc, poolLiquidityUsd, maxImpactBps?, spacingMs?})`**: constant-product impact math — per-clip cap = `(bps/10k) × oneSideDepth` where `oneSideDepth ≈ poolLiquidityUsd/2`, µUSD-floored DOWN (house-favorable), full-cap clips + exact remainder (BigInt µUSD sum == queued amount exactly); REFUSES `no_liquidity` (null/0/NaN depth, or a dust pool whose cap floors to 0 µUSD) / `invalid_amount` / `clip_count_excessive` (>10k clips). DRY-RUN worker (`startClvSwapWorker`, boot-wired in index.ts beside the oracle; stopped in graceful shutdown) scans planned rows every `CLV_SWAP_POLL_MS` (default 5 min) and LOGS the plan it WOULD execute (once per outcome-change, FIFO-capped memory) — marks NOTHING executed, NO signing, NO tx; the exact spot real signing would go is a marked `CODEX-GATED SEAM: live execution`. **HARD GATE:** `CLV_SWAP_EXECUTE='true'` throws `'CLV_SWAP_EXECUTE=true but live CLV swap execution is Codex-review-gated — refusing to execute'` at MODULE LOAD (statically imported by index.ts ⇒ the box refuses to boot — the x402-config crash-loud pattern), at worker start (re-escalated to `process.exit(1)` in the boot IIFE), and on every tick. **Swap wallet:** `treasury_purpose` enum += `'clv-swap'` (`ALTER TYPE … ADD VALUE IF NOT EXISTS` in 0014); NEW singleton provisioning script `scripts/generate-clv-swap-wallet.ts` (mirrors `generate-treasury-keypair.ts`: local keygen → immediate AES-256-GCM under `VANITY_ENCRYPTION_KEY` → insert; prints pubkey ONLY; re-run reports the existing row, never a second one); the executor reads ONLY the pubkey via **`getClvSwapWalletPubkey()`** (5-min cache; never touches the encrypted secret). **ORACLE LP-DEPTH (`clv-price-oracle.ts`):** the DexScreener `liquidity.usd` previously fetched into a local `bestLiq` and DISCARDED is now surfaced as `getClvPrice().poolLiquidityUsd` (+ `liquidityAsOf`); `fetchSpot` now runs Helius + DexScreener in PARALLEL each poll (price preference unchanged Helius-primary; depth always refreshes from DexScreener when it answers; one extra keyless request/poll); depth is max-stale-gated exactly like the price and memory-only (no snapshot column — re-warms within one poll of boot). Env (`.env.example`): `CLV_SWAP_EXECUTE` (MUST stay unset/false) · `CLV_SWAP_MAX_IMPACT_BPS` (default 100 = 1%, floor 1) · `CLV_SWAP_POLL_MS` (default 300000, floor 30000) · `CLV_SWAP_CLIP_SPACING_MS` (default 60000, floor 1000). - 2026-07-03. **Cove Hold'em Increment 1b — resync + deal/action/close replay idempotency (`feat/cove-holdem-1b-resync`; backend only, NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump — no `[ACTION:]` whitelist verb touched).** Closes the gap memory `holdem-nonterminal-action-not-idempotent` flagged: idempotency previously covered ONLY terminal settles, so a lost-response DEAL or a lost-response NON-terminal `/action` left the client stranded with no way back to the live hand. **A1 deal-replay:** `holdem_hands.deal_idempotency_key` (NEW column, DELIBERATELY separate from the settle-anchor `idempotency_key` — sharing would couple deal-replay to settle-replay) + partial unique `(table_id, deal_idempotency_key) WHERE deal_idempotency_key IS NOT NULL`; `POST /hand/deal` checks it UNDER the table `FOR UPDATE` lock BEFORE the `hand_in_progress` 409 — a re-POST with the same key replays the SAME hand (in-progress peek or settled outcome) instead of racing the 409 with the original hand unreachable. **A2 resync read:** NEW exported pure helper `buildInProgressHandView(table, hand)` (`cove-holdem.ts`) is now the SINGLE source for the fresh-deal in-progress body, the deal-replay in-progress body, AND the new optional `hand` field on `GET /session/current` + `GET /session/:id` — reuses `peekState` verbatim so the no-board-leak street-truncation is inherited, never re-derived. New shared type `HoldemResyncHandView` (`packages/shared/src/types/cove-holdem.ts`); `CurrentHoldemTableResponse` promoted to its own interface (was `= OpenHoldemTableResponse`) so the pre-1b open-table shape stays untouched. **A3 terminal-replay-window fix:** `/action` no longer 409s `not_human_turn` when the recorded actions are ALREADY terminal before appending (the exact window a duplicate/retried terminal action lands in) — it now settles/replays inline via the already-idempotent `settleHand`, safe on every interleaving. **A4 close-replay:** `holdem_tables.cash_out` (NEW column) persists the cashed-out figure so a retry against an already-`closed` table (owner-matched) reconstructs the ORIGINAL close response instead of 409ing `table_not_open` — NEVER re-credits; the open→closed flip under `FOR UPDATE` is the idempotency anchor, not the optional `Idempotency-Key` (accepted + logged for audit only). **A5 guest parity on resync:** `GET /session/current` + `GET /session/:id` relaxed from ledger-subject-only to ALSO serve guests (by `guestFpHash`) — a guest could already open/deal/act on a demo table but had no way back to a wedged in-progress hand; guests still cannot close (`guest_cannot_close_table` unchanged). **Schema:** both new columns are ADDITIVE + nullable, applied via idempotent `packages/database/migrations-manual/2026-07-03_holdem_1b_columns.sql` + `packages/database/scripts/apply-holdem-1b-columns.ts` (bespoke `HOLDEM_1B_DATABASE_URL`, never auto-loaded `DATABASE_URL`) — NOT `db:push`. **Unwedge:** NEW `apps/api/scripts/cove/unwedge-holdem-tables.ts` (dry-run default) frees tables wedged BEFORE this fix by voiding the stuck `in_progress` hand (status`='voided'`, NO `playerStack` write — the stack was never decremented mid-hand, so the player keeps it whole) then closing the table through the SAME ledger path as `/session/close` (`creditClawTokens`, never a direct `avatars.clawTokens` write); for a GUEST table it closes with NO ledger write at all (the demo stack is discarded — no `claw_token_transactions` audit row). **Blast-radius gate (Codex 2026-07-03):** `--execute` REFUSES to run unless SCOPED by `--table <uuid>` (repeatable) and/or `--stale-hours <n>` (n≥1, no default) — an unscoped mutate would void EVERY open+in_progress hand, including a player mid-`/action`; and the void is re-validated UNDER the row lock against the DB clock (the hand must be ≥ `--stale-hours` old, or a 10-minute typo-guard floor in bare `--table` mode) so a live/just-dealt hand is SKIPPED. Targets the 2 known prod-wedged tables generically (query is NOT hardcoded to their ids). **Tests:** NEW `apps/api/src/routes/__tests__/cove-holdem-resync.test.ts` (8 tests, pure/deterministic, no DB) proves `buildInProgressHandView`'s board-truncation + no-bot-hole-leak + no-serverSeed + blind-seat-derivation invariants; the required `holdem-engine.test.ts` + `holdem-betting-machine.test.ts` suite stays green (70/70). A1/A3/A4's transaction-level idempotency (the DB-backed paths) has NO existing route-test harness for cove-holdem (none existed pre-1b either) — exercised by the orchestrator's staging smoke, not a unit test. Gates: `packages/shared`/`packages/database`/`apps/api` `bunx tsc --noEmit` all clean; scoped `turbo run build --filter=@clawville/api` (shared+database+agent-templates+agent-runtime+wager-program+api) green; apps/web NOT built (zero web files touched by this increment). PARITY: human path unchanged (Lucia cookie on all 6 routes); agent path unchanged (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar, `isLedgerSubject` collapses user+agent — this increment touches NO subject-resolution code); settlement binds to the SAME `getSubject()`/`ownerMatch()` resolver as before, now ALSO exercised by guests on the two GET routes (guests were never ledger subjects and still aren't — no settlement change). Same-diff docs: this entry + the `holdem_tables`/`holdem_hands` rows above + `.claude/plans/cove-texas-holdem.md §11` REST-flow section (brain repo, junctioned). Server-implementer-only diff — no web client consumption of the new optional `hand` field yet (client work is a follow-up increment).

Reserved partner rows (hatcher) are NEVER minted here (TTL-refresh-only, hash untouched — the Hatcher wire is byte-identical). `PROTOCOL_VERSION 8→9` (manual §5 documents the contract). - 2026-07-02. **Phase D — partner direct-USDC storefront (GATED; ADDITIVE to the PROTECTED partner surface; backend only, NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump — still 7).** New router `apps/api/src/routes/partner-storefront.ts` mounted at `/api/partner/storefront` (`index.ts`, AFTER both `/api/partner/hatcher` groups so the LIVE partner routes match FIRST — the new base never shadows them). A vetted partner sells REAL off-platform services for USDC paid DIRECTLY to the partner's own Solana pubkey — **WE NEVER CUSTODY and credit NO ClawTokens**. Reuses the SAME x402 verify→settle facilitator primitive as the USDC→CT on-ramp: two new thin `services/x402-payai.ts` exports — `buildPartnerPurchaseQuote({payoutPubkey,asset,usdCents,network,…})` (payTo = partner `payoutPubkey`, never our merchant wallet) and `settlePartnerPurchase({paymentHeader,requirements,expectedPayoutPubkey})` (a NO-CUSTODY recipient binding over the same `verifyAndSettle`; never touches the ledger). **Routes:** `POST /register` (ed25519 partner-signed via `verifyPartnerWriteSignature`, upsert on the UNIQUE `slug`, `payoutPubkey` base58-validated, a payout-pubkey CHANGE force-resets the gate to `false`/`pending`; NEVER sets `fulfillment_enabled`), `POST /admin/fulfillment` (`adminOnly` flip — never the partner key; enabled ⇒ status `active`, disabled ⇒ `suspended`), `POST /quote` + `POST /settle` (`requireAuthOrAgentSession` — Rule E5 human/agent parity → `identity.avatarId`; settle requires an `Idempotency-Key` + serializes the future critical section under `withKeyedMutex(partner-settle:<slug>:<key>)`). **Data-flow / the GATE:** load storefront by `(slug, partnerId='hatcher')` → 404; `isStorefrontFulfillmentGated` (`!fulfillmentEnabled || status!=='active'`) → **503 `partner_fulfillment_gated`** for every storefront today (schema default `fulfillment_enabled=false`) → `resolvePartnerOffering()` returns null (the server-priced offering CATALOG is land-owned + DEFERRED) → **400 `offering_required`** (NO client-supplied price is ever accepted — the fix over the stale 58d0caf3 design). When ungated + an offering resolves (future), /quote returns a 402 x402 challenge bound to the partner payout (belt-and-suspenders `accepts[0].payTo===payoutPubkey` → 500 `quote_binding_error`) and /settle verify+settles then returns `{settled,txSignature,buyerAvatarId}` with NO CT credit. **FEATURE_GATE `partner_storefront_tier`** (graduate: a custody/KYC/age-reviewed partner + admin-enabled fulfillment + a real settled buyer→partner purchase). Uses the PRE-EXISTING `partner_storefronts` table (`schema/land.ts` — NO migration) + the existing `X402_TOPUP_NETWORK` env (devnet-first — NO new env var). Gates: `apps/api bunx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `bun test src/routes/__tests__/partner-storefront.test.ts` 4/4 (routing-integrity — hatcher NOT shadowed; unsigned-register 401; admin-flip 401; gate-predicate). PARITY: human path `POST /api/partner/storefront/{quote,settle}` (Lucia cookie); agent path the SAME routes via `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → bound avatar; settlement binds the buyer to `identity.avatarId` and pays the partner `payoutPubkey` directly. Same-diff docs: `docs/hatcher-integration-spec.md §7a` (+ stale `PROTOCOL_VERSION 5→7` correction) + Last Audited. Team `x402-partner-usdc-2026-07-02`.

the founder drops them as a separate approved op. **KEPT:** `building_skills` (the 10 residents TEACH skills), agent skill-USE, the first-party Cosmetics shop, and `exchange` (peer items/services — not in the remove list). **No `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump** — no `[ACTION:]` verb existed for these verticals.
- 2026-07-02. **Agent-metaverse P1 — money/security + tests fix pass (M1–M5, T1–T3; backend only, NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump — still 7).** Post-build hardening of the P1 house-agent surface (concurrent with the R1–R5 reliability track on the driver/orchestrator/seeder/sweeper). **M1 (bearer leak, MAJOR):** `app.onError` (`index.ts`) logged `console.error('API Error:', err)` and alerted `Uncaught error on <method> <path>` + `err.stack`; for an agent route the path is `/api/agent/oc-<bearer>/…`, so the replayable real-CT bearer reached stdout/Coolify + Telegram (the request logger already redacted, onError did not). FIX (both): `services/alert-error.ts` now `redactBearerTokens` the message at the top (covers all 3 callers — event-logger, onError, business-logic), the no-credentials `console.warn` fallback, AND the Telegram context block (`redactBearerTokens(JSON.stringify(context,null,2))`); `index.ts` onError redacts the direct `console.error(String(err))`. JSON 500 response shape unchanged (partner contract). **M2 (CT faucet, MAJOR):** `/visit-building` + `/building/:buildingId/chat` credited 1 CT UNCONDITIONALLY (`agent-gateway.ts`) → a ledger-bound agent parked within radius could loop-farm CT. NEW `creditBuildingRewardOncePerDay()` gates ONLY the `creditClawTokens` call (NOT `logEventFromContext` — the leaderboard keeps its own caps + must still record every event) with an idempotent per-(avatarId, buildingId, reason, UTC-day) dedup: one `db.transaction` that `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`s the SAME `avatars` row the ledger locks, then an existence probe on `claw_token_transactions` (`avatar_id` + `reason` + `metadata->>'buildingId'` + `created_at >= startOfUtcDay`), crediting only if absent → concurrency-safe (row lock serializes check-then-credit, no double-credit) with NO legit-visit regression (distinct building / new UTC day = fresh key). Both sites now set `tokenAwarded = (await creditBuildingRewardOncePerDay(...)) ? 1 : 0`. **M3/M4 (proto-key proximity bypass, MINOR/INFO):** `/building/:buildingId/chat` (was bare `NPC_BUILDING_CENTERS[buildingId]` + ad-hoc `CHAT_RADIUS=2000`) and `/move` (bare bracket) now use the shared `resolveBuildingCenter` own-property guard + `BUILDING_INTERACTION_RADIUS` (1000), matching `/visit-building`; an inherited prototype key can no longer NaN-bypass the proximity gate. `resolveBuildingCenter` extracted to NEW dependency-free `services/building-center.ts` (only `@clawville/shared`) so all three routes AND the F1 test share ONE guard. **M5:** NEW `packages/database/scripts/apply-openclaw-is-house.ts` (mirrors `apply-bounty-escrow-linkage.ts`; explicit `AGENTS_DATABASE_URL`, NEVER db:push/auto-loaded `DATABASE_URL`) applies the idempotent `migrations-manual/2026-07-01_add_openclaw_is_house.sql` + verifies the column. **T1/T2/T3:** the F1 test now imports + asserts the REAL `resolveBuildingCenter` (proto key → null, real id → coords) instead of an inline re-impl; NEW no-money invariant test (a `driveOnce`→`enter_building` + near `talk_to_npc` emits ONLY `agent_chat` speech, never a settlement/token event); the stale `partner-hatcher.ts` P5-2 comment reworded to the actual `ocb-<base64url(agentId)>` body id (`avatarBodyId()`), not `oc-<sessionId>`. Gate: `apps/api bunx tsc --noEmit` exit 0; `bun test src/services/__tests__/agent-autonomy-p1.test.ts` 12/12. Same-diff: `docs/agent-metaverse-p1-plan.md`. NOT committed by this track (orchestrator commits both tracks together).
- 2026-07-01. **P0 — Agent lifecycle truth (restart survival) — RE-FOUNDED on v7, BUILD IN PROGRESS, gates pending** (`feat/agent-metaverse-p0`, base `origin/staging` @ `7ff0d793`; supersedes the discarded 818-commits-stale `feat/agent-metaverse-build` v3 attempt). Phase P0 of `docs/agent-metaverse-model.md §9` — the smallest correct first diff: ONE lifecycle authority so `session-status` (RAM+DB, `agent-gateway.ts`), the bearer gate (`require-auth-or-agent.ts validateLiveAgentSession`, RAM + DB-TTL), restart-survival rides v7's EXISTING lazy restore ALONE (NOT a new eager boot-rehydrator): on a bearer-gate Map-miss `validateLiveAgentSession` (`require-auth-or-agent.ts`) calls `agent-session-restore.ts` `restoreAgentSessionFromRow` → hashes the incoming bearer → finds the row by `session_key_hash` → TTL-revalidates fail-closed → rebuilds `{config,client}` under the agent's EXACT ORIGINAL bearer (restorable: hatcher-proxy/nanoclaw/milady/anonymous need NO reconnect; NOT restorable: openclaw/ironclaw/custom real-gateway, no persisted `auth_token`). `session-status` is made RESTORE-AWARE via NEW `isSessionRestorable(identityType, protocol)` (`agent-session-config.ts` = `hatcher-proxy || isRowRestorableFromIdentity`, shared with the restore module so status can't drift from what restore does): `connected:true` when a RAM session exists OR the row self-heals; it returns `410 { needsReconnect:true, reason:'session_not_live' }` ONLY for NON-restorable real-gateway (openclaw/custom) rows (reuses 410 — **NO `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump**; still 7). Restore stays fail-closed **NON-LEDGER** (real-CT only after a proof-carrying `/connect`/`/reconnect`). The sweeper (`agent-session-sweeper.ts`) now `unregisterOpenClaw`s the zombie body; `/disconnect` unchanged — all agree, fixing restart-desync at the source. **Eager boot-rehydration (a proactive fresh-id body mint for server-driven agents) is DEFERRED to P1** — it would COLLIDE with lazy restore (double body / 'already overridden' lockout when the real agent presents its surviving original bearer); the built + adversary-reviewed design is preserved in commit `c711234e` for a P1 restore-compatible re-add. **Folded pre-existing LIVE vuln B1:** the public snapshot serializers (`npc-simulation.ts getSnapshot`/`getRoomSnapshot`, unauth `/api/npc/state|stream` + `/api/world/:room/stream`) leaked an avatar-mode connected agent's body wire `id = oc-${sessionId}` (the bearer) → impersonation / real-CT theft; the fix is at the SOURCE, not a boundary serializer: an avatar body's in-world id (both the `this.npcs` Map key AND its `.id`) is now the non-secret, deterministic `ocb-<base64url(agentId)>` (`avatarBodyId()`), so the bearer is structurally absent from EVERY serializer at once — public snapshot, the authenticated `/perception` cross-agent harvest, `buildHatcherWorldState`, combat/arena events — and no future serializer can reintroduce it. The boundary-sanitize helpers (`publicNpcId`/`toWireNpc`/`toWireConversation`) the first attempt added were REMOVED (0 refs). `talk_to_npc` resolves for free (the perceived id IS the Map key; `this.npcs.has(target)` passes). The id is DOM-safe (base64url is colon-free, so a namespaced `hatcher:`/`milady:` agentId can't break an unescaped client `querySelector('#…')`) and the `ocb-` prefix can't collide with a resident/wanderer/override npc id or the client's `milady-`/`chibi-` render heuristic. Reverse lookup (bodyId→sessionId) stays `npcOverrides`; forward (sessionId→bodyId) is the pure `avatarBodyId(agentId)`; internal Map bearer key `oc-${sessionId}` is gone from bodies. Bonus sub-fix: `getBrowserClawSnapshots` now emits `derivePublicId(sessionId)` (salted, non-reversible) instead of the raw low-entropy browser-claw sessionId (a non-CT griefing/impersonation vector). Client verified (impl-2 map) to treat `npc.id` as an OPAQUE stable entity key (render / entity-interp / React-key / VRM-instance-cache key), never `oc-`-parsed and never round-tripped to a session-scoped route (world-stream auths via cookie/agent-session + the `/join` presence id); `agentId` is stable across a sessionId re-mint, so entity interpolation is preserved across reconnect/rehydration. **Residuals — CLOSED by the `ocb-<base64url(agentId)>` shape (the client entity key is the encoded body id, not the raw `agentId`):** the design flagged three concerns if the RAW `agentId` were the client key — (a) an `agentId` colliding with a sim-NPC id (`milady-miu`) / `milady-`/`chibi-` prefix (`arena-npcs.tsx` heuristic), (b) a namespaced `hatcher:<raw>` COLON reaching a client DOM `id` + unescaped `querySelector`, (c) raw-namespace provenance disclosure. All three are resolved because the wire id is base64url-encoded under a distinct `ocb-` prefix — colon-free, collision-free, no raw-namespace leak — i.e. the "optional hardening" the design proposed was ADOPTED at the source, not deferred. **Scoped constants (D-4):** `movement.ts` autonomous-avatar clamp 5120→22528 (canonical world dim; a LIVE latent-bug fix — autonomous avatars were pinned to [16,5104] while pathfinding targets the 22528 world); `avatars.ts` heartbeat cap ALREADY correct in v7 (`WORLD_PX_WIDTH`); pathfinding grid grown 360→**704** (full 22528px world, derived drift-proof from `WORLD_COLLIDER_MAP_HALF*2/TILE`) — fixes a REAL unreachable-building bug (regress-auditor D3: messaging-channels center is tile 482 / game-px 15200, beyond the old 360 grid → `enter_building` returned null). The design-doc "360→576" target was stale (world grew 576→704). No autonomy perceive→decide→act engine, no substrate rename, no `[ACTION:]` generalization (all P1+). **REMAINING GATES (before merge/done):** mock-Hatcher harness green before/after, Codex adversarial pass on the B1 + rehydration + session-status money/bearer surface, live restart-survival proof on staging, founder sign-off. PARITY: human path unchanged; agent path = lazy-restored session (original bearer) + restore-aware truthful `session-status` + `/reconnect` for every connected/hosted agent; settlement stays fail-closed NON-LEDGER until reconnect. Protected-partner-surface change (session/bearer/`registerOpenClaw`/`openclaw_bots`) — full backend team + harness gate bind. Design: `docs/agent-metaverse-p0-lifecycle-design.md` + `docs/agent-metaverse-p0-v2-refound.md`.

Serves `GET /agents/:sapAgentPda/eip-8004.json` — the exact URI baked into HermesTest's minted MPL Core AgentIdentity plugin (mainnet asset `E3tgkW9s…`, registered via the 1DREG `RegisterIdentityV1` registry program, the only attach path the deployed mpl-core accepts). - 2026-06-20. **Wager SOL-lobby balance gate + graceful insufficient-funds (kills an uncaught-500 critical page)** (`fix/wager-sol-gate`; `apps/api/src/services/wager-program-client.ts` + `apps/api/src/routes/wager.ts`). Root cause of a prod critical alert on `POST /api/wager/lobbies`: `createSolLobby` is paid by the creator's custodial wallet (fee payer + first depositor, see the fn docstring), so an UNFUNDED wallet (0 lamports, devnet) failed the on-chain `CreateLobbySol` simulation with a web3.js `SendTransactionError` (`insufficient lamports 0, need 2164560`). That is NOT an `AnchorError`, so `withChainErrors` let it escape raw → the route's `handleWagerClientError` only matched `WagerClientError` → `throw err` → uncaught 500 → critical page. NOT a settlement-authority outage (that keypair is fine) and unrelated to any avatar/NPC change. FIX (gate SOL, keep free/ClawToken lobbies working): (1) NEW `WagerClientError` code `'insufficient_funds'`; `createSolLobby` now pre-checks `connection.getBalance(creator)` ≥ `wagerAmount + LOBBY_CREATE_RENT_FEE_BUFFER_LAMPORTS` (6.5M ≈ lobby+vault+creatorPlayer rents + tx fee) and throws `insufficient_funds` with a "fund your wallet or use a free / ClawToken lobby" message BEFORE the chain call; (2) `withChainErrors` now also wraps non-Anchor on-chain tx/simulation failures (`Simulation failed` / `Transaction simulation failed` / `custom program error` / `insufficient lamports` / `SendTransactionError`) as `on_chain_error` so any residual shortfall on a partially-funded wallet is graceful too; (3) route maps `insufficient_funds` → 400. Net: unfunded SOL-lobby creation returns a clean 400, no critical page; settlement / signing / escrow logic UNCHANGED (read-only balance guard + error-classification only — the chain stays the final authority). Shipped isolated to prod (cherry-pick → PR `fix/wager-sol-gate-prod` → master, NOT staging→master, to avoid dragging unrelated staging commits). PARITY: human + connected/hosted-agent create paths both gated identically; no economy/settlement change. Team solo + Codex/solana money-path review.

- 2026-06-15. **Portal outbound SSRF guards + CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_* boot preflight** (security; `fix/portal-ssrf-cloudflare-boot`; `apps/api/src/routes/portal.ts` + `apps/api/src/index.ts`). Two HIGH partner-protected-surface hardenings, both invariant-compliance/defense-in-depth (the URLs are server-configured, not attacker input). **#2 Portal outbound SSRF:** the two outbound partner session-issue `fetch()`es in `portal.ts` skipped the SSRF guard the cognition path (`agent-substrate-client.ts`) uses. Now the Hatcher fetch (`POST /api/portal/hatcher`, was `portal.ts:738`) routes through `validateHatcherProxyUrlResolved` (hatcher: host allowlist + DNS private-IP reject) and the 'scape fetch (`POST /api/portal/scape`, was `portal.ts:250`, previously UNGUARDED — no validate call existed above it) through `validateOutboundUrlResolved` (DNS private-IP reject, NO host allowlist — 'scape is not a Hatcher host). Both validators are reused from `hatcher-config.ts` unchanged. Each fails CLOSED on a rejected/unresolvable URL: 503 (`scape_url_unconfigured` / `hatcher_url_unconfigured`) + a `portal.<partner>.cross_failed` event with `reason:'ssrf_blocked', detail:<reason>`, and NEVER fetches the un-validated URL. Each guarded fetch sets `redirect:'error'` (stricter than the cognition path's `redirect:'manual'`; both treat a 3xx as a hard fail) so an allowlisted-but-compromised/rebinding host can't 3xx-bounce us to 169.254.169.254 / RFC1918 / localhost and re-leak the signed issuer payload. Failure mode for a non-allowlisted / private-resolving / unresolvable URL: 503 fail-closed, no outbound request. **#3 CF Worker boot preflight:** CLAUDE.md declares `CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_*` "hard-required on boot", but `keypair-vault.ts` only threw LAZILY (first envelope-encryption use) — a misconfigured box booted and failed silently on the first wallet op. Added an explicit BOOT assertion in `index.ts` (just after the port log, before `startSimulation`): if `CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_URL` OR `CLOUDFLARE_WORKER_BEARER` is unset, `console.error` + `process.exit(1)` (mirrors the `FINGERPRINT_SECRET` crash-loud pattern). The lazy `requireWorkerEnv` in `keypair-vault.ts` is KEPT as defense-in-depth. Both vars are confirmed set on staging, so the assertion is safe and makes the doc true. PARITY: pure security/infra hardening on the Phase-5.1 custodial + Hatcher partner surface; no human-path, agent-path, or settlement-math change. Gates: `apps/api` `bunx tsc --noEmit` exit 0 (zero new type errors). DOCS same-diff: this entry + §7 (envelope-encryption boot preflight + both portal SSRF notes) + Last Audited bump. Team `portal-ssrf-cf-boot-2026-06-15`.

SUPERSEDES the FIX-1 "v1 autonomous/spectate-only" framing: the shipped deliverable is the OWNER driving the agent's own avatar. **Flow:** owner clicks Launch on Hatcher → `POST /api/portal/mint-for-hatcher` magic link (logs the browser into the agent's bound ClawVille user, `portal.ts:854`) → `/game` runs `POST /api/partner/hatcher/launch/exchange`, which now sends Hatcher `mode:'controlled'` (was `'autonomous'`) and returns `agent.mode:'controlled'` → the launch handler lands the owner in `controlMode:'player'` (NOT explore/spectate; removed `setHatcherSpectate(true)`+`requestCameraFocus`, and defensively clears any stale spectate flag) so `PlayerAvatar` mounts and drives the bound SQL avatar. **Two-body problem + server-side suppression:** the agent's in-world body is a SEPARATE sim NPC (`oc-${sessionId}`, `npc-simulation.ts`) from the human `'player'` avatar, and it is server-driven (`autonomyMode:'server-managed'`) — the browser can't stop it. So `npcSimulation` gains a `humanControlledOpenClawUntil` Map (agentId→epoch-ms) + `isHumanControlledOpenClawNpc`/`markHumanControlledOpenClaw`/`refreshHumanControlledOpenClawForUser`. While the owner drives, the proxy NPC is HIDDEN from `getSnapshot`/`getRoomSnapshot`, SKIPPED by `planNpcBehaviors`/`getIdleAliveNpcs`/`findNearestIdleNpc`/`moveNpcs`, FROZEN on prime (path cleared), and its Hatcher `[ACTION:]` tags are stripped un-executed in `dispatchHatcherActions`. The exchange primes the 3s window (keyed on the namespaced agentId); `POST /api/world/position` (the 5 Hz / 200 ms player-drive signal) refreshes it for every proxy bound to `presence.userId`. Suppression auto-EXPIRES (lazy prune) when uploads stop — switching to explore mode (uploads gated off there) resumes autonomy in ~3 s, and `unregisterOpenClaw` drops the entry. **Identity invariant ENFORCED (Codex review):** suppression only fires when the launching Lucia session IS the agent's `boundUserId`, so the exchange now hard-rejects `!row.userId` → 409 `agent_not_bound` and `row.userId !== user.id` → 403 `agent_not_owned` (both new on `HatcherLaunchExchangeResponse.error`) instead of silently producing a duplicate body. **Multi-agent precision + bubble-leak FIXED (follow-up, same session):** suppression is now keyed to the LAUNCHED agent, not all of a user's bound proxies — a server-side `humanControlledOpenClawLaunchesByUser: Map<userId, Set<agentId>>` (npc-simulation.ts) is set by `bindHumanControlledOpenClawLaunch(user.id, namespacedAgentId)` in the exchange success path, so `refreshHumanControlledOpenClawForUser` re-ups ONLY the launched agent(s) (a user's other Hatcher proxies keep running autonomously); the binding outlives the 3s TTL so a resumed upload after a >3s stall re-primes the exact driven agent (no permanent two-body after a stall), and `unregisterOpenClaw` calls `forgetHumanControlledOpenClawLaunch` to clean it. Both snapshots also now filter active conversations where either participant (`npc1Id`/`npc2Id`) is suppressed, so a hidden proxy can't leave a lingering speech bubble. CONFIRMED 2026-06-15: Hatcher aligned `/launch/exchange` to the spec + deployed to prod + repointed Hatcher PROD → ClawVille PROD (issuer `.well-known`s smoke-checked both ways); controlled launch live end-to-end prod↔prod. PARITY: human path = magic-link `/game` → `'player'`; agent path = the SAME bound avatar the owner now drives (the agent's autonomous proxy is the one suspended); settlement/identity bind to `row.userId`.
