- 2026-06-12. Multiplayer Phase 1 STICKY ROOM REJOIN across restarts (`apps/api/src/services/room-ticket.ts` NEW + `room-registry.ts` + `routes/world.ts` + web `hooks/use-world-stream.ts`; tests `__tests__/room-ticket.test.ts` NEW + `room-registry.test.ts`). PROBLEM: `RoomRegistry` is an in-memory singleton, so every API deploy/restart wipes all rooms; real clients auto-recover (`recoverFrom409` re-runs join) but a bare rejoin gets AUTO-FILLED, so a deploy could SPLIT a group of friends who were together in one room. MECHANISM (mirrors `agent-session-restore.ts`: persist the minimum durable anchor, rebuild on demand, fail closed — here the durable anchor is a signed ticket the CLIENT holds, ZERO server state / DB writes). On `/join` the server mints an HMAC-SHA256 recovery ticket `base64url(payload).base64url(MAC)` over `{roomId, sub, exp}`, signed with a DERIVED sub-key `sha256(FINGERPRINT_SECRET + "room-rejoin-ticket-v1:")` (NOT the bare secret — avoids cross-domain key reuse with `derivePublicId`+`fpHash`, and the `v1` allows independent rotation; the master secret is env-stable across restarts, which is exactly why a pre-deploy ticket verifies post-deploy; right blast radius — a leak forges only room PLACEMENT, never CT/identity, so NOT the issuer key) and self-expiring at `ROOM_TICKET_TTL_MS=15min` (comfortably above a Coolify restart + client reconnect-backoff window so a deploy never expires stickiness mid-recovery). `signRoomTicket`/`verifyRoomTicket` use `createHmac`+`timingSafeEqual` (admin-only.ts pattern), fail-closed on every malformed/tampered/expired/wrong-key input. SECRET-BOUND SUBJECT (auditor trap — do NOT bind to `publicId`): the ticket commits to `sub = sha256(sessionId + subject-salt).slice(32)` (`deriveTicketSubject`), a non-reversible commitment to the RAW sessionId — a secret only the real session can present (Lucia cookie bearer / `a:<agentId>` handle / guest fp) — with a salt DISTINCT from `derivePublicId`'s so `sub` is never the value broadcast on the wire as `publicId` (binding to the wire-public publicId would let any co-member who saw it redeem "as you" given a valid MAC). The raw bearer itself never enters the token (a logged/dumped ticket must not yield a spendable credential — same rule as agent-session-restore.ts hashing the bearer into `session_key_hash`). The client stores the ticket (`roomTicketRef`) and replays it ONLY on a RECOVERY rejoin (the `recoverFrom409` path + the SSE-reconnect retry — first-time joins unchanged, send only the `?room=` deeplink). `POST /join` accepts `roomTicket?`, verifies it, AND requires `claims.sub === deriveTicketSubject(liveSessionId)` so a captured ticket authorizes ONLY its own session — a guest CANNOT replay someone else's ticket to pin arbitrary ID-space, keeping the B2 anti-spam guard intact (the bare-`requestedRoomId` guest path is UNCHANGED, still drops unknown codes to auto-fill). A verified ticket sets the new `JoinOptions.recoveryRoomId`; `pickOrCreateRoom` honors it at HIGHEST precedence: room exists with capacity → re-land; room WIPED by restart → RECREATE with the same id (guests included — the ticket, not an auth check, is the proof); room at the HARD cap (20) → spill to auto-fill. Recovery deliberately BYPASSES the soft cap (12) — reconverging a group of up to 20 is the whole point — but NEVER breaches the hard cap (VRM/draw-call ceiling). An already-seated session ignores `recoveryRoomId` (idempotent in-place refresh wins). If recovery lands in a different roomId than before, the client re-points the SSE (old stream is no longer a member). PARITY: agents recover identically — `/join` runs the same `resolvePresence`→ticket path; an agent's ticket subject derives from its `a:<agentId>` sessionId; no separate code path. DOCS same-diff: this entry + `GameFeatures.md` (deploys no longer split rooms) + §13 Last Audited bump. Gates: `bunx tsc --noEmit` apps/api exit 0; room-registry suite + room-ticket suite 58/58 green (31 pre-existing soft-cap/registry + 8 sticky-recovery + 19 ticket incl. secret-bound-subject + route-redemption-decision lock cases); `bunx next build` exit 0; selftest-e2e 75/75. AUDIT NOTE: auditor APPROVED the design with two must-fix conditions, both implemented — (1) bind the ticket to a SECRET-derived subject of the raw sessionId, NOT the wire-public `publicId` (publicId is broadcast to every room member, so binding to it is forgeable by a co-member); (2) HMAC with a domain-separated sub-key off `FINGERPRINT_SECRET`, not the bare secret; plus the id-pinning security property is TEST-LOCKED via a `resolveRecoveryRoomId` suite. `world.ts` `/join` DELEGATES to the EXPORTED `resolveRecoveryRoomId(ticket, liveSessionId)` (the verify + `claims.sub === deriveTicketSubject(liveSessionId)` guard), so the unit suite exercises the LITERAL route code, not a mirror that could drift — a regression that drops the subject check fails here (wrong-session ticket / tampered roomId / expired → rejected, falls through to auto-fill). verifyRoomTicket is total (no throw on garbage) with a pre-`timingSafeEqual` length guard; the join body's `roomTicket` is `z.string().max(512)`. Team `sticky-rooms-2026-06-12`.
- 2026-06-12. Multiplayer Phase 1 soft-cap flexible room fill (`apps/api/src/services/room-registry.ts` + `__tests__/room-registry.test.ts`). Added `ROOM_SOFT_CAP_PLAYERS = 12` (loose auto-fill target) alongside the unchanged hard cap `ROOM_MAX_PLAYERS = 20`. `pickOrCreateRoom` auto-fill now packs a joiner into the FULLEST room still under the soft cap (tie-break lowest id, deterministic) and mints a fresh room only once every room has reached 12 — replacing the old "lowest-id room with capacity < 20" rule. This keeps rooms cozy (29 sequential auto-joins distribute 12 / 12 / 5; the 29th joins the 5-room) and never seeds the 12-to-20 band, which is reserved headroom for friends joining a specific room via `requestedRoomId`. The invite-code path is UNCHANGED: honored up to the hard cap of 20, never-before-seen codes minted for authenticated callers only (B2). Tests: rewrote the old "21st player spills" case to fill via invite to the hard cap (auto-fill no longer reaches 20) and added a soft-cap suite (fullest-under-soft selection, tie-break determinism, 13th-auto-join mints, headroom-band-skip, invite-into-band, invite-at-20-rejected, 12/12/5 distribution). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api/tsconfig.json` exit 0; room-registry suite 31/31 green.
- 2026-06-06. Multiplayer Phase 1 security + agent parity (audit fix; `apps/api/src/routes/world.ts`, `services/room-registry.ts`, `services/npc-simulation.ts`, `packages/shared/src/types/world.ts`, `middleware/require-auth-or-agent.ts`, `services/skill-protocol.ts` + web `stores/players.ts`, `hooks/use-world-stream.ts`, `lib/three/{lod-orchestrator,remote-players,remote-player-proxy}.tsx`). **(1) Session-token leak CLOSED (BLOCKING).** `getPlayerSnapshots` was emitting each player's raw `sessionId` on the wire, and for a logged-in user `resolveSessionId` returned the Lucia `session.id` (the session-cookie BEARER TOKEN in Lucia 3.x), broadcast to every SSE subscriber. `PlayerState` now carries a non-reversible `publicId = sha256(sessionId + FINGERPRINT_SECRET).slice(0,16)` (new `derivePublicId` in `room-registry.ts`); the wire `PlayerSnapshot.sessionId` was renamed to an opaque `id` and only `publicId` is ever emitted (the raw sessionId / guest fp / agent id never leave the server). `POST /join` echoes that same `id` so the client can resolve `isLocal`. **(2) SSE membership gate (BLOCKING).** `GET /api/world/:roomId/stream` had NO membership check, so any caller could subscribe to an active room and harvest its roster; it now resolves the caller's presence and 403s unless they are a member of that room (`roomRegistry.getRoomForSession`), with the `solo-` alias exempt (no other session's data to leak). **(3) Admin `/rooms` roster** no longer emits raw `sessionId` (uses `publicId` + `kind`). **(4) Agent parity (Rule E5).** `resolvePresence` now resolves precedence Lucia user > agent session (`X-Clawville-Agent-Session` validated by `validateLiveAgentSession`, exported header constant) > guest; an avatar-bound agent joins AS ITSELF (real name/species/position, counted toward `ROOM_MAX_PLAYERS`, swap-eligible) with `kind:'agent'`. New `kind: 'human'|'guest'|'agent'` field on `PlayerState` + `PlayerSnapshot` drives the connected-agent indicator dot (`remote-players.tsx` maps `kind==='agent'` → `isOpenClaw`). PARITY note: human path = `POST /api/world/join` via Lucia cookie; agent path = same route via `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` → `validateLiveAgentSession` → bound avatar; presence id is the hashed `publicId`, counted toward the room cap and swap-eligible. **(5) Collab log re-enabled (MAJOR).** `broadcast()` drained the collaboration broker only in the legacy global path (no `/game` consumer after the SSE room swap), so the COLLAB tab + the 40-pt `agent.collaboration.turn` signal were invisible; the broker is now drained EXACTLY ONCE per tick and the shared array is attached to every per-room snapshot AND the global snapshot (dead `buildBroadcastSnapshot` removed). **(6) MINORs:** guests/avatar-less agents now spawn at town center (2560,2560) instead of (0,0); stale "tick * 500ms" comments corrected to 200ms. **(7) Protocol manual** §3 documents the agent world-join capability + `PROTOCOL_VERSION` 2 → 3; Nori `knowledge[]` gains a multiplayer co-presence/parity line. `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api/tsconfig.json` exit 0; `packages/shared` build clean; room-registry tests 24/24 green.
- 2026-06-03. Agent-session shared fail-closed live-session resolver (round 3, Codex auth-lens follow-up: the round-1 TTL fix was INCOMPLETE). The same `X-Clawville-Agent-Session` bearer was trusted by OTHER real-CT/auth paths that round-1 never patched, so they could drift. Structural fix: ONE shared `validateLiveAgentSession(sessionId)` in `apps/api/src/middleware/require-auth-or-agent.ts` (Map membership AND DB `session_expires_at > now`, NULL = expired/fail-closed, unregister stale body, returns `{config, bot}`), and EVERY bearer-trusting path now routes through it. Paths converted: (a) `resolveSession` (the gateway resolver for `/api/agent/:sessionId/*`, DISTINCT from the cove `resolveAgentSession`) was bare Map membership with NO DB TTL (its consumers `visit-building` + `building-chat` credit REAL CT, so an EXPIRED session kept earning); now async + shared-validated, all ~17 callers awaited, the `/events` SSE re-validates each tick through it too (BLOCKING 1); (b) `POST /api/auth/milady-session-exchange` (`auth.ts`) trusted Map membership then minted a LUCIA COOKIE, so an expired-but-in-memory bearer exchanged into a full authed browser session; now shared-validated before the cookie (BLOCKING 2); (c) the three remaining bare `isValidAgentSession` reads (`/:sessionId/knowledge`, `/:sessionId/cove/blackjack/skill-memory`, the cove-tool forwarder pre-filter) upgraded for consistency. `resolveAgentSession` refactored to call the shared gate (single source of the TTL logic). (BLOCKING 3) First-contact stays NON-LEDGER, by design (orchestrator decision): the inconsistency the lens flagged was "first-contact claims `ledgerCapable=true` but can't actually play": its `boundUserId` is null and the bot row's `userId` is null, so the round-2 resolve-time backstop (`config.boundUserId === liveBot.userId`, both non-null) DEMOTES it. That demotion RESOLVES the contradiction: first-contact is simply non-ledger, consistently, and the cove rejects it like any no-active-avatar session. We deliberately do NOT bind the bot row's `userId` back to the agent's self-resolved user here. "A first-contact /connect agent plays its OWN avatar for real CT" is a deferred FEATURE (needs the bot-row userId bind + active-avatar creation), tracked as FOLLOW-UP #6, NOT this security pass. First-contact agents reach real-CT play via the owned-connection-token claim or the ed25519 partner-signed Hatcher path (both ledger-capable). (MINOR 5) `/api/agent/session-status` treated `sessionExpiresAt === null` as connected, contradicting fail-closed; now NULL returns 410 `{connected:false, expired:true}`. (The `/me/agent-session` UI-status sibling is a requireAuth self-read with no bearer trust / no cookie mint / no CT, so it was intentionally left as-is to avoid an unrequested banner-UX change.) (MINOR 6) connect-token single-use: deterministic input validation (override targetNpcId) moved BEFORE the `pending.connected=true` reservation so a 400 no longer burns the token, and the reservation now ROLLS BACK (`pending.connected = false`) on a post-flip pre-session failure (DB upsert error, override-already-taken 409) so the human can retry with the same token. cove `getSubject` confirmed correct, untouched. `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api/tsconfig.json` exit 0. No new test failures (16 pre-existing slot-engine/bumper/room-sweeper/avatar, flaky-timing on the sim/bot set, unrelated). Team: `cove-bj-parity-2026-06-03`.
- 2026-06-03. Agent-session ledgerCapable rebind hardening (round 2, Codex auth-lens + adversary-sec follow-up). Closes a CT-theft vector that survived the round-1 `ledgerCapable` work: the flag is frozen at registration, but the bound `userId` on the live `openclaw_bots` row can CHANGE underneath a still-registered session. An attacker registers a ledger-capable session while an agentId is unbound (first-contact grants `ledgerCapable=true`), then a later OWNED-token connect rebinds that same agentId's row to a VICTIM; the stale session would then resolve against the victim's avatar with its frozen-true flag and spend the victim's real CT. Belt-and-suspenders on a money path: (Option B, primary) `/api/agent/connect` now EVICTS every prior in-memory session for the agentId (via `npcSimulation.findActiveSessionsByAgentIds` + `unregisterOpenClaw`, the same helper partner-hatcher uses) BEFORE registering the new session WHEN the upsert rebinds the row's owner (`tokenUserId !== null && tokenUserId !== existingBoundUserId`); (Option A, backstop) a new in-memory `boundUserId` field on `OpenClawBotConfig` records the userId the session proved ownership of, and `resolveAgentSession` grants `ledgerCapable` ONLY when `config.boundUserId === liveBot.userId` (both non-null), else demotes to non-ledger and, on a real rebind to a DIFFERENT user, unregisters the stale session and returns null. `boundUserId` is set to the value actually PERSISTED on the row: `tokenUserId ?? existingBoundUserId` on `/connect`, `row.userId` on both partner-hatcher register + patch (NOT the request-local `userId`, since the upsert keeps `userId ?? existing.userId`), and explicit `null` on legacy `/api/openclaw/register`. Legacy `/openclaw/register` deliberately does NOT evict: it never writes `openclaw_bots.userId` so it cannot rebind, and a blanket eviction would let an unauthenticated caller knock a victim's live ledger session out of the map by re-registering the victim's known agentId (a griefing/DoS vector). A first-contact/anonymous session (boundUserId null, row userId still null) stays alive (perceive/chat) but is non-ledger, which matches the cove's existing no-active-avatar 403. Cases covered: /connect agentId rebind (evict + backstop), partner-hatcher rebind (already evicts; backstop added), resolve-time re-validation for any missed eviction, legacy-register non-rebind (no-evict by design). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api/tsconfig.json` exit 0; shared rebuilt. No new test failures (16 pre-existing slot-engine/bumper/room-sweeper/avatar, unrelated). Team: `cove-bj-parity-2026-06-03`.
- 2026-06-03. Agent-session bearer-credential hardening (Codex auth-lens review of the cove-blackjack agent-parity, 5 BLOCKING). The agent session id is the bearer the cove trusts for REAL-CT play, and the model leaked/forged it. Fixes: (1) `GET /api/openclaw/active` (PUBLIC) no longer returns any recoverable session id. `npcSimulation.getActiveOpenClawBots()` dropped the raw `sessionId` AND stopped emitting the avatar `oc-${sid}` npcId (which embedded the sid); it now returns only non-secret ids `{ agentId, mode, npcId?(override targetNpcId), name? }`. Web `api.ts` typedef updated (no live UI caller). (2) `GET /api/agent/connect-status/:token` now gates the session id to the ORIGINAL Lucia user who issued the token (cookie userId === pending.userId). Non-owners (leaked-URL holders) get a redacted response (connected flag only); the pending row is deleted once the owner reads the connected session id (read-once bearer). Handler made async. (3) TTL enforcement: `resolveAgentSession` (`require-auth-or-agent.ts`) previously trusted in-memory Map membership alone, so a session the sweeper marked expired in the DB kept spending real CT; it now hard-gates on `openclaw_bots.session_expires_at > now`, returns null on expiry (NULL expiry treated as expired, fail-closed), AND `npcSimulation.unregisterOpenClaw()` tears down the stale in-memory body. (4) Ledger-capability model for bound-bot takeover: a new in-memory-only `ledgerCapable` flag on `OpenClawBotConfig` (`packages/shared/src/types/agent-substrate.ts`, defaults FALSE, fail-closed) is set TRUE only on proven ownership: owned Moltbook connection token (`tokenUserId !== null`), genuine first-contact (`existingBoundUserId === null`), or the ed25519 partner-signed Hatcher path (`partner-hatcher.ts`, both register + patch). An `agentId`-only reconnect to an ALREADY-bound bot, and every legacy `/api/openclaw/register` session, are FALSE. `resolveAgentSession` forwards it; `cove-blackjack.ts` getSubject rejects a non-ledger agent session with 403 `agent_session_not_ledger_authorized` BEFORE the avatar binding (NOT a guest demote, NOT real-CT play; perceive/chat/move still allowed). (5) connect-token single-use race: `/api/agent/connect` flipped `pending.connected` only after awaited DB work, so two concurrent claims both passed; it now atomically reserves the token (flips `connected` synchronously, captures the pending object once as `pendingConn`) BEFORE any await. Unaffected legit flows: Hatcher partner-signed register (ledger-capable), Moltbook owned-token claim (ledger-capable), first-contact self-avatar (ledger-capable), signed-challenge magic-link `/reconnect` (mints a Lucia ticket, registers no cove session). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api/tsconfig.json` exit 0; shared rebuilt. Plan/team: `cove-bj-parity-2026-06-03`.
- 2026-06-03. Cove blackjack agent parity (connected/hosted agents play AS THEMSELVES; Rule E5 + human-parity directive). (1) Identity resolver in `apps/api/src/routes/cove-blackjack.ts` extended additively: alongside authed-user XOR guest it now resolves a connected/hosted AGENT SESSION (via the agent-gateway session resolver / Phase 5 magic-link ticket) to the agent's bound avatar, so settlement (`claw-token-ledger.transferClawTokens()`) and leaderboard credit bind to the agent's avatar in REAL CT (agents never fall into the guest/demo tier). Human + guest paths intact. (2) Play surface (hybrid): in-world `[ACTION: enter_cove()]` verb (no params) added to the `apps/api/src/services/npc-simulation.ts` `dispatchHatcherActions`/`executeHatcherAction` whitelist (Object.hasOwn guarded) plus four session-bound TOOLS (`cove_blackjack_open_session`, `cove_blackjack_deal`, `cove_blackjack_action`, `cove_blackjack_close_session`) served at `/api/agent/:sessionId/cove/blackjack/tools.json` + `POST .../:tool`; server authoritative, agent never sees dealer hole / undealt shoe / seed before reveal; `blackjack-engine.ts` provably-fair logic byte-for-byte unchanged. (3) NEW `apps/api/src/services/game-skill-memory.ts`: per-hand earned-skill memory (`metadata.subtype:'game-skill'`, `skill:'blackjack'`, `subskill:'<situation>'`) via `createMemory()` for hosted agents (memory_recommendation payload for connected), and pre-existing skill memory informs play; read at `GET /api/agent/:sessionId/cove/blackjack/skill-memory`. One live autonomous surface is the agent playing from its OWN runtime via these tools (no browser, no human). (4) NEW relay `POST /api/cove/blackjack/agent/decide` on `coveBlackjackRouter` (`apps/api/src/routes/cove-blackjack.ts`) + the frontend in-modal supervised driver in `apps/web/src/components/cove/blackjack/BlackjackModal.tsx` (LIVE): when a human at /game has a connected agent and flips THEIR open table to Autonomous, the browser POSTs `{shoeId}` to the relay; the relay (requireAuth, human's own cookie; ownership-checked `shoe.userId===user.id`) resolves the human's bound connected agent, derives the authoritative in-progress hand + slot from the shoe, asks the agent's runtime for a move, and returns `{action, amount?, handId, handSlot, rationale, source}` (a pure decision oracle, never deals/settles, never returns cards/hole/seed). The browser shows the rationale in the read-only advisor panel and applies the decision through the same `/hand/deal` (`bet=amount`) or `/action` (`handId,action,handSlot`) endpoints AFTER the 8s/15s human-veto window; a human tap pre-empts via a monotonic run token (buttons stay enabled in Autonomous for takeover). Gated on `AUTONOMOUS_RELAY_LIVE = true` + an agent connected. Fallbacks: 503 (self-managed nanoclaw agent that can't be push-asked) / 403 / 404 / 409 degrade to Control with a notice; 422 `agent_undecided` skips just that one decision and stays in Autonomous. Client helpers `fetchAgentBlackjackDecision` + `AgentDriverUnavailableError` + `AgentUndecidedError` in `apps/web/src/lib/cove/blackjack-api-client.ts`. (5) Three-surface sync same-diff: `PROTOCOL_VERSION` 1 to 2 in `apps/api/src/services/skill-protocol.ts`, `buildProtocolManual` §7 documents the cove flow (contentHash auto-recomputes for manifest + pointer parity), Nori `knowledge[]` (`town-guide.ts`) + `CLAWVILLE_ORIENTATION_KNOWLEDGE` (`orientation-skill.ts`, which also feeds new-avatar characterConfig and the export bundle = the hosted-agent runtime surface) updated. The `/agent/decide` relay is browser-facing (not agent-facing) so it adds NO further `PROTOCOL_VERSION` bump beyond the 1 to 2 that ships the agent tools. FEATURE_GATE `blackjack_autonomous_agent_mode` = WIRED (gateway-cognition agents; nanoclaw degrades to Control; review 2026-07-15). Known LATENT follow-up: a server-side rate-limit on `/agent/decide` (today only the 8s/15s client window throttles). Plan: `.claude/plans/cove-blackjack.md`. PARITY: a connected/hosted agent and a human play the identical server-authoritative blackjack for real CT. The agent plays autonomously from its own runtime, and a human can hand the wheel to its connected agent in-modal via the decide relay (gateway agents; nanoclaw plays from its runtime). Hardening (Codex dual-review): settleTransaction now re-asserts shoe ownership under the FOR UPDATE lock (defense-in-depth; not exploitable via current call sites). Hardening (Codex dual-review): /agent/decide returns handVersion + /action enforces an optional expectedHandVersion precondition under the lock, so a human tap beats a stale in-flight agent decision server-side. Hardening (Codex money + concurrency lens, 2026-06-03): (a) settleTransaction now loads the hand by BOTH (id, shoeId) under the lock, never by handId alone, so a caller passing their own shoeId plus a victim hand id resolves to no row (409 hand_shoe_mismatch) instead of settling the victim hand outcome to the caller avatar; (b) the agent DEAL is now epoch-guarded: /agent/decide returns expectedHandsPlayed (the shoe handCounter snapshot at decision time), the driver threads it to /hand/deal, and /hand/deal rejects (409 stale_agent_deal) under the shoe FOR UPDATE lock if handCounter advanced since (an intervening human deal opened, possibly natural-settled inline, a hand) so a stale agent deal cannot open an extra unwanted hand; handCounter is the chosen epoch because it strictly increments by 1 at every deal independent of natural-vs-in-progress outcome. Both new fields are OPTIONAL and OMITTED on human manual deal/action, so human play stays byte-for-byte unconditional. Driver minors: the agent action apply now targets the relay server-authoritative decision.handId (not the modal local handId), and mergeActionInProgress stamps res.handId into the local view so the UI tracks the server hand after an agent action. Stale-409 modal-strand fix (Codex cove lens BLOCKING, 2026-06-03): a 409 stale_agent_decision/stale_agent_deal now re-derives the AUTHORITATIVE state from the server before the next Autonomous loop via a NEW read-only GET /api/cove/blackjack/hand/current (subject-resolved, ledger subjects only, no mutation/money/ledger; stand-only peek returns the visible player cards + dealer UPCARD only, never the hole/seed) which returns the in-progress hand view or {hand:null} when the prior hand settled. resyncAfterStaleDecision RESTORES the live hand when one exists and CLEARS only when the server confirms none, so the modal never strands in idle while the server still holds an in_progress hand (the prior shoe-only refetch left the autonomous loop spinning: runAction no-ops on !hand and a deal 409s hand_in_progress). Client helper fetchCurrentBlackjackHand + CurrentHandResponse/isCurrentHandLive in blackjack-api-client.ts. Correct for ALL staleness sources, not just same-tab takeover.

- 2026-05-29 — Cove casino economy fixes + CT-economy monitor. (1) **Baccarat** (`apps/api/src/services/baccarat-engine.ts` `settleBet`): banker WIN now floors the PLAYER's winnings `floor(stake*95/100)` (new const `BANKER_WIN_NUMERATOR=95n`); `commission = stake - winnings`. Closes the sub-20-stake faucet (commission ≥ 1 at every stake). Player 1:1 / Tie 8:1 / P/B push UNCHANGED; serialized `commission` field semantics unchanged (the kept fraction). (2) **Hold'em** (`holdem-engine.ts` new pure `computeHoldemRake` + consts `HOLDEM_RAKE_PERCENT=5n`/`HOLDEM_RAKE_CAP=5n`; applied in `apps/api/src/routes/cove-holdem.ts` settle under the table FOR UPDATE lock): pot rake `min(floor(pot*5/100),5)` removed before awarding, distributed pro-rata to winners; the human's raked share credited to `player_stack`; `holdem_hands.payout`/`net` + `cove_game_events.payout` store post-rake figures; `outcomeJson` gains `rake`/`humanRakedPayout`/`humanRakedNet`. (3) **Blackjack** (`blackjack-engine.ts` new pure `computeBlackjackRake` + const `BLACKJACK_RAKE_PERCENT=5n`; applied in `cove-blackjack.ts` settle under the shoe lock): rake `floor(max(0,totalPayout-totalBet)*5/100)` on net winnings (winners only); credits `rakedPayout`; `blackjack_hands.payout`/`net` + `cove_game_events.payout` store post-rake; `outcomeJson` gains `rake`/`rakedPayout`/`rakedNet`. All idempotent — computed once at settle, stored, never re-applied on a settled-replay. Shared wire types `cove-holdem.ts`/`cove-blackjack.ts` gained optional `rake`/raked fields. (4) **NEW admin-only route** `apps/api/src/routes/cove-economy.ts` mounted `app.route('/api/cove/economy', ...)` — `GET /summary?window={24h|7d|30d|all}&subjects={all|users}` aggregates `cove_game_events` `minted=Σpayout`/`burned=Σbet_amount`/`houseNet=burned-minted` per `game_type` (+ grand total + `faucets[]` alarm). `adminOnly` + `sessionMiddleware`. FEATURE_GATE `cove_ct_economy_monitor`. No schema change. `bunx tsc --noEmit` clean on `apps/api` + `packages/database`; 146 engine unit tests green (baccarat/holdem/blackjack). Design: `.claude/plans/cove-casino-economy.md`.

- 2026-05-22 — NPC pathfinding made collider-aware. `apps/api/src/services/pathfinding.ts` rasterizes every `getServerColliders()` AABB (10 buildings + 8 props) onto the walkability grid with per-axis pad `ceil(half/32)+4`, replacing the fixed `BUILDING_EXCLUSION_PAD=11` that under-covered messaging-channels/api-integrations and ignored all town-center prop AABBs (shisha-oasis, bazaar-stall, auction-podium, quest-bounty-pavilion, marketplace-stall, town-directory-sign, quest-npc, town-guide). New exports `isCollisionFreeWorld()` + `findNearestWalkable()` + `WORLD_COLLIDER_TILE_SIZE`. `apps/api/src/services/npc-simulation.ts`: new `stuckTicks` field on `NpcRuntimeState`, private `snapPlannerTarget()` helper applied to all 6 planners, abandon-on-clamp added to `moveTowardTarget` (previously path-step had it but direct-target walkers did not — silently wedged forever), stand-off-toward-chaser in `planApproachNpc`. New shared constants `ENTITY_HALF_CHIBI`/`ENTITY_HALF_HUMANOID` re-exported from `world-colliders-data.ts` (server cannot import the browser-only client file). `bunx tsc --noEmit -p apps/api` exit 0; `-p packages/shared` exit 0; web typecheck no new errors related to these files.

- 2026-05-19 — Concern 6.1.6 + 6.1.7 (slice 5): casino slots frontend wire + verifier UI. `apps/web/src/lib/casino/mock-engine.ts` DELETED. New `slot-api-client.ts` (TanStack Query — open/spin/close/session-detail/session-spins/paytable/verify hooks; `Idempotency-Key = crypto.randomUUID()` per spin press; BigInt as string on wire). New `verifier.ts` (WebCrypto port of `provable-rng` + `slot-engine.runSpin`; HMAC keyed by `hexToBytes(serverSeed)` raw bytes, sha256 over UTF-8 of hex string; identical bytes to server). New routes `/casino/verify` (anonymous manual replay form, local-vs-remote side-by-side, sha256 commit recompute) and `/casino/verify/[sessionId]` (auth-gated owner-only — re-derives every spin in a closed session, green/red per row, commit-hash check). `SlotScreenModal.tsx` rewired: lazy-open session on first spin, fairness chip + tooltip with deeplink, cash-out reveals seed, toast for 4xx/501. `SlotHUD` bet chips now `[20,40,100,200,500,1000]` (must be divisible by 20). `casino/page.tsx` gets top-right "🔐 Verify" link. `casino.ts` Zustand now tracks server session metadata. Tests at `apps/web/src/lib/casino/__tests__/verifier.test.ts` (20 pass) — TV1/TV2/TV3/TV5/TV6 byte-identity + `runSpinLocal` byte-identity (`reels=[[7,1,1],[1,3,0],[3,1,1],[1,4,3],[2,2,0]]`, `winAmount=55`, `cursorAfter=20`). No new API routes, no new DB tables.
- 2026-05-19 — Concern 6.1.5 (slice 4): Monte Carlo RTP CI gate + reel strip retune. New `scripts/casino/rtp-sim.ts` — 1M-spin simulator on top of `runSpin` + `CLASSIC_PAYTABLE`; flags `--spins <n>`, `--bet <n>`, `--seed <64hex>`, `--client-seed <hex>`, `--strict-rtp <lo>,<hi>`, `--exit-on-fail`; reports RTP + hit freq + max win + 6-bucket histogram + per-symbol middle-row hit rate. 1M spins in ~11s, 100k in ~1.1s on Iris Xe baseline. New `.github/workflows/rtp-gate.yml` — triggers on PR touching `slot-paytables.ts`/`slot-engine.ts`/`provable-rng.ts`/`rtp-sim.ts`/`rtp-gate.yml`; runs 100k Monte Carlo under `--strict-rtp 0.95,0.97 --exit-on-fail`; band wider than local 1M acceptance band [95.5%, 96.5%] to absorb CI-runner Monte Carlo stderr. New fixture test `apps/api/src/services/__tests__/rtp-fixture.test.ts` (10k spins inside `bun test`, asserts [92%, 100%] band + hit-freq sanity + max-win sanity ceiling — catches gross drift on every `bun test` run). Reel strips in `packages/shared/src/constants/slot-paytables.ts` retuned from L=80 third-pass (98.57% analytic / 98.54% sim — over-paying) to L=84 fourth-pass with per-reel composition (Cherry=22, Lemon=22, Orange=14, Plum=14, Bell=7, +1 each of BAR/Seven/WILD/BAR×2/BAR×3) → 96.00% analytic / 95.89% sim @ 1M spins (within ±0.5% target). Payout multipliers in `CLASSIC_SYMBOLS` and the engine itself (`slot-engine.ts`, `provable-rng.ts`) UNCHANGED — only reel strip CONTENTS rebalanced. Strip generator and analytic search helpers were used during tuning but deliberately not committed (one-shot scripts). Tuning rationale (in source comment): Plum 16→14 + Bell 9→7 + Cherry/Lemon 17→22 cuts the Plum-2 (-3.3% RTP) and Bell-2 (-2.5%) contributions; Cherry/Lemon 2-of-kind goes up only ~+5% combined because their 2× multiplier is half of Plum's 4×.
- 2026-05-19 — Concern 6.1.3 + 6.1.4 (slice 3): casino slots fun-money backend wire. New `packages/database/src/schema/casino.ts` — `slot_sessions` (partial unique index on `(user_id) WHERE status='open'`) + `slot_spins` (unique `(session_id, idempotency_key)`). Money columns as `text` so future lamport/µUSDC precision survives. New `apps/api/src/routes/casino-slots.ts` mounted at `/api/casino/slots/*` — open/spin/close/list/paytables/verify. Single-transaction spin path: row-locks session FOR UPDATE, asserts pre-engine counter snapshot still current (concurrent-spin guard), debits bet via `claw-token-ledger`, inserts spin row (idempotency-key 23505 maps to cached replay), updates session, credits winnings. ClawTokens currency live; SOL/USDC → 501 `CURRENCY_COMING_SOON`. Wire bigint serialization in `apps/api/src/routes/casino-slots.types.ts` (`serializeSpinResult` + `serializeWinningLine`) — NO `BigInt.prototype.toJSON` monkey-patch (rationale: global side effects bite event-logger sanitization + third-party deps). User-scoped (not IP-scoped) rate limit at 60 spins/min via in-process bucket. Events: `casino.slots.session.{opened,closed}` + `casino.slots.spin.executed`. Tests at `apps/api/src/routes/__tests__/casino-slots.test.ts` — paytable + verify run unconditionally; lifecycle tests gated on `DATABASE_URL`. Schema `bun run db:push` deferred to deploy environment (local has no DATABASE_URL per testing rule).

Zero new deps — pokie deliberately not pulled in (rationale in module docstring).
